ciṟappuppāyiram.
tamiḻppirama cūttirāciriyarākiya maṟittiruvaṉ
cuvāmi. viruti. civañāṉayōkikaḷ (āyuḷvēta pāskarar) iyaṟṟiyatu.
āciriyappā.
tamiḻ mukkaḻakam
			tirumalar maṇameṉa vorumiyiṉ malki
			eṇṇil poḻiṟpayir paṇṇik kākkum
			oppil perumi meypporu ḷaṉpu
			pāṅki ṉilipeṟ ṟōṅkun tamiḻakat
		5.	tāṟaṟi vuṭimip pēṟuṟu makkaḷ
			muṉṉart tōṉṟi maṉṉik keḻumi
			imiḻiya lolcār tamiḻmoḻi pēci
			maṇṇiṟ palaviṭa naṇṇik kuṭiyirun
			tavvav viṭattuk kovviya vaṇṇam
		10.	kūṟṟu naṭiyuṭi vēṟṟumi yeyti
			paṟpal liṉappeyar peṟṟup perukiṉar
			tamiḻakat tiṉiya tamiḻccol vaḷarntu
			teṉmā maturiyiṉ maṉṉiya pāṇṭiyar
			āṭciyiṟ paṉṉī rāyira vāṇṭu
		15.	muṉṉar mutaṉmik kaḻakan tōṉṟi
			nikaḻnta tataṉil nīḷaṟi vakattiyar
			iyalici nāṭaka meṉumut tamiḻuk
			korunū liyaṟṟi variyā vaḻaṅkap
			paripā ṭalpala varuḷā lurittaṉar.
		20.	mutaṉmik kaḻaka muṉṉīrp paṭṭapiṉ
			eḻā yirattinū ṟāṇṭumuṉ ṟuvakkiya
			kavāṭa purattiṭik kaḻaka mataṉiṟ
			cikaṇṭi yārici nuṇukkañ ceytaṉar.
			mūvā yiratteḻu nūṟiṟ ṟuvakkiya
		25.	kaṭikkaḻa kattuk kaviṉpari pāṭal
			āṟṟaṟ pērici kūttuc ciṟṟici
			nimirvari muṉṉiya vamiḻteṉat tōṉṟiṉa.
āyntu kāṇal
			mutaliṭik kaḻaka muṉṉīr koḷḷap
			paṭutali ṉintiyap paṭiyiṉ vaḷamvavi
		30.	ayaṉāṭ ṭavarī rāyira māṇṭāp
			paṭiyeṭut tuttuṉ puṟuttiya paṭiyāl
			nūlkaḷu mavaṟṟi nuṇṇiya vaḻakkum
			aruki maṟintaṉa vātali ṉavvaḻi
			tirumuṟi kaṇṭa perumic cōḻaṉ
		35.	nīla kaṇṭayāḻp pāṇar marapil
			vantapeṇ vaḻiyā yaṟintu parappiṉaṉ
			nantuta lillā vanta muṟiyil
			tēvā rappēr mūvāt tamiḻmaṟi
			naciyoḻi mēlō ricitiru vicippāp
		40.	paṇṇoṭu paṭikku maṇṇiṉ vaḻakkum
			icinūṉ muṟitē riḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ
			cilappati kārac cevviya moḻiyum,
			paṭimēṟ pulava raṭiyārkku nallār
			uriyiṟ kūṟiya vuyarmēṟ kōḷum,
		45.	paripā ṭalpayil paṇṇiṉ muṟiyum,
			maṟṟuḷa paṉṉū ṉuṭpamum, āyntu;
			ciṟpanūl vallār ciṟitō ruṟuppuk
			kippeṟiṉ maṟṟelāṅ kaṇṭu kaṇakkāṟ
			ṟērtal pōlat teyva nallaruḷ
		50.	kāṭṭap poruḷelāṅ karutti lōrntē;
tamiḻici nūl
			āṭu mutalām paṉṉiru vīṭṭil
			kuraliḷik koṉṟoṉṟu koṭuttin tiṉukkum
			iraṇṭiraṇ ṭāka vīyiṟ paṉṉiraṇ
			ṭilaniṟi veytu; mituvē yāḻiṟ
		55. 	paṉṉiru vīṭṭiṟ payilici yākum;
			atiyiraṭ ṭikka virupattu nāṉku
			kēḷvi varunili mūṉṟiṉuṅ keḻumum;
			otta vaḷaviṟ pāttaṟ kovvum;
			iṇikiḷi naṭpup pakimuṟik kiyalum;
		60.	paḻantami ḻicinūṟ pālika ṇāṉkiṟ
			paṉṉī rilliṟ payilva tāyam;
			āya miraṭṭikki lākum vaṭṭam;
			vaṭṭa miraṭṭikkil varuntiri kōṇam;
			kōṇa miraṭṭikkiṟ kulavuñ caturam;
		65.	aṟunāṉ kalakuka ḷiliyē loṉṟumūṉ
			ṟintē ḻalaki loṉṟā kēḷvi;
			kōṇañ catura miliyēṟ kēḷvik
			kālari mukkāṟ kaṇakkuk kiṭamili;
			ātali ṉuḷki yaṟimi ṉeṉṟum;
		70.	vaṭṭap pāliyil yāḻvaki nāṉkum
			paṉṉiru pāliyum pāliyē ḻēḻum
			perumpaṇ ṇāṉkum cāti nāṉkum
			uḷavatil viḷari kikkiḷik kovvōr
			alaku kuṟittiru pattiraṇ ṭākki
		75.	icikka nūlka ḷicittaṉa veṉṟum;
			pāli nāṉkiṟ paṉṉī rāyiram
			paṇkaḷ tamiḻiṟ palkiṉa veṉṟum;
			teṉṉaṉ civaṉē maṉṉevi kaṭku
			muṉṉiṟi yākko ḷiṉṟamiḻ vaḻakkāl,
		80.	tamiḻkkaḻa kaṅkaḷ taḻikkac ceyta
			teṉṉavar marapiṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟik
			kaṭalkōṭ paṭṭa kālit tamiḻakam
			marakkalat tuyntu maṟuttu mūrceyta
			catyavi rataṉeṉac catapatap pirāmaṇam
		85.	eḻiṟpā kavata mēṉip purāṇam
			palavum pakarvi vaccuta maṉuvum
			avarvaḻi vanta pāṇṭiya raṉivarum
			tamiḻici vaḷartta taliva rātaliṉ
			teṉṉā teṉāveṉu māḷatti yituvari
		90.	maṉṉi vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkuḷa teṉṟum;
			irukku muṉṉiya veḻiṉmaṟi mūṉṟum
			nālici yōṭu naṇṇip piṉṉart
			tamiḻa ṉirāvaṇa ṉamarē ḻiciyoṭu
			cāmam pāṭit taṇṇaruḷ peṟṟaṉaṉ
		95.	atumuta lammaṟi yēḻici yōṭu
			vaḻaṅkal kēṭṭaliṟ paḻampuri yēḻici
			tamiḻark kuritteṉal cālpām eṉṟum;
			tamiḻici nūliṉ ṟakimi viḷakki
vaṭamoḻi yicinūl
			āyirattu nāṉū ṟāṇṭiṉ muṉṉart
		100.	tōṉṟiya paratar colvaṭa nūlil
			pañcama mintaḷam pāṇaṟ kucikam
			vēḷā vaḷicī rāka muṉṉiya
			tamiḻppaṇ kaḷumaṟu nūṟṟi yeṇpa
			tāṇṭumuṟ ṟōṉṟiya cāraṅka tēvar
		105.	iyaṟṟiya ratnā karatti lintaḷam
			kāntāram pañcamam kāntāra pañcamam
			cātāri kucikañ cutta cātāri
			kuṟiñciyoṇ takka rākaṅ kuccari
			naṭṭapāṭi mēka rāka muṉṉiya
		110.	tamiḻici nūlkaḷ cāṟṟiya paṇkaḷum
			eṭuttuk kūṟali ṉivitē vārat
			tuḷaveṉac cāraṅka tēvar kuṟittaliṉ
			aṉṉavar tamiḻiṟ paṇkaḷi yāyntu
			vaṭamoḻi yiṉiṉūl vakutta kāli
		115.	īrala kukuṟit ticikkap peṟṟa
			vaṭṭap pāliyi liḷikura lāṉa
			neytal yāḻkkuriya nāṉmūṉ ṟiraṇṭu
			nāṉāṉ mūṉṟiraṇ ṭākiya kēḷvi
			irupat tiraṇṭiṉi yorunilik kuriya
		120.	alakeṉa mayaṅki yākkam pēci
			yurittaṉa rammuṟi yotta pakuppiṟ
			kovvā taṉṟik kōṇañ caturap
			pāliyi ṉāṟpat teṭṭun toṇtoṇṇūṟ
			ṟāṟumāp pakuttaṟ kiyalā tammuṟi
		125. 	muṉṉark kūṟi nāṉū ṟāṇṭu
			munti yakōpilar pārijā tattiṟ
			piṉṉar yāḻiṟ paṉṉī riciyē
			nāratar vaḻiyeṉ ṟirākam vakuttaṉar
			muṉṉūṟ ṟaṟupa tāṇṭu muṉṉar
		130.	naṉittamiḻ veṟutta rāmā māttiyar
			taṉiccura mēḷa kaḷāniti cāṟṟiṉar
			muṉṉū ṟāṇṭu muṉcutā nitinūl
			kōvinta tīkṣitar kuyiṟṟiṉa rataṉpiṉ
			irunūṟ ṟimpati yāṇṭu muṉṉar
		135.	viḷaṅkiya vēṅkaṭa makippēr mikkōṉ
			pēṇcaturt taṇṭip pirakā cikiyil
			ēḻupā liyilvaru paṉṉiru vaṭṭat
			tiyalu meḻupat tiraṇṭu mēḷam
			vakuttavi kaṭku vaṭamoḻip putuppeyar
		140.	vaḻaṅkiṉa ṉaṉṉavar muṉṉiya pallōr
			icikkuḷa kēḷvi yirupat tiraṇṭeṉ
			ṟurittami muṟṟu movvā teṉṟum,
			aḷantō rāma lavvaḻi nampip
			paṟpala rinnāṭ pāṭakar coṟṟa
		145.	kaṇakku kaḷiluḷa kaṟiyivi yeṉṟum
			vaṭanūṟ kaṇṇuḷa vaḻuveṭut tōti,
mēṟkōḷ
			āṅkila vallā raḷaviṭ ṭaṟintu
			kēḷvi yaliyiṟ kiḷakkuṅ kaṇakkum
			pirama mēḷat tirupā ṉāṉku
		150.	kēḷvi muṟiyiṟ kiḷanta nalamum
			icinūṟ ṟoṉmi yiṉitu viḷaṅkak
			kiṟittu maṟinūl kiḷanta palavum
			paṟpala vaṟiñar pakarmēṟ kōḷum
			eṭuttuk kūṟi yiyipukaḷ kāṭṭi
		155.	īrala kukuṟit ticikkum vaṭṭap
			pālik kuriya virupā ṉiraṇṭē
			niliyoṉ ṟukkiyal kēḷviyā meṉṉal
			ceḻumpaṇ ṇiyaltiṟan tiṟattiṟaṅ kaṭkuṟu
			mirupatu patiṉā ṟīṟāṟu kēḷvi
		160.	kaḷiloṉṟu kūṟal kaṭukku mataṉāl
			kēḷvi yorunilik keṇmūṉ ṟēyām
			iṉṟēl naṭipeṟu mirākaṅ kaḷiluḷa
			kālari mukkā lalaku kaḷukkōr
			iṭamili yinnāṭ pāṭaka rataṟkuk
		165.	kamaka meṉappēr kaṇṭurik kiṉṟaṉar
			cāraṅkar kamaka miciyaci veṉṟaṉar
			iciyoṉṟu piṟiti ṉeḻilic cārtal
			kamaka meṉavēṅ kaṭamaki cāṟṟiṉar
			iciviṉi yāku meṉṟu miṉpam
		170.	payakkak kuṟittup pāṭutal pāṭa
			lamuta meṉṟu maṟintaṉar tamiḻar
			icinuṇ ṇalakum palavaki viṉiyum
			vevvē ṟeṉpatu veḷippaṭi yataṉāl
			alaki ṉuṭpa maṭitaṟ kuriya
		175.	vaṭṭaṅ kōṇañ caturap pālik
			kēḷvi yiliyēṟ kiḷakkak kaṇakkili
			nilikkaṟu nāṉku kēḷvi nikaḻttici
			marapunūl kaṇṭār mayakkaṅ kaṇṭilar
			eṉṟu pakaru meṉvaḻak kuvantu
nūṟpayaṉ
		180.	iruntamiḻp paṇkava riruḷpō yakala
			arumpoṟ paṇṇamu tamarntiṉi tarunti
			aṉpi ṉulakō riṉpan tiḷikka
			ulakelān tamiḻi ṉuyarvun toṉmiyum
			kaṇṭuḷa naṉmalar viṇṭu kaḷikka
		185.	paṇkaḷuk kuriya payaṉuṭaṉ palikkat
			teyvan tāḻpavar meyyaṉpu perukik
			karutip pāṭik kaṉintuḷa murukap
			putuvaḻi vāṭṭi mututamiḻ neṟiyik
			kāṭṭi nilikkuk kēḷvi yeṇmūṉ
		190.	ṟēyeṉat tīṭṭi yuṇmiyi nāṭṭi
			yāvaru muṇara naṟṟamiḻ naṭiyil
			karuṇā miruta cākara meṉṉum
			nūloṉ ṟiyaṟṟi ñālam pukaḻati
araṅkēṟṟal
			añcu pakuppiṉu mañcā tāṭci
		195.	naṇṇic celuttu maṇṇaṟ kōmāṉ
			aintām jārjeṉu mintār perumi
			maṉṉiṟi yaṉpu tuṉṉic cāṉṟa
			kalvi poruḷeṉuñ celva nirampiya
			marāṭa mākiya parōṭā nāṭṭi
		200.	tuykkanaṉ ṟāḷuṅ keykkavā riṟivaṉ
			kiṟittā yirattut toḷḷā yirappati
			ṉāṟā māṇṭi laṉivarum vēṇṭa
			iṉitu naṭāttiya vicippē raviyil
			paṟpala koḷkiya roppi makiḻa
		205.	naṉṟaraṅ kēṟṟi maṉṟiṉi luḷḷār
			aiyan tīra meyyelām virittu
			ellām valla viṟivaṉ mecciya
			vaṇmip pāṇar marapiṟ ṟōṉṟit
			tamiḻmaṟip paṇkaḷ taḻikkac cōḻaṉ
		210.	aviyiṟ pāṭiya varuṭpeṇ maṇiyeṉat
			tōṉṟi yaṟivu cāṉṟu teyva
			aṉpuṅ kalvi vaṉpuṅ kaṟpum
			tēṉiṉu miṉimi yāṉanaṟ pāṭṭum
			niṟintu paṇpiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkum
		215.	marakata valli kaṉakavalli yeṉṟa
			taṉpeṇ maṇikaḷ yāḻoṭu pāṭi
			nūṟpaṭi vaḻaṅkum nuṭpaṅ kāṭṭac
			ceytu teḷittu meymmik kaṭavuḷ
			aruḷuñ celvamu malakil nallōr
		220.	naṉku pōṟṟu nalamuṅ kaḷippum
			aṟamum pukaḻu maṭintu ciṟantaṉaṉ
ākkiyōṉ
			maṉuviṉ kulattu vanta pāṇṭiyar
			āṇṭanaṉ ṉāṭṭi lamintu ciṟanta
			cāmpūr vaṭakaric cāmpuva ṉōṭiyiṟ
		225.	cāṉṟā riṉattiṟ ṟalimipūṇ ṭōṅkiya
			pallak kūrnta celvar vaḻivaru
			mettunaṟ cīrtti muttuc cāmivēḷ
			naṉmaṉi yāntiru vaṉṉammi yiṉpāl
			aṉpuṅ koṭiyum aṟamum aruḷum
		230.	oruṅkuru veṭuttiv virumpuvi vanteṉat
			tōṉṟiya viṉco lāṉṟanaṟ ṟōṉṟal
			meypporu ḷiṉpā luytta maṉattaṉ
			kalviyuñ celvamu malkiya nallōṉ
			nattumoṇ perumic cittar kuḻumiya
		235.	curuḷi maliyiṉ maruvi yuṟiyum
			karuṇā nantat teruḷcāṉ muṉivaṟ
			kaṇṭupē raṉpu koṇṭava raruḷāl
			vātam maruttuva mātinūṉ muṟikaḷ
			aṇṇaru ñāṉa vuṇmi yuṇarntōṉ
		240.	ulaka muḻuvatum nilavat taṉmarun
			tūkka moṭumuyaṉ ṟākkam peṟṟōṉ
			ceṉṉik kavarṉar taṉṉilam vantu
			viruntuṇ kiṉṟa peruntaki yuṭiyōṉ
			tañcic caṅkīta vityā makājaṉa
		245.	caṅkam nāṭṭiya maṅkāp pukaḻōṉ
			kiṟittu maṟinū ṉiṟitta vuḷattaṉ
			āṅkila vāṭciya raṟintuyar vaṇmi
			paṇittarāv cāhip paṭṭam puṉintōṉ
			ākinaṟ ṟañciyi lamuteṉa vōṅkum
		250.	āpira kāmeṉu maruntavat tōṉē.

taṉiyaṉ.
	kāmaṇakkun tañcivaḷa rāpirakām paṇṭitaṉaṟ kalika ḷāyntu
	pāmaṇakka viyaṟṟukaru ṇāmiruta cākaranūṟ paṭikki liṉpa
	nāmaṇakkuṅ kēṭpavartañ cevimaṇakkum piḻikkoḷki naviyu muḷḷiṟ
	ṟēmaṇakku micinūliṉ ṟēṇmaṇakku maṇakkumiṟi tirunā raṉṟē.
poruḷ muṭimuṟi.
	

tañci kalyāṇa cuntaram aiskūl tamiḻt talimip paṇṭitar,
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi  L.ulakanātapiḷḷi avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
nērici yāciriyappā.
			pūviṉ maṇameṉa veḷḷiṉu ṇeypōl
			iyaṅkuva nilippaveṉ ṟirupāṟ ṟiṇiyilum
			uḷḷum puṟamum veḷḷiṭi yiṉṟi
			nīkkaṟa niṟinta pōkkaṟu moruvaṉ
		5.	oppuyar vikanta varuḷā lolikeḻum
			āḻi māṉilam vāḻiya veṉṉap
			pakikkaḷi ṟuḷḷam patipatit toṭuṅka
			maṭaṅkaṟ paṭāmeṇ ṭiciyiṉu nuṭaṅka
			urukeḻu mērit tirumaka ṭaṉṉōṭu
		10.	nantā veṉṟi aintām jārjumaṉ
			ariyaṇi mēviya yāṇṭē ḻataṉil
			varamiku kiṟittup paramaṉ piṟantapiṉ
			pattoṉpa taṭukkiya nūṟṟup patiṉā
			ṟāṭṭiyi lirunāṉ kāan tiṅkaḷil
		15.	oṉṟoḻi yirupati lirupatiṟ kūṭiya
			mañciṉanta tavaḻu miñcicūḻ tañci
			caṅkīta vittiyā capikkaḷa mataṉil
			vayavā ḷuḻavaṉ cayamā kīrttiyaṉ
			nilattaru tiruviṟ pāṇṭiya ṉirīiya
		20.	paṭutiri vāykkoḷu naṭuva ṇavikkaṇ
			akattiya ṉōṭirun taruntami ḻāynta
			tavalaruṅ kēḷvit tuvarik kōmāṉ
			ticimayak kaṟāta vicippula vōrkaṭam
			piṇakkanta tīrttici nuṇukkaṅ kāṭṭuvāṉ
		25.	mēviṉaṉ pōṉmeṉa nāṉila micippat
			tiruvuṅ kalviyu maruvati kāramum
			oruṅku paṭitta peruntaki vicumpiṉ
			vāṉavar kōmāṉ maṇikkalap pēḻiyiṉ
			vāytiṟan teṉṉa vayaṅkiṭu parōṭā
		30.	māṭcimi mikka cūḻccicā lamiccar
			vī.pi. mātava rāv. si.i.i.
			akkirā caṉatti lamarntiṉi tiruppa
			iyalu miciyuṅ kaṇitamu meṉṉu
			muttuṟi pōkiya vittakar kuḻīi
		35.	nuṇṇitiṟ ṟerintu paṉmuṟi viyappak
			kaḻaka miraṇṭuṅ kārkōḷ koṇṭapiṉ
			aruntami ḻicinūl peruvaḻak koḻiya
			mūṉṟāṅ kaḻakamu muṭinta piṉṟi
			iḷaṅkō vaṭika ḷuḷaṅkaṉin turitta
		40.	tēṉpaṭu cuviyiṉ mūṉṟutamiḻ virāya
			uriyiṭi yiṭṭa pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷil
			ilimaṟi kāyeṉa maṟipaṭak kiṭanta
			icinuṇuk kaṅka ḷacivi lūkkamuñ
			curuḷiye maṭikaḷ karuṇā ṉantar
		
45. 	kāṉmalar poḻinta karuṇiyuṅ kāṭṭa
			yāṇṭupala kaḻiyat turīik kaṇṭu
			centamiḻ nilattoṭu muntuṟu makkaḷ
			vaḻivaḻi yāka muṟiyiṉiṟ payiṉṟa
			palliyan taḻuum pāṭali ṉiyalpum
		50.	nāṟperum paṇṇeṉu nālvaki yāḻataṉ
			pāṟpaṭu nāṉmic cātiyiṉ ṟiṟaṉum
			paṇṇoṭu paṇṇiyam paṇṭiṟan tiṟattiṟam
			eṉṉum peṟṟiyiṟ paṇṇi ṉīrmiyum
			ciṟumi perumik kiruvaram peyti
		55.	aintiṉu mēḻiṉum vantiṭu neṟiyiṟ
			paṉṉiru vakippaṭūum pāliyi ṉērmiyum
			munnāṉ kiraṭṭi kēḷviyu mavaṟṟiṉu
			nuṇṇiya vāya curutiyum viḷakkum
			āya mutalā vaṭṭamī ṟākap
		60.	pālikaḷ varitaru pāṉmiyum piṟavum
			evva nīṅki yettaki yōruṅ
			kippaṭu nelliyiṟ kāṇat tokuttu
			mēṟkōṇ mutalā vēṟpaṉa tōṟṟip
			piṟarmataṅ kaḷiit taṉkō ṇirīip
		65.	parīik kaḻaka mirīiyaraṅ kēṟiya
			pāṇṭiya reṉṉa mēvi yīṇṭavi
			iṉṉici māṭciyi ṉiyalpuṇa rāru
			mayalaṟa nāṭi māṇporu ṭēṟak
			karuṇā ṉanta cākaram eṉumici
		70.	yuricā ṉūlaraṅ kēṟṟiṉaṉ puritapu
			teṉṉavar nāṭu ceytamā tavattāṉ
			muraṇpaki paṉikku maraṇṭaku kāppi
			ṉāvaloṭu peyariya vūrvaṭa karikkaṇ
			nalaṉoruṅ kuṟṟeṉa naṇṇiya cīlaṉ
		75.	tamiḻmoḻi yēmoḻi tamiḻici yiciyeṉat
			tāviṉṟi niṟuvun tamiḻnar kōmāṉ
			tāḻici vaṇṭun tamiḻyāḻ muralum
			ēḻicic cūḻa liṭameṉa vāḻvōṉ
			mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟimiyiṟ ṟirintu
		80.	pāli yāmeṉum pāṉmi vaṟitākap
			payiṉiṉ murampuṭip pāli tirittu
			vaṇṭu varipāṭat taṇpō talarntu
			tātun taḷiru mētakat tuvaṉṟiya
			palpūñ cōliyum payaṉṟaru maraṉum
		85.	nāṇmalark koṭikaḷu naṉantali mayaṅka
			mayiloṭu kuyilkūuṅ kuralum payilap
			paṉṉiṟaṅ kañaliya viṉcuvik karumpum
			kaḷiṟu māykkuṅ kaḻaṉiyuñ ceṟitara
			mulliyu marutamu meṉṉac colliyal
		90.	karuṇā ṉanta puraṅkā ṇuravōṉ
			orukuṭi niḻaṟṟi yirunilam purakku
			maṉṉavar maṉaṅkoḷu naṉmatip puṭiyōṉ
			payaṉmaram paḻutteṉa vūruṇi niṟinteṉa
			nayaṉuṭic celvam piṟarkkeṉa vāḻvōṉ
		95.	illa mutalā vellāp poruḷkaḷum
			aruṭkuru nātaṟ kākkiyoṉ ṟēṉun
			taṉakkeṉa vāḻāt takimi yāḷaṉ
			tiruttaku marapiṉ maruttunū laṉṟi
			yellām valla paṇṭita ṉivaṉeṉa
		100.	mīppuka ḻulakelā mōṅkum
			āppira kāmpeya raṟiñarperu māṉē.

maturic caṅka vitvāṉum, cīkāḻi luttaraṉ hiskūl tamiḻppōtakāciriyarumākiya, paṟaṅkippēṭṭi,
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi muttuttāṇṭavarāyapiḷḷiyavarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyavi.
		matikku micippāṇa mānilat tempōṟ
		katikkum variyatariṟ kāṉpōyt - tutittakkāṟ
		ṟañci nakarāḷ taruvaṉā ṉāpirakām
		tañca muṉakkut tarum.	
pāṭāṇṭiṇi, pāṇāṟṟuppaṭi. (1)
		malicēr matiya vataṉattar kūttiṟ
		ṟalicēr talivanī cāriṉ-nilicēruṅ
		kāraṉṉa tañciyar kāvala ṉāpirakām
		tāraṉṉa vākkan tarum.	..., 
kūttarāṟṟuppaṭi. (2)
		tarikoḷ peruvaḷat tañcittēñ cāriṉ
		marikoḷ tamiḻaṇaṅkiṉ māṇa - virikoḷ
		aruḷīn taḷikku mavaṉā pirakām
		poruḷīn tuvakkum pukaḻ.	..., 
pulavarāṟṟuppaṭi. (3)
		matiyam vataṉan tāṉā matiyak
		kayalpāy taṭattuk kuvaḷi kaṇṇā
		viyalara viṉpaṭa makalviḷak kākap
		parampara ṉaḷippap paṟṟiṉi yiyalaṇi
	5.	yeṉiṉu miṉippaṇ viṟalī yiyamputu
		naṉimakiḻ tuṭavi naṟumatu tuḷippak
		kaḷivaṇ ṭāṭuṅ kāmar tañci
		mānakarc celīi yāṇṭuḷa kōmā
		ṉāṭalcā ṉāyakaṉ kūṭalam patikka
	10.	ṇaracu purīip paracumut tamiḻic
		caṅkat tirīi yiṅkitap paṭuttiya
		vaḻutiyar ceḻiyar vaḻivarun tōṉṟa
		liyalē yiciyē yiyalunā ṭakamē
		paṉṉaru mavaṟṟiṉ paṉṉiru pālē
	15.	yakattiya ṉikarppac cakattiṭi yāyupu
		muppā ṉūṟku mippār paravu
		miyalpiṉ kariyip payilvuṟuṅ kaliñaṉ
		āpirā muyariya vappa ṉeṉṟum
		āpira kāmpal liṉattiṉark kappa
	20.	ṉeṉṟum vallavar coṉṉavap paricē
		naṟṟamiḻ nayatti yuṟṟanam marapō
		ruṇartara vurittaliṟ kuṇañceṟi periyō
		ṉiruṅkaṭa luṭīiya perumpuvi vakuttup
		purantaruḷ tūyōṉ varantaru pōḻti
	25.	ṉuṉṉip periya vuṟaviṉa ṉākkuva
		luṉṉiṟ puṭavi yuyartavar yāvarum
		naṉṉaya muṟuva reṉṟavam māṇpē
		nammavark kittamiḻ nalamelām pakarvō
		ṉiruvē ṟulakat tiyaṟki tiruvēṟu
	30.	teḷḷiya rātalum vēṟeṉat tēva
		rōtiya māṟṟa muvappa nītiyi
		ṉiruporuṭ ceṟivum viraviya cemmal
		tampiyar tanayar taṉṉil lattuṇi
		tumpuru viyakkun tuppuṟu maṭantiyar
	35.	marakata maṉṉa marakata valliyuṅ
		kaṉakanēr ciṟappiṉ kaṉaka valliyuñ
		cevviti ṉillaṟañ ceḻippa vuṟuvōṉ
		pāvu mirāca pattiyiṉ mutirntu
		rāvu cāhipu mēviya paṭṭaṉ
	40.	iciyi ṉuṇukka micikeḻīip payiṉṟu
		peruṅkāp piyameṉa varuṅkali vāṇar
		cāṟṟukar ṇāmirta cākaran taṉṉi
		mātava ṉaṇiyacīr mātava rāya
		makipaṉ parōṭā maṉṉavaṉ pāṅka
	45.	ṉavikkaḷat tiruntiṅ karaṅkēṟṟu nipuṇaṉ
		viruntōm puntava ṉaṟuntēṉ ṟārāṉ
		palvaḷa nilaṉuñ colvaḷat toḻilu
		māṭa māḷiki kūṭakō puramu
		mamitarat tañciyiṟ kamipeṟu kuṇattu
	50.	nāṟkavi rāyari mēṟpaṭap purappōṉ
		āpira kāmeṉa mēṟpula meṅku
		mīta liciyi ṉicintōṉ kīrtti
		yāṭi yuḻuvaliṟ pāṭip paṭartiyēṟ
		kaḻakañ cātta viḻiceya laṇiyuṅ
	55.	koṭuppa milintu viṭuppa vuvantu
		pūtta koṭiyiṟ polintiṅ
		kiṉṉē varuti yiṟimaka ḷeṉṉavē.	
..., viṟaliyāṟṟuppaṭi (4)
tirunelvēli, makā vitvāṉ
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi kavirāja nelliyappapiḷḷi avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
cāṟṟukavi.
cīrārun tañcinaka rāpirakām paṇṭitaṉaṟ celvaṉ caṅkat
tārāṟu tikaḻcaṭiya ṉoṭutērnta vicinūlit takiyi ṉāyntē
yīrāṟō ṭīrintāñ curutiyeṉa vicippavarcol līrntu pōkki
nērāruñ curutiyeṇmūṉ ṟeṉattamiḻnūṉ muṟiviḷakki nilikka nāṭṭi (1)
paṇṇiyalpun taṇṭamiḻiṉ maṟiyilavi yaṇṇiyalpum palavu maṟṟār
eṇṇariya nuṇmikaḷu miṉitamuta meṉaviyintu cālum
viṇṇavarum pukaḻkaruṇā mirutacā karanūli viḷampi yinta
maṇṇulakil naṇṇumuyar pulavartiṉam pōṟṟunalam vāyttāṉ maṉṉō. (2)
ampācamuttiram, tīrttapati aiskūl tamiḻppaṇṭitar,
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi arikarapāratiyār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
taravu koccakak kalippā.
	1.	kārparavu kaṉamparavak karuṅkōṭṭi liḻitaruveṇ
		ṇīrparavu nilamparavu nīḷporuni natiyōṭuñ
		cīrparavun tamiḻoṭumat tiṟamparavuñ cittacaṉār
		tērparavu mērparavu teṉpāṇṭi naṉṉāṭṭil
	2.	tāṟṟōṭi vaṉamaruntu taṭappuḻakkip puḻutiyaḷāyt
		tūṟṟōṭik kaḷiṟukurān toṭarmaliyak kīḻcāral
		mēṟṟōṭic cuḷipiḷappa viyaṉpalavi ṉiḻitēṟal
		āṟṟōṭi niṟipeṟucām pavaṉōṭi yāmpatiyil
	3.	īṉṟōriṟ puvipuranta eḻilvēmpaṉ paḻaṅkiḷiyāy
		ēṉṟōrī ḻattaraciṉ muṭipaṟippa itaṉimittam
		vāṉṟōyuñ cōlinakar nāṭāṇṭa vamcameṉac
		cāṉṟōrkaḷ palaviḷakkuñ cāṉṟārtam perumarapil.
vēṟu
	4.	puvicērap pukaḻantac cāṉṟārta māṉṟakulam poruvil cīrtti
		cevicērap peruṅkēḷvi pālacuppi ramaṇiyappērc celva ṉuṇmic
		cavicēru mariyāḷtaṉ taṉayaṉiru tāṇmalarkkē tāca ṉākic
		cuvicēṭa mutteṉumpē reytiṉaṉṟaṉ pōṉeṉac ceppu vōṉum.
	5.	coṟṟakavi cēṭamuttu palanāḷu maṉuṭṭitta tūyta vattāṟ
		peṟṟacuku māraṉmuttu cāmiyeṉpā ṉillaṟattiṉ perumi yeytak
		kaṟṟakaṟpi ṉāluyarnta aṉanaṭiyaṉ ṉammāḷik kaṭima ṇattil
		uṟṟaṉaṉīṇ ṭavarkkutitta makkaḷeṇmar tamiṉmutalā yutittō ṉeṉṟum.
	6.	kalikūrnta lārṭupeṇṭu laṇṭārtar lāliyeṉuṅ kavarṉar kaṭkun
		talikūrnta ārpiṭaṭṭu peṭpōrṭṭāṅ kalikṭarukkuñ cakāya māṉa
		nilikūrnta nitittalimi eytiyacā mikkaṇṇu nipuṇa rukkum
		alikūrnta talampukaḻum āṉarapiḷ civacāmi aiya rukkum.
	7.	caṇmukamey ñāṉacivā cāriyarāñ cuvāmikaṭkum cārntārk kellān
		taṇmukamē tarumoruti vāṉmāta varāvavarkkum taṉṉē ruṟṟār
		uṇmukanī raṟiyūki kamiṣaṉarām parāṅkucanā yuṭukā rukkum‘
		maṇmukamē luṇmiyiṉiṉ ṟeṇmiyunīṅ kātuneṟi yaccel vōrkkum.
	8.	iṉṉicinūṉ muṟiterintu maṉṉiciyi evarcevikku miṉitu peytu
		taṉṉiciyi vaḷarttiṭumut tiyaṉeṉum pākavatar taliva ṉukkum
		miṉṉicimāṟ ṟāyirañcē raparañci yoḻukkameṉa miṭaṟṟiṉ cevvit
		teṉṉiciyi yicikkumakā liṅkaṉeṉap peyarpūṇṭa cīrā ḷarkkum.
	9.	orutivañ civamateṉa orumoḻiyun tamiḻateṉa vulaku ṟutti
		virutinili nāṭṭiyacīr viruticiva ñāṉiyarkkum vitta kattōr
		irutayamān tamiḻāyntu vacaṉāti nāṟkaviyi ṉiṉiya nūlkaḷ
		porutalaṟap palavicitta arikarapā ratiyeṉṉap pukalu meṟkum
	10.	paruttaparum pātakattiṉ uyirnīttup piṟakeḻunta parama ṉārkkē
		karuttutavu paṭivarukkuṅ kāyakaṟpa niliyirunta kaṉayō kaṟku
		maruttuvarkkuñ, cōtiṭarkkum iyalicinā ṭakattavarkkum vaṟumi yōrpoṉ
		peruttavarkku muḷaṅkaṉintu nēcikku nēcaṉeṉap pēcu vōṉum.
	11.	cakamēya taṇmiyiṉā luṇmiyiṉāl vaṇmiyiṉāl taṉattā liṉpam
		akamēva vacaṉikku māṟṟaloṭu kalvimati atinuṭ pattāl
		ikamēvu nāṭṭārām nāṭārtam vaṅkicamā miraṇi yaccīr
		makamēruk kōṭṭilvitta maṅkāta cuṭarviḷakkiṉ vayaṅku vōṉum.
	12.	centamiḻan teḷḷiciyun tēvarpirāṉ kātikaḷuñ ceviyē kēṭpa
		kantamuṟu pōṉakamuṅ kaṉakamuṭaṉ pulavarukkuk kiyē nalka
		vantavartam moḻikēṭṭu vēṇṭuvaṉa vucitampōl vāyva ḻaṅka
		antaraṅka muḻutoruvark kālayamē yākanita mākki ṉōṉum.
	13.	iruḷiyalu mikavāḻkki yeṉaveṟuttē vikinati yeḻuntu lāvum
		curuḷiyilē ceṉṟukaru ṇānitiyām muṉivaraṉic cūḻntu pōṟṟa
		aruḷiyalu mavaṉaḷitta varaṉmuṟiyē maruttuvaṅka ḷaṉittun tērntu
		teruḷiyalum vittiyattāl maṉṉuyiriṉ nōypalavun tīrkkiṟ pōṉum.
	14.	pollāta viṉipurintiṅ kuyirvāḻtal pāvamelām poruntu meṉṟē
		nallāṉa vuvāttimiyān toḻilataṉi maṉiviyuṭa ṉaṉimēṟ koṇṭu
		pallāṉa nakaruṟintu cōḻarkaṭan talinakarāp pakarā niṉṟa
		allāṉa cōliyuṭi cūḻtañci māṉakari aṭuttō ṉeṉṟum.
	15.	aṭuttiṭṭa tañciyilē eṭuttiṭṭa muṉviṉiyiṉ āṟṟāṟ cennel
		maṭuttiṭṭa pūmpaḻaṉap parappiṉoṭuṅ kaṉikaḷellām malkit tūṅkat
		toṭuttiṭṭa cōlikaḷu mirunitiyu maṇimāṭat toṭarpuñ cūḻal
		uṭuttiṭṭa tōṟṟamuṭaṉ appatikkō rēṟṟamuṭaṉ uyarntō ṉeṉṟum.
	16.	uritaruvōṉ apitāṉam āpirakām paṇṭitaṉeṉ ṟulakaṅ kūṟum					taritarumōr tarumameṉu mavaṉappāṟ ṟaraṇiyelān taṉikkō lōccu
		varitarutōḷ āṅkilacak rātipati yaruṇōkka mariti ṉōkkit
		tiritarumā ṉilattaḷitta rāvucā yapuppaṭṭañ ciṟappap peṟṟōṉ.
	17.	māṇmikka aṇikalaṉām paṭṭappēr taṉivaṉiyav vaḷḷal peṟṟa
		āṇmakka ṇālvarukkum peṇmakka ḷaṟuvarukku marumi yāṉa
		pūṇmikka vaṇitaralpō lāṅkilamum pintamiḻum poliyac ceytu
		pāṇmikka vūṭṭiṭappeṇ makkaḷukkō ricivalaṉip pārtta mittāṉ.
	18.	pārttamitta kāyakaru makārkkaruttu caraḷimutaṟ palavi yīṟā
		ārttamuṟi taṉikkaṭantaṅ kicittaliyu miciyaṟiyā ranēkar kūṭi
		nīrttavici nūṟpiṟappu vaṭamoḻikka ṇallātu nilattōr pāṅkar
		pōrttatami ḻiṭamileṉap pukaltaliyuṅ kēṭṭuḷattiṟ puṇpā ṭuṟṟē.
	19.	paccaṅkat tariyaṟiyāp paraṉaṭatti ṉiṭimuḷittup paranta kālam
		muccaṅkat tiṭik kiṭantu tēcamelān tavaḻntēṟi muḻaṅki mēṉmēl
		uccaṅko ḷiciyiṉiya muttamiḻi ṉorutamiḻā yulāvak kaṇṭum
		accaṅko ḷātiṅka ṉavaricittal viyappeṉatta ṉakatti luṉṉi.
	20.	pāṇpiṟanta neṟiyiteṉa atuvaḷarnta viṭamiteṉap paripā litta
		māṇpiṉaru mivareṉṉa maṟṟataṉik kiyāṇṭār vakuppī teṉṉac
		cēṇpiṟaṅku navanavamā yirākaṅkaḷ kītāti ceytaṟ kāṉa
		vēṇpiṟaṅkil vaḻiyiteṉac curutikaḷit tuṇiyeṉṉa virippa vēṇṭi.
	21.	patiṉāṉki yāṇṭaḷavāy manumutalōr vakuttaḷitta paṇṭi nūlum
		vitinūleṇ paratavayit tiyanūluñ cittarpalar viritta nūlun
		tutimēvu mitikāca purāṇamutaṉ matanūliṉ ṟokiyum vēṇṭum
		nitimēvu mēṉāṭṭār nūlpalavu māyntataṉṟi nirampak kēṭL.
	22.	cāraṅkar uyarpāri jātaroṭu paratarivar tanta nūliṉ
		cāraṅkaḷ paḻiyatamiḻk kāviyamō ṭakampuṟamāc cāṟṟu nūliṉ
		cāraṅkaḷ tamiḻmaṟiyi ṉamiḻtiṉeḻu tarumiṉiya caṅkī tattiṉ
		cāraṅkaḷ cattiyavē tatticiyiṉ virikiṉṟa cārām caṅkaḷ.
	23.	iṉṉavici nuṇukkamelāñ cenneṟiyiṟ ṟeratteḷintu cinti yellām
		teṉṉiciyiṉ mayamāka vaṉupavittav vaṉupavattiṉ cīrmi yellām
		ennilamun teḷintuyvāṉ uṇminili kaṭippiṭittē iyampa lāṉāṉ
		muṉṉanaviṉ ṟiṭṭamuṟi viṉākkaḷukkiṅ kēṟṟaviṭi moḻiyak kēṇmiṉ.
	24.	mutaṉaṭippak kōcikatti ṉiṭiyeḻunta ticiyeṉṟu muṉṉi mūṉṟu
		vitamaruvu tamiḻkkaḻaka matuvaḷarppa vaḷarntateṉṟum virumpi nāḷum
		pataṉamuṟak kāttavarkaḷ tiraviṭamū vēntareṉṟum parāvu maṉṉōrk
		kitaṉamarnta tamiḻarkaḷē kiyāṇṭār annāḷtoṭ ṭiṉṟu meṉṟum.
	25.	carimutalā meḻucuraṅkaḷ nilimāṟa irākaṅkaḷ caṉikku manta
		varimuṟiyiṟ cilaviciyē īṇṭuvaḻak kattuḷatu maṟṟu māṟṟal
		terimuṟiyi lirākaṅkaḷ pallāyi rattokiyiṟ ceṟiva veṉṟum
		uriyavanta muṟinavamē yāyiṭiṉum paḻamimuṟik kuṟṟa teṉṟum
	26.	avvaḻiyē kītaṅkaḷ kīrttaṉikaḷ irākaṅkaḷ āḷat tikkāñ
		cevvaḻikaṭ kutāraṇamāyp palaviyaṟṟit tamiḻiciyiṉ ciṟappī teṉṉa
		evvaḻiyun talituḷakka vēṟṟaneṟi taṉippukaṉṟu micikkōr tāyiṉ
		ovvariya curutikaḷi oruvāṟu kaṇittaṟivā ṉuḷatti luṉṉi
	27.	eḻumūṉṟō ṭoṉṟeṉṟum oṉṟeṉṟum palaveṉṟu moḻuṅkē tiṉṟi
		vaḻumūṇṭa mayakkattāl vakuttavaṭa nūlkaḷiṉi vakiyiṉ māṟṟit
		toḻumūvil tamiḻmaṟiyāl parampariyāl aṉupavattāl curuti taṉṉāl
		eḻumūtu curutiiru patiṉāṉkē yāmeṉavu miyampuṅ kāli
	28.	kaṭuttacilar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukaṉṟatuvē cālumeṉak kaḻaṟi vātan
		toṭuttiṭalu maṅkavaric curutiyutti yaṉupavattiṉ ṟuṟika ḷālē
		maṭuttapiṭi viṭappurintāṉ maṟṟeṉakku mimmuṟiyiṉ mayakkameytal
		aṭuttaṟintuṅ katinīppa āppirakām paṇṭitaṉā mariya cīlaṉ.
	29.	tāṉīṉṟa taṉayimara katavalli kaṉakavalli tampāṟ kūṟa
		mīṉīṉṟa viḻicciyarac caṅkīta viṟpaṉikaḷ vīṇi taṉṉāl
		tēṉīṉṟa miṭaṟṟiciyāl curutiiru patiṉāṉkāyt teḷiyac ceytār
		āṉīṉṟa kaṉṟeṉavē aṟiyātu tuḷḷukiṟpārk kaṟiva teṉṉē.
	30.	muṉpanta muṟinikaḻnta vicinuṇukka mutaliyaṉa muṭintu pōkap
		piṉpanta vicinuṇukkam puntivaḻi evvaḻiyō peritu tōṉṟa
		aṉpanta muṟākkaḷippi ṉampuviyōr aṉupavikku māṟu ceytāṉ
		iṉpanta muḷamperukku mittalañcey kimmāṟiṅ keṉṉē yeṉṉē.
	31.	innūṟkiṅ kēṟṟakaru ṇāmiruta cākarappē riyiya vittē
		ennūṟkun taliparōṭā maṉṉamiccar mātavarāv iṟimi mēya
		toṉṉūṟka ṇaṉipayiṉṟār niṟiyavikka ṇaraṅkēṟṟal ceytāṉ kāṉap
		paṉṉūṟku minnūṉmēm paṭṭateṉat tutimoḻikaḷ pārit tārāl.
	32.	maliviḷaṅku maṇimāṭat tañciyilum tamiḻiciyil vaṭāta tāṉa
		kaliviḷaṅku pulavarpalar cirantuḷakka araṅkēṟṟal kaviṉac ceytē
		aliviḷaṅku kaṭalpuṭicūḻ avaṉiyelām pukaḻnilavā lalaṅka rittāṉ
		taliviḷaṅka vutittavari livaṉṟuṇiyā riruminayan taṉippeṟ ṟōrē.
	33.	ilakkaṇamē ilakkiyamē kāviyamē purāṇāti itikā cammē
		talakkaṇuyar tamiḻnāṭṭuc carittiramē vittiyamē camāti yōkan
		tulakkumoru nūlāti yākamamē cōtiṭamē curuti kīta
		valakkaṇatē yeṉpakaru ṇāmiruta cākaramā vaḻaṅkinnūlē.
maturi
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi mu.rā.kantacāmik kavirāyaravarkaḷiyaṟṟiya
ciṟappuppāyiram.
	1.	pūmiyiyōr curapiyeṉap paratamatiṉ maṭiyeṉṉap politeṉ ṉāṭu
		tēmalipāṟ curiyeṉṉa vaṟiñarpukal vaḷañciṟanta ceḻiyar nāṭṭiṉ
		māmalipūn taṭaccāmpūr vaṭakariyān tirunakariṉ māṭci mēvum
		pāmalicīr peṟucāṉṟōr palaruḷḷuñ cāṉṟōṉām pāṉmi mikkōṉ.
	2.	tavamaruvum pālacuppi ramaṇiyamā līṉṟamuttu cāmi vēḷu
		muvamiyilā vaṉṉammā ḷumpurinta nalantiraṇṭō ruruva mākik
		kuvalayatti lutitteṉṉa vantamutaṟ kumaraṉuyar kuṇaṅkaḷ yāvu
		mivaṉiṭattu vaḷaravaḷarn teṇṇilkali niṟivuṟappeṟ ṟilaṅkuñ cīlaṉ.
	3.	meṉmatura vākkuṭiyāṉ miḷirumuḻu matikkuṭiyāṉ mēva lārcey
		vaṉmimiku ceyaṟkuṭiyāṉ māḻinū larikkuṭiyāṉ variyā teṉṟum
		naṉmiceyat tāmatiyāṉ nalamilari yuḷamatiyā ṉaṉṉūl kūṟun
		taṉmiyelā niṟimatiyāṉ ṟakavilviṉi cammatiyāṉ ṟayavāṉ mikkāṉ.
	4.	aintaruvō virunitiyō māmaṇiyō karumukilō vāvō yāvu
		mintavula kiṉiloṉṟā yaṭuttālu miṇiyākā vīki mēlōṉ
		centamiḻāṅ kilamāti palapāṣā paṇṭita vicēṭa cīlaṉ
		cantatamum piṟarkkutavi ceyvatilē maṉañceluttun taruma vaḷḷal.
	5.	kaṉaviṉilu maṟavātu kaṭavuḷaṭi malarpēṇuṅ karutta ṉiṉcol
		maṉavamiti poṟumiyaṉpu vāymiviṭā muyaṟcimaṉa makiḻcci nallō
		riṉamaruvi yiruttaleḷi yavariṭatti lirakkamivi yellāṅ koṇṭa	
		taṉataṉikar āpirakām paṇṭita kuṇākara vutāra tīraṉ.
	6.	pūruvattup puṇṇiyattāṟ ṟāṉiṉitta naṉmiyelām poruntac ceyya
		nēraṭutta tuṇiviyāyk kaṟpiṉukkō raṇiyāki nilaṅkoṇ ṭāṭum
		pērpaṭittu miḷirñāṉa vaṭivupoṉṉam māḷuṭaṉē peritāṅ kalvi
		cīrpeṟappō taṉipuriyun toḻilēṟṟut tañcinakar ciṟappa vantāṉ.
	7.	taṇṭamiḻi ṉilakkiyamu milakkaṇamum piṟanūlun takavi ṉāyntu
		koṇṭucevi vaḻippukuntu maṉattuṟintu karumpukaṉi kōṟṟē ṉaṟkaṟ
		kaṇṭamuta meṉaviṉikkuñ caṅkītat tuṟitērntu kaḷippu mēvi
		yeṇṭiciyu micipaṭittāṉ āpirakām paṇṭitappē ricival lōṉē.
	8.	nīraritāyk kallumuḷḷu niṟivatalāṟ pullumilā nilaṅkaḷ vāṅki
		yārumika vaticayippak kiṇaṟukaḷvā vikaḷtōṇṭi yakilat teṅkum
		pērirukka māpalā teṉṉimutaṟ cōlivaḷam perukac ceytu
		vāramikka vaḷamaruta vippākkic ciṟantatoḻil valimīk koṇṭōṉ.
	9.	karuṇimika niṟipiriṭṭiṣ makarājar pukaḻōṅkak kaviṉcēr ceṉṉi
		varukavarṉar sarārtar lālimahāp pirapuvivaṉ māṭci yellām
		perituṇarntu rāvcāhip eṉṟakavu ravappaṭṭam piṟaṅkat tantā
		rirunilattil rāvcāhip āpirakām paṇṭitamāṟ kiṇiyā rammā.
	10.	vātamvayit tiyamyōkam ñāṉamivi kaḷitteḷivāy maṉatiṟ ṟēṟuṅ
		kātaluṟṟā yiratteṇtoṇṇūṟ ṟeḻupattē ḻāṇṭiluyar karuṇā nantar
		pātatari caṉicuruḷi maliyileyti yavaṉaruḷāṟ palavun tērntu
		pōtamikkī rāṟāṇṭukaḷ vayittiyattā lulakamelām pōṟṟap peṟṟāṉ.
	11.	tāṉpeṟṟa piḷḷikaḷuṅ kuṭumpattār piḷḷikaḷun tamiḻā rāyntu
		tēṉpeṟṟa cuvimaliyuñ caṅkīta cāttiraṅka ṭeḷivi ṉōrntu
		vāṉpeṟṟa teḷḷamutan tuḷippavici pāṭavumyāḻ vakikik koṇṭu
		kāṉpeṟṟa nalantikaḻa vicimaḻipey yavumpurintu kaḷikkum cemmal.
	12.	uttiyō kampuriya vantupala perunalaṅka ḷuñaṟṟic cīrāl
		nittiyā tipaṉivaṉē yeṉavāḻun tañciyilē nikaḻcaṅ kīta
		vittiyā mahācaṉaca piyeṉupē raviyoṉṟu mēvat tāpit
		tetticā mukattiluntaṉ ṉicivaḷarum paṭiceyumpē rēṟṟa muḷḷōṉ.
vēṟu.
	13.	tittikkuñ centēṉō tēmpākō teḷḷamutō
		puttikku ḷiṇiyākap poruntumeṉuñ caṅkīta
		muttikku vaḻikāṭṭu muṟiyaṉittun teḷivittē
		yettikkuṅ koṇṭāṭu mēṟṟamiku tōṟṟamuḷōṉ.
	14.	kaṇṇaḷavuk kaṭaṅkātu kaṟpakakkāk kaḷimēvi
		yuṇṇamutac cuvikkaṉika ḷutavumivaṉ cōlivaḷa
		meṇṇaḷaviṟ poruntuvatō vicivēntar kavarṉar muṉṉōr
		naṇṇimika makiḻpūttu nāviṉikkap pukaḻvārāl.
	15.	uṇṇīrō veṉappalari yoruṅkaḻittiṉ ṉuṇavaḷippōṉ
		maṇṇīrum viṇṇīrum vaṟṟukiṉum vaṟṟāta
		teṇṇīrāṅ kiṇaṟukallit tēkacukam peṟavintat
		taṇṇīrē pōtumeṉat takkavarcol coṟpeṟuvōṉ.
	16.	illārkkup poṉṉaḷippā ṉiraṅkivantārk kuṇavaḷippāṉ
		pollānō yuṭiyavarkkup poruntumuyar maruntaḷippāṉ
		pallārkkum vēṇṭuvaṉa pārttuppārt tiṉitaḷippāṉ
		ellārkku mupakāri mivaṉpōlvā riliyulakil.
	17.	uṇṭikoṭup pavartamiyu muyirkoṭuppō reṉparpiṇi
		miṇṭiyiṟak kuntitiyiṉ melipavaruk karumaruntu
		koṇṭutavi yuyiriyē koṭukkuminta āpirakām
		paṇṭitamāl ceyaṉōkkiṟ pakarvateṉṉō vaṟikilamāl.
	18.	karuṇānantap periyōṉ karuṇipurin tatimaṉatti
		lorunāḷu maṟavāmi yulakaṟiyap palamaruntu
		nirumāṇam purikaruṇā nitivayitya cālivitta
		perumāṉṟāy tantinikar peṟṟiyaṉpal luyirkaḷukkē.
	19.	taṉṟiruppē ruṭaṉērāv cāypeṉṉum paṭṭamoṉṟu
		naṉṟutavuṅ kavarṉarsrī lāliyaṇṇal peyarviḷaṅka
		veṉṟumuyar tarulāli elekṭrikpiras ēṟpaṭuttit
		teṉṟamiḻnā ṭiṉitēttac ciṟappitta peruntakiyōṉ.
vēṟu
	20.	makkaḷelāṅ kītamuṟip paṭipayilac curutinili vakuttuk kūṟat
		tokkavaṇup piramāṇac curutikaḷi yunteḷivāc colanan nāṭṭup
		pukkakī tattiṉilē kītaṅkīrt taṉamrākam poruntap paṇṇun
		takkamuṟi yilliyeṉuṅ kuṟiyaṟavē ḻiraṇṭāṇṭu takamu yaṉṟāṉ.
	21.	ōraṟivē mutalāka vāṟaṟivu varikoḷḷu muyirka ḷellān
		tāraṇiyiṉ makiḻntupara vacamēvak kaṭavuḷaruñ cantō ṣikkac
		cīratikam peṟukīta muttiyiyuṅ koṭukkumeṉuñ ciṟappit tērntē
		yārumiṉi titiyuṇara veḷiyiṭuvā ṉoruperunū lākka nērntāṉ.
	22.	annūṟkup peyarkaruṇā mirutacā karameṉavē yamittā ṉanta
		naṉṉūliṟ ṟamiḻttoṉmi tamiḻuyarvu tamiḻoṉṟē naṟṟāyp pāṣi
		yeṉṉumuṟi maṟukkamuṭi yātaniyā yaṅkaḷuṭa ṉeṭuttuk kāṭṭip 
		paṉṉuperum pulavaruḷaṅ kutukulippa viḷakkiyirup patuṭaṉ piṉṉar.
	23.	teṉṉintiya caṅkītat toṭakkamata ṉuyarvupaṇṭē cekatti yāṇṭa
		maṉṉavarā tarittamuṟi kōyilkaḷiṟ kaṭṭaḷikaḷ vakutta māṭci
		tuṉṉucilā cācaṉaṅka ḷiviyiviyeṉ ṟeṭutteṭuttuc coṟka ṭōṟuṅ 
		kaṉṉalamu tantuḷippak karatalā malakameṉak kaviṉak kāṭṭi.
	24.	parataruṭaṉ cāraṅka tēvarmuṉṉōr nūlkaḷiru pāṉi raṇṭē
		curutiyeṉal tavaṟeṉṉac caṅkanū leṉacciṟantu toṉmi mēvum
		paripāṭal cilappatikā rampiṅka lantimutaṟ palavaṟ ṟāluṅ
		karutuyukti yālumanu pavattālum viḷakkamuṟak kāṭṭi vittē.
	25.	aṉiyapala pirapalani yāyaṅka ḷāṟcuruti yaṟunāṉ keṉṟē
		niṉivukoḷak kaṇakkukaḷā tāraṅka ḷoṭukāṭṭi nilikkac ceytum
		iṉiyavaki yicinalaṅkaḷ paṟpalavum viḷakkiyunal licival lōrkaḷ
		puṉiyumapi tāṉamelāṅ kāṭṭiyumiṉ ṉumpalacīr poruntac cērttē.
	26.	coṟceṟivāṟ poruṭpolivāṟ paṟpalavi cēṭamali tokiyā liṅṅaṉ
		naṟperunū liḵtoṉṟē yeṉavulakat tellavaru nayantu kūṟap
		poṟpumika niṟikaruṇā mirutacā karanūlip puṉintu tantāṉ
		aṟputamāṅ kītavallōṉ āpirakām paṇṭitaṉpō lārval lārē.
	27.	maṅkāta pukaḻmēvum parōṭāmaṉ ṉivvāṇṭu mārccu mātam
		iṅkārum piramikkat taṉmuṉṉark kūṭṭuvitta ellā intya
		caṅkīta capiyiṉilē tivāṉākum mātavarāv takkō ṉāti
		poṅkārvaṅ kalviyicip pulamipaṭit tavarpalarum poruntum pōtu.
	28.	intavuya rāpirakām paṇṭitamāṟaṉ ṟavattā līṉṟe ṭukka
		vantamara katavalli kaṉakavalli yeṉumpeyarppeṇ maṇika ḷōṭu
		muntavuṟṟa peṇmaṇikaḷ vāyālum vīṇikoṇṭu muṟiva ḻātu
		cantavitip paṭiyicittēm poḻiyaccey tavimakiḻntu cāṟṟap peṟṟē.
	29.	curutiyaṟu nāṉkeṉaṟkuk kaṇakkuniyā yamumeṭuttuc colli yāruñ
		caricariyeṉ ṟiṭappeṟṟut tāṉceykaru ṇāmiruta cāra nūliṉ
		maruvupala viṣayamum viyācamā vācittam maṉṉaṉ muṉṉē
		piriyamuṟa varaṅkēṟṟap peṟṟateṉiṉ maṟṟitaṉcīr pēcaṟ pāṟṟē.
	30.	iṭamutavip poruḷutavi yēvalā ḷariyutavi yiṉiya kūṟi
		yuṭaliluḷa piṇiyakala maruntutavi yupakarippā ruṇṭō vuṇṭō
		kaṭalulaki liviyaṉittum āpirakām paṇṭitaṉpāṟ kaṇṭōṅ kaṇṭōn
		tiṭacukaca rīracampat tātiyuṭa ṉivaṉvāḻka cekattiṉ maṉṉō.
ceṉṉi veslikālēj talimittamiḻppulavar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tiru. vi. kaliyāṇacuntara mutaliyār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
nēriciyāciriyappā.
			icivaṭi vāku macivilāp poruḷil
			tōṉṟip paṉmoḻi yīṉṟa veṅkaṉṉi!
			miṭaṟṟi liciyu miṭanāvi liyalum
			viḻiki yāṭaluṅ keḻumiya vaṉṉāy!
		5.	vaṭavari nīriṟ kiṭanta nāḷiṉu
			nilamāyt teṉkaṭa ṉilaviya nāḷiṉum
			vayaṅku toṉmi yiyaṅkap peṟṟavaṉi
			ūrūr tōṟu mūrntu pukuntē
			nāṭuka ṭōṟu nāṭi yaṭintē
		10.	vākkē yāṇṭum vaḻaṅkac ceytē
			mākkaḷ palariyu makka ḷākkiṉi
			mūṉṟu kaḻakamu māṉṟa pulavarum
			aṉpu nūloṭu miṉpuṟak kaṇṭaṉi
			muṉṉi yūḻō piṉṉip piṟaril
		15.	ceṟintiya luṟuppic ciṟitē yiḻantaṉi
			āṭa liciyeṉu nāṭaru muṟuppō
			māṟṟār vayappaṭa vāṟṟā tayarntaṉi
			iciyuṟup pillā ēḻiyeṉ ṟuṉṉi
			vaciyuṅ kūṟi vaḻakki vīḻttiṉar
		20.	niṉperuñ cēypalar niṉṉi maṟantē
			aṉṉavarp pōṟṟu maṉṉava rāyiṉar
			intap pōḻtiṉiṟ centamiḻc celvi!
			niṉṉici yōṅki muṉṉili peṟavē
			oruva ṉeḻuntē yarumpaṇ toṇṇūloṭu
		25.	* nilanīr vilaṅkē yolinūl palavum
			āyntāyn taṭikkē vēyntaṉa ṉiṉṟu
			karuṇā mirtacā karameṉu mōraṇi
			avvaṇi yiṭiyē cevviya curuti
			irupā ṉāṉkeṉu mirumpaḻaṅ kōṇmaṇi
		30.	polivuṟa vamittaṉaṉ pulavarkaṇ makiḻa
			aṉṉava ṉevaṉeṉiṟ ceṉṉi nāṭṭiṭit
			tañciyi luṟiyuñ ceñco lāḷaṉ
			karuṇā ṉanta ṉaruṇmikap peṟṟōṉ
			kalvip poruḷuñ celvap poruḷuñ
		35.	cāla nirampiya mēlava ṉaviyum
			piṟarkkup payaṉpeṟa vaṟacceyal koṇṭōṉ
			maruntu nūṟkaṭa laruntiya vaṟiñaṉ
			nātoṇṇūl kōtoṇṇūl pēṇum periyōṉ
			yāḻiṉ pulamiyum ēḻiṉ pulamiyun
		40.	tōṟṟiya tanti cāṟṟu tamiḻuyir
			uṇmi vaḻuvā naṇpaṉ
			āpira kāma māpula vōṉē.

civasrī-kaliyāṇacuntarayatīntira cuvāmikaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
	paṟpalavā yiramāyi ramvaruṭaṅ kaṭkumuṉarp parata kaṇṭam
		paraviyavōr taṉimoḻiyā yoppilatā yamiḻtiṉunaṟ paṇpu vāyntu
	poṟpuṟucaṅ kītacuvi yuṭaṉulavi nākarikap pulami vīcip
		puṟamoḻika ḷuṟpattik kātāra māviḷaṅkip pōṟṟa yārum
	aṟputamvi ḷittatami ḻoṉṟēyeṉ ṟataṉuṇmi yārāyn tōrntē
		yataṟkātā raṅkaḷeṇ ṇilavāka vēkāṭṭi yāru mecca
	naṟpuviyi luyarkaruṇā mirtacā karameṉunū ṉāṭṭi vāymi
		naviṉṟiṭṭāṉ tañcirāv cākēpā pirakāmnaṉ ṉāva lōṉē.
tañci ceṉṭ pīṭṭars hiskūl, talimit tamiḻp paṇṭitar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ā. cēturāma pāratiyār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
aṟucīrkkaḻiṉeṭilāciriya viruttam.
	1.	cīrpūtta vimayamali yeṉappoliyum potiyattiṟ ciṟantu tōṉṟip
		pērpūtta tamiḻamutat toṭumpaḻakik kaṅkiyilum perumi vāynta
		nīrpūtta poruninati pāytaliṉāṟ palavaḷaṅka ṇiṟiyap peṟṟu
		nārpūtta periyōrkaḷ vāḻvataṟku nalliṭamām pāṇṭi nāṭṭil,
	2.	pāṟkaṭaliṉ mītukaruṅ kārkkaṭalpōṟ kaṇvaḷarum paramaṉ mārpiṟ
		cēṟkaruṅkaṭ ṭirumakaḷun ticimukaṉā vamarntaruḷpā makaḷuñ cērntu
		nāṟkaṭalcū ḻavaṉiyuḷōr koṇṭāṭa vīṟṟirukku nalama tāki
		mēṟkaviñar vāḻtaluṟu cāmpavā ṉōṭiyeṉum patiyiṉ mēvi,
	3.	taṅkupuka ḻuṭiccāṉṟōr kulattalimi peṟumuttu cāmi vēḷum
		maṅkiyaruk karaciyā maṉṉammā ḷeṉumpuṉita mātu muṉcey
		poṅkutavap payaṉākip piṟantamutaṟ kumaraṉeṉap poliyuñ cīrāṉ
		caṅkaniti yeṉappulava ruḷaṅkaḷikkat tarupukaḻcēr takimi yuḷḷōṉ,
	4.	aṉṉiyiṉu maṉpuṭiyā ṉamitarumiṉ colluṭiyā ṉaṟivo ḻukkan
		tuṉṉuperum pukaḻuṭiyāṉ colluṟuti māṟāmi yuṭiya tūyōṉ
		taṉṉikari lātakaru ṇāṉanta cittaṉaruḷ cārta lālē
		maṉṉupati ṉeṇcitta nūlvakiyu mataṉporuḷu maṉatti ṉōrntōṉ,
	5.	avartalimā nilattilaruk kaṉikkaṇṭa paṉiyeṉṉa vaṭintōr nōykaḷ
		vilakiyiṭa naṉmaruntu mētakucā liyumvaḷañcēr cōli tāṉum
		kulavukaru ṇāṉantaṉ peyarālē kuyiṟṟiyaṟaṅ kuṟivi lātu
		nilipeṟupoṉ māṭamali tañcinakar taṉileṉṟu niṟuvi yuḷḷōṉ,
	6.	iyalicinā ṭakamūṉṟu miyintilaku tamiḻkkaṭali yiṉiti ṉōrntu
		mayalaṟumam mūṉṟiṉici nūṟperumi yituveṉṉa makita lattil
		nayamoḻicē ricippulava ravikkaḷatti ṉālāṟu curuti nāṭṭi
		viyaluṟuyāḻk karuviyiṭi yuḷḷaṅki nelliyeṉa viḷakkip piṉṉum,
	7.	muṉṉāḷiṟ ṟamiḻnāṭṭiṉ perumiyuman nāṭṭiṉiṉmut tamiḻōr vāḻntu
		paṉṉāḷu mattamiḻi yiyalicinā ṭakameṉṉap pakuttuk kāṭṭic
		coṉṉārka ḷeṉṉavataṉ perumiyumat tūyatamiḻp pāṭik kellām
		ennāḷun tāyāku meṉṟumataṟ kiciyapala vētuk kāṭṭi,
	8.	aintuvaki nilappaṇṇu mataṟkuriya yāḻiṉvaki yamippuṅ kūṟi 
		mintuṭiya veḻuvakippā liyumavaṟṟiṉ vakinalamum vakuttu māntar
		cintimakiḻn tiṭaviṉikkun tēvārap paṇvakiyuñ cīrtti mikka
		centamiḻiṟ poruṇṭicoṉ ṉaṭicēra vacaṉattiṟ ciṟappa tāka,
	9.	eṉṟumaḻi yātukaru ṇāmiruta cākarameṉ ṟiṉiya pērāṟ
		ṟuṉṟucuvi nūliyaṟṟic curutivallōrk kiṉitākat tokuttu rittāṉ
		naṉṟunama taracarmakiḻn tutaviyarāv cākēppe ṉalamun tāṅkic 
		ceṉṟupukaḻ ticitōṟuñ ciṟakkumā pirakāmeṉ cīmāṉ ṟāṉē.
arikēcanallūr makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi li. muttiyā pākavataravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatu.
nilamaṇṭila āciriyappā.
			pāṉila virinta māṉila meṉṉum
			kaṉṉi vataṉamāṅ kaṉṉi nāṭṭil
			āmpalam poykicūḻ cāmpava ṉōṭiyil
			cāṉṟār palarpukaḻ cāṉṟār marapiṟ
		5.	kaṉṉi mariyāḷ taṉṉikar kātalaṉ
			poṟpatam pōṟṟu maṟputa ṉoḻukkiṟ
			puvicē rappukaḻ cuvicēṭa muttaṉār
			naṉṉaya makāriṉ muṉṉava ṉāvōṉ
			aṉpum aruḷum iṉpuyar cīlamum
		10.	kalviyum aṟivum colviṉi yāṟṟalum
			īkiyum nītiyum vākiyum ūkiyum
			kalantoru vaṭivam nilantaṉi leṭutteṉak
			karutuṅ kaṉavā ṉiruniti vaḷattōṉ
			kiḷipala cūḻtara viḷinilaṅ kaṉimarap
		15.	poḻiloṭu tañciyil eḻilpeṟa vuṟivōṉ
			nitamakiḻn tēcuviṉ patamalar paravum
			pattaṉā yiṉumōr cittaṉār aruḷiṉāl
			māyiru ñālattōr āyur vētiyar
			uccimēṟ ṟāṅkiṉar meccu maruttuvaṉ
		20.	kaṟṟava revarkkuñ cuṟṟa māvōṉ
niṟipeṟu kalipala muṟipeṟat teḷintōṉ
			ōṅkiya pukaḻcēr āṅkila maṉṉavar
			kōvaruḷ ceyta rāvu cāyapup
			paṭṭa maṇintiv vaṭṭavā rulakil
		25.	māppira cattikūr āppira kāmeṉum
			paṇṭitaṉ paṇṭit taṇṭami ḻicinūl
			paṟpala āyntatiṉ naṟpala ṉākac
			centami ḻiciyē muntiya teṉṟum
			nālvakip pāliyiṉ mēlvaki virippa
		30.	varaṉmuṟi ēṟpa curanili maṟṟa
			iṉṉum icipala maṉṉu meṉṟum
			nāṭṭiya taṉṟik kūṭṭiya utāraṇam
			poruntiya paṟpala kirutika ḷiyaṟṟiyum
			curanili vevvēṟu karutiya matattiṉic
		35.	curuti yukti maruviya aṉupava
			mukattā laṭakki makattāñ curuti
			irupati ṉāṉkeṉa niṟuviṉaṉ nūṟpēr
			karuṇā mirtacā karameṉap puṉintumaṉ
			cirōmaṇi yākiya parōṭā vēntark
		40.	kariyatō ramiccar viritarak kūṭṭiya
			kīta avikkaṇum mētaku tañciyiṟ
			cērntōr capikkaṇu mārttiyi ṉaraṅkam
			ēṟṟic cīrttiyāl nāṟṟici muḻutum
			viḷaṅkiṭap pōrttaṉaṉ vaḷaṅkiḷar taravē
paṉṉīrilakkaṇam payilu muttamiḻc caṅkat talivar muttamiḻk kaviyaracu
civāṉanta yōki ṭākṭar, caṇmukampiḷḷi avarkaḷiyaṟṟiya
ciṟappuppāyiram.
	1.	iṟivaṉaru ḷilaṅkavemi yāṇṭaruḷu mammiyappa ṉiyalva lattāl
		maṟiyuṇarnta mōṉartiṟa lilaṅkumara ṇakaḻimatil vaḷañcēr tañci
		kaṟitapunaṟ patiyiltamiḻ ceḻittōṅkap potikimuṉi kaviṉkoṇ ṭārun
		tiṟikoḷmaṉa ṉāpirakāñ cumiyaṉcī raṟintapaṭi ciṟitu colvām.
	2.	miṉpūtta makarantam putunaṟavat tuṇcōli vekuvā yōṅkik
		koṉpūtta vamutameṉap puṭiparanta poṉṉinati kulavu kaṅkiṟ
		poṉpūtta ceṉṉalviḷi marutanilam polintilaku cōḻa nāṭṭiṟ
		ṟeṉpūtta varacarkaḷāṟ ceḻittāṇṭa tañcinakar ciṟakkuñ srīmāṉ.
	3.	pūmātu malarmātu pukaḻmātu polintilaku poṟpiṉ mikkāṉ
		nāmātu nēmātu nakimukattuñ colliṭattum nakāri yāṉōṉ
		tūmātu viḷaṅkumaṉat tūymiyoṭu vāymiyuḷan tuḷaṅkuñ cīrtti
		tēmātu kāvalpurin teḻiṉmātu viḷaṅkucem piyaṉā maṉṉō.
	4.	āṅkāṅku māṭaniṟi māḷikiyiṉ ṟokiyu
			mamippamippā muṟaviṉarka ḷamiyillat tokiyum
		tūṅkuperuñ cālicuṟṟil nuṭpamoṭu cuḻalum
			nūtaṉamār calayanti raṅkaṇmāru tattāl
		pāṅkupeṟa viyaṅkaḻakum palamarutak kaṅkiṟ
			piṅkarumpu vāḻicuvi yoṭṭumāṅ kaṉikaḷ
		ōṅkuteṅku vakikaḷuṭa ṉuyarmarutaṅ kaḷilē
			yuyarcālip peruṅkuviya luṉatavari yāmāl.
	5.	vāṉulakak kaṇkāṭci yintiraṉpōl vāḻum
			vaḷaṅkulavu māḷikiyil maññikaḷit takavum
		mīṉulavu cuṉikaḷilē miñiṟukaḷit tōṭa
			viḷiyāṭip pacumpulliṉ mītutuḷḷuṅ kāli
		āṉulavuṅ kaṉṟuṭaṉē yantiyelvi yiṉilē
			yaḻakākac cērntoḻuku maṭukkaṇi cevviyiṉik
		kōṉulavuñ cēṭaṉatu kulicanā viruntāṟ
			kuṟitturikka lākumeṉuṅ koḷkiyilnā ṇiṉaṉē.
	6.	varakuṇapāṇ ṭiyaṉṟoṭarpi lapirāma pāṇṭiyaṉ
			vaḻivanta vēṉāti nāyaṉār marapiṟ
		cikaramuḷa vaḻutimuttuc cāmiyaṉṉam māḷcey
			tivyatavaṅ kalināṉkoṉ paḵtaṟupa tiraṇṭil
		virakamaṟu teyvaravut tiriyutti rāṭam
			viyaṉcōma pūrvapakka mīroṉpāṉ nāḷiṟ
		kirakavamip patilācā ṉucca yōkaṅ
			kiḷarccimaṅ kaḷamataṉiṟ * keḻumiyutit taṉaṉē
	7.	araciyalā rarivimukurt tañciliyi lāral
			amicciyā makaratti lirākukaṭa kattiṟ
		paracukuru putaṉkētu ravicuṅka ṉariyiṟ
			patintacani yirāciyamip patippatintu pārkkiṟ
		caracakuṇa campaṉṉaṉ ṟavayōki cīlaṉ
			cācuvata yōkavāṉ ṟayimikukaṇ ṇiyaṉṟēr
		muracamoṭu yāḻkīta vakimuḻuṅkun tivviya
			muṉṟilāṉ ṟaṉvantiri mutalvaḷḷa lāmāl
	8.	teyvamuṟip paṇkaṭeri māvaracaṉ pāliṟ
			ṟikaḻrāṇi ripatūrmā tavarāvti vāṉuṅ
		kivanta kaṉiyeṉanā ṭakaviciyiṟ ṟērntē
			kaṇṭacati varicicuti muṟipicakā neṟiyil
		meyvanta pallavikaḷ viyaṉpāṭal patti
			mikukīrtta ṉivicitaṅ kēṭṭumakiḻ vuṟṟār
		poyvanta pulavarpuṟap pōkkiloḷit tēṅkap
			pukaḻmaṅka ḷamvāḻttup pulamimuḻaṅ kiṉavē
	9.	pantaṉañcey yākaṅkaḷ palavirata māṟṟum pakkuvattiṟ kēṟṟapala caṉaṉaṅkaḷ toṭarpāl
		intaṉañcēr tavantēruṅ karuṇā ṉanta viruṭitava rājayō kiyarpāli liḻuttuc
		cantamā rutamvīcuñ curuḷimalic celluñ camayattiṟ cārntāryā varuntaṉittu mayaṅka
		vantaṉicey muṟimiyoṭu mātavarka ḷuṭaṉē makimiyaṇi makiḻvāka vaṉacurattē kiṉarē
	10.	ēkiyavat tavattarpā liḷakumaṉa tuṭaṉē yīṟilāṉ pōloḷiru miruṭiyarpāl viṭukka
		yōkiyav virācamuṉi yuvantivaṉi nōkki yuṇmineṟi tavaṟiṭā tuyarniliyik kaṇṭuñ
		cēkarañcēr kulamuṟiyiṟ kuṇaccelva ṉeṉṟun teyvavaḻi pāṭuṭiya tīravā ṉeṉṟuñ
		cākarañcū ḻulakilivaṉ ṟaṉmipolin tōṅkat tavānilitēr mūlikic caraḷaṅkāṭ ṭiṉarē.
	11.	ivvitamāy maṉumuṟiyiṟ pirapalamā māṟaṅ kiyaṟṟiyiru ḷakaṟṟiyapiṉ paḵtāṇṭu kaḻittu
		evvamelān toliyappiṉ pivaṉulakiṟ kāmā ṟiyaṟṟiviṉit toṭararuppan toliyavaruḷ 													purivāṉ
		cevvitēr cikicciyaṇi maruntucin tūran teḷipaṟpa mutalāya vilēkiyaṅkaḷ kuḷiki
		avviyami lāmaṟṟan tammarapu viḷaṅka vamimakaṉā kaccitta roruvaṉittan taṉarē.
	12.	maruttuvantēr toḻilivaṉāl vaḻivaḻiyēyulakar varuntutuṉpa makaṉṟumaṅ kaḷameyti makiḻap
		poruttamuṟuñ cikiccipalap purintivaṇē ruṟṟa puṇṇiyaṅkaḷ tiraṇṭupala vūḻimikap peruka
		aruttamiṭa mēvalā tiyavellā mamintiṅ kaṉpelā maṇimaṇiyā yaḻakamintu peruka
		tiruttamiñcuñ ceykiyelān teyvaruṭaḻikkat tēcavara cāḷmaṉṉar cevviyilvāḻ 													pavaṉē.
vēṟu
	13.	aḷitūṅkuñ curumpārkku muyyā ṉatti laḻakuyarun teṅkupuḷi kōṅku vāḻi
		kaḷitūṅku macōkatti yāccā vātti kāṉmara milantipaṉi kaṉakaṟ cūram
		muḷitūṅku koṉṟinelli nāval canta muruṅkicaṇ pakamīntu puṉṉi nārtti
		kiḷitūṅku māppalā vilvam pāli kēḻpuṉṉi pātiriyun tamālamā palavē.
vēṟu
	14.	makara murukkum paṭrōjā koṭicam paṅki vaḷarmalli
		cikara kucumam maṉaracitan tērpam pilmās mātuḷiyum
		pakara vilañci mariyāmpaṟ pakarum nīlōṟ palampicaṅkan
		takarak kariya kuḻalārkaḷ taṅkuñ cōli kaḷiṟcamiyum.
	15.	kāṇu meḻutā veḻuttataṉiṟ kalanta vaṉēka piriveḻuttun
		tōṇuñ ceṭipūṅ koṭiyuruvan tulaṅku viḷimpiṉ pūṅkoṭiyun
		tāṇu meḻuttiṉ vakiyaṟikaḷ camiyum vaḷiyen tiracāli
		pūṇu māṭi paṅkaḷā pukaḻkal mēji pīrōkkaḷ.
	16.	karumpo ṉaḻakār kēṭṭamittuk kitē rūliṅ piṇṭiṅkum
		arumpu māṭpiras kiḷēsrīmkaḷ alamār mika ḷamiyuruḷum
		virumpu mulakar kaṭkiṉitāy viḷaṅku nōṭīs pukpāraṅ
		karutum ṭipskaḷ vārppiṭaṅkaḷ kāṭcik kiyalu maticayamē.
	17.	kāla tēya nikaḻvataṉāṟ kalanta vulakar tamivāṭṭak
		kōla viṉiyiṉ toṭarpu toṭṭuk kūṟṟāyvanta nōykaḷvaki
		mūlaṅ kālan terintuṇarntu mutirun tēkak kūṟuṇarntu
		ñāla mataṉil mūlikki nāṭṭan terinta nayacukuṇaṉ.
vēṟu
	18.	tēṉoḻuku milēkiyamāt tirikaṭṭuvāti ciṟantaracā yaṉaṅkaḷcin tūranaṟ paṟpam
		vāṉoḻuku mamutaṣar pattumutalāya māmaruntu vakikaḷvaṭa kaṅkaḷkuḻit tilam
		kāṉoḻuku puṭpavaki paṉṉī rattar kāṇumnō yāḷikaḷki kaṇṭapari kāram
		mīṉoḻuku vāṉulaka vintiraṉippōla mēṉmiyil laṟamnaṭattum vivēkakaṇ ṇiyaṉē
vēṟu
	19.	pūrkku mulaka pāṣikaḷiṟ polivāy viḷaṅkip pukaḻmalintiṅ
		kārkkum nalantē rarumiyuya raraṅkat tirunta mūvēntar
		cērkkun teyvac cuvipaḻuttut teḷinta vamiḻtān tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ
		ṟūrkku maṉātit toṉmiyiṉit tulaṅka viḷakkuñ cuyātipati.
	20.	naṟavu tuḷikkun tuṇarmaliyu napacayat tōṅkum paṇiyillil
		uṟavō ruṭaṉē vuṇṭukaḷit tuvakkuñ cīla ṉuḷappuṉitaṉ
		tuṟavōr kāṇiṟ cūḻntaṉṉōr tuṇiyi nāṭun tōṉṟalaṉāṉ
		puṟavik kātta puravalaṉpōṟ pukaḻtēr niliyiṟ puṟañcellāṉ.
	21.	aṉpa ritaya vuḷakkōyi lārnta paramā ṉantaveḷḷaṉ
		tuṉpa maṉatiṟ ṟaṭṭāta tuṅkat tuṇiyō raviyakalāṉ
		iṉpa niṟivān tulākkōli liruntiv vulakōr iṭartīrppāṉ
		muṉapi ṉuṇarnta nēyampōl mukavi lāca muṟuvaluḷāṉ.
vēṟu
	22.	kalipayiṉṟa vaṭivaḻakaṉ kaliyāṇa mukavaḻakaṉ kaṉinta viṉcoṉ
		malipayiṉṟa peruṅkuṇattaṉ varampayiṉṟa vaḷḷalaṉā uḷamā ṟātu
		nilipayiṉṟa neṟiyaḻakaṉ nicampayiṉṟa collaḻakaṉ niṉiviṉ mēlān
		talipayiṉṟa tuliyaḻakaṉ tāratam miyamilāc caturaṉ maṉṉō.
vēṟu
	23.	kaṇṇār nutalā ṉaruḷcuranta karuṇi vilācan tērtamiḻiṉ
		eṇṇārn toḷiru miyaltērntiṅ kēkat tuvatti ṉiliterintu
		paṇṇārn toḷirpāṭ ṭiyaltērntu payiluntāḷa catilayicīr
		viṇṇārn tiyalkiṉ ṉararāti mēṉmi viḷaṅkac ceyumvituraṉ.
	24.	ulakat taṉāti nilikaḷilē yōṅki yoḷirun tamiḻmoḻiyi
		vilaki yoḷiru meyññāṉat tiyaltē ricinā ṭakattamiḻiṉ
		ṟilakam pōlu marumiyiṉic celuttun teyvac curutilayi
		kalakaṅ kāṇā virākattiṉ kaṇakkit terikkuṅ kaṇkāṇi.
	25.	ūṟu tōṉṟā niliyuṇarntaṅ kutikku mēlā mupacāntan
		tēṟu niliniṉ ṟaṟiviṉmayat tiyāṉan tērciṉ mayamuṟṟu
		vīṟu mirāja yōkiyarkaḷ mēlām nilitēr vivēkattaṉ
		āṟu terintō ramutūṟum vaḷavāṇ ṭakiyā riyaṉāvāṉ.
vēṟu
	26.	iyalicinā ṭakattiyali yakattiyarā ṟiraṇṭākku mērpō rāmal
		ayalāṉā riṭumpipala vāṟṟimiñcu macaṭṭikuṇa vakanti māḷu
		mayalāṉār kaṭkelā maṉṉumeḻu tāveḻuttil makiḻnta ḷittāṉ
		kayalpuraḷuñ cuṉicōli tañcivā ḻāpirakāṅ kaṇyaṉ maṉṉō
vēṟu
	27.	poṉṉā lilaku maṉiyāḷum puviyā lilaku makaṇmārun
		teṉṉā teriya licināṭa kantēr ceḻippiṉ ciṟappōci
		muṉṉē viḷaṅku māṭakattiṉ mutita mōṅka muḻakkumeṉil
		eṉṉē teyva mātarkaḷi ṉiyalpi viḷakku meṇṭikkum.
	28.	vāṇi nikartta maṉiyāḷum valattiṟ kiyinta makaṇmāruñ
		cāṇi piṭitta virattiṉampōṟ ṟakunta muṟiyiṟ cuticērttu
		vīṇi mutalām vāttiyattil viḷaṅkak kitēr vititavaṟā
		tāṇi yuṭaṉvī cattarikkā larimuk kāliṟ curavamippi.
	29.	palañcē raṉēka kālattiṟ palitta tavattiṉ paṇpataṉāṟ
		kulañcē rariya māṉuṭattiṟ kuṟikkun teyvak kaṇavakiyiṟ
		palañcē rīcaṉ pattiyupā caṉiyō ṭiciyā ṉantattil
		alañcēr pākki yantaḻikku marumi yataṉi yacipparitē.
vēṟu
	30.	caṅkīta rattiṉā kararstāyi yoṉṟiṟ
			ṟakuñcuruti yirupatti raṇṭeṉanic cayittār
		aṅkatamā yamintapala ruṭkarutti yakaṟṟi
			yataṟkiyinta kaṇakkālaṅ kamimēṟkōḷ kāṭṭi
		caṅkatamā yirupatti nāṉkucuru tikaḷē
			cācuvata meṉṉanic cayattakimi yuṟṟāṉ
		iṅkitamā kiyacukuṇa ṉēntaleṉu māprakām
			iṉṉamuta curutiyiṟṟē riruṭiyeṉa lāmē.
	31.	mutaṟcaṅka muṉpallā yiravaruṭa kāla
			muṉmuḻaṅku māyppāli vaṭṭap pāliyiyu
		mataṟkiṇiyān tirikōṇañ caturappā likaṭ
			kannāḷi lakattiyaṉār ciṟuperunū lamittār
		kutarkkamiṟol kāppiyaṉār cārpaṇiyiṟ ciṟanta
			koḷkiyiyaṟ paṉṉiraṇṭāyk kuṟittaṇinūṟ koṭuttār
		putalvarkuru muṉivarvaḻi paṉṉiruvar ceyunūṟ
			puṇarpaṟiyā viḷampulavar puṟampōntā roḷittē.
	32.	āyappā liyiluḷḷa veḻupāli kaḷiyum
			āmvaṭṭap pāliyī riraṇṭeṉumyāḻ kaḷiyum
		nēyamuṭa ṉatilnāṉku cātikaḷiṉ vakiyil
			nēruriya virākaṅka ḷattaṉiyun teḷivāy
		āyakali karnāṭa pūrvacaṅkī tatti
			lamiyicippā vaḻaṅkunuṭpa māñcuruti vakiyiṟ
		ṟūyavaṟu patumēlu tāraṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟac
			colliyeḻu tāveḻuttiṟ ṟulaṅkakkāṭ ṭiṉaṉē.
	33.	virumpukarnā ṭakaccuruti viḷinuṭpak karuttil
			mēṉāṭṭār kaḷiṉkaṇakki mēṉiṟutti yotukki
		arumpumuṟip paṭinuṭpa māṅkaṇakkiṉ ṟokiyi
			aṟintukoḷḷum paṭiteḷivāyk kāṭṭiṉatu maṉṟi
		tirumpuñ cā pāmūṉṟi loṉṟukālmā kāṇi
			tērkāṇi yarikkāṇi kīḻariyē yarikkāl
		karumpāmuk kāṇimun tiriyimmi yēḻu
			kaṇakkiṭumpō tiṟpalariṉ muṟikaḷik kaṇṭittē.
	34.	pūrvatamiḻ muṟiyāku miciyilā ḷatti
			poruntaccey muṟikaḷilum naṭañceymuṟi kaḷilum
		ārvavapi nayañceymuṟi koṭṭuṅkaru vikaḷi
			laṇitāṉaṅ kāṭṭiyāḻ mīṭṭumuṟi kaḷiluñ
		cārvētaṅ kaṇitacō tiṭavittiya caranūṟ
			ṟakumoḻikaḷ vakicaṅkī tattiluḷa teṉṉap
		pārvipeṟuṅ kaṇakkukaḷiṟ kitērnta nipuṇaṉ
			pākkiyaṉāp pirāmpaṇṭi taṉṟiṟami palavē.
	35.	ērpōṅkun tūlacūk kumakāra ṇattiṟ
			kiyalyāḻi ṉamippukaḷmā ṉuṭatēkat tamippāyc
		cārpōṅkuñ curuticuraṅ kāraṇatē kattiṟ
			ṟakuṅkaṇakki ṉōcinili yalikaḷaḷa voṭumuṉ
		pūrvatamiḻ makkaḷ ṉātiyiṉi lāciyā
			pukaḻturukki yiccārnta mespittō miyāvil
		ārvamuṭaṉ pāpilō ṉiṉivēyā mutalā
			mamināṭu kaḷiṟceṉṟaṅ kamittārpaṭ ṭiṉaṅkaḷ.
	36.	kiramamuṟip paṭivekuvā yaracāṇṭa vaṉṉōr
			kiruṭṭiṉaṉkoḷ maccāva tāravaṭi vataṉi
		piramiyilā voḻukkamuṭaṉ pūcitta tavantēr
			piratāṉa tamiḻmakkaṭ peruntokiyā reṉṟuñ
		ciramamilā mīṉvaṭivāṟ kāppāṟṟap paṭṭōr
			ciṟappārcat tiyavirata pāṇṭiyaṉṟeṉ kūṭaṟ
		piramanili terintāṇṭu mīṉkoṭiyi yuyarttum
			pirapalapāṇ ṭiyareṉṉap perituvakkiṉ ṟaṉarē.
	37.	iraṇṭāmā yiravaruṭa muṉṟamiḻnūṟ kaḷilē
			yēpirēyu cītyakirīk jarmaṉcāṉs kirīṭiṟ
		ṟiraṇṭamoḻik kalappukaḷic ciṟituṅkaṇ ṭaṟiyāc
			ciṟappataṉāṟ ṟamiḻmoḻiyē mutalmoḻiyān takimi
		taraṇimuḻu tōṅkivaḷarn tulaviyatoṉ miyiṉāṟ
			ṟaṉitteyva moḻiyeṉat tavamakimi yuṟṟōr
		iraṇiyampōṟ pōṟṟutiru mantirantē vāram
			iyaṟṟiruvā cakavaruṭpā vēṟṟattiṟ palavē.
	38.	iyalamippāṟ pirāṇikaḷi leḻumolika ḷellā
			mēṟkaṉavē tamiḻeḻuttā leḻutoṉiyik koṇṭum
		ceyaṉmikumōr kacaṭatapa veḻuttukkaḷ ciṟappiṟ
			ṟērntaviṉa moṉṟākit teyvamoḻi yāki
		ayalākum pāṣikaḷiṉ ṟuṇiyetirpā rāmal
			aṟitaṟkum payilutaṟku matikavilē cāki
		mayaloḻiyat tutittaṟku mamiḻtiṉuntēr teyva
			vallapattit tarumoḻiyāy vaḻaṅkivanta tituvē.
	39.	kāraṇakā riyantērntu katimiyara cāḷuṅ
			kikkuvār makārājā kaṉakaparō ṭāvil
		āraṇacaṅ kītavitya kāṉparasi laruṭēr
			āpirakām kiḻattiyammāḷ pākkiyavarun tatiyum
		pūraṇacaṅ kītavitya vātyavī ṇikaḷiṟ
			poruttamuṟap pāṭuṅkiṉ ṉaramituṉa makaṇmār
		tāraṅkoḷ marakataval liyumavaṇēr cakaca
			catittuvaṅkoḷ kaṉakavallik kiṇiyāva revarē.
	40.	aṉṉāṇīḷ tavantēṟuñ cuntarapāṇ ṭiyappēr
			amiputalvaṉ aṇiyiciyiṟ ṟumpuruva raṉṉāṉ
		poṉṉārnta turirāja marumakaṉpāṇ ṭiyaṉum
			pukaḻpāka vatarāmpañ cāpakēca vācāṉ
		teṉṉāri rājacapi yiṉilāyap pāli
			tirikōṇa caturavaṭṭap pālippaṇ kaḷiyum
		maṉṉārntu pukaḻkūṟa makiḻperiyar vāḻttu
			valamaṅka ḷampāṭi variyaṟiyuṟ ṟaṉarē.

civacittānta makācamāja attiyaṭcarum, ceṉṉi meykaṇṭa cantāṉa capikkāriyatariciyum, cempiyam hāṉareri mājesṭirēṭṭumākiya
paṇṭitaratṉam. makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi puḻali tirunāvukkaracu mutaliyāravarkaḷiyaṟṟiyatu.
aṟucīr viruttam.
	pañcanati cūḻtañci mānakarcey mutiyatavap payaṉāy vantu
	mañcaṇiyum vāṉulaka maruttuvaruṅ kaṇṭuvakka vakiyiṉāyntu
	tañcameṉa vantaṭivārk kañcaleṉa nōynāṭit tavirkku mēlām
	viñcimiku muyarkaruṇā ṉantaṉavaṉ vittiyattiṉ mikunta celvaṉ.(1)
	taṉṉikaril kaṭalpuṭicū ḻulakamelān taṉikkuṭikkīḻt taṉitteñ ñāṉṟum
	maṉṉiyavāṉ ṟoṭumuṉiceṅ kōlōcci yaracāḷu makimi cāṉṟa
	tuṉṉiyavāṅ kilanaṟṟā yaṉiyaturit taṉattavarāṟ ṟulaṅkat tanta
	paṉṉariya mikupukaḻvāyn tilakuperum rāvcāhep paṭṭam peṟṟōṉ(2)
	aṉṉavaṉyā reṉilaṉittum peṟṟilaṅku miyalpuṟumey yaṟivāṉ mikka
	coṉṉatoru moḻikkumoḻi tittikku miṉimitarun tūymi yāḷaṉ
	naṉṉalaṅkaṭ kuriyavelām puriyuñcaṟ kuṇaṅkaḷoṭu navilun toṉmi
	eṉṉavuṅkaṟ ṟuṇarntavuyar āppirakām paṇṭitareṉ ṟiyampu nallōṉ (3)
	palakalikku muyariyatā yucciyiṉmēl vittumakiḻ parivā leṉṟum
	nilavaliya melāntutikkap poṟpamaraṟ putamviḷitta nikari lāppēr
	nilaviṭuñcen tamiḻkkuriya palakōṭi muṟipiṟaḻā niyāyaṅ kāṭṭi
	nalamikutaṉ perumuyaṟcik kuṟutuṇiyā rāycciyiṉi naṉku nāṭṭi.(4)
	vaṭamoḻikku muṉmoḻiteṉ moḻiyeṉuntol kāppiyaṅkaḷ valivāc coṟṟa
	tiṭamuṟunaṟ pirapalapra māṇavaki vakuttatoru tiṟami nōkki
	kaṭaṉikarkkuṅ kalviyiṉi yuṇṭēppa miṭupavaruṅ kaviṉcēr vāca
	aṭalamaruñ ciṟapputaṉi yeṭutturikka varitariteṉ ṟaṟiva tōrntu(5)
	pāliyeva ruṅkaṇṭa vuṭaṉparuku vāralatu paḻippār yārin
	nūliyeṭut tōtavali yaṟivumilā nuṭpamati nōkkuṅ kāṉāṉ
	mēlivaru vatumaṟiyā vavāvataṉi muṉṉiṭṭu mīkkū ṟuñcīr
	cālitavaḻn tōṅkukaru ṇāmiruta cākāranūl tantiṭ ṭāṉāl.	(6)
ceṉṉi mayilāppūr seṉtōm kalācāliyiṟ ṟalimit tamiḻp paṇṭitarāyiruntavarum kavaraṉmeṇṭil upakāraccampaḷam peṟṟu varupavarumākiya
tilliyampūrt tamiḻppaṇṭitar sōmayāji
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi vēṅkaṭarāma aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ pāṭiyutaviya cāttukavi
āciriyappā
			ulakiṟ palakali vittika ḷōṅkum
			ikkali nāḷiṉ kaviṉi yurikkiṉ
			aṟaneṟi yaruḷneṟi yaṉittu muṉṉili
			māṟip putunaṭi malintaṉa veṅkum
		5.	nūtaṉa vivēkikaḷ nuḻintār palarum
			pataviyuñ cukamum pōkamum palikka
			maṟṟati neñcil matiyā māṇpiṉar
			muṉṉilā nōyum muṟiyilāk kalviyum
			iyāṉeṉa teṉpatu miṇintu vērūṉṟiṉa
		10.	ittati kaṇṭē yiṇiyilā varamām
			camayacañ cīvi tarimē leṉṉak
			karuṇiyē nitipōṟ kaṇṭa toṉṟeṉa
			ānanta veḷḷa matuvē yuruveṉa
			orumā cukuṇari yutaviṉaṉ paramaṉ
		15.	kaliyumiṅ killi kaṭunō yilli
			āviṉik kāppatil āvalē mikkavar
			aṟiviṉ mikkavar arumaṟi kāppavar
			taṉvan taripōṟ ṟarinō yaḻippavar
			irupat tintā māṇṭi ṉaḷavum
		20.	kāṭum maliyuṅ kaḷittē tirintavar
			paḻanaṭi taṉṉip pāriṟ kākka
			karuṇā nantarāyk kāṇavē vantavar
			curuḷi maliyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya cittari
			aṭipaṇin tavarpāl muppūc cūkkumam
		25.	 muṟiyā yuṇarntē muṉiyaruḷ peṟṟā
			roppilāc cittarum ōruri viṇṭaṉar
			nīyē yulakil nīḷici peṟuvāy
			maṟṟoru cittarum makiḻntē yaruḷuvar
			āyuḷum perukum aṟivā rāyccit
		30.	 tuṟiyuḷ mutaṉmi muṟṟuvi niccayam
			cōtiṭam vittiyam cūkkuma nāta
			vintuviṉ cūkkumam viricaṅ kītam
			āmin nāṉkiṉu maṉittula kōrum
			pōṟṟuvar cāṟṟuvar puṉitanī yeṉavē
		35.	cantati yuṭaṉē campat tellām
			tāmē vantiṭun tarimē lintu
			kirakamē kātacam kiṭitta ciṟappāl
			kaṉṉiyē lakkiṉam kaṉacuka nāṉkiṟ
			cantiraṉ cevvāy catiruṟac cārntār
		40.	pakiyē yulakiṟ pārā rivareṉa
			cūriyaṉ ṟōṉṟal cūḻntāṉ virayam
			cūriyaṉ ṟaciyē mutalāy nērntatu
			kaṭali ṉeḻunta kaṭumā viṭattik
			kaṇṭat taṭakkiya kāla kaṇṭar
		45.	pōlavē piṇiyip pōkka vantavar
			uruttirar taṉmiyi yulakiṟ kuṇartta
			iravutri yāṇṭi littari vantavar
			caṅkīta caṅkan tañciyi lākkip
			pallāṇ ṭataṉip pālit tappāṟ
		50.	parōṭā varacar paṇpuṟu capiyi
			licinā lāṟeṉa vicivuṟak kāṭṭiṉar
			tañciyil vāḻpavar taṅkiḷi yōṭum
			matuvaṉaṅ kaṇṭō maṟṟit tañciyil
			eṉavē pulakō rēttuñ cōliyum
		55.	ākki mūliki yaṉittuṅ kaṇṭaṉar
			rāvcā kippeṉa rāṇiyiṉ pōr
			tanta peyarun tarimēṟ peṟṟavar
			karuṇā mirutamām cākarap perunūl
			icinūl vakiyi liyaṟṟiya mēlōṉ
		60.	mutaṉmik kuṟimu muntuṟu peyarār
			āpira kāmeṉu mariyapaṇ ṭitarē.
The Opinion of Dr. Sir. Subramania Iyer, Avl. K.C.I.E.,L.L.D.,
Dewan Bahadur, Retired Judge High Court, Madras.

SIR. S. SUBRAMANIA IYER.					BEACH HOUSE,
								MYLAPORE,
								MADRAS, 9TH APRIL 1917.

Rao Saheb M. Abraham Pandither Avl’s work, Karunamrita Sagaram, is a technical treatise on which it is not competent for me to express any opinionworth recording. This much however must be clear to all, that the subject dealt with has been investigated with great throughness. The work cannot but prove invaluable and though my suggestion may seem strange yet I would venture to add that the substance of his discoveries should. if possible, be made accessible to English readers in a small book. I am sure the author will not find it impossible to carry out my suggestion and add to the great services he has rendered to the community during these many years. I wish his work every success.
(Sd.) S. SUBRAMANIA IYER.
Rao Sahib M. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ eḻutiya karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṟa nūlāṉatu caṅkīta cāstira viṣayattip paṟṟip pēcuvatāl caṅkītan teriyāta nāṉ atip paṟṟi apippirāyam colvatu takutiyāyirātu. āṉāl anta nūli vācippōrukkut teḷivākat terivateṉṉa veṉṟāl, collappaṭukiṟa viṣayamāṉatu pūraṇa ārāycciyiṉ payaṉākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. inta nūlāṉatu attiyanta pirayōjaṉamuḷḷatāyirukku meṉpataṟkut taṭiyēyilli. nāṉ colluvatu cilarukku vittiyāca mākat tōṟṟiṉālum tōṟṟalām. ateṉṉa veṉṟāl avarkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭitta putu muṟiyāṉatu iṅkilīṣil ciṟu puttakamāka eḻutappaṭṭu iṅkilīṣ vācikkakkūṭiya yāvarukkum koṭukkappaṭum paṭcattil atika naṉmi payakkum. innūlāciriyarkku itu kūṭāta kāriyamalla. avarkaḷ itiyum ceyvārkaḷāṉāl kaṭanta pala varuṣaṅkaḷāka jaṉaṅkaḷukku avarkaḷ ceytirukkum pala naṉmikaḷōṭu ituvum oru naṉmiyākum. inta nūl taḻittōṅki naṉmi payakkavēṇṭumeṉpatē eṉṉuṭiya maṉappūrvamāṉa eṇṇam."
(oppam) S.  cuppiramaṇiya aiyar.
The opinion of the Honourable
Sir P.S. Sivaswamy Aiyer, Avl., K.C.S.I., C.I.E.,

SUDHARMA,
EDWARD ELLIOT’S ROAD,
MYLAPORE.
Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandither has for several years been an enthusiastic devotee and patron of the science and art of South Indian Music and has been with tireless industry prosecuting an investigation into the number of Srutis in vogue in Karnatic Music. He has also from time to time offered practical demonstrations of his theoris at the various musical conferences convened or attended by him.
Expert opinion may be divided as to the correctness of his conclusions but there can be no two opinions as to the zeal and learning of the author or the value of the example set by him of a scientific study of the theory of Hindu Music. He deserves to be congratulated on the issue of the portly volume named ‘Karunamirthasagaram which is the outcome of his labours in a neglected field.
12-4-1917.						(Sd.) P. S. SIVASWAMY AIYER
Rao Sahib M. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ anēka varuṣaṅkaḷākat teṉṉintiya caṅkīta cāstirattiyum ataṉ vitikaḷi aṉupōkamāy upayōkikkum muṟiyiyumpaṟṟi veku cirattiyuṭaṉ ārāyntiruppatu mallāmal, caṅkīta vittikku oru pōṣakarāyum iruntirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ curutikaḷi niccayam ceyvatil iṭiviṭāta muyaṟciyōṭu uḻittirukkiṟārkaḷ. ituvu mallāmal tāṉ kūṭṭiya caṅkaṅkaḷilum tāṉ ājarāyirunta caṅkīta caṅkaṅkaḷilum itu viṣayatti anēkantaram rucuppaṭuttiyumirukkiṟārkaḷ.
avarkaḷ muṟi cariyō tappō eṉpatippaṟṟic caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷil palar pala apippirāyam koḷḷalām. āṉāl nūlāciriya ruṭiya virākkiyam kalvikaḷippaṟṟiyāvatu, intiya caṅkīta cāstira ārāycci viṣayattil avarkaḷ kāṇpitta muṉ mātiriyip paṟṟiyāvatu iraṇṭu apippirāyam illi. ellārum ēkavākkāy avarkaḷip pukaḻavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. vekukālam acaṭṭi paṇṇap paṭṭirunta caṅkīta viṣayattil avarkaḷ ceyta vēliyiṉ palaṉāka ippōtu acciṭap paṭṭirukkum karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṟa perunūli eḻutiyataṟkāka nām avarkaḷukku maṉappūrvamāṉa vāḻttutal collukiṟōm."

The opinion of  Dewan Bahadur, the Honourable
Mr. Justice, T. Sadasivier, Avl., B.A., M.L., Madras
A copy of Karunamirta Sagaram by M. R. Ry. Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandither of Tanjore has been sent to me. It is an elaborate treatise on the theory and practice of Music, covering about a thousand pages of the demi-quarto size. One of the many knotty problems that the work grapples with is the exact number of Srutis in the Octave. Whereas Saranga Deva and those who profess to follow him clim to have fixed the number at twenty two, Mr. Pandither seeks to establish that it is twenty four. His conclusion rests upon a scholarly investigation into the art of music in general and that of the ancient Tamils in particular - a conclusion, moreover, whose truth is sid to have been verified in the actual singing of his dughters.
It is not given for a Iayman like myself to speak with authority on the relative merits of the two theories. But it may be observed that there are musical experts who have given their hearty support to the theory which Mr. Pandither clims to have discovered. There can be no doubt that those interested in the divine art, especially as it previls and has previled in India, will find the present work highly interesting and instructive.
In the course of his researches into the state of Karnatic Music, the author has collected a body of valuable information regarding the greatness of the language, the literature and the arts of the ancient Tamils. which must prove very helpful to students of the history of Tamilagam. 
5-3-17.								(Sd.) T. SADASIVIER.
"makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi rāv cāyap āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṟa nūl eṉ pārvikku aṉuppap paṭṭatu. atu intiya caṅkīta cāstirattippaṟṟiyum, ataṉ vitikaḷi upayōkikkum viṣayattippaṟṟiyum pēcukiṟa oru vistāramāṉa nūl. ṭemik kuvārṭṭēra cicil āyiram pakkaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyatu. anta nūl eṭuttup pēcum cikkumukkāṉa anēka viṣayaṅkaḷil oṉṟu ēteṉṟāl oru stāyiyil ettaṉi curutikaḷ uṇṭu eṉpatām. cāraṅkatēvarum avaric cērntavarkaḷum  curutikaḷ 22 eṉṟu collukiṟārkaḷ. 
Mr. paṇṭitaravarkaḷō atu 24 eṉṟu stāpikka muṉ vantirukkiṟārkaḷ. intiya caṅkīta cāstiratti, vicēṣamāyp pūrva tamiḻariṉ cāstiratti veku cāmarttiyattuṭaṉ ārāycci ceytataṉ palaṉāka inta apippirāyam cātikkappaṭukiṟatu. atuvumallāmal intac curutikaḷiṉ uṇmi avarkaḷ kumārattikaḷ tāṅkaḷē pāṭi rucuppaṭuttiṉatāl niccayamākkappaṭṭirukkiṟa tākavum teriyavarukiṟatu.
iraṇṭuvita apippirāyaṅkaḷil etu cariyeṉṟu atikārattuṭaṉ eṭuttuc colvatu caṅkītam teriyāta eṉṉip pōṉṟavarkaḷukku iyaluvatalla. āṉāl nāṉ collakkūṭiyatu Mr. paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kaṇṭu piṭittirukkiṟa muṟi cariyāṉateṉṟu oppukkoḷḷum caṅkīta viṟpaṉṉarkaḷum uṇṭu eṉpatoṉṟē.

karnāṭaka caṅkītattippaṟṟiya ārāycci ceyta viṣayattil tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ mēṉmi paṇṭit tamiḻaruṭiya nūlkaḷ maṟṟa vittikaḷ mutaliya viṣayaṅkaḷippaṟṟi arumiyāṉa anēka uṇmikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu āciriyar colliyirukkiṟārkaḷ. inta apippirāyaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattippaṟṟi ārāycci ceypavarkaḷukkup perum payaṉ tarumeṉpataṟkuc cantēkamilli."

T.V. SESHAGIRI AIYAR						ANNANDALE
								HALL’S ROAD, EGMORE,
								MADRAS, S.C.
Rao Saheb M. Abraham Pandither is well known throughout Southern India as a gentleman who has studied Indian Music scientifically, and one who has interested the public by his scholarly disquisitions on it. The present great work while it is primarily devoted to the exposition of the science and to correcting some popular misconceptions regarding it, indirectly gives a part of the history of Southern India as far as it is gatherable from musical compositions. The history of Tamil literature is also touched upon in the course of the book.
I have gone through portions of the book and I find the disquistions both instructive and highly interesting. The Pandither has lid the Tamil public under deep obligations to him.

The opinion of M. R. Ry. T. Chelvakesavaroya Mudaliar, Avl., M.A.,
	SUPERINTENDENT OF VERNACULAR STUDIES,
	PAICHAIYAPPA’S COLLEGE, MADRAS.
Karunamrita Sagaram by Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandithar is a voluminous work in four parts on the theory and art of music. In his systematic and comparative study of the different systems, and from his original researches, the author arrives at the conclusion that the primeval system of the Dravidas (the Tamils) is indigenous and unexcelled. A revival of the study of the ancient classics and their publication have confirmed the status of the Dravidas in the civilization of the old world. Silappathikaram, one of these classics, has been instrumental in determining the age of the Madura Tamil Academy very approximately. The glossary of Jayangondan and the elaborate commentary of a major portion of this epic by Adiyarkunallar have been instrumental in the hands of Abraham Pandithar in proving beyond doubt the scientific development and finish of the Dravidian system of music. These commentaries were written at a time when only parts of some of the ancient treatises on music were extant.
The compilation and array of facts, and patient research in establishing his thesis/ show how the author has accomplished the Herculean task imposed by himself upon himself. Extracts from a number of out-of-the way authors of antiquities afford evidence of the vast erudition of the author and his collaborators.
The book is written in simple modern Tamil prose. Any ordinary student can read and understand the author’s ideas. There can be no doubt that a reader with some knowledge of the theory of music can be immensely benefited by a careful study of the work.
(Sd.) T. chelvakesavaroyan.
“Rao Sahib M. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa karuṇāmirtacākaram eṉṟa nūlāṉatu caṅkīta cāstirattiyum aṉupava muṟiyiyum paṟṟippēcukiṟa 4 pākaṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya vistāramāṉa nūl. palamuṟikaḷiyum kavaṉamāy ottup pārttut taṉ conta ārāycciyiṉ palaṉāka innūlāciriyar, tirāviṭar allatu tamiḻaruṭiya pūrva tamiḻmuṟiyē ātimutal tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkivantateṉṟum ataṟku miñciya caṅkītam vēṟoṉṟu milliyeṉṟum kaṇṭupiṭittirukkiṟār.
paḻiya tamiḻnūlkaḷ piracurañceyyappaṭṭu yāvarālum paṭikkappaṭṭatiṉ palaṉ eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tirāviṭar ātikālamutal kalvi nākarīkam mutaliyavaṟṟil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṉum apippirāyamāṉatu uṟuticeyyappaṭṭatu. intac ciṟanta nūlkaḷil oṉṟākiya cilappatikāramāṉatu maturit tamiḻccaṅkam irunta kālattit tīrmāṉampaṇṇuvatil mukkiya utaviyāyiruntatu. jeyaṅ koṇṭāṉuṭiya arumpata vuriyum, cilappatikārattiṟku aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiya uriyum tirāviṭa caṅkīta cāstirattiyum ataṉ ciṟappiyum stāpippa taṟkup paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kiyil pōtumāṉa ātāraṅkaḷāy akappaṭṭaṉa. caṅkīta cāstira nūlkaḷ anēkam aḻintu maṟintupōṉa kālattiltāṉ ivvurikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.
viṣayaṅkaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭuttiyirukkiṟa aḻakiyum taṉ apippirāyatti stāpippataṟku attiyanta poṟumiyuṭaṉ nūlāciriyar ceytirukkiṟa ārāycci yiyum kavaṉittāl, taṉ mītu tāṉē ēṟṟikkoṇṭa malipōṉṟa inta vēli yiṉ ciṟappu maṟṟavarkku naṉkuviḷaṅkum. taṟkālam atikamāy vaḻakkattilirāta pala nūlkaḷiliruntu nūlāciriyar eṭuttuccollum mēṟkōḷkaḷik kavaṉittāl, āciriyaruṭiya kalvittiṟamiyum avarkaḷōṭu vēli ceytavarkaḷiṉ cāmarttiyamum viḷaṅkum.
yāvarum aṟintukoḷḷakkūṭiya culapamāṉa tamiḻ naṭiyil innūl eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. cāmāṉiyamāṉa yāvarum nūlāciriyaruṭiya apippirāyatti eḷitil aṟintukoḷḷalām. coṟpa caṅkīta ñāṉam uṭiyavarkaḷuṅkūṭa in nūlip paṭippatiṉāl atika pirayōcaṉatti aṭivārkaḷ eṉpatil cantēkamilli."

tañcāvūr makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi rāvsākip mu. āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ ulērakōpakāramāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa karuṇāmirutacākaram eṉṉum arumiyāṉa pustakattiṟ ciṟcila pākaṅkaḷi uvappuṭaṉ paṭittup pārttēṉ.

valampaṭac cuḻalum varivaṭivu vāyntu taṉittiyaṅkum vaṉmi peṟṟu eṉṟum iḷami māṟātu viḷaṅki niṟkum nantam tamiḻ moḻi toṉṟu  toṭṭu iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttiṟa amippiṉik koṇṭatōr mutumoḻiyākum. uṟṟu nērakkiṉ avi oṉṟiṉeraṉṟu iṇintu muṟiyē uyarvuṟa viḷaṅkuvateṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. eṉṉi, icittamiḻukku iyaṟṟamiḻum nāṭakattamiḻukku ēṉiya iraṇṭu tamiḻum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavaṉṟō?
ippakutikaḷ iyaṟṟamiḻ, icittamiḻ, nāṭakattamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅku māṟṟāl tamiḻ moḻiyē mūṉṟu vakiyāka māṟupaṭṭateṉak karutuvār palar. avi tamiḻiyal, tamiḻici, tamiḻ nāṭakam eṉat tamiḻiyalum iciyum nāṭakamum ēṉiya moḻip pakutikaḷukku māṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkum tiṟattik kuṟittaṟkup pōnta toṭarkaḷēyāmeṉka. iṅṅaṉam vitantu kūṟutaṟkuṟṟa kāraṇam eṉṉiyōveṉiṉ, āriya iyalum āriya iciyum, āriya nāṭakamum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉkaṇ vantu kalanta kālattē tamiḻ makkaḷ avaṟṟip pōṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭa kālattē avaṟṟiṉ ivi vēṟā meṉpati uṇarttaṟ poruṭṭēyāmeṉka. āriyakkūttāṭiṉālum kāriyattil kaṇṇāyiru eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum itaṉi valiyuṟuttum. vaṭamoḻi eṉpataṟku māṟākat teṉmoḻi eṉpatum āriya maṟi eṉpataṟku māṟākat tamiḻ maṟi eṉpatum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉavaṉṟō?
iṉi nāḷaṭivil iyaṟṟamiḻ oṉṟum nilittu niṉṟu, ēṉiya iraṇṭum iṟantu paṭṭaṉa. avi iṟantu paṭṭamikkup palar pala kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar. kūrntu nērakkiṉ māntaratu vāḻkkikkum nilikkum nākarikat tiṟkum iyaṟṟamiḻ iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ici nāṭakaṅkaḷō veṉiṉ avaratu makiḻccikkum maṉērallācamāṉa kālappōkkiṟkum karuviyāṉavi. nāṭeṅkum ceḻittu nalam paraviya kālattiṉē pāṭṭuṅ kūttum eṅkeṅkum parantu viḷaṅkum. nantam nāṭṭil nāḷēṟa vanta vaṟumip piṇiyum araciyal māṟṟamumē avi iṟantu paṭṭamikkuk kāraṇaṅ kaḷākum. ituvēyumaṉṟi, tamiḻar āriya moḻiyi mēṟkoṇṭu avartam mataṅkaḷiyum, koḷkikaḷiyum kippaṟṟic ciṉṉāḷiṉ piṉṉar tāmum āriyarē eṉakkūṟap pukunta alvaḻic ceykiyāṉē āriyaratu ici nāṭakaṅkaḷip peritum pōṟṟit tamakkuṟṟa ici nāṭakaṅkaḷik ki naḻuvaviṭṭuk karutātiruntamiyē ēṟṟakāraṇameṉalām. kūtteṉṉum tamiḻ moḻiyi oḻittu nāṭakameṉṉum āriyamoḻi ittoṭarkaḷil iṭam peṟṟatum itaṟkuccāṉṟākum.
iṉip paṇṭit tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil kāṇum icikaḷum icik karuvikaḷum ittakiya veṉṟu pulappaṭuvatilli. antō, avaṟṟiṟkūṟum maraṅkaḷēṉum terikiṉṟaṉavā? malarkaḷēṉum terikiṉṟaṉavā? ētu milliyē. poruḷkaḷi uṇarttak karuvikaḷā niṟkum coṟkaḷi uṇarkiṉṟōmaṉṟi avi yuṇarttum poruḷkaḷi uṇarntō milliyē! ivviṉṉal tīra etteyvam etir niṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭumō! ikkālattē tamiḻ maṟikaḷil kāṇum paṇkaḷiṉ icittiṟamum paḷiṅkupōl terintapāṭilliyē!
uriyāciriyar kālattilēyē iṟantu paṭṭaṉa ici nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ eṉiṉ, innāḷil evaṟṟikkoṇṭu avaṟṟi ārāyvatu? āḻiyiluḷḷa ariyamaṇikaḷi mūḻkutaṟkāṉa karuvi oru ciṟitumiṉṟi uyirit turumpāka eṇṇi avvāḻiyiṉ kaṇ mūḻki ammaṇiyi eṭuttuk kaṭintu ulakiṟkaḷikkum uyarkuṇamum maṉavaṉmiyum vāynta māṉiṭaṉēpōl vaḷaṅkuṉṟāta vaḷḷiyōṉum centamiḻc celvaṉum aruṅkali vinōtaṉum uṇmitērum uttamaṉum aṟiñarip pōṟṟum āṇṭakiyum nalliyal vāynta naṇpaṉum ākiya oruvaṉ vēṇṭum. aṉṉavaṉē ittakiya ariya ceykiyi yamivuṟa āṟṟiṭavallāṉ. itaṟkut tañci rāvcākip āpirakām paṇṭitar eṉṉum aṇṇalik kūṟavē eṉ uḷḷam oruppaṭukiṉṟatu.
nantam paṇṭitar iyaṟṟiya karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṉum icittamiḻ nūlic ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil paṭittuppārttēṉ. paṇṭittamiḻ nūlkaḷil āṅkāṅkē arukikkiṭakkum viṣayaṅkaḷit tiraṭṭi avaṟṟiṟku ātaravāṉa amicaṅkaḷik kūṭṭi aiyamaṟa viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. tam matam niṟuvap pala aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiya aṭivukaḷiyum mēṟkōḷkaḷiyum piṟanāṭṭiṉar kūṟum amitikaḷiyum taṭiviṭikaḷāṟ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. icinūṟ payiṟci yuṭiyārē tuṇintu kūṟuvataṟkāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷippaṟṟi yāṉ ētuṅ kūṟutaṟkillēṉ. iciyippaṟṟiya viṣayaṅkaḷyāvum oruṅkē ōriṭattiṟkāṇumāṟu, kāṇpōr kaṇṇiyum maṉattiyum kavarum vaṇṇam aḻaku peṟa acciyaṟṟi ariyaviṣayaṅkaḷi iṉiya naṭiyil ulakukkaḷitta paṇṭitar paṇpu eṉṟum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatām. celvam peṟṟataṉāṟ ceyyattakka ceykiyum aṭiyat takka kīrttiyum iḵtēyaṉṟō?
nantam paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kīrtti muṉṉarē eṅkum paraviniṟpiṉum in nāḷil ivvici nūlāl varum iciyē iyinta ēṟṟamuṭiyatākum. ennāḷum niṉṟu nilavum innūliṉ māṭciyē pōl ivartam kīrttiyum niṉṟu nilavumaṉṟē.
tēvāram mutaliya tamiḻ maṟikaḷiṟ kāṇum paṇkaḷukku ottuvarum irākaṅkaḷi innūlil ōr aṭiviṟkāṭṭi itippaṟṟiya nūleraṉṟu virivil veḷivarum eṉṟu paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kāṭṭiya kuṟippikkaṇṭu perumakiḻvaṭikiṉṟēṉ. avvakiyil tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ maṉatti virivil makiḻvippārāka.
innūl peritum tamiḻiciyip pōṟṟa vantatākum. āyiṉum itaṟkuk karuṇāmirutacākaram eṉappeyar puṉintaṉar. itu tamatu ñāṉaciriyarām karuṇāṉantar karuṇiyittuṇiyākak kōṭaṟkuk kuṟippāṉ oṟṟumi nayampaṭavitta peyarātal viḷaṅkum. āyiṉum tāmiyaṟṟiya nūliṉ uḷḷuṟi teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅka icit tamiḻnūl eṉṉum karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉakkūṟiṉ cāla amivuṭittākum.
(oppam) kā. namaccivāyaṉ
The Opinion of Mr. J.S. Chandler
	Chirman, Tamil Lexicon Committee.
	ROYAPETTAH

I am very much interested in the work of Rao Sahib M. Abraham Pandither of Tanjore in developing the theory and practice of Karnatic Music.
A glance at the table of contents makes one wish to read it. It is well worthwhile for scholars like him to do much work, and in the clash of expert opinions a solid basis will be found for treating these subjects.
His clims for Tamil as a language are large, but they are worth careful study.

								(Sd.) J.S. CHANDLER.
"karnāṭaka caṅkīta cāstirattiyum ati upayōkikkum muṟi yiyum cīrtiruttuvatip paṟṟip pēcukiṟa Rao Sahib 
M.  āpirakāmpaṇṭitar avarkaḷuṭiya nūliṉ mēl eṉakku attiyanta pirīti uṇṭākiṟatu. poruḷaṭakkattip pārttālē ati vācikkavēṇṭu meṉṟa avāvuṇṭākiṟatu. avarkaḷippōlak kalviyil tērcci yuḷḷavarkaḷ anta vēliyic ceyvatil atika naṉmiyuṇṭu ippērp paṭṭa viṣayaṅkaḷippaṟṟip palarum pala apippirāyam collip pōr purintāl tāṉ naṉmi piṟakkum. tarkkikkappaṭum viṣayattiṟkum tiṇṇamāṉa ātāram ēṟpaṭum.
tamiḻp pāṣiyippaṟṟi ati cilākkiyamāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷic collukiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl avikaḷ kavaṉittu vācikkat takuntavikaḷ."
(oppam) J. S.  cāṉṭlar.
tamiḻ leksikaṉ kamiṭṭit talimip paṇṭitar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi mu. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷ apippirāyam.
tañci rāvsāhip āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ eḻutiya "karuṇāmirta cākaram" eṉṟa caṅkīta kōcattip pārviyiṭalāṉēṉ. itu nāṉku pākamākap pirikkappaṭṭu 1184 pakkaṅkoṇṭa oru peru nūlākavuḷḷatu. itara caṅkīta cāstirattiṉ āti carittiraṅkaḷum curutip pākupāṭukaḷippaṟṟiya vaṭanūṟ kuṟippukkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ paṇṭikkālattil vaḻaṅkiya icit tamiḻ viṣayaṅkaḷiṉ ārāyccikaḷum avvicittamiḻ muṟippaṭi amiyum curutikaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷum piṟavum innūluḷ virittuk kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷilum nikaṇṭukaḷilum icinūṟ pakuti kaḷākak kaṇṭa vaḻakkukaḷi yellām ciṟanta ārāycciyuṭaṉ paṇṭitaravarkaḷ viḷaṅkacceytiruppatu mikavum pukaḻattakkatām.
cilappatikāram araṅkēṟṟukāti mutaliyavaṟṟil iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum aṭiyārkku nallār arumpatavurikārarkaḷum eḻutiya icittamiḻ viṣayaṅkaḷellām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ eḻutiya innūlāl karatalā malakamāka viḷakkamuṟuvatik kāṇalām. vaṭanūlkaḷilum, paḻiya icittamiḻ nūlkaḷiluṅkaṇṭa curutikaḷiṉ pākupāṭukaḷip paṇṭitaravarkaḷ nuṭpamāka ārāycci ceytiruppatippaṟṟi, ici nūlaṟivupōtā empōliyar muṭivu colla iyalātāyiṉum, icittamiḻ viṣayamāṉa ariya periya nuṭpaṅkaḷit tam aṟivāṟṟalāl kaṇṭu paṇṭitaravarkaḷ ittakiya nūli mutaṉ mutalāka veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu tamiḻp pāṣikkuc ceyta oru pērupakāra meṉpatil taṭiyilli.
karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṉum peyarkkēṟpa muṟkālattum piṟkālattu muḷḷa caṅkīta viṣayaṅkaḷellām innūliṭip parantu kiṭattalāl, itu tamiḻmakkaṭku oru periya nitiyē eṉṉalām. ittakiya ariya peruṅ kāriyattic ceytu tamiḻ nāṭṭārkku upakaritta paṇṭitaravarkaḷ, nammavaratu pōṟṟaṟkum pukaḻccikkum urimi pūṇṭu, itupōlum peruṅ kāriyaṅkaḷic ceytu nīṭūḻi vāḻat tiruvaruḷic cintittu vantirukkiṉṟēṉ.
						(oppam) mu. irākaviyaṅkār.
u
tirucciṟṟampalam.
ceṉṉi, muttiyāluppēṭṭi hiskūl talimit tamiḻppulavar maṇimaṅkalam
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi tirunāvukkaracu mutaliyār avarkaḷ eḻutiya
ciṟappuppāyiram.
	ātiyiṟ ṟamiḻnūl akattiyark kuṇarttiya
	mātoru pākaṉi vaḻuttutum
	pōta meyññāṉa nalampeṟaṟ poruṭṭē
tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē moḻiyiyal varampu muṟṟum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya taṉic ciṟappu vāynta teṉpatu naṭunili tiṟampā ārāycciyāḷar yāvarāṉum oruṅku oppukkōṭaṟpālatu. itu mikappaḻiya moḻikaḷākiya ilattīṉ hīpru āriyam mutaliyavaṟṟeraṭu oppakkoḷḷum toṉmipeṟṟu, aviyāvum vaḻakkāṟu aṟṟeraḻiyavum iṉṟu vari iṟavāp perum pukaḻ koṇṭilaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉip piṟantakamākak koṇṭeḻunta kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, maliyāḷam mutaliyaṉavum oṉṟu pala vāy virintu māṟupaṭavum iḵtoṉṟē vēṟupāṭu oru ciṟitum iṉṟi niṟkum māṭcittāy cevviya viḻuppam vāyppap peṟṟeraḷirkiṉṟatu. itaṉkaṇ eḻutappaṭṭa ariya periya nūlkaḷāṉ it tīviya moḻiyip payiṉṟōr ciṟanta nākarikamum aṉṉērar vikiya tamiḻakam eṉappaṭum nāṭṭiṉ vaḷamum perumiyum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nāṟpāṉ oṉpatu naṟṟamiḻ nāṭukaḷ kaṭalkoḷḷap peṟṟaṉa eṉṉum uṇmi, pulavar perumāṉākiya āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum, piṟatēya carittirakkārarāṉum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉi aṟiyum ovvoruvar uḷḷamum ettuṇit tuṉpattuḷ aḻuntum eṉpatu aṟutiyiṭṭurikka iyalātu. mēṟṟiciyil nākarikam eṉpatē muḷikiḷampāta akkālattut tamiḻmakkaḷ ellā nākarikac ciṟappum, kalvip perukkum, palakalikaḷil nirampiya aṟivum, nuṇmiyum āḻamum uṭiyarāy miḷirntaṉar eṉpatu paṇṭit taṇṭamiḻ nūlkaḷākiya kuṟuntoki, naṟṟiṇi, akam, puṟam, tolkāppiyam mutaliyavaṟṟi oru muṟip potu nērakkiṟ kāṇpōrkkum iṉitu viḷaṅkāmaṟ pōkātu. ivaṟṟip pala āṅkilap pulavarkaḷum ārāyntu nūṉmukattāṉ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. pala tamiḻc cāṉṟōrum nūl vāyilākavum paṉuvalāṉum eṭutteḻuti niṟuviyuḷḷār. niṟka.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiṉi aṟitaṟku ataṉpāl iyaṟkiyiṉ amintuḷḷa akapporuḷ iyal oṉṟē cālum eṉka. aḵtu ettuṇi nuṇṇiya āḻnta karuttukkaḷi uṭkoṇṭu, makkaḷatu mutal nili toṭṭu vaḷarcci muṟi oḻuṅkukaḷit teḷittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. nālvaki nilaṉ vakuttatum avaṟṟukku uripporuḷ kiḷantu kūṟiyatum nuṉittārāyaṟpālaṉa. ikkaṇkoṇṭu pārkkum matuki illār tattamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟē kūṟi orutalippaṭātu iṭarppaṭuvar. meyppāṭṭuvamam, iṟiccip poruḷ mutaliyaṉa ettuṇic ciṟanta nākarikampaṟṟi nikaḻvaṉa eṉpatik karuttūṉṟi yoruṅki yuṇarka. kalivaḻakku, kāl vaḻakku, koṭi vaki, araciyal, maruttiyalpu, kōḷnili, kuṟinūl, uṟuppunūl mutaliya yāvum tamiḻar uṇarntaviyē eṉpataṟku uṟucāṉṟukaḷ pala nūṉmukattiṟ kiṭikkaṟpālaṉa.
muccaṅkam pāṇṭi maṉṉarāṉ niṟuvappaṭṭut tamiḻ ārāyappeṟṟatu. iyaṟṟamiḻukkuc caṅkam iruntatu pōṉṟē icikkūṟṟukkum caṅkam viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpataṟkut tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyāriṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa ēḻiciccūḻalpukkō eṉṉum toṭar moḻiyum iṉṉum cila kāṭṭukkaḷum ētukkaḷeṉa pulavar oruvar ārāyntu kūṟiṉār. avvāṟē iciccaṅkam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku eṭṭuṇiyum aiyamiṉṟu. kūttu nūlkaḷum pala nilaviṉa eṉpatu uriyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār eṭuttōtum nūṟkaḷāṉ aṟiyaṟpālaṉa.
iṉi, ivvāṟellām toṉmiyum mellēraciyum maṉa vamitikku icinta poruttamum parappum nuṇmiyum virikkattakka taṉmiyum piṟamoḻikkalappiṉṟit taṉittiyaṅkum pērāṟṟalum ciṟantu viḷaṅkun tamiḻ moḻiyiṉi oṉṟum vēṇṭātār cilar, uṇmi kāṇum viḻiviṉṟit tamiḻil yātum illi eṉṟu vāy kūcātu moḻivar. avarit teruṭṭutal mikavum aritu eṉka. tamiḻiṟkum vaṭamoḻikkum potuvāṉa eḻuttukkaḷ pala iruttaliṉ aviyāṉ ākkappaṭṭa moḻikaḷik kēṭṭa tuṇiyāṉē vaṭamoḻi eṉpar. ellāvaṟṟiyum āriyam eṉṟu ivar iṉpuṟuvatu eṟṟukku? ivar pōl ēṉiyarum niyāyamiṉṟik kūṟa muntuvarēl ivaratu koḷki talicāykku maṉṟō. ākavē, ovvoruvarum uṇmiyiṉip palatiṟattāṉum kūṟupaṭuttu vittu ārāyntu kūṟutaṟku muyaṟal vēṇṭum. oru cilar uṇmi kūṟuvār pōṉṟu avinayittut tamatu kōṭpāṭukaḷiyē cūḻcci valiyāl kāṭṭuvar. aḵtu aṟiñar kaṇkaḷukkup poḷḷeṉat tōṉṟum eṉpati avar uṇarvārāka.
allatūum ēṉi nāṭukaḷ pōlātu nan tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉkaṇ maṭṭum maṟṟeraru peruṅkuṟi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. aḵtu antaṇar tamiḻit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭum ataṉiṟ pulavarkaḷākap polintum atu koṇṭu piḻittum perumiyiṟ ciṟantum ammoḻikkaṭpaṟṟiṉṟi iruttalām. ikkuṟi nīṅkiṉ nalam pala palkum. tamiḻ muṟpōntu vaḷarcciyaṭiyum maṭṭil tamiḻnāṭu taḻiyavē taḻiyātu eṉpatu mukkālum uṇmi.
iṉi, piṟkālattuc camaya nūlkaḷ veḷippaṭṭu paṟṟumikkut tamiḻc cuviyiṉip perumpāṉmiyum kuṟikkat talippaṭṭaṉaveṉpatu iḻukkātu. pallāyiram centamiḻc coṟkaḷ viṭupaṭṭaṉa. vaṭacoṟkaḷ kalantaṉa. tamiḻc col oruvaṉ karutiyataṉi iṭattukku iciya uṇarttuvatāyiruppavum ataṉi viṭuttu vaṭacollip peyturippōṉ tamiḻkkup peruntīṅku iḻittavaṉāvāṉ eṉpatit tamiḻar ovvoruvarum uḷḷattamippārāka.
tamiḻ nūlkaḷ pāṭṭuttuṟiyilum, uri vakiyilum, ārāyccit tiṟattiṉum palap palavākap palkutal vēṇṭum. paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cevviyāka acciṭappaṭṭu ellērarukkum kiṭikkattakka vakikaḷil vikkappaṭavēṇṭum. tamiḻar ittakiyamuyaṟcikaḷ ceytu tam arumaruntaṉṉa moḻiyiṉi vaḷarttu ulakiṉpuṟakkaṇṭu, tāmum iṉpuṟuvārāka.
ikkālattut tōṉṟiyuḷḷa cila kiḷarccikaḷālum karuttu nuṭpaṅkaḷālum tamiḻ muṉpōl piṟimatipōl vaḷarntu ciṟakkum kālam vantu viṭṭatu eṉṟu niṉittuk kaḷikūrukiṉṟōm. tamiḻar pala paḻiya nūlkaḷic ceppañceytu acciṭukiṉṟaṉar. palar pāṭṭiyalpi viḷakki niṟkiṉṟaṉar. palar pala vaki ārāycciyiṟ pukkaṉar. kālañceṉṟa pulavar perumāṉ kārttikēya mutaliyār avarkaḷ pōṉṟa cāṉṟōrkaḷāl moḻi nūl pōṉṟa taṉiyārāycci nūlkaḷ eḻuvaṉavāyiṉa.
ittakiya kālattup patamamiyak kāli eḻūuṅ katiravaṉ eṉṉat tōṉṟiyatu nam naṇpar tañci rāv cākip āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ karuṇāmiruta cākaram eṉṉum viḻumiya nūl. iḵtu ovvoru tamiḻar uḷḷattiyum uyirppikkum amiḻtat tuḷikaḷ pala aṭaṅkap peṟṟuḷḷa pariya nūl eṉpati oru muṟi nērakkuvērarum teḷḷat teḷiya uṇarvar. nam paṇṭitar avarkaḷ pala āṇṭukaḷākap peritum muyaṉṟu uḻittataṉ payaṉāka iccīriya nūl veḷivantulava nērntatu. celvamuṭiyāruḷ perumpāṉmiyōr ikkālattuk kalvi eṉṉum iṉpak kaṭaluḷ tiḷittalip peruṅkuṟiyāka niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ eṉṟāl kippar; pulavareṉṟāl eḷḷuvar; aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi ivarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kaṇ uṇmippaṟṟuvittuḻittup pulavar pala tīṭṭiyuḷḷa ceḻuntamiḻ nūlkaḷit tuṟipōkak kaṟṟu nuṇukkamāka ārāyntu cemporuḷi uḷaṅkoḷa amittut tamatu nūlil yāvarum pulaṅkoḷa viḷaṅka virittuḷḷār. tamiḻiṉ toṉmi kuṟittu eḻutiya āṅkila nūlāciriyaṉmār palar poṟitta oṇporuḷi āṅkāṅku eṭuttukkāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭip paṟṟiya pala poruḷkaḷ ataṉkaṇ potin tuḷḷaṉa. kaṭalpōl parantu oḷirum itaṟkuk karuṇāmirta cākaram, eṉak kuṟiyiṭṭami cālapporuntum eṉka. iyaṟṟamiḻc caṅka varalāṟṟit tiṟampaṭa muṟṟum eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār. tamiḻi iḻitturippār kūṟṟi niyāya vāyilākak kaṇṭittu aṟivu  koḷuttum nam paṇṭitar avarkaḷ niyāya vaṉmi peritum pārāṭṭaṟpālatu. aṅṅaṉam pōlik koḷkikaḷ pala parikarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṉi, icittamiḻārāycci kuṉṟiliṭṭa maṇiviḷakkeṉa eññāṉṟum oḷiyārntu miḷirum polivupeṟṟut tikaḻum itaṉic cevikkamuteṉa evarum pōṟṟal vēṇṭik karuṇāmirta cākaram, eṉat talikkuṟi puṉintaṉarēra eṉṟum eṇṇi makiḻkūrntu uṭal pūrikkiṉṟōm. ivvārāycciyil vaṭanūliṉkaṇ ici nūliṟkūṟum irupattiraṇṭu curuti, nuṭpak kaṇakkiṟku ottuvārāmiyit tērntu teḷintu ataṉik kaṇṭittut tamiḻiṉkaṇ cilappatikārattu uri mukattukkāṭṭiyuḷḷa cila kuṟippukkaḷāṉ tamiḻc curuti muṟi irupattu nāṉkātalē ellā nuṭpa tiṭpaṅkaṭkum icintatu eṉṉum uṇmiyiṉi yāppuṟuttu niṟuviyuḷḷār. ici nuṇukkam, icimarapu, paripāṭal mutaliya toṉṉūl tērcciyum maṟṟiya nūlkaḷiṉ kūṟṟukkaḷiyum puṭipaṭavittuṇarntu pala kūriya poruḷkaḷi valiyuṟuttik kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. iḵtu āṟṟavum matittaṉ māliyatu. kālak kaṇakku vakiyiṉiyum kōḷkaḷiṉ nili iyakkaṅkaḷiyum jīvacuraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟa veraṭukkaṅkaḷiyum naṉkuṇarntu putitu putitākap pala curuti pētaṅkaḷit tāmē kaṇṭuṇarum tiṇmiyum amiyap peṟṟuḷḷār. itaṉi nūlaḷavil kaṇṭu makiḻtal pōtāteṉavuṉṉit tam arumic celvikaḷukku ivaṟṟiṉ oṭpa nuṭpaṅkaḷip payiṟṟuvittu avarkaḷāl kēṭṭu makiḻntu anupava vāyilākavum viḷakkivarukiṉṟaṉar. ammuṟi innūlil cevvaṉam eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṉ poruṭṭut tañci caṅkīta mahājaṉa caṅkam oṉṟu niṟuvip pala caṅkīta vittuvāṉkaḷi varuvittuc ciṟappuṟa naṭattikkāṭṭit tamiḻiciyiṉ oppaṟṟa uyarvi yāvarum uṇaravikkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉi viyantu pala caṅkīta vitvaṉmaṇikaḷ pārāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. atuvēyumaṉṟi, makkaḷ uṭaṟkūṟu pañcākkarattiṉkaṇ aṭaṅku muṟi innūliṉkaṇ viḷaṅkat tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻ icic ciṟappu ippōtu iṉitu oḷirvatāyiṟṟu. ittuṇi arumip pāṭṭi nantan tamiḻmoḻi amiyappeṟṟatu nammavar aruntavamaṉṟō? avaṟṟi uṇarātiruttal perum piḻiyākum eṉpatu mikiyākātu.
tamiḻip pāṇṭiyarēpōl ivarkaḷum iṉṉum paṉṉūlit tāmāka eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭum, pala nūlkaḷip piracurittum, palavaṟṟiṟku uri kaṇṭum, palavaṟṟiṟkum paṉuval avvattuṟivallērarāl eḻutuvittum vaḷarttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu viḻikiṉṟōm.
iṉi, innūl iṉimiyāṉa naṭipeṟṟut taṭṭaṟa āṟṟeraḻukkāka naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. nalla kākitattil muttuppōṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷāl acciṭappeṟṟuk karuttukkaḻaku taruvatēpōl kaṇṇukkum aḻakiyūṭṭik kavarkiṉṟatu. tamiḻar ovvoruvar pālum iḵtu iṉṟiyamiyātu iruttaṟkuriyatākum. pala ariya poruḷkaḷi ōriṭattu oruṅkē kāṭṭum ciṟappuḷḷatu. tamiḻiṉkaṇ iḵtōr cintā maṇi eṉṟu kūṟutal uyarvu naviṟciyākamāṭṭātu.
iḵtu eññāṉṟum niṉṟu nilavavēṇṭum eṉṟum itaṉip paṭikkum naṇparkaḷ, tamiḻp perumi uṇarntu kalviyiṟ ciṟantu aṟivup peruñcelvam tēkkappeṟṟu iṉpuṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟum ataṉāṉ ittamiḻnāṭu ōṅkippolital vēṇṭumeṉṟum ellām valla muḻumutaṟ ceḻumporuḷi maṉamoḻimeykaḷāṉ cintittu vantittu vāḻttukiṉṟōm.
nērici āciriyappā
