eṭṭiyāpuram camastāṉam teluṅku vitvāṉ
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ti.cu. murukēca piḷḷi avarkaḷ apippirāyam.
tañcimānakar makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi rāv cākēp ā. āpirakām paṇṭitaravarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa karuṇāmiruta cākaram eṉṟa ici nūlik kūṭiyavari yāṉ pārttatil annūl icipayil māṇavar yāvarkkum iṉṟiyamiyāp payaṉaḷikkum peṟṟiyuṭittu. ataṉuṭ poruḷkaḷerav veraṉṟum paṉṉūliyumeraruṅkēyāyntu avaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷit tiraṭṭik kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.
tamiḻmoḻi maṟupulac coṟkaḷiṉ cārpiṉṟit taṉiyēyiyaṅku māṇpuṭit taṉit toṉ moḻiyeṉṟum, ammoḻikkōraṇiyeṉa veraḷirum cilappatikārattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa icittamiḻiṟ cārnta āyappāli mutaliya paṉṉiru pālikaḷāṟ kiṭitta pallāyiram paṇkaḷuḷ vaṭṭappāliyiṉaṭiyāyp piṟanta paṇkaḷ iraṇṭu curuti kuṟittu irupāṉiraṇṭu curutikaḷōṭu pāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, uṇmiyil oru stāyikku irupattunāṉku curutikaḷē uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
taṟkāla vaḻakkilicikkappaṭum narampuk karuvikaḷuḷ yāḻiṟkiṭakkum paṉṉiru vīṭu (curaṅ) kaṭku vaṭamoḻi ici nūlāciriyar pallērarum tatta nūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷa irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷ appaṉṉiru vīṭṭiṉum ottiyaṅkum aḷavikkāṭṭāmiyiṉ, kaṇakkiṟkovvāvaḻi vaḻakkiṟ kevvāṟerakku meṉṟa aiyappāṭeraṉṟu eṉ maṉatuṭṭēraṉṟik kiṭantatu. 22 curutikaḷip pākupaṭuttalilaḷavu ovvāviṭiṉum 12 vīṭukaḷiṉerali yottiyalkiṉṟatu.
sa, ri, ka, ma, pa, ta, ni eṉṟa ēḻu svaraṅkaṭku muṟiyē curutikaḷ 4, 3, 2, 4, 4, 3, 2 eṉṟa vaḻi, yāḻiṉiṟuviya vīṭukaḷil ri eṉṟa mutal vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 3, cutta kāntāramum pañca curuti riṣapa mumeralikkāniṉṟa iraṇṭāvatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 2, sātāraṇa kāntāramum ṣaṭcuruti riṣapamumeralikkāniṉṟa 3-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 1; antara kāntāra veraliyik kiḷappum 4-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 2; cutta mattiyama veraliyi yeḻuppum 5-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti1 piratimatyamaveraliyip piṟappikkum 6-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 3; pañcamam olikkāniṉṟa 7-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 1 eṉa vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ākavē pūrvāṅkattiṉ 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 vīṭukaṭku muṟiyē curutikaḷ 3, 2, 1, 2, 1, 3, 1 eṉak kiṭittatu. āyuṅkāl, otta aḷavippeṟṟa vīṭukaṭku ēṟṟat tāḻvāṉa curutikaḷevvāṟu poruntumeṉṟa kaṭāviṟku emmuṟiyāṉēra muṉṉērar vakutta muṟikku nām evvāṟṟāṉeraḻuṅku paṭuttakkūṭum eṉattamiḻ ici nūlik kāṇṭalum peṟāta vaṭanūṟ payiṟciyār palarum viṭiyaḷikkā niṉṟaṉar. mutal vīṭuniṟka, 2-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curutikaḷ 2 eṉṟum mūṉṟāvatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 1 eṉṟum kūṟiṉ, tāḻnta veraliyiyuṭiya 2-vatu vīṭṭiṟkuc curuti 2 āyirukka ativiṭa valittolikkum 3-vatu vīṭṭiṟku amitta curuti 1 eṉpatu evvakittāṉēra?  niṟka, yāḻil mēlē pōkap pōka oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu yarnta veralikku aḷaviṭam muṟiyē caṟṟuk kuṟintēyiruttalāl 2-vatu vīṭṭiviṭa 3-vatu vīṭu kuṟivāṉa curutiyeṉa ovvāviṭi pukalvārāyiṉ 3-vatu vīṭṭiviṭa uyareraliyuṭiya 4-vatu vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭum avvāṟiṉṟi ēṟṟamāṉa 2 curutikaḷamitaleṅṅaṉēra? ivvāṟē kuṟintuṅ kūṭiyum pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷa curutikaḷiṉ muṟi muraṇuṭiyatu. ivvitam aḷavu muraṇpaṭṭa pākupāṭṭiyuṭiya vīṭukaḷ otta aḷaviṉiyuṭiyaṉa pōliyoli yolikkāniṉṟaṉaveṉpatu mikkaviyappē! itaṉāl, vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaṇ kūṟiya curutip pākupāṭu neṟitavaṟiyateṉak kōṭalē cālpuṭitteṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
nam paṇṭitaravarkaḷ mikavārāynteṭuttīnta putiporuḷ pōṉṟa tamiḻp paṇ muṟiyāṉa 24 curutikaḷē, mēṟkūṟiya paṉṉiru vīṭukaṭkum vīṭeraṉṟukku iraṇṭiraṇṭu curutikaḷāyp pakutta uṇmi neṟiyē ematiyatti yoruṅkēyakaṟṟiyatu. otta aḷavuṭiyaṉavāyp pakukkap paṭṭa vīṭu (curaṅ)kaḷē iyaleraliyi yeḻuppavallaṉaveṉpatu yāvarkkum oppamuṭinta uṇmiyē.
virivañci, pūrvāṅkattippōl uttarāṅkattiyum viḷakkātu viṭuttu, ulakukkōrariya periya naṉmi payakkattakka ivvicinūlippaṟṟi ulakil nilaviya curuti campantamāṉa aiyappāṭṭi nīkki viḷakkiyamip palarum koṇṭāṭattakkateṉṟē collat tuṇintēṉ.
							(oppam) murukēcaṉ, eṭṭayāpuram.
tirunelvēli tirikūṭarācappakkavirāyar parampariyic cārntavarum muṉ
iñciṉīrumākiya kāci vāci srī murukānantam cuvāmikaḷ iyaṟṟiya veṇpā.

	kītattā lummutti kiṭṭumeṉu muṇmiyelā
	nītiyā yīti ṉikaḻttiṉār - ātaravā
	yaṇṭiṉarik kākkavala āpirakām paṇṭitarik
	kaṇṭārkku miṉpamuṟuṅ kāṇ

tirunelvēli nelliyappar kōyil tēvārappaṇiviṭi
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cālivāṭīcura ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ parampari
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi caṇmuka cuntara ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
tañcāvūr rāvcāypu āpirakāmpaṇṭitaravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya "karuṇāmirta cākaram" eṉṉum nūliṉ mutaṟpākattip pārviyiṭṭēṉ,
atil iṉiya tamiḻiṉ paḻamiyāṉa mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷum virivāy viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
iyal, ici, nāṭakameṉṉu muttamiḻil icittamiḻiṉ ēḻici kaḷiyum avaṟṟiṟkātāramāy viḷaṅkum irupattu nāṉku curutikaḷiyum, "paripāṭalkaḷ" "cilappatikāram" mutaliya caṅka nūlkaḷiṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷik koṇṭu viḷakki maṟṟapāṭiyiṟ kūṟum irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷ niyāyattiṟkum, aḷavuppiramāṇaṅkaḷukkum aṉupavattiṟkum ovvāmiyi naṉkeṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
aṉṟiyum tamiḻvētameṉṉun tēvārattirumuṟiyiṉ paṇkaḷiṉ muṟikaḷiyum avikaḷi yōtavēṇṭiya pārampariyak kiramattiyum paṟṟi viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
innūl ici nūṟpayiṟci ceyya virumpuvērar aṉivarukkum mikka payaṉ paṭuvatuṭaṉ intiyā tēcattil 12000 varuṭaṅkaḷukku muṉ caṅkīta mirunta vuyarnilimiyiyun teḷivuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
ātalā linnūlaṟiñarkaḷaṉivarālum pārāṭṭattakkatē.
Remarks of Mr. A.G. PICHAIMUTTHU, B.A., L.T., Headmaster,
S. Peter’s High School. Organist, S. Peter’s, and
Honorary Secretary to the Sangeetha Vidya Mahajana Sangam, Tanjore.
It has been sid aginst Indians that they generally lack originality and application. But this statement has been given the lie by the voluminous treatise on Indian Music, ‘Karunamirthasagaram’ written by Rao Sahib M.A. Pandither of Tanjore, S. India. Music is a subject fought shy of by many able scholars, who are experts in other departments, as it is highly technical in character. Those who are credited with a practical knowledge of it at the present day are those musicians who make it their means of livelihood, and most of these are utter strangers to the science of it. It is, therefore, a matter for extreme gratification that the author has combined in himself a complete knowledge of the Science and Art of Music, and has published this great work as the result of his disinterested labours.
This exhustive work which is the result of nearly 15 years of the author’s life, while dealing directly with the musical system of South India, throws a flood of light also on the literature and history of South India in general and that of the Tamil country in particular, as its literature and Music are inseparable. The indisputable originality displayed throughout the Book by the author, the innumerable authorities quoted in support of statements, the wealth of illustration he has brought to bear on various subjects, the fund of knowledge he possesses in many departments of life, the sparkling humour that now and then relieves the seriousness of such a technical work and the fearlessness and thoroughness with which the author establishes his theory on Srutis - all these clearly show his remarkable genius. The reader may find repetitions in many places, but the author has been quite conscious of them, and they have been made with the definite purpose of striking the ideas home. The author, no doubt, richly deserves a very high place among the literary writers of the day.
The chief aim of the author is to show what the music of the ancient Tamil country was, and how modern Karnatic music is its counterpart. In this he has admirably succeeded. He proves by apt quotations from Tamil works, after careful study, how in the ancient Tamil music of S. India, the Octave was divisible into a number of equal tones. This is his fundamental position. This fact is supported by no less an authority than the great Sanskrit writer on Indian Music - Sarnga Dev of Kashmere - who lived about the 13th Century A.D. According to Sarnga Dev the Swarams of an Octave should be a gradually ascending series arranged like the equal rungs of a ladder without the possibility of admitting any other sound between.  According to the author, the Octave is so divisible into 12, 24, 48 and 96 equal intervals, the series in all cases rising gradually by G.P. The author not only supports this theory by minute mathematical calculations, logarithams, decimals down to eight places as regards length of string, number of vibrations, cents etc., but it has also stood the rigid test of practice and demonstration vocally as well as on instruments. The practical side of it will be clear even to a close reader of the book, but to those who have actually witnessed the demonstration thereon the truth, of this will be quite evident and they will certinly testify that there could be no other system of music possible. Any ordinary Indian musician will accept that the Octave is divisible into 12 equal intervals, that a number of Indian Ragas are sung in these 12 Swarams only and that when change of Graham is made many different Ragas result from the process. This one fact alone strikes at the root of any theory which advocates unequal and irregular intervals for Indian music.
In the course of the treatise the author, condemns the theory of 22 Srutis for the Octave and says that the advocates of it altogether misinterpret their leader Sarnga Dev and that their confusion is worse confounded by the fact that ganam in ancient India was made in the 22 Srutis of the series 24.
I should personally like to see an array of these advocates with their Srutis and instruments in front of them. No two of them will agree as to the location of the different Swarasthanams. If they begin to argue they will be cutting each other’s throats in two minutes! A look into page 416 of the book will convince one of the truth of my statement. Except for the Swaram SA and its Octave there will be no unanimity!
Many readers may think there is not much difference between the 22 and the 24 Srutis. It is not so. The two series are entirely different. All ancient and modern Karnatic Ragas are sung in the 24 Srutis only. The series 22 is only in the imagination of the singer! The professionals sing in the 24 Srutis only, but they are unconscious of the fact. If the advocates of the Dwavimsati Srutis would examine the Srutis they are using in singing, they will find that their theory is one thing and their practice another! If they accept the Saptaswarams, the 12 Swarams of the octave, the mother ragas derived from them and the principle of change of graham - which most of them do accept - then the 22 Srutis exist nowhere.
The members of this school along with Mr. Clements, their captin, base their theory on the system of obtining their so called harmonic or natural series by proceeding by perfect fifths or taking 2/3 the length of the string for each step or by the SA PA principle. But, to speak the truth, a few steps of this progression will land them in difficulties and they will tumble into the ditch! and never live to see the end of the Octave! How could the product of a recurring decimal like 2/3 ever come to an end? English musicians know that progression by fifths can never completely end the octave unless some manipulation is made. Piano tuners always tune the pianos by slightly flattening the fifths or else the perfect fifths in the course of progress will gather momentum and the result will be like the howling of the wolf! ‘Beware of the wolf” is the cution given to Piano tuners! The trouble will commence even at the third step when you come to decide the interval from D to A and from A to E. From this step forwards a number of artificial aids will be necessary such as cutting down a few vibrations here and there to keep up the farce of the progression by 2/3!! This is why they cry down artificial intervals and say that “Artificial music is an abomination”. I quite endorse this opinion and say, “yes, it is execrably so”. Only he advocates of the 22 srutis have recourse to this abomination. But the author does not base his system on this “equalised abomination” but establishes the existence of a gradually ascending equal series in nature which was in vogue among the Tamilians for centuries. The demonstration of the practical side of this system has been very ably done on several occasions by the author’s two dughters Srimati Maragathavalli Ammal and Kanagavalli Ammal, about whose admirable efficiency in Indian and English music I can personally testify.
The author has given a number of tables and statements which give the calculations & c., of the various systems, reducing them all to one common standard (32 inches wire) for purpose of easy comparison, and also Rasi chakrams where his series are arranged in the four Palis of the ancient Tamils. This part of his work reflects, in a remarkable degree, the intelligence and patience of the author. A close study of them will be well worth the trouble.
The Indian is a highly conservative being, and any theory, whether right or wrongs, which has been honoured by time will never be easily given up by him. Yet in the midst of this conservative nation we find many rational beings who are not afrid to proclim the truth from the housetops when they know it to be true. It is a matter for pride that the truth of this theory has been accepted by such distinguished musical experts like Seshanna and H.P. Krishna Row of Mysore, Veeni Venkataramanadoos of Vijianagaram, Mutthaya Bhagavater of Harikasavanallur and others. Truth will triumph.
I have not the slightest doubt that the publication of this book, thought it may cuse a fllutter in the dovecotes of the Dwavimsatiites, will be an epoch making event in the history of the Music of South India. That it will create a wide interest in musical circles is certin. But, I hope, it will popularise the ancient Yal or the Veena used so much by the ancient. Tamils on account of its capability to reproduce faithfully the human voice and the minutes Srutis. It is sure to rouse the Karnatic Music from its present lethargy; it is sure to rescue it from the hands of the few professionals who blindly follow it and keep it as their heritage and throw its portals open to all without distinction of caste or creed so that it may become a part of the dily life of every Indian.
Ist May, 1917.							(Sd.) A.G.. PICHAIMUTTHU.
tañci, ceṉṟ pīṟṟars hiskūl heṭmāsṭarum ceṉṟ pīṟṟars carc ārkaṉisṟumāṉa Mr. A.G. piccimuttu B.A., L.T., avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam.
intiyarkaḷ putitāṉa oru kāriyattic ceyvatilāvatu ataṟkeṉṟu pirayācappaṭuvatilāvatu ēlātavarkaḷ eṉṟu avarkaḷmēl cātāraṇamāyk kuṟi kūṟuvatuṇ L.āṉāl tañci, Rao Sahib M.A, paṇṭitaravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya karuṇāmirtacākaram eṉṟa intapperiya icittamiḻ nūlippārppavarkku appaṭic colluvatāṉatu poyyeṉṟu viḷaṅkum. caṅkītam arumiyāṉa cāstiramāṉatāl maṟṟa cāstiraṅkaḷil tērnta nipuṇaruṅkūṭa caṅkīta meṉṟāl vilakiviṭuvārkaḷ. taṟkālam anta vittiyil tērntavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum vitvāṉkaḷuṅkūṭa ati vayiṟṟuppiḻippiṟkāka upayōkippatāl avarkaḷil perumpāṉmiyōrukku cāstira viṣayam kiñcittēṉum teriyātu. kāriyaṅkaḷ taṟkālam innilimi yilirukkiyil, nammuṭiya nūlāciriyar caṅkīta cāstirattilum ati aṉupōkamāy upayōkikkum muṟiyilum nipuṇarāyirukkiṟār eṉṟu inta nūliṉāl veḷiyāvatu mallāmal, yātoru pirati upakāratti eṇṇi itic ceyyavilli yeṉṟum terikiṟapōtu namakku mikunta makiḻcciyuṇṭākiṟatu.
intap perunūli eḻutuvatil innūlāciriyar 15 varuṣam iṭi viṭāmal  uḻittiruk kiṟārkaḷ. innūlāṉatu teṉṉintiya caṅkītattip paṟṟi nērāyccolluvatōṭukūṭa teṉṉintiya carittirattiyum ataṉ pūrva nūlkaḷiyum paṟṟip perukkamāyp pēcuvatumallāmal vicēṣamāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ carittirattiyum yāvarukkum naṉku viḷaṅkumpaṭi eṭuttuccollukiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ nūlkaḷiyum caṅkītattiyum pirittuc collutal kūṭāta kāriyamaṉṟō? mutal toṭaṅki puttakattiṉ kaṭicivari collappaṭṭirukkum putitu putitāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷiyum, viṣayaṅkaḷi stāpippatil nūlāciriyar upayōkittirukkum kaṇakkillāta mēṟkōḷkaḷiyum, apippirāyaṅkaḷi maṉatil paṭumpaṭi collukiṟa viṣayattil upayōkittirukkum ērāḷamāṉa upamāṉaṅkaḷiyum, anēka cāstiraṅkaḷil nūlāciriyar aṭintirukkum pāṇṭittiyattiyum, iṭikkiṭiyē maṉakkiḷarcciyi yuṇṭupaṇṇum paṭikkāka avarkaḷ upayōkittirukkum cilēṭippirayōkaṅkaḷiyum (aviyillāviṭṭāl pustakam paṭikkak kaṣṭamāy irukkumē) caṟṟum payamiṉṟi taṉṉuṭiya apippirāyatti muṟṟilum cariyāka stāpikkum mēriyiyum nām nērakkumpōtu, nūlāciriyaruṭiya āccariyappaṭattakka pāṇṭittiyam naṉku viḷaṅkum. innūli vācippavarkaḷ aṅkaṅkē cila kāriyaṅkaḷit tirumpattirumpac colliyiruppatik kāṇalām. āṉāl nūlāciriyar vēṇṭumeṉṟē appaṭic colliyirukkiṟārkaḷ. appaṭic coṉṉāltāṉ kāriyaṅkaḷ naṉṟāy maṉatiṟkuppulappaṭum eṉpataṟkākavē appaṭic collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivikaḷi yellām nām kavaṉikkumpōtu taṟkāla nūlāciriyaruḷ ivarkaḷukkum oru perumiyāṉa iṭam kiṭikkavēṇṭiyatu eṉṟu cantēkamaṟaccollalām.
nūlāciriyariṉ mukkiya nērakkam pūrvatamiḻariṉ kāṉam iṉṉatu eṉṟu eṭuttuc colvatum, taṟkāla karnāṭaka cṅkītattiṟkum ataṟkumuḷḷa oṟṟumiyi stāpippatum tāṉ. ituviṣayatti āccariyappaṭattakka vitamāyc carivara ceytu muṭittirukkiṟārkaḷ. ātitteṉṉintiya caṅkītattil oru stāyi eppaṭi camamāṉa iṭiveḷikaḷuḷḷa curutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntateṉṟu pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ takutiyāṉa mēṟ kōḷkaḷik koṇṭu veku jākkiratiyuṭaṉ stāpikkiṟārkaḷ. itutāṉ avarkaḷuṭiya muṟikku ātāra apippirāyam. iti stāpikkum viṣayattil avarkaḷukku akappaṭṭa periya ātāram ēteṉṟāl caṅkīta cāstirattic camaskiruta pāṣiyil eḻutiṉavarum, yāvarālum periya caṅkīta nūlāciriyareṉṟu oppukkoḷḷappaṭupavarumāṉa kāṣmīr tēcattic cērnta cāraṅkatēvartāṉ. ivar 13-m nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntavar. curuti viṣayamāy ivar colluvateṉṉaveṉṟāl, oru stāyiyiluḷḷa curaṅkaḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu tīviramāy paṭippaṭiyāy oru ēṇiyiṉ paḻukkaḷippōla cama aḷavuḷḷavikaḷāy naṭuvil vēṟu curaṅkaḷ vara iṭamillāmal oḻuṅkāṉaviyāyirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatē. itaṟkicintē nammuṭiya nūlāciriyarum oru stāyiyi 12, 24, 48, 96 camapākaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟārkaḷ. curaṅkaḷ ellām oḻuṅkāy oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu tīviramāy Geometrical Progression il amikiṉṟaṉa. inta apippirāyatti stāpippatil nuṭpamāṉa kaṇakkukaḷum, lākiritamum, eṭṭustāṉam vari tacāmsa piṉṉaṅkaḷum, tantiyiṉ nīḷamum, ōci alikaḷ ceṉṭskaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭiruppatu māttiramalla, avikaḷ muṟṟilum cariyeṉṟu pāṭiyum vīṇi mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷil vācittum rucuppaṭuttiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. pustakattik kavaṉamāy vācikkum evarum avarkaḷ apippirāyam aṉupōkattiṟku ottirukkiṟatu eṉpatip pustakattiliruntē eḷitil kaṇṭukoḷvārkaḷ. āṉāl avarkaḷ ceytu kāṭṭiya rucuvi nēril pārttavarkaḷukku anta muṟiyiṉ uṇmiyum ativiṭa vēṟuvitamāṉa muṟiyeṅkum akappaṭātu eṉṟa cattiyamum uṭaṉē maṉatil utikkum. enta intiya cātāraṇa caṅkīta vitvāṉum, oru stāyi 12 cama pākaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭukiṟateṉṟum, kaṇakkillāta intiya irākaṅkaḷ inta 12 curaṅkaḷilēyē pāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, kirakamāṟṟum poḻutu inta 12 curaṅkaḷiliruntu vevvēṟu irākaṅkaḷ jaṉikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum taṭiyillāmal oppukkoḷvāṉ. intiya caṅkītam oḻuṅkaṟṟa iṭiveḷikaḷuḷḷa curaṅkaḷālāṉatu eṉṟu collum enta muṟiyiyum aṭiyōṭē veṭṭic cāyppataṟku inta oru viṣayamē pōtum.
oru stāyiyil 22 curutikaḷuṇṭu eṉṟu cātikkum koḷkik kārari maṟukkiṟār nūlāciriyar. cāraṅkatēvar colluvatiṉ karutti maṟṟavar tappāy arttam paṇṇiṉārkaḷeṉṟu pōtumāṉa niyāyaṅkāṭṭic collukiṟār. pūrva intiyāvil 24 curutikaḷil 22 curutiyil tamiḻar kāṉam paṇṇiṉārkaḷ eṉṟa apippirāyamum itōṭē cērntavuṭaṉ periya kuḻappamāy muṭintatu.
tuvāvimcati curutikkāraril cila vitvāṉkaḷiyum avarkaḷuṭiya curutikaḷiyum vāttiyaṅkaḷiyum eṉ muṉ nēril pārkka virumpukiṟēṉ. uṭaṉē avarkaḷuṭiya veṭṭaveḷiccamellām terintuviṭum, avarkaḷuṭiya curastāṉaṅkaḷippaṟṟi iraṇṭu pērkūṭa oṟṟumiyāṉa apippirāyam collamāṭṭārkaḷ. tarkkampaṇṇa ārampittuviṭṭālēra iraṇṭu nimiṣattiṟkuḷ oruvar kaḻutti yoruvar aṟuttuk koḷvārkaḷ. nāṉ colluvatu nijamō tappō eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭu meṉṟu virumpukiṟavarkaḷ innūl karuṇāmirta cākarattil pārkkalām. caṭjamattiyum mēl caṭjamattiyum tavira maṟṟa enta curaṅkaḷilāvatu oruvarukkoruvar oṟṟumiyilliyeṉṟu aṅkē teḷivākak kāṇpōm.
caṅkītan teriyātavarkaḷ 22 curutikkum 24 curutikkum atika vittiyāca milliyeṉṟu niṉippārkaḷ. atu tappu. iraṇṭu curavaricikaḷukkum koñcamāvatu campantamilli. pūrva karnāṭaka irākaṅkaḷum taṟkālam upayōkattilirukkum karnāṭaka irākaṅkaḷum pāṭappaṭuvatu 24 curutikaḷil tāṉ. 22 curutikaḷ antak koḷkikkārariṉ maṉatil māttiramtāṉ irukkiṟatu. uṇmiyākavē avikaḷ vēṟeṅkum illi. caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ pāṭuvatellām 24 curutikaḷiltāṉ. āṉāl avikaḷil pāṭukiṟōmeṉṟu avarkaḷukkut teriyātu. avarkaḷ pāṭum curutikaḷi ārāyntu pārppārkaḷāṉāl tāṅkaḷ cātippatu oṉṟu pāṭuvatu vēṟoṉṟu eṉṟu aṟivārkaḷ. captacuraṅkaḷ, stāyikku 12 curaṅkaḷ, avikaḷil uṇṭākum tāy irākaṅkaḷ, kirakamāṟutal uṇṭu eṉṉum mukkiyamāṉa caṅkatikaḷi oppukkoḷvārkaḷāṉāl - perumpāṉmiyōr oppukkoḷvatum vāstavam-22 curutikaḷ eṉṟa curutikkoḷki eṅkum irātu.
intak koḷkikkārar, avarkaḷuṭiya kāpṭaṉākiya kiḷameṇṭs turiyuḷpaṭa, ca-pa, ca-pa vāka 2/3 , 2/3 ākap pōvatiṉāl taṅkaḷuṭiya ārmōṉik curastāṉaṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu cātikkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl uṇmiyic collavēṇṭumāṉāl ippaṭi iraṇṭaṭi tūram pōvārkaḷāṉāl avarkaḷ ellārum tiṭīreṉakkuḻiyil viḻuvārkaḷ. stāyiyiṉ muṭivik kāṇamāṭṭārkaḷ. taliyilum pakkattilum puḷḷiyōṭu tirumpat tirumpa varum tacāmcapiṉṉamākiya 2/3 eppaṭi muṭivukku varum? iṅkilīṣ caṅkītam terintavarkaḷ, aintuaintākac curaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittuk koṇṭupōkumpōtu ciṟcila kuṟittu vikkāmal pōṉāl stāyi muṭivukku varātu eṉṟu aṟivārkaḷ. piyāṉāviṉ curaṅkaḷukkuc curuti cērkkumpōtu atic ceypavarkaḷ
ca-pa vāka varum aintukaḷic caṟṟukkuṟittu vittukkoṇṭē pōvārkaḷ. appaṭic ceyyāviṭṭāl ca-pa muṟiyāy varum ovvoru curamum vara varak kūṭik kaṭiciyil ōṉāy ūḷi pōlāyviṭum. ākiyāl "ōṉāy varāmal pārttukkoḷ." eṉṟa eccarikki koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟāvatu aṭukkilēyē atāvatu riṣapattiliruntu tivatamum, tivatattiliruntu kāntāramum uṇṭākum pōtē tuṉpam ārampittu viṭum. appoḻutu aticcarippaṭuttumpaṭi aṅkē koñcam ōci alikaḷiyum iṅkē koñcam ōci alikaḷiyum kuṟittuc cimpukaṭṭa nēriṭum. appaṭic ceyyāviṭṭāl 2/3 ākap pōkiṟateṉṟa kūttu muṭivukku varātē! itaṟkākavē "kikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa curutikaḷuḷḷa caṅkītamāṉatu aruvaruppu" eṉṟu collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. nāṉum atu cariyeṉṟu colli oppukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ. āṉāl nūlāciriyar 22 curutikkārarkaḷip pōla ippaṭik kikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa curutikaḷālāṉa aruvaruppit taṉ muṟikku ātāramākak koḷḷāmal  pūrva tamiḻarukkuḷḷē vaḻaṅki vantatum cama iṭiveḷikaḷuḷḷatum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu tīviramāy iyaṟkiyilē uḷḷatumāṉa curutikaḷālāṉa taṉ caṅkīta muṟiyi stāpikkiṟār. inta muṟiyāṉatu anēka taṭavikaḷil nūlāciriyaratu kumārattikaḷ srīmati marakatavalli ammāḷālum kaṉakavalli ammāḷālum vistāramāy rucuppaṭuttik kāṭṭap paṭṭirukkiṟatu. inta iraṇṭu peṇmaṇikaḷum intiya caṅkītattilum iṅkilīṣ caṅkītattilum aṭintirukkiṟa āccariyappaṭattakka vitamāṉa pāṇṭittiyattip paṟṟi nāṉ nēril naṟcāṭci koṭukkakkūṭum.
ivikaḷiyallāmal nūlāciriyar anēka Tables, Statements mutaliyavikaḷik koṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. ivikaḷ pala muṟiyiyum 32 aṅkulam eṉṟa orē muṟikkuk koṇṭuvantiruppatāl paṟpala muṟikaḷi eḷitil ottuppārppataṟku upayōkamāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷ pala muṟikaḷiṉ kaṇakku mutaliyavaṟṟic collukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷiyallāmal pūrvatamiḻariṉ 4 pālikaḷilum curavaricikaḷik kāṇpippataṟku irāciccakkaraṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. itik koṇṭu nūlāciriyaruṭiya putti kūrmiyum poṟumiyum iṉṉateṉṟu ilēcil aṟintukoḷḷalām. avikaḷik kavaṉamāyp paṭippavarukku avarkaḷ atil celavaḻittata kālam vīṇpōkāmal payaṉiyuṇṭākkum.
paḻiyakāriyaṅkaḷē tiṟam, avi cariyōtappō, veku kālam avikaḷ iruntapaṭiyāl avikaḷiyē cariyākak koḷḷavēṇṭum, ilēcil viṭṭuviṭakkūṭātu eṉpatu intiyariṉ iyaṟki. ippērppaṭṭa iyaṟkiyuṭiya jātiyiṉ mattiyil, oru kāriyatti uṇmiyeṉṟu aṟinta piṟaku atik kūrikaḷiṉmēl niṉṟu yāvarukkum teriya pirastāpikka payappaṭakkūṭātu eṉṟu niṉikkum anēka ñāṉikaḷip pārkkiṟōm. inta muṟi caritāṉeṉṟu pērpōṉa caṅkīta vitvacirēramaṇi kaḷākiya micūric cērnta makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cēṣaṇṇā, makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi
H.P.  kiruṣṇarāv, vijayanakaram makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi vīṇi vēṅkaṭa ramaṇatās, makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi muttiyāpākavatar mutaliyavarkaḷ oppuk koḷvatāṉatu namakkup perumiyē, cattiyam jeyam peṟumallavā?
inta nūliṉ piracuramāṉatu tuvāvimcati curutip puṟākkūṭṭaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ ciṟakukaḷi aṭittup pītiyi aṭiyumpaṭi ceyta pōtilum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṟku oru mukkiyamāṉa putu uyirkoṭutta campavamāy irukkumeṉpatil koñcamāvatu cantēkamilli. caṅkītam aṟintavarkaḷukkuḷ itu oruvita maṉakkiḷarcciyiyum pirītiyiyum uṇṭupaṇṇum eṉpatu niccayam. āṉāl itaṉ mūlamāy pūrva tamiḻarukkuḷ maṉitacārīrattiyum nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷiyum appaṭṭamāy captittukkāṭṭakkūṭiya yāḻ allatu vīṇi atikamāy upayōkikkap paṭṭatupōla taṟkālamum atu upayōkikkappaṭṭuvarumāṉāl nalamā yirukkum. kṣīṇittuppōṉa karnāṭaka caṅkītattit tirumpavum uyirp pikkavum, taṟkāla caṅkītattit taṅkaḷuṭiya conta celvamākap pūṭṭi vittu atik kuruṭṭuttaṉamāy upayōkikkum vitvāṉkaḷiṉ kiyiliruntu tappuvikkavum jāti matavittiyācamillāmal yāvarum paṭippatiṉāl ovvoru intiya vīṭṭilum atu upayōkappaṭuttappaṭavum inta nūl pirayōjaṉamāyirukkumeṉpatu niccayam. 
(oppam) A.G. piccamuttu
Opinion of Mr. N.P. SUBRAMANIA IYER, M.R.A.S., Journalist
Author of “Astrological Series”
Secretary, Sangeetha Vidya Mahajana Sangam, etc. Tanjore.
Having had the good fortune to follow closely, for years, the labors undertaken by Mr. Pandither for the solution of the problem of the number of Sutis in the Hindu musical scale, I am thoroughly convinced of the immense value of this book to the enrichment and advancement of Indian music. It is, I think, safe to assert that a knowledge of the Srutis is an important factor in the ground-work of musical trining. It is the law of the Srutis that makes Music an art, and a knowledge of that law is so much capital, held in reserve, aginst the encroachments of inharmony, however cleverly concealed. We have too few among professional musicians to-day who have a taste to cultivate a knowledge of the scienctific side of the art. That there are but 22 Srutis for the Octave is to them an inherited theory which lies somewhere at the back of their minds, but neither in vocal nor in instrumental music are they able to express the twenty two Taking this theory as a text, Mr. Pandither examines each assertion with the fairness of one trined to critical methods and with the exactness of a scientist, and we have a full and luminous presentation of the theory of 24 Srutis for the Octave, as also of the minute Srutis, 48 and 96. I am aware that this theory, so lucidly mintined in the pages of this book, has proved disquieting to the so-called votaries of the 22. But to talk of 22 without being able to demonstrate it, it in any manner, and, at the same time, to attack the conclusions of one who has handled the problem in a reverent andserious spirit is compatible neither with national pride nor with professional sincerity.
The book is replete with information that must add to the culture of the reader while the historical facts, the tables and the illustrations lead him from page to page with increasing fascination. The reader is brought into touch with the civilization of the ancient Tamils and the marvellous efficiency attined by them in the practice of Music. This is another merit about the book and herein we see that the system of 24 Srutis is no forcible creation or re-arrangement but rather an interpretation of the ancient Tamil works whichallude to the theory of 7, 12, 24, 48 and 96 Srutis-a system which flourished in the blood of our ancestors thousands of years ago.
No examination of Mr. Pandithers theory of 24 and the minute Srutis could have been more thorough than the one that was held at the All India Music Conference in Baroda, last year. There the exposition of the theory appealed to the reason of every one in the grand assemblage of musicians, while the practical demonstration thereof, by the accomplished dughters of Mr.Pandither, elicited unqualified prise. Similarly, the demonstration in the Palace, in the presence of their Highnesses the Gaekwar and the Maharani, in response to their special invitation, was a unique success. I had the happiness of being present on both, among many other, occasions. At the 7th Conference of the Sangita Vidya Mahajana Sangam, the theory and its practice, the mathematical calculations of Swarams and other detils were minutely gone into by experts who were qualified to pronounce an opinion on the different aspects of the question. And, as will be seen from the following pages, they have borne unanimous testimony to the soundness of the theory and the conformity of the practice to the same. Scepticism and timid conservatism may feel dissatisfied and try to impir the value of the work, for some time, but the secrets of the science lay open to those who study it in a disinterested spirit, unhampered by prejudice. It is they who will try to follow in the footsteps of careful thinkers like Mr. Pandither who has spoken at length after years of pinstaking and extensive research.
(Sd.) N.P. SUBRAMANIA IYER.
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi N.P. cuppiramaṇiya aiyar avarkaḷ apippirāyam.
intiya caṅkīta stāyiyil vaḻaṅkum curutikaḷippaṟṟi ārāycci ceyta viṣayattil Mr. paṇṭitaravarkaḷ anēka varuṣaṅkaḷākac ceyta muyaṟciyi nēril pārkkum cilākkiyatti nāṉ aṭintavaṉāṉatāl intap puttakam intiya caṅkītatti vaḷaracceyyum viṣayattilum ataṟku ariya pala viṣayaṅkaḷic cērkkum viṣayattilum atika pirayōcaṉattit tarumeṉṟu niccayamāṉa apippirāyam koṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. caṅkītatti appiyācikkum viṣayattil ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mukkiyamāṉa mūla ātāram curutikaḷippaṟṟiya ñāṉamtāṉ eṉṟu taṭiyillāmal collalām. curutikaḷippaṟṟiya piramāṇamē caṅkītatti aṉupavattukkēṟṟa oru cāstiramākkukiṟatu. kātukku iṉimiyaṟṟa caṅkītatti evvaḷavu mūṭivittup pirayōkam paṇṇiṉapōtilum atu cariyāṉa caṅkītattik keṭuttuviṭātapaṭi intac curutip piramāṇamāṉatu eppōtum kiyiliruntu utavukiṟa mūlataṉampōlirukkiṟatu. taṟkālam caṅkītatti vayiṟṟup piḻippukkāka upayōkikkiṟa anēkar ati aṉupōka cāstiramāka viruttikkukkoṇṭu varuvataṟku vēṇṭiya caktiyillātavarkaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷ. stāyikku 22 curutikaḷ uṇṭeṉṟa apippirāyam vamca parampariyāy avarkaḷ oppukkoṇṭu vanta oru koḷkiyāy avarkaḷ maṉatil māttiram irukkiṟatēyallāmal antac curutikaḷi vāyiṉāl colliyāvatu vāttiyaṅkaḷil vācittāvatu kāṭṭamuṭiyātavarkaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷ. intak koḷkiyip paṇṭitaravarkaḷ taṉmuṉ vittuk koṇṭu atippaṟṟip palarum colvati oru cāstiriyiṉ nuṇmi yuṭaṉum takutiyāṉa ārāycciyuṭaṉum eṭuttuk kaṇṭittirukkiṟatu mallāmal, oru stāyiyil 24 curutikaḷum maṟṟum nuṭpamāṉa 48, 96 curutikaḷum uṇṭeṉṉum muṟiyit teḷivākavum vistāramākavum eṭuttuc colli stāpikkiṟārkaḷ. ivvaḷavu teḷivāy intap puttakattil inta muṟiyic coṉṉatāṉatu 22 curutikkārar maṉatil oruvitak kuḻappattiyuṇṭu paṇṇiṉateṉpatu eṉakkut teriyum.  22 curutikaḷ eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu ati rucuppaṭutta muṭiyāmal viḻippatum, atē camayattil anta muṟiyic caṅkiyiyōṭum pakti vinayattōṭum ārāycci ceytavarkaḷuṭiya muṭivāṉa apip pirāyaṅkaḷik kāraṇamillāmal etirppatum nammuṭiya kulattiṉ perumikkāvatu caṅkītattik kiyāḷupavariṉ uṇmikkāvatu aḻakalla.
pustakamāṉatu pala viṣayaṅkaḷippaṟṟi ērāḷamāṉa kuṟippukaḷuḷḷa tāṉatāl vācippavaruṭiya aṟivi virutti ceyyak kūṭiyatu. atiluḷḷa carittira viṣayaṅkaḷum aṭṭavaṇikaḷum upamāṉaṅkaḷum vācikka vācikka maṉati vacappaṭuttakkūṭiyavi. ātittamiḻaruṭiya nākarīkam, caṅkīta viṣayattil avarkaḷ aṭintirunta āccariya vitamāṉa pāṇṭittiyam mutaliya viṣayaṅkaḷ vācippavarukkut teḷivāyt teriyum. pustakattip paṟṟiya iṉṉeraru mukkiyamāṉa pukaḻccikkuriya viṣayam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, avarkaḷ collum 22 curutikaḷ muṟi kaṭṭāyappaṭuttiyāvatu maṟṟa curutikaḷi vēṟu vitamāṉa oḻuṅkuppaṭuttiyāvatu uṇṭākkappaṭṭatā yirāmal ātittamiḻ nūlkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa 7, 12, 24, 48, 96 curutikaḷ muṟiyiliruntu piṟantateṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum. immuṟi namatu muṉṉērarkaḷiṉ irattattōṭu āyiramāyiram varuṣaṅkaḷākak kalantu vantirukkiṟatu.
pōṉavaruṣam parōṭāvil naṭanta kāṉpareṉcil paṇṭitaravarkaḷiṉ 24 curuti muṟi parīki ceyyappaṭṭatupōl vēṟentac camayattilum ceyyappaṭavilli. accamayam ammuṟi viḷakkikkāṭṭappaṭṭa pōtu aṅkuk kūṭiyirunta pirapala vitvāṉkaḷ yāvarālum atu cariyeṉṟu aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭatumallāmal paṇṭitaravarkaḷ kalvi vāynta kumārattikaḷāl ammuṟi rucuppaṭuttikkāṭṭappaṭṭapōtu avarkaḷ ellārālum pukaḻappaṭṭārkaḷ. avvitamākavē kikvār makārājāviṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉ pēril avarkaḷ muṉṉiliyilum makārāṇiyavarkaḷ muṉṉiliyilum araṇmaṉiyil ammuṟi rucuppaṭuttappaṭṭapōtum avarkaḷ peṟṟa kīrttip piratāpam collamuṭiyātu. iraṇṭu camayaṅkaḷilum maṟṟavarkaḷōṭukūṭa nāṉum ācarāyirukkum cilākkiyattip peṟṟēṉ. tañci caṅkīta makājaṉa caṅkattiṉ 7-vatu kāṉpareṉcilum immuṟiyum ataṉ rucuvum ceytu kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. atōṭu curaṅkaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷum maṟṟum nuṭpamāṉa viṣayaṅkaḷum anta viṣayattip palavitamāyum nērakki ārāyntu apippirāyam collak kūṭiya vitvāṉkaḷāl nuṭpamāyp parīkṣikkappaṭṭatu. intap pustakattiṉ piṉ pakkaṅkaḷil collappaṭṭirukkiṟapaṭi ammuṟi cāstira viṣayamāyc cariyeṉṟum aṉupavattiṟkum atu ottatākavē irukkiṟateṉṟum avarkaḷ ellārum naṟcāṭci koṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. cantēkakkārarum paḻiyatē cariyeṉṟu cātikkum kōḻikaḷum ipputtakattippaṟṟit tiruptippaṭāmal atiṉuṭiya nalla vēliyik keṭukkum paṭi pirayācappaṭalām. atuvum cila nāḷikkuttāṉ āṉāl niṣkaḷaṅka maṉatuṭaṉum uṇmiyi aṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa avāvuṭaṉum atip paṭippavarukkuc cāstirattiṉ maṟiporuḷāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷellām eḷitil viḷaṅkum. paṇṭitaravarkaḷ anēka varuṣaṅkaḷāy vistāramāṉa muyaṟciceytuvantatiṉ palaṉāka inta muṟiyi stāpikkiṟārkaḷ. appērpaṭṭa niṣkaḷaṅka maṉatuḷḷavarkaḷ tāṉ jākkiratiyāṉa maṉatuṭaṉ ivarkaḷ ceyta vēliyip pōlac ceyyakkūṭiyavarkaḷ.
(oppam) N.P. cuppiramaṇiyaiyar.
ceṉṉi eppā hiskūl tamiḻppaṇṭitar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ā. tēvappiracātam piḷḷi avarkaḷ koṭutta cāttukavi.
	1.	kaṭalcūḻum nilavulaki luḷavuyirkaḷ yāviṉumā katitta tāṉa
		uṭalcūḻuṅ kaṟiyaṭiyi naṭampuriya mikkamakiḻ vūṭṭu vikkum
		aṭalcūḻuṅ koṭumpuliyi vayavariyic cātukuṇat tamara vikkum
		miṭalcūḻu mivuḷiyiṉic cerukkaḷattē yuṟcāka mēvac ceyyum.
	2.	iriyuṇṇa nīrarunta veṇṇātu neṭuṅkaḻutti ravaṇan taṉṉuḷ
		kariyilāk kaḷippeytik kaḷippiṉiyuṅ karutātu kālā ṟātu
		virivākap pālivaṉaṅ kaṭantiṭavē celuttiṭumim meymi tēril
		tarimītu caṅkītan taṉakkiṇiyā vēṟeraṉṟic cāṟṟa lāmē.
	3.	vaṇṭikaḷi yōṭṭukiṉṟōr pāṭukiṉṟa tempāṅkum maṟṟuṅ kēṭṭuc
		caṇṭiniṟa māṭukaḷu miṟumāntu kaṭumpāran tāvi yīrttuk
		koṇṭivaru māṉirikaḷ kōpāla rūtupullāṅ kuḻali ṉēracik
		kaṇṭavarum pālkaṟakku muṭṭavanta cutipāṭaṟ kāṉan tittē.
	4.	vaṉavēṭar pāṇkēṭṭu maṉamuruki yaciyātu māṉka ṇiṟkum
		cuṉakaṉunal licikēṭkiṟ kurippiṉṟi yamarntiruntu cukittup pōkum
		puṉaliṟcel vaṅkamatiṟ kuḻaleraliyic ceviyuṟṟup puṇari nāykaḷ
		iṉamellān tiraṇṭeraṉṟāyt toṭarntuvarun tolitūra mekka ḷittē
	5.	kāṉaraca maṉupavittuk kaccakamun taliyacittuk kaḷiyik kāṭṭum
		vāṉamatiṟ cañcarikkuñ cātakamuṅ kiṉṉaramu maṟṟum puḷḷum
		ñāṉamuṭa ṉicikēṭṭaṅ kēmuṟuva taṉṟittan nāvāṟ pāṭi
		mōṉamiku muṉivariyu mēṉiyava riyumiṉpiṉ mūḻttu mātō
	6.	mātavameṉ paruvattē matiyaniṟin toḷikāla malimēṟ kāṉil
		pātavaṅkaḷ ceṟintuḷḷa paṇiyōram vīṇiyiṉaṟ paṇivā cikkil
		ātaramō ṭavvicikēṭ ṭāṉantit tantarattē yacuṇa mākkaḷ
		pōtamuñcērarn tacivaṟṟu niṟkumeṉi licipperumi pukalvār yārē.
	7.	taṇṭaliyiṟ kampaliyiṟ ṟāruviṉiṟ ceṭikoṭiyiṟ ṟaṭattiṟ kāṉil
		uṇṭupaṭu malarmatuvi yuṇṭumika vuḷamatarttē yuḻika ṭēraṟum
		vaṇṭukaḷcev vaḻimuralum vāviyiṟṟā marivaḷiya macca mārntu
		koṇṭematu cevikkamutañ corivateṉak kātampaṅ kūvip pāṭum.
	8.	maṇṇilat taṟpamā macakan tāṉumē
		paṇṇuṟap pāṭiyē paṟantu lāviṭum
		taṇṇiya vēraciyic cārntu kēṭṭiṭum
		eṇṇiliv vicivali yāvi tāmpaṭā.
	9.	illiṭan tōṟumaṟ ṟeṅku muḷḷatām
		palliyu micimikap parintu kēṭkumāl
		nalliya lēraciyi nayantu pōntupiṉ
		melliya nūlvali mīḷum lūtiyē.
	10.	kuttivā ḻuyirkaḷuṭ koṭiya nākamum
		mattamuṟ ṟatuveṉa makaṭi vāyeḻu
		nattupuṉ ṉākava rāḷi kēṭṭalum
		uttiyār pivirit tuvappo ṭāṭumē.
	11.	toṭṭiliṟ kiṭantaḻat tuvakku mōrcicu
		kiṭṭivan tammaṉi kiḷattu tālcevip
		paṭṭiṭap porumalpōyp parumi taṅkoḷum
		iṭṭaḷan tīrttici yiṉpu mīyumē.
	12.	avviya nīkkiyē yaruḷa ḷittiṭum
		cevviyil cērampiṉit tīrttut tāṭarum
		kavvicēr mūrkkamuṅ kaḻittuc cāntamē
		ovvuṟac ceytiṭu muyarcaṅ kītamē.
	13.	kavalika ṭīrttuḷak kaḷippi yīntiṭum
		puvaṉiyi yuvaṇipōṟ poliyac ceytiṭum
		avalama toḻittumik kāṟṟa ṉalkiṭum
		tuvalkolum viṭattiyun tolikkuṅ kītamē.
	14.	naḷḷalar tammiyu naṇpa rākkiṭum
		uḷḷapal luyirkaṭam muḷḷa mīrttiṭum
		aḷḷiyik kūḷiyi yaṭakkik kaṭṭiṭum
		eḷḷarum valiyuṭit tiṉiya kītamē.
	15.	toḻilkaḷip purintu taḷarcciyuṟ ṟavarkkut toyvunīk kuvatucaṅ kītam
		oḻivilā tōti yeyttavar mūḷik kuṟutinal kuvatucaṅ kītam
		kaḻiperun tuyara maṉattaṭin tavarkkuk kaḷippiyī kuvatucaṅ kītam
		aḻivilāṉ puvikkō ramutaṉa vitimuṉ ṉaḷittaṉa ṉaṟiñarā laṉṟē.
	16.	vilaṅkukaḷ puṭka ḷūrvaṉa nīril viḷaṅkupal luyirkaḷu miciyiṉ
		valaṅkoṭiṇ miyiṉā liḻupaṭu muruki makiḻntumē paravaca mākum
		nalaṅkoḷu maṉitap piṟaviyiṟ piṟantum nallici kēṭṭuru kātāṉ
		ilaṅkaṟi villāk kaṟkaḷō virumpō viṉṉaveṉ ṟurikkaṟi yōmāl.
	17.	kāmaraṅ kēṭṭu murukiṭā maṉitar kacaṭariṟ ṟāḻvuṟu kacaṭar
		īmanēr maṉamu narakiṉu miruṇṭa teṇṇamō carppaṉi catiyē
		yāmavar naṭpum vañcaka niṟinta taṉpumē kaṉaviluṅ koḷḷār
		tīmicē ravari nampoṇā teṉavē ceppiṉar pulamiyiṉ mikkōr.
	18.	nallici valiyi nāṭṭuvāṉ karuti nakkaṉmā tavaṉeraṭā ṉāyaṉ
		mulliyam paṇṇi yāḻiṉi licikka muraḷiyi liṉṉici terikkat
		tolliyin teḻutti vēṇuvi leralikkat tōṟṟiya carācara maṉittum
		olliyi ṉurukik kaḷittaṉa veṉṟiṅ kurittiṭum paṟpala purāṇam.
	19.	irunilat taṭiyā revarkaḷē yeṉiṉu miṉimiyāyp pāṭalkaḷ pāṭit
		tiruvaṭi tutikkiṉ ṟaṉatuḷaṅ kaṉintu tikaḻuva raṉpeṉum valiyiṉ
		maruviyē cikkuṇ ṭaṉṉavar kēṭkum varaṉelām poruḷelām maṟukkā
		torutaṉip parama ṉaruḷuva ṉeṉṉi luyarntatu pāṇiṉu muṇṭēra.
	20.	eṉṉuyirk kuyiri yepporuḷukku miṟivaṉi yimiyavar kaṇaṅkaḷ
		tuṉṉiyem maruṅkuñ cūḻntukoṇ ṭiyaṉ cupakuṇaṅ kaḷiyeṭut tōti
		iṉṉicik karuvi muḻakkiyē pāṭi yēṟṟavā ṟāṭiyē tutippār
		poṉṉula kiṉilum polintilaṅ kuvatu poruvaṟu kītamē yaṉṟō.
	21.	ittuṇip perumi vāynta caṅkīta mēṉiya kalikaḷip pōlum
		atticū ḻulakil varavarac curuṅki yarilpala kalantiṭal kaṇṭu
		cittamē caliyā ticivalērar yāruñ cērntiṭa verarucapi nāṭṭi
		vittakak karuṇā mirutacā karappēr mēmpaṭu nūlvaḻaṅ kiṉaṉāl.
	22.	aṉṉāṉ yāreṉ ṟemiviṉavi ṉaṟivēraṅ kēṇmi ṉippuviyil
		muṉṉā ṭeṉṉā ṭāṇṭuvanta mutaṉmi peṟumū varacarkaḷuḷ
		oṉṉā rañca nakuvēlā ṉucitaṉ vaḻiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉavar
		eṉṉā naviluñ cāṉṟārtam miṉatti lutitta kuṇamēru.
	23.	tañci nakaruk katipaṉeṉat tariyil viḷaṅku vāḻvuṭiyōṉ
		eñca liṉṟit tikkeṭṭu meṭṭuṅ kīrtti mikappaṭittōṉ
		kañcam pōṉṟa vataṉamatiṟ karuṇi yoḻuku miruviḻiyōṉ
		nañca meṉavē poyveṟuttu nāḷum vāymi pukaṉāvāṉ.
	24.	nāḷuṅ kalvi racamataṉi naccip paruku maṇicceviyāṉ
		kāḷa mukilu mintaruvuṅ kaṇṭu veṭkap poḻikarattāṉ
		vēḷiṉ carattuk kiṭamiṉṟi vimali vāḻun taṭamārpāṉ
		āḷuṅ kēḷu mōvāma laṇukip paṇiyu malarttāḷāṉ.
	25.	tēca māḷu matipatikaḷ ciṟanta pirapuk kaṇmikkōr
		pācam vittē koṇṭāṭip paḻaki yuṟavā ṭuṅkuricil
		nēcaṅ koṇṭu caṟkuruvāy nērnta karuṇā ṉantarupa
		tēca mataṉi maṟavātu tiṉamuñ cevvi neṟiniṟpōṉ.
	26.	muṉṉam muttu cāmiyeṉu mutalva ṉaṉṉam māḷēraṭum
		paṉṉāṇ muyaṉṟa varuntavattiṉ payaṉā vanta pākkiyavāṉ
		piṉṉa rutitta jēcu tācaṉ pīṭār tāṉi yēlvilyam
		eṉṉu mūvar tamiyunta ṉiṉiya vaṉuca rāyuṭiyōṉ.
	27.	teṉṉaṉ kulattut tōṉṟaleṉat teḷivāyk kāṭṭuntirunāmam
		maṉṉuñ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉi maṉērakya jēratip pāṇṭiyaṉi
		tuṉṉuṅ kuṇamā pāṇṭiyaṉic cērarvil cavuntara pāṇṭiyaṉit
		taṉṉiṉ putalva rāyppaṭitta taṉṉē rillāt tāṭṭīkaṉ.
	28.	eṉṟun tēvap piracāta meṉṉu mempōṟ pāvalari
		yoṉṟuṅ kuṟivey tāmaṟṟaṉ ṉuyirpōṟ ṟāṅki yātarippōṉ
		iṉṟu maṉṟē pōlaṉpi liṟiyuṅ kuṉṟā vuttuṅkaṉ
		naṉṟun tayiyuṅ kimmāṟu nāṭā tevarkkum purimakipaṉ.
	29.	pūtalatti lātavaṉi yottaperu viḷakkam
			poruntiyeṉṟum nīṅkāta peruṅkoṭiyō ṭirakkam
		ōtukali yuṇarccitavā māṇpu peruṅkīrtti
			orunāḷuṅ kuṟiyāma lēraṅku peruvipavam
		nātaṉaṭit tāmariyi maṟavāta vaṉpu
			nallakuru paktitaḷa rāvūkkap pāṭu
		cātukuṇa mītaṉittu merarucēra vāyntōṉ
			tañcinaka rāpirakām paṇṭitaṉā mālē.
	30.	aṉṟu tirumā lamarark kamutakuṭam
		oṉṟutan tāṉatika muṇṭukolēra-ceṉṟutañci
		yākaramē yeṉṉakkoṇ ṭāpirakā mālamuta
		cākaramē tantiṭṭāṉ ṟāṉ.

tañcāvūr, catāvatāṉam
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cuppiramaṇiya aiyaravarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya
āciriya viruttam.
	
	1.	annāḷi liyaṟṟamiḻi lakattiyaṉā merarumuṉiva ṉaṉittu mārāyn
		teṉṉāku moḻikkumuṭi vāmeḻuttuc curutiyeṇmūṉ ṟeṉṉak kaṇṭāṉ
		innāḷi licittamiḻiṟ curamuṭivāñ curutimūveṭ ṭeṉṉak kaṇṭāṉ
		maṉṉāku māppirakām paṇṭitaṉṟāṉ pulamippēr māṇpi vaṟkē.
	muṭivām eḻuttu e-tu āvi ñaṇanamaṉa yaralavaḻaḷamey cāyumukara nālāṟu mīṟē eṉṟapaṭi,
	curuti-oli. curam-catta curaṅkaḷ. pulamip pēr e-tu paṇṭitaṉ eṉṉum peyar. māṇpu - ciṟappu. ivaṟku-inta āppirakām eṉpavaṉukku. ēkāram pirinili.

	2.	muttamiḻu ḷiyaṟṟamiḻiṉ muṭivāku meḻuttukkaḷ mūveṭ ṭeṉṟa
		cattiyaṉṟērn tāṉāṭa kattamiḻi ṉiruttavaki kaḷimūṉ ṟeṭṭeṉ
		ṟattamiḻnā ṭaṉṟērntā ṉicittamiḻiṟ curaccuruti yāṟu nāṉkeṉ
		ṟattamiku māppirakām paṇṭitaṉṟērn tāṉaṭunā yakamī tāmē.
muttamiḻ- iyal, ici, nāṭakam. nirutta vakikaḷ-kūttiṉ vikaṟpam. attamiḻnāṭaṉ-oru pāṇṭiyaṉ. curaccuruti-catta curaṅkaḷiṉ oli. naṭunāyakam-naṭuviluḷḷa icittamiḻccuruti. mītu ām-mēlākum. ītu āmeṉṟuṅ koḷḷalām.
periya kuḷantālūkā pūcārikkavaṇṭaṉ paṭṭi
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ca. tirumalivēluk kavirāyar avarkaḷ colliya kavikaḷ.

viruttam.
	poṅkutirik kaṭaṟpuviyiṟ paṇṭinū letuveṉattāṉ porunta nāṭic
	caṅkiyaṟat teripunaṟcaṅ kītacaṅka meraṉṟamittut taṉatu cīrtti
	yeṅkumviḷak kiyateṉaviṉ ṉicittamiḻi viḷakkiṉaṉā leḻilcēr tañcit
	tuṅkamuṟu māpirakām vaḷḷalperun taṉmiyiyār colaval lārē.
veṇpā.
	nāmē vicittamiḻi naṉkuviḷak kikkaṭalcūḻ
	pūmē laḻiyāp pukaḻpaṭittāy-māmēṉmi
	taḷḷā viṉimit tamiḻuṇarnta vāpirakām
	vaḷḷā luṉakkevarnēr vār.
viruttam.
	karavaṟucīrt tañciyiṟcaṅ kītavityā makācaṉacaṅ katti runtu
	caratakuṇa vāpirakām paṇṭitamē lēraṉarumit tavattāl vanta 
	marakata valliyuṅ kaṉaka valliyumpā ṭiyavatika maturakīta
	viratamuṇarn tavartamakku velappākuṅ kacappāku mitumey tāṉē.

makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi ca. tirumalivēluk kavirāyaravarkaḷ kumārar
makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi caṅkukkavirāyar iyaṟṟiyatu.

viruttam.
	muttamiḻu ṇaṭuttamiḻā micittamiḻi ṉilakkaṇa nūṉ muṭivi yellāñ
	cittamakiḻ vuṟṟumika puttiyaṉu pavattiṉāṟ ṟērntu naṉṟāyp
	puttamuta vācakamā varintulakeṅ kaṇumviḷakkip pukaḻpeṟ ṟāṉā
	letticiyum pukaḻnturikkun tañcinaka rāpirakā meṉumē lēraṉē
	pañcakā viyattuḷeraṉṟāñ cilappatikā rakkavikaḷ payiṉmēṟ kōḷā
	veñcalilā vakiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭiyici yilakkaṇamu meṭuttuk kāṭṭit
	tañcinaka rāpirakām vaḷḷalpuri caṅkapira caṅka mōrntu
	miñciyamut tamiḻvalaru mikaviyantā revarataṉi viyavā tārē.
	patamuṟunan tamiḻnūliṟ pakarumici yilakkaṇattip pārttē miṉṟi
	yituvariyav vitippaṭiyē pāṭiṉā reraruvariyu mīṅkuṅ kāṇēñ
	catataḷamā malarttaṭañcūḻ tañciyā pirakāmvit tāraṉ peṟṟa
	putalviyarav vitippaṭiyē pāṭiṉā raṟputamaṟ putamī tammā.



cōḻatēcattil viḷaṅkum pañcanatapurattil stāpikkappaṭṭirukkum kavarṇmeṇṭu
samaskiruta pāṭacāli mukkiya upāttiyāyarum rātāmaṅkala kirāma vāciyumāṉa
kāṉakavi pirammasrī nārāyaṇa cāstirikaḷ apippirāyam.
1. ulakattikkāppāṟṟa vēṇṭiya apilāṣiyāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa aḻakāṉa tōṭṭaṅkaḷālum amirutampōla maturamāṉa auṣataṅkaḷālum rāvsāhēp āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷ jaṉaṅkaḷukku upakāram ceytum taṉporuḷ utaviyāl tamiḻppāṣiyil eḻuti taṉ apip pirāyatti veḷiyiṭṭu taṉ kirantattil kīta cāstirattip paravacceytu ataṟkuc cērapi uṇṭākkiṉār.
2. saṅkīta ratṉākara kirantatti eḻutiyavariṉ iṣṭamāṉa curuti kiramattic caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ ikkālattil pāṭa caktiyaṟṟavarkaḷ. ākiyāl paṇṭitar avarkaḷ yatṉam ceytatu jayamaṭintateṉpatu eṉ apippirāyam.
3. cemmiyāka vicāraṇiceytu paṇṭitarāl vistāramāka eḻutappaṭṭirukkum kirantattip paṟṟi vistāramāka eḻuta muṭiyātatāl saṅkirahamāka eḻutukiṟēṉ.
4. tamiḻppāṣiyil 3 caṅkaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭṭa vātakkalakkattuṭaṉ kūṭiya saṅkīta campiratāyam vekukālamāka iruppatākac cemmiyāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār.
5.	tēcāntaraṅkaḷil saṅkīta piracuram niṟintirukkum kālattil intat tēcattil saṅkīta sampiratāyatti ēṟpaṭutti, eṭuttuc colpavar illi.
6. tamiḻp pāṣiyil vekukālamāka ēṟpaṭṭa caṅkatitāṉ itara pāṣikaḷil veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭateṉṟu taṉ kirantattil vistāramāka eṭuttuk kāṇpikkiṟār.
7. tamiḻ vitvāṉkaḷāl appiyasikkappaṭṭa caṅkīta cāstira pirastāpa kōlāhalattāl vēṟu apippirāyam kolpavarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam kaṇṭikkappaṭṭatākat teriyavarukiṟatu.
8. ratṉākara kirantattil colli irukkum uṇmiyik kaṇita mūlamākap piracuram ceytal caṅkīta cāstirattil āciyuḷḷavarkaḷic cantōṣappaṭutti vikkiṟatu.
9. tamiḻ vitvāṉkaḷukkup paḻakkamākiya 96 curutiyi paricīlaṉi ceytu atip pātipātiyāka vakuttu curuti kiṭikkiṟateṉṟu kaṇakkiṉāl kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
10. kāyatrī mantirattiṉ akṣaraṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyil 2 akṣaraṅkaḷi kuṟittu 22 curuti kiramam cāraṅkatēvar ēṟpaṭuttiṉār.
11. curutikaḷ 96 ākavum 48 ākavum 24 ākavum 12 ākavum pirikkappaṭukiṟatu.
12. intak karṉāṭaka kītam kāyakarkaḷāl pāṭappaṭukiṟateṉpatu piracittam. eṇṇikkiyil pētam ēṟpaṭuvatu rākam pāṭuvatil uṇṭākum pētam.
13. mēṟkūṟiyavikaḷil rāvcākēpu eṉṟu paṭṭappeyar aṭinta āpirakām paṇṭitar taṉṉuṭiya karuṇāmirutasākaram eṉkiṟa kirantattil vistāramāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟār.
14. vittiyam, yōkam, āyurvētam, jēratiṭacāstiram, vētānta cāstiram campantam iruppatāka avar curutikaṇakku eṭuttatil kāṭṭap paṭṭiruppatāka nāṉ aṟintu koṇṭēṉ.
15. vīṇi rūpam atiṉ curam, curuti, captam mutaliyavikaḷ uṇṭāvatu maṉuṣiyaṉuṭiya kaḻuttu, cura cuvāsam kali mutaliyavikaḷi ottu ceyyappaṭṭatu.
16. mēṟku tēcattiya caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ collum parimāṇattiṟkut takkappaṭi tvaṉicāmyam muṉkālattilēyē tamiḻ vitvāṉkaḷāl aṟiyap paṭṭateṉpatu pala vitamāka intak kirantattil collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
17. maṟṟavarkaḷ pāṭum sa ma pa ni eṉkiṟa 4 svaraṅkaḷiluṇṭākum 4 vīṇikaḷil sa ka pa ni eṉkiṟa svaraṅkaḷiliruntu 4 jāti uṇṭākiṟatu.
18. *   *   *  *   *   *  *   *   *
19. ratṉākaram ceytavarum, paratamuṉiyum muṉ irunta tirāviṭarkaḷum ivvitamāṉa saṅkīta rahasyatti collavilli eṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
20. oru stāyiyil curuti 22 eṉpatu oru iṭattilum collappaṭavilli eṉṟum curuti 24 tāṉ eṉpatu collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉṟum collappaṭṭatu.
21. curuti 22 eṉṟu colpavarkaḷiṉ koḷkiyi taṭuppataṟku ivvaḷavu pirayāsam eṭuttuk koṇṭu taṉ apippirāyam eḻutappaṭṭatu.
22. curutisvaravivēkam ciramatti eṭuttukkoṇṭu ituvariyil oruvarālum aṟiyappaṭavilli. intak kirantattil tirāviṭa piramāṇattāl kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
23. tamiḻ coṟkaḷ mirukapakṣi, tvaṉikku camāṉamāṉa vinyācattuṭaṉ kūṭiyavikaḷāka veku kālamāyirukkiṟatu. pāṣiyiṉ uṟpattikkuk kāraṇam pirākiruta mūlaṅkaḷ eṉṟum collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
24. maṟṟavarkaḷālaṟiyakkūṭāta kīti, curuti svarastāṉaṅkaḷic coṉṉa kāraṇattiṉāl tirāviṭarkaḷ muṉṉamē aṟintavarkaḷeṉṟum keḷarava maṭintavarkaḷeṉṟum collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
25. parōṭā rājāviṉāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatum caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ camūkamum aṭaṅkiyatumāṉa capiyil taṉ peṇkaḷ pāṭi rājā mantiri mutaliyavarkaḷ cantōṣamaṭintatik koṇṭē ivar apippirāyam oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu.
26. āpirakām paṇṭitar ciramappaṭṭu eḻutiya kirantattil cūkṣamamāka aṟiyakkūṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷi vicāraṇi ceytirukkiṟār.
27. jaṉaṅkaḷukkuk koṭuppataṟkākavē taṉārjaṉam ceyta ip pirapu mēṉmiyāka irukkak kaṭavar. ivvitam nārāyaṇakavi taṉ apippirāyatti oḷikkāmal veḷiyiṭṭār.


eṭṭiyāpuram camastāṉam samaskiruta vitvāṉ
pirammasrī kurunāta cāstirikaḷ apippirāyam
1. muṉkālattil mahāviṣṇu camuttirattik kaṭintu amirutam eṭuttatupōla caṅkīta cāstira camuttirattiliruntu rāvcāhēp āpirakām paṇṭitar karuṇāmirutam eṉkiṟa curuti viṣaya kirantamāṉa amirutatti aṭintār.
2. evvitam muṉkālattil camuttirattiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa amirutam tēvarkaḷukkup pirītiyi uṇṭākkiyatō avvitamākac caṅkīta sāhityam aṟintavarkaḷum kāṉam ceypavarkaḷumāṉa vitvāṉkaḷukku intak karṇāmiruta kirantam pirītiyi uṇṭākka vēṇṭum.
3. pāṭumantiratti uccāraṇam ceypavarkaḷi rakṣippatāl kāyatrī eṉkiṟa peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu. antak kāyatrī mantirattil irukkum 24 akṣaraṅkaḷum 24 curutistāṉaṅkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
4. japam ceyvatil mantirattil uḷḷa akṣaratti viṭuvatu tōṣa mākum. kāṉam ceyvatil curutiyik kuṟippatu tōṣamākum. antac curutiyiṉ eṇṇikki 24 curutistāṉaṅkaḷālum 24 eṉpatil santēka milli.
5. ākiyāl uttamavitvāṉkaḷ ellā pirayatṉaṅkaḷ ceytum 24 eṇṇikkiyuḷḷa curutistāṉaṅkaḷi aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
(tañcāvūr vakkīl makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi pi.vi. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaravarkaḷāl tamiḻil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭatu.)
caṅkītattil tērnta pala vitvacirēramaṇikaḷiṉ apippirāyaṅkaḷi karuṇāmirta cākaram mutal puttakam - nāṉkām pākattil kāṇalām.
"kuṟinilattāṉattiyaṉṟa pāṭalamutam parukiṉāṉ" eṉṟapaṭi kuṟinta curamāka varum nuṭpacurutikaḷiṉ iṉimiyiyaṟinta pala vitvacirēramaṇikaḷ caṅkīta vityāmakājaṉa caṅkattiṉ ēḻāvatu kāṉpareṉsil koṭutta apippirāyaṅkaḷum piṟavum ipputtakattiṉ nāṉkām pākattil cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.	

1.	parōṭā, tivāṉ cākip makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi V.P. mātavarāv C.I.E.  avarkaḷ.
2. 	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi viṇīkacikāmaṇi cēṣaṇṇā avarkaḷ, micūr.
3.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi H.P.  kiruṣṇarāv avarkaḷ, micūr.
4.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi V. N. paṭkaṇṭi B.A.,L.L.B., avarkaḷ.
5.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi vēṅkaṭaramaṇatās avarkaḷ, vijayanakaram.
6.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi muttiyā pākavatar avarkaḷ, arikēcavanallūr cāttukavi.
7.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi pañcāpakēca pākavatar avarkaḷ, tañci.
8.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi M.S.  rāmacāmi aiyar avarkaḷ, maturi.
9.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi mu. L.naraciṅkarāv avarkaḷ, vijayanakaram.
10.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi R. cakrapāṇirāv avarkaḷ, tañci.
tañci caṅkīta vityā makājaṉa caṅkattiṉ ēḻāvatu kāṉpareṉciṉ pañcāyattār.
11.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi V. A. vāṇṭiyār avarkaḷ.
12.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi A.G.piccimuttu B.A.,L.T. , avarkaḷ.
13.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi appācāmi aiyar avarkaḷ.
14.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi makāliṅka aiyar avarkaḷ.
15.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi patmanāta aiyar avarkaḷ.
16.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi cāmiyāpiḷḷi avarkaḷ.
17.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi paratam nārāyaṇacāmi aiyar avarkaḷ.
18.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi captariṣipākavatar avarkaḷ.
19.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi citrakavi civarāmapākavatar avarkaḷ.
20.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi viruti civañāṉayōkikaḷ avarkaḷ.
21.	makā-rāca-rāca-ciṟi T. K. vēmpu aiyar B.A.,L.T. , avarkaḷ.
maṟṟum cila aṉparkaḷiṉ kaṭitaṅkaḷilum kāṇalām.



karuṇāmirta cākaram
mutal puttakam - mutal pākam
intiya caṅkīta carittirac curukkam
innūluḷ . . 
mutaṟpākam : caṅkītam pūrvakālattil iyal, ici, nāṭakameṉṉum, muttamiḻil oṉṟāyiruntateṉpatiyum, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ pūrvīkattiyum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ toṉmiyiyum, icit tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya vivarattiyum cila ici vallōriyum paṟṟic collum.
kaṭavuḷ tuṇi.


karuṇāmirta cākaram.
mutal puttakam.
mutal pākam.
intiya caṅkīta carittirac curukkam.
I. caṅkītattiṉ perumiyum ataṉ uṟpattiyum.
ulakellām taṉatu oru collāl uṇṭākki, avi yāvaṟṟiṟkum tāṉē uyirāy, āṉanta mūrttiyāy, aṉanta kalyāṇakuṇacīlaṉāy āṇṭu naṭattivarum karttaṉ iru poṟpāta kamalaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅki,  karuṇāmirta cākaram eṉṉum iccaṅkīta cāstiratti eḻutat toṭaṅkukiṟēṉ.
ulakukku mutalvaṉāṉa karttaṉ, mutal mutal nātacorūpiyāy, piṉ jīvaṉāy, jīvarkaḷiṉ uṇarvāy, uṇarvil aṟivāy, aṟivil āṉantamāy, āṉantattil nātamāy, nātattil kītamāy, kītattil layamāy, āṉanta narttaṉañceytu ulakip paripālikkiṟāṉ.
cattu cittu āṉantaṉāy, aṇṭapuvaṉa carācaraṅkaḷ aṉittum niṟintu niṉṟa apparamaṉi uḷḷuṇarnta periyōr, tamatu tūla tattuvaṅkaḷiyum cūṭcama tattuvaṅkaḷiyum kāraṇaṉākiya ak karttaṉiṭattil oṭukki, avaṉil viḷaṅkum kōṭi cūriya pirapiyil tāṅkaḷum pirapi peṟṟu, jīvakāruṇyam pārāṭṭi, mutti nilipeṟa catā tutittu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. vaṇaṅkiya periyōr aṭinta mēmpataviyik kaṇṭu, tāmum appatam peṟa ācittu, āṉanta mūrttiyi iṭiviṭātu tutittu, avaravarkkākum aḷavaṟṟa kirupi peṟṟār maṟṟavarum. kirupi peṟṟōr yāvarum, mēlāṉa avaṉ pukaḻic collit tutittuk kāṉam ceykiṟārkaḷēyoḻiya, tammi iḻivu paṭuttum lukīka kāṉaṅkaḷic ceyyār. ikkāṉamum, kāṉa mūrttiyiṉ ciruṣṭi titi cammāram eṉṉum muttoḻil pōlavē, tōṟṟam viruttilayattiyuṭiya apinayam rākam tāḷam eṉṉum mūṉṟu aṅkaṅkaḷiyuṭiyaJ. immūṉṟiyum oṉṟākap pāvittu, muttoḻil mūrttiyip patticeyyavēṇṭiyatu avaciyam. appaṭic ceyvatāl avaṉ aruṭceyalil kāṇappaṭum yāvaṟṟilum, avaṉē aṅkaṅkup paripūraṇaṉāy iruntu apinayittu āṭippāṭi āṉanta narttaṉañ ceykiṟāṉeṉṟu, pirattiyaṭcamāy aṟiyalām. āṉantak kūttāṭum mutalvaṉik kāṇum paktaṉ, tāṉum iṉṉiciyuṭaṉ āṭippāṭik karttaṉik koṇṭāṭuvāṉ.
karttaṉik koṇṭāṭiya paktarkaḷuḷ taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ aṉu pōkattiṟku ēṟṟavāṟu teyvatti rājaṉākavum, peṟṟeṭutta tāy tanti yākavum, kuruvākavum, āpatcakāyaṉākavum, tuyartīrtta maruttuvaṉākavum, arumimakavākavum, iṉiya maṇavāḷaṉākavum pāvittu vāyāra vāḻttiṉar cilar; tōttirittu vaṇaṅkiṉār palar; iṭar tīrkka iṟiñciṉār cilar; niṉittavi peṟa vēṇṭiṉar anēkar; pirivāṟṟāmiyāl piralāpittār cilar; kaṇṭaṭinta cilar āṉantak kūttāṭiṉār; kāṇavirumpiya palar tūtupāṭi ucāviṉār; uḷḷaṉpu mēliṭṭu, uvantututittār. avaṉ kuṇaṅkaḷi ovvoṉṟāy eṭutteṭuttu vistarittu, maṟṟavar aṟiya viyantu pāṭiṉār. taṅkaḷ apāttiratti niṉintu iraṅkiṉār. tam tavaṟutalkaḷi niṉittu maṉṉikkap pirārttittār. kātal mikunta cilar, avaṉ tiru uruvi niṉittu, uṇaviyum ulakiyum maṟantār. uttamarkaḷiṉ jīviyattiyum avarkaḷ pakavāṉit tutittup pāṭiyavaṟṟuḷ uttamamāṉa pākaṅkaḷiyum oṉṟāyt tiraṭṭi, maṟṟavarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟap parampariyāyc collivittārkaḷ nam muṉṉōr. piṉṉuḷḷōr, atiyē vētameṉa eḻuti vittārkaḷ. vētatti ātāramākakkoṇṭa ovvoruvarum, teyva cannitikaḷilum potu stalaṅkaḷilum, taṅkaḷ vaṇakkattilum, vētattiluḷḷa cilapākaṅkaḷit tāmē pāṭṭākappāṭi ārātittu, āṉantamaṭintu vantārkaḷ. pāṭaccaktiyillātavarkaḷ, pāṭakarkaḷi niyamittup pāṭaccollik kēṭṭu āṉantittu vantārkaḷ. pāṭutaṟkut tuṇikkaruvikaḷ palapalaceytu, mikunta toṉiyuṭaṉ ārpparittārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ varuttam yāvum maṟantu, ēkamaṉatāy iṉṉiciyil īṭupaṭṭu, pakavāṉi ārātikkum innili naṉṉiliyeṉṟu niṉittu, mēlulakattilum teyvatti ippaṭiyē ārātippōmeṉṟu ācittu, mēlum mēlum ūkkam koṇṭārkaḷ. taṅkaḷ kāṉattip pala mukamāy virutticeyya, iṉiya curaṅkaḷiyum avikaḷiṉ ālāpaṉattiyum, ellōrum ēkōpittuc collakkūṭiya kāla aḷavākiya tāḷattiyum, oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāykkaṇṭu viruttiyaṭintu vantārkaḷ ivaṟṟil aṉukūla maṭintu varukiyil, taṅkaḷukkuc cantōṣamāṉa kālaṅkaḷilum, teyva cannitiyilum, tāṅkaḷ cāppiṭuṅ kālattiluṅkūṭa, pāṭattuvaṅkiṉārkaḷ.
teruvil viḷiyāṭum ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ kāṉaviruttiyippōla, nām kēṭkum iṉiya kāṉamum, palayukaṅkaḷākap pala upakaraṇaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu viruttiyāyiṟṟeṉṟu nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. eppaṭiyeṉṟāl, ōcitarum palakikaḷ peṭṭikaḷ katavukaḷ takarattakaṭukaḷ mutaliyavikaḷit taṭṭi, jati kaṇam layameṉṉum tāḷattukkuriya aṅkaṅkaḷ oruvarum pōtikkāmalē cuyamāy aṟintukoṇṭa pēccaṟiyāta pālarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ koñcam periyavarkaḷākumpoḻutu, ciṟu tampaṭṭam koṭṭāṅkaccittampaṭṭam keñjirā mutaliyavikaḷil paḻaki viḷiyāṭukiṟārkaḷ. ataṉ mēl atil piriyamuḷḷa cilar periyavarkaḷākumpoḻutu, irupakkamum tōl kaṭṭiya mattaḷam mirutaṅkam pēri uṭukki nakarā tapilā pōṉṟa koṭṭuṅ karuvikaḷicceytu, kaṭippiṉāl aṭittut tāḷattil viruttiyāṉārkaḷ. tāḷa ñāṉam mutal mutal ellārukkum iyalpāy amintirukkiṟaJ. kuḻantikaḷ piṟantu nālintu mātattiṟkuḷ caṅkītattik kēṭkumpōtu tāḷattuṭaṉ cāyntāṭuvati nām aṟivōm. mēlum ovvoruvaruṭiya cuvācamum irattācayattuṭippum, kālappiramāṇatti yuṭiya tākavē yirukkiṟatu.
itupōlavē, teruvil viḷiyāṭum ciṟuvarkaḷ, pūvaracu pōṉṟa marattiṉ ilikaḷic curuṭṭi, atil ciṟutta oru pākattik kiyiṉāl amukki, oṭuṅkiya tuvāramirukkumpaṭi ceytu atil ūtiyum, ataṉpiṉ kuḻaliṉ parumaṉukkum ataṉ vāykkum takunta vitamāyc cattam piṟakkiṟateṉṟu kaṇṭu, kīḻ mēlāyuḷḷa iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu nālu cattaṅkaḷic cērttum atil oṟṟumi kaṇṭu cantōṣittārkaḷ. piṉpu tuvāramuḷḷa pūcaṇi ilikkāmpukaḷi kuḻalkaḷāka naṟukki avikaḷiṉ oru pakkattil ilik kuḻalkaḷi vittu ūta, kuḻalkaḷ evvaḷavu nīḷukiṟatō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu cattam mantamākiṟateṉṟum, kuḻalkaḷ kuṟukakkuṟukat toṉiyuḷḷa nātam piṟakkiṟateṉṟum kaṇṭu, ēkōpittu āṉantak kūttāṭiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil iṉṉiciyil piriyaṅkoṇṭa cilar, periyavarkaḷāṉapoḻutu kompu caṅku mūṅkil īrakkaḻi nāṇal mutaliyavikaḷil tuḷiyiṭṭum, maram poṉ veḷḷi pittaḷi mutaliyavaṟṟil pala vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kuḻalceytu tuḷiyiṭṭum, iṉṉici piṟakka ūtiṉārkaḷ. itupōlavē, narampuk karuvikaḷilum paṭippaṭiyāy viruttiyāṉārkaḷ. vaḷiyakkūṭiya oru kucciyil, melliya kayiṟu allatu narampukaḷik kaṭṭi mīṭṭa, ōci piṟappati aṟintārkaḷ. atiṉiṉṟu periya mūṅkilkaḷi vil pōlum vaḷittut tōl vaṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi, kuṇattoṉiceytu maṇikaḷiyum cataṅkikaḷiyum kōttut tāḷattiṟku iṇaṅkak kuṟukiya kaṭippiṉāl aṭittu, villaṭippāṭṭukaḷ paṭittārkaḷ. tantikaḷ allatu narampukaḷ, ākāyamaṭaṅkiya oru pāttiram peṭṭi allatu curik kuṭukki vaḻiyāyc cellumpoḻutu, atika nātam koṭukkiṟa teṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāyk kaṇṭu, tuntiṉāmā, oru tantic curikkāykkiṉṉari pōlotta vāttiyaṅkaḷ ceytu, taṅkaḷ cārīrattiṟkēṟṟa curuti vittuk koṇṭu, pakavāṉuṭiya kuṇaṅkaḷi varṇittup pāṭiṉārkaḷ. kikaḷāl mīṭṭuvataṟkup patil, villukaḷāl tantikaḷi iḻuttu vācikkakkūṭiya akappikkiṉṉarikaḷ pōṉṟavi ceytu vācittārkaḷ. piṟaku māṉiṭa cārīrattiṟku ottatum curastāṉaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭatumāṉa mikac ciṟappup poruntiya vīṇi ceytārkaḷ. ataṉiṉṟum caṅkītam, ataṟkuriya cila vitikaḷuṭaṉ viruttiyākikkoṇṭē varukiṟatu.
ciṟuvarkaḷ taṭṭi viḷiyāṭum palakik kataviṉ ōciyiliruntu, tampaṭṭam pēriki mattaḷam mirutaṅkam tapilā uṭukki kittāḷam cēmakkalam callari maṇi cataṅki mutaliya koṭṭuṅ karuvikaḷum, ūti viḷiyāṭiya ilikkuḻalkaḷiliruntu, tāri ottu nākacuram mukavīṇi makaṭi pullāṅkuḻal kompu caṅku mutaliya tuḷikkaruvikaḷum, palliṉāl kaṭittu kiyiṉāl piṭittiḻuttu oru kiyiṉāl mīṭṭip piṟanta iṉiya nātattiliruntu, tuntiṉāmā kiṉṉari tampuru akappikkiṉṉari cuntari vīṇi ruttiravīṇi pēriyāḻ makarayāḻ mutaliya mīṭṭum tanti vāttiyaṅkaḷum ciṟuvar viḷiyāṭṭiliruntē tōṉṟi, paṭippaṭiyāy viruttiyāyiṉaveṉṟu nām aṟikiṟōm. nātaṉatu tiruviḷiyāṭṭi eṭuttukkūṟat tiṟamiyuṭiyōr yāvar!
mēṟkūṟiya vāttiyaṅkaḷ, tēcattāriṉ kittoḻil tiṟamikkut takuntapaṭi, vevvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷiyum pāṣiyāl vevvēṟu peyarkaḷiyum peṟuvatēyaṉṟi, kāriyattil caṅkītattiṟkut tuṇik karuvikaḷākavē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāṉa vitiyiṉ arumi paṟṟiyum, vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ utavipaṟṟiyum, ovvoru tēcattārum, tāṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkum kāṉattit teyvamē koṭuttāreṉṟum, upatēcittāreṉṟum, tāmē ceytu kāṭṭiṉāreṉṟum mēṉmi pārāṭṭun takutiyuṭiyatā yirukkiṟatu. tiṉi aḷavu paṉittuḷi, etirniṟkum maliyiyum veku tiṭṭamākat taṉakkuḷ piratipimpittuk kāṭṭi niṟpatu pōla, ovvoru tēcattiṉ kāṉamum, antantat tēcattār maṉatip paravacappaṭutti, tēṭarum tiraviyakkuṉṟākiya teyvattiyum taṉṉiṟkāṭṭa caktiyuṭiyatā yirukkiṟatu.
“ātiyilē vārtti (nātam) iruntatu, anta vārtti tēvaṉiṭatti liruntatu, anta vārtti (nātam) tēvaṉāyiruntaJ. avar ātiyilē tēvaṉōṭu iruntār. cakalamum avar mūlamāy uṇṭāyiṟṟu; uṇṭāṉa toṉṟum avariyallāmal uṇṭākavilli. avarukkuḷ jīvaṉiruntatu, anta jīvaṉ maṉitarukku oḷiyāyiruntaJ. ulakattilē vantu enta maṉitaṉiyum pirācippikkiṟa oḷiyē anta meyyāṉa oḷi. avar ulakattil iruntār, ulakam avarmūlamāy uṇṭāyiṟṟu, ulakamō avari aṟiyavilli. inta vārtti (nātam) māmicamākik kirupiyiṉālum cattiyattiṉālum niṟintavarāy namakkuḷ vācam paṇṇiṉār.” (yōvāṉ 1 - 1 mutal)
ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ātikāraṇamāyirunta nātattiṉāl ulakam paṭippaṭiyāy cūṭcuma tūla tōṟṟattiyaṭintaJ. tūla cūṭcama kāraṇa meṉṉum munnilikaḷiyum aṭinta jīvarkaḷuṭiya uḷḷattiliruntu, avarkaḷ karuttit terivikkac cūṭcamamāṉa palapala iṉiya toṉikaḷum, tūlamāṉa palapala apinayaṅkaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa. karuttit terivikkum toṉikaḷum apinayaṅkaḷum, kālakkiramattil pāṣiyāki varivaṭivamāy eḻutappaṭṭu, nūlkaḷum cāstiraṅkaḷum kalikaḷumāyiṉa. tāṅkaḷ mikavum arumiyāka niṉittavikaḷum, vāḻiyaṭivāḻiyāy vanta parampariyārukkup pāṭam collivittu vantavikaḷumāṉa teyva tōttiraṅkaḷiyē, mutal mutal eḻutivittārkaḷ. atiṉālēyē ataṟku mutal nūl eṉṟu peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kātiṉālēyē kēṭṭup pāṭamākkikkoṇṭiruntakālattil, atic curuti eṉṟārkaḷ. mutal nūl uṇṭāṉapiṉ, ataṉ uṭporuḷi aṟivataṟku ētuvāṉa upāyaṅkaḷiyum upaniṭataṅkaḷiyum āṟu cāstiraṅkaḷiyum aṟupattunālu kalikaḷiyum, avikaḷi nuṭpamāy viḷakkuvataṟku ētuvāka itikācaṅkaḷiyum purāṇaṅ kaḷiyum ceytārkaḷ. teyva paktarkaḷiṉ irutayamākiya kaḷañciyatti liruntu varum ceyyuḷkaḷil maṟintirukkum karuttukaḷ, uḷḷapaṭi arttamāvatu kūṭiyatallavē. nātap piramattiyē kītamāyt tōttirikkum vētam eppaṭi mukkiyamāṉatō, appaṭiyē nātappiramattiyē vistarikkum caṅkīta cāstiramākiya kāntarva vētamum mukkiyamāṉaJ. vētattiṉ upāṅkamākiya kāntarvavētatti allatu caṅkītatti, kilācattil niruti mūliyil niruttam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ parama civaṉ upatēcittatāka iṉṉum collappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. immutalnūlukkuppiṉ, anēkavaḻi nūlkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
    iti vācikkum kaṉavāṉkaḷē! iṟṟikku aṉēka āyira varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷatāka eṇṇappaṭum mutal nūlil, atāvatu, cāma vētattil, iṉṟu nām ellā vāttiyaṅkaḷilum iṉṉiciyuṭiyateṉṟu matikkum vīṇiyum, ataṉ ceymuṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭiruppatumaṉṟi atik koṇṭē pakavāṉit tutikkavēṇṭumeṉṟum colliyirukkumāṉāl, intiya caṅkītattiṉ pūrvīkatti eṉṉeṉṟu colvatu carasvati nāratar tumpuru anumāṉ mutaliya periyōrkaḷ, taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīṇiyik koṇṭu iṭiviṭāmal teyvattit tutittuk koṇṭirukkiṟār kaḷeṉṟum, paramacivaṉ tamatu irukātilum kampalar acuvatarar eṉṉum viṇīka cirōmaṇikaḷik kuṇṭalamākat tarittukkoṇṭu avarkaḷ kāṉattic catā kēṭṭukkoṇṭirukkiṟāreṉṟum collappaṭumāṉāl, vīṇākāṉattiṉ mēṉmiyiyum caṅkītattiṉ pūrvattiyum nitāṉikkattakka vallavar yāvar?
aruḷ nātaṉ ulakil avataritta camayattil, tēva cēṉikaḷ vāṉattil tōṉṟi, pūmiyiluḷḷōr kēṭkumpaṭi “uṉṉataṅkaḷilirukkiṟa tēvaṉukku makimiyum pūmiyilē camātāṉamum maṉuṣarmēl piriyamu muṇṭāvatāka” veṉṟu pāṭiṉārkaḷāṉāl, caṅkītattiṉ upayōkamum upayōkikkum camayamum evvaḷavu mēlāṉaviyeṉṟu kāṇkiṟōm. mēlum, tēvalōkattil tēvācaṉattiṉmuṉ cakala paricuttavāṉkaḷum tēvatūtarkaḷum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ curamaṇṭalaṅkaḷi vācittukkoṇṭu, putuppāṭṭic catā pāṭukiṟatākavum, “cēṉikaḷiṉ karttar paricuttar, paricuttar, paricuttar; vāṉamum pūmiyum avaruṭiya makimiyāl niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa” veṉṟu muḻaṅkukiṟatākavum collappaṭumāṉāl, immi maṟumi eṉṉum irunilikaḷilum caṅkītattiṉ mukkiyatti eṭuttuc collavum vēṇṭumō? immukkiyamāṉa viṣayattil, atāvatu, caṅkītattiṉ mēṉmiyiyum ataṉ pūrvattiyum collavēṇṭiya viṣayattil, teyvattiṉ nittiya kālattiyum, pūmi uṇṭāṉa kālamutal ituvariyum ceṉṟa nāṭkaḷiyumē ataṟkuk kālamākavum, mōṭca pirāptiyiyē atu tarum mēṉmiyākavum collak kūṭiyatēyaṉṟi, vēṟu ciṟappuccolla iyalāta mēmpāṭuṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.
mēlum, periyōrkaḷāl collappaṭṭa paṟpala pākkaḷum, pakavāṉit tutikkum pākkaḷum, iṉṉicikalantapiṉpē, maṉatip paravacappaṭuttak kūṭiyavikaḷā yirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉpati nāmellārum aṟivōm. pūmiyil maṟintukiṭakkum vittukkaḷ maḻiyiṉāl muḷittut taḻittup pūttuk kāyttu naṟpalaṉittaruvatupōla, muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ pākkaḷ yāvum, iṉṉici kalantapiṉpē uyarvāṉa arttaṅkaḷi maṉatiṟkup pulappaṭutti āṉantam aṭivikkiṟaJ.
	“kallēṉum aiya orukālattil urukum eṉkaṉṉeñcam urukaviliyē.”
	“nāṭakattāl uṉṉaṭiyārpōl naṭitJ.”
eṉṟa pāṭṭukaḷ oru irākattil vevvēṟu vitamāṉa ālāpaṉam ceyyappaṭum pōtu avaṟṟil aṭaṅkiya karuttukaḷ ettaṉiyō, attaṉiyum maṉatukkup pulappaṭum. aṉṟiyum, iṉṉiciyāṉatu, maṉavaruttam tīrttuc camātāṉattittarum; carīra tuṉpattiyum pōkkaṭittu iḷippāṟutal tarum; vēliyiṉkaṣṭam tōṉṟātapaṭi uṟcākam ceytuvikkum. iravil kaṇviḻittu irāṭṭiṉam nūṟkum stirīkaḷ, taṅkaḷ varuttamtīra iṉṉiciyōṭu pakavāṉit tutikkiṟārkaḷ. ēṟṟam iṟikkum camucāriyum, mā arikkum stiriyum, ōṭamtaḷḷum ōṭak kāraṉum, nāṟṟunaṭum peṇkaḷum, cāntu iṭikkumkūlikaḷum, pakavāṉit tutittup pāṭit taṅkaḷ varuttatti maṟantu, taṅkaḷ vāḻnāṭkaḷi veku ullācamāyk kaḻikkiṟārkaḷ. kappari ēnti yācikkum piccikkāraṉum cēmakkalaṅkoṭṭi yācikkum tātaṉum, upātāṉam vāṅkum piramacāriyum, aṉṉamyācikkum aṉṉak kāvaṭikkāraṉum ekkālakkaṇṇi, parāparak kaṇṇi, uṭaṟkūṟu, neñcaṟiviḷakkam mutaliya ñāṉappāṭalkaḷi iṉiya paṇṇōṭu paṭittut taṅkaḷ nāṭkaḷik kaḻikkiṟārkaḷ. tāyiṉ iṉiya tālāṭṭup pāṭṭik kēṭṭut tūṅkum kuḻanti iṉṉum kēṭka virumpi ūmkoṭṭukiṟaJ. kaṉṟukkup pālkoṭāta muraṭṭup pacukkaḷ, kītāri pāṭum icikku vacappaṭṭup pūraṇamākap pālkoṭukkiṟaJ. piṟar kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭāmal maṟintu vacikkum nākappāmpum, pāmpāṭṭikaḷ ūtum makaṭiyiṉ iṉiya nātattikkēṭṭut taṉṉi maṟantu āṉantattiṉāl paṭamvirittāṭukiṟaJ. kītāriyiṉ iṉiya pullāṅkuḻal ōciyikkēṭṭu, māṭukaḷ piṉ cellukiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭāḷattil vācikkum vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ ōciyāl, pōrvīrarkaḷ uṟcākamaṭikiṟārkaḷ. kutirikaḷ, vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ tāḷaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavitamāy kālkaḷ vittu naṭantu veku tīviramāy muṉṉēṟic cellukiṉṟaṉa. iṉiya ōciyuṭiya paṭcikaḷ, atikāliyil viḻittuppāṭuvatiyum, atē kālattil kōvilkaḷilum rāja araṇmaṉi kaḷilum caṅkīta vāttiyaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkuvatiyum, maṇikaḷ aṭippatiyum, nām kēṭkiṟōm. māliyilum appaṭiyē nām yāvarum pakavāṉi iṉṉiciyuṭaṉ ārātikkiṟōm. ippaṭi, palavitattilum maṉuṣaṉukkuc cantōṣattit taruvataṟkuc caṅkītattiviṭac ciṟantatu vēṟoṉṟu milli. caṅkītamē, ivvulakattilum maṟu ulakattilum mēṉmiyuṭiyaJ. caṅkītattiṉāl tutippatē nammi uṇṭākkiṉa karttaṉukkup piriyamāṉaJ. nāratar tumpuru anumār irāvaṇaṉ tāvītaracaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷ caṅkītattik koṇṭē teyvattiṉ kirupiyip peṟṟārkaḷ.
caṅkītamāṉatu maṉatic cāntappaṭutti, teyvattōṭu oṟṟumippaṭac ceykiṟaJ. caṅkītamāṉatu cakala naṟkuṇaṅkaḷiyum vaḷarttut teyvapatam peṟac ceykiṟaJ. immēmpāṭuṭiya caṅkītatti appiyācikkum jaṉaṅkaḷ evarkaḷō, avarkaḷi atu uyartti avarkaḷirukkum tēcatti mēṉmip paṭuttic cakala kalikaḷilum celvattilum teyvapaktiyilum virutti yaṭiyacceykiṟaJ. rājaṉum kuṭikaḷum caṅkītatti mēlāṉatāka matittu appiyācippārkaḷāṉāl, avarkaḷit tēvarkaḷeṉṟum tēvajaṉaṅka ḷeṉṟum, avarkaḷ kuṭiyirukkumiṭattit tēvapūmiyeṉṟum, maṟṟavarkaḷ koṇṭāṭum uṉṉata nilikkuḷḷākac ceykiṟaJ. caṅkītattikkoṇṭu teyvatti ārātikkum uttama aracarkaḷ, teyva piramāṇaṅkaḷ kaṭukaḷavum picakāmal, puliyum pacuvum oru tuṟiyil taṇṇīr kuṭikka, maṉṉuyir yāvum taṉṉuyirāka niṉittup paripālaṉamceytu, nīti irakkam poṟumi aṉpu camātāṉam mutaliya uttama kuṇaṅkaḷōṭu kuṭikaḷi naṭatti, ivvulakattilēyē mōṭcāṉantatti nili nāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. ivvaḷavu mēmpāṭuṭiya caṅkītatti aṟpamāka niṉittulukika vaḻikaḷil upayōkappaṭuttukiṟavarkaḷ puṉṉeṟiyaṭintu maṟintu pōvārkaḷ eṉpatu niccayam. teyvattit tutippatiyē mutaṉmiyākakkoṇṭa nam muṉṉōr caṅkīta cāstirattippaṟṟi mika virivāka eḻutiyiruntārka ḷeṉṟālum, eḻutappaṭṭavikaḷ ikkālattil perumpālum aḻintum nūtaṉamāṉavikaḷ uṇṭākiyum palatappaṟikaḷ kalantum iruppataṉāl, teḷivāy aṟintu koḷvataṟku aritāyirukkiṟatu. caṅkītam uṟpattiyāṉa kālam iṭam ātarittu vaḷarttavarkaḷiṉ peyar mutaliyavikaḷit tiṭṭamāy aṟintu koḷvatu ilēcāṉa kāriyamalla. eṉṟālum, oruvāṟu ulakattavar vaḻakkattilum paḻakkattilum muṉ nūlkaḷilum vaḻaṅkivarum cila kāriyaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu, nām aṟiyakkūṭiya cila ātāraṅkaḷip pārppatu, oruvāṟu namakkut tiruptiyit tarumeṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
II. caṅkītam pūrvamāyuḷḷateṉpaṟkuc cattiyavēta ātāramum akkālattil vaḻaṅki vanta caṅkīta vāttiyaṅkaḷum.
1. jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉ kiṉṉaramum nākacuramum
iruntaṉa veṉpaJ.
teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ kālatti nām pārppataṟkumuṉ, itaṟku appuṟamāyirunta tēcaṅkaḷil oruvāṟu niccayikkakkūṭiyaviyum carittira ārāyccikkārar oppuk koḷḷakkūṭiyaviyumākiya cattiya vētattil uḷḷa cila ātāraṅkaḷip pārppōm: iṟṟikkuc caṟṟēṟak kuṟiya 3400 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta mōcē muṉivarāl eḻutappaṭṭa ātiyākamam 4-m atikāram 20, 21, 22-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil
“ātāḷ yāpālip peṟṟāḷ. avaṉ kūṭāraṅkaḷil vācam paṇṇukiṟavarkaḷukkum manti mēykkiṟavarkaḷukkum takappaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ cakōtaraṉuṭiya peyar yūpāl. avaṉ kiṉṉarakkārar nākacurakkārar yāvarukkum takappaṉāṉāṉ. cillāḷum tūpāl kāyīṉip peṟṟāḷ. avaṉ pittaḷi irumpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ toḻilāḷar yāvarukkum ācāriyaṉāṉāṉ.”
eṉṟu eḻutiyirukkiṟatu. kiṉṉarakkārarukkum nākacurakkārarukkum tantiyākiya yūpālum, pittaḷi irumpu mutaliya lōkaṅkaḷil vēli ceyyum toḻilāḷikaḷukkut tantiyākiya tūpālkāyīṉum kiṟistu piṟantataṟkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 4000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉiruntatāka vētacāstirikaḷ kaṇittirukkiṟārkaḷ. kiṟistuvukkup piṉṉuḷḷa 1914 varuṣaṅkaḷiyum atōṭu cērttup pārppōmēyāṉāl iṟṟikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 5900 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷatākum. ivarkaḷ irunta kālattiṟkuc cumār 1650 varuṣaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ jalappiraḷayam vantatākat terikiṟatu. jalappiraḷaya kālattiṟku muṉṉālēyē ciṉṉa āciyāviluḷḷa pūrvattār, caṅkīta vittiyilum maṟṟum kittoḻilkaḷilum pēr pōṉavarkaḷāy iruntārkaḷeṉṟu veku tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu.
mēlum kāyīṉ oru paṭṭaṇattikkaṭṭi ataṟkut taṉ kumāraṉākiya ēṉōkkuṭiya peyariyiṭṭāṉeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. akkālattil rākṣatar pūmiyiliruntārkaḷeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟa maṉuṣarākiya palavāṉkaḷ 969, 962, 900, 800, 700 mutaliya varuṣam varikkum nīṭitta āyuḷuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum, aṅkē collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivarkaḷ jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉiruntavarkaḷ. itupōlavē, jalap piraḷayattiṟkumuṉ intiyāvilum, nīṇṭa āyuḷ peṟṟa maṉitarkaḷum irākṣatarkaḷum pirapalamāṉa paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷum aracarkaḷum kiṉṉaram nākacuram vīṇi pullāṅkuḻal tāḷam apinayam mutaliyavikaḷippaṟṟic collum caṅkīta cāstiramumiruntaṉaveṉṟu itaṉpiṉ pārppōm.
2. mōcē muṉivariṉ pāṭṭum, miriyāmiṉ naṭaṉamum
tampurōṭu kūṭiya pāṭṭum
jalappiraḷayattiṟkup piṉpu, isravēlarkaḷ ekiptiviṭṭup puṟap paṭukiyil ekiptiyar ceṅkaṭalil amiḻntapōtu, catturupayam nīkkit taṅkaḷik kāttaṟkāka mōcē muṉivarum isravēl puttirarum, karttarip pukaḻntu pāṭiṉārkaḷeṉṟu yāttirākamam 15-m atikāram mutalāvatu vākkiyattilum,
“ārōṉiṉ cakōtariyākiya miriyām eṉṉum tīrkkat tariciyāṉavaḷum taṉ kiyilē tampuri eṭuttukkoṇṭāḷ. cakala stirīkaḷum tampurukaḷōṭum naṭaṉattōṭum avaḷukkup piṉceṉṟu karttarip pāṭiṉārkaḷ”
eṉṟu atē atikāram 20-m vacaṉattilum colliyiruppatip pārkkalām. avarkaḷ pāṭiya vacaṉaṅkaḷip pārkkiyil, taṟkālattiṉ nākarīkattilum mēlāṉatākavē teyvattip pukaḻntu tutittirukkiṟārka ḷeṉṟu aṟiyalām. avarkaḷ pāṭṭuppāṭap paḻakiyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum stirīkaḷ aṉēkar tampurōṭu pāṭi naṭaṉam paṇṇiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum colvatik kavaṉikkiyil, iṉṉiciyittarum tampuru eṉṉum vāttiyam anēkamiruntatākavum atōṭu pāṭap paḻakiyiruntatākavum tāṅkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭṭukkiṇaṅka naṭaṉampaṇṇa stirīkaḷum paḻakiyirunta tākavum aṟikiṟōm. itu iṉṟikkuc cumār 3400 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ eṉṟu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu.
3. teporāḷiṉ pāṭṭu
ataṉpiṉ niyāyātipatikaḷ 5-m atikārattil, lapitōttiṉ maṉiviyāki teporāḷ eṉṉum tīrkkatarici, 900 iruppu rataṅkaḷōṭu etirttu vanta cicerāviṉmēl taṉakku veṟṟikiṭitta camayattil, teyvattit tutittup pāṭiṉatākac collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. avaḷ pāṭiya vacaṉaṅkaḷi avvintām atikārattil muḻutum kāṇalām. appāṭṭu oru yuttattiyum atil palavāṉkaḷkāṭṭiya parākkiramattiyum, yuttam naṭanta iṭattiyum catturukkaḷ aṭinta tōlviyiyum tāṉ aṭinta veṟṟiyiyum, nēriṟ kaṇṭatu pōl yāvarum teḷivāy aṟintukoḷḷak kūṭiya vitamāy, mikavum alaṅkāramāy eḻutap paṭṭirukkiṟaJ. oru stirī mikunta karppaṉālaṅkārattuṭaṉ kavi ceytiruppatip pārttāl akkālatti lēyē cākittiyam ceyyavum alaṅkāramāyp pāṭavum kūṭiyavarkaḷāyp pūrvattār iruntirukkiṟārkaḷeṉṟu terikiṟatu. atu iṟṟikku 3200 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟu aṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
4. yeptāviṉ makaḷ naṭaṉamum tampurōṭu kūṭiyapāṭṭum
ataṉpiṉ niyāyātipatikaḷ 11-m atikāram 34-m vākkiyattil, yeptā eṉṉum niyāyātipati, ammōṉ puttirari muṟiyaṭittu veṟṟivēntaṉāyt taṉ vīṭṭukkut tirumpivarukiyil, avaṉ kumāratti tampuru vācittu naṭaṉamceytu avaṉukku etir koṇṭuvantāḷ eṉṟu collap paṭṭirukkiṟaJ. atu iṟṟikkuc caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 3070 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eṉṟu aṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
5. tāvītu rājāviṉ caṅkītamum paktiyum naṭaṉamum avar
kālattil vaḻaṅki vanta vāttiya vakikaḷum
i cāmuvēl 16-m atikāram 23-m vākkiyattil
“oru pollāta āvi cavulippiṭikkumpōtu tāvītu cura maṇṭalattiyeṭuttu, taṉ kiyiṉāl vācippāṉ; atiṉālē pollāta āvi avaṉi viṭṭu nīṅka, cavul āṟutal aṭintu costamāvāṉ.”
eṉpatik kavaṉikkiyil pollāta āvikaḷiṉ cēṣṭi caṅkītattāl cukkiyamākumeṉṟu ataṉ muṉvākkiyaṅkaḷil colli yirukkiṟatiyum appaṭiyē cukkiyamaṭintatiyum nām kāṇkiṟōm. itil collappaṭum tāvītu eṉpavar, caṅkītattil mikavum tērntavar. cākittiyam ceyvatilum atic curamaṇṭalam vīṇi mutaliya vāttiyaṅ kaḷil vācippatilum, vitamvitamāṉa vāttiyakkaruvikaḷ uṇṭākkuvatilum kitērntavar. teyvattiṉiṭattil mikunta paktiyuḷḷavar. teyvacamūkattil pāṭuvatum naṭaṉampaṇṇuvatum maṟṟavarkaḷiyum appaṭippāṭiyāṭac ceyvatum avarukku mikavum piriyam. 2 cāmuvēl 6-m atikāram 5, 14, 15-m vākkiyaṅkaḷ:
“tāvītu isravēl cantatiyār aṉivarum tēvatāru marattāl paṇṇappaṭṭa cakalavita kītavāttiyaṅkaḷōṭum curamaṇṭalam tampuru, mēḷam, vīṇi, kittāḷam ākiya ivikaḷōṭum karttarukku muṉpāka āṭip pāṭikkoṇṭu pōṉārkaḷ. tāvītu caṇal nūl ēpōttit tarittuk koṇṭu taṉ muḻuppalattōṭum karttarukku muṉpāka naṭaṉam paṇṇiṉāṉ. appaṭiyē tāvītum isravēl cantatiyār aṉivarum karttaruṭiya peṭṭiyik kempīra cattattōṭum ekkāḷa toṉiyōṭum koṇṭu vantārkaḷ.”
mēl vākkiyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu, caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya iṟṟikku 3000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉē, cakalavitamāṉa vāttiyaṅkaḷum naṭaṉamum caṅkītattōṭu cērntu tēvacannitiyil upayōkikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu nām kāṇkiṟōm. mēṟkāṭṭiya caṅkītakkāraṉākiya tāvītu, isravēl jaṉaṅkaḷiṉ oru mukkiyamāṉa rājaṉ eṉṟum, caṅkītamē uruvāka avatarittavaṉeṉṟum nām aṟiyavēṇṭum. ivvācaṉ eḻutiya caṅkītaṅkaḷi, nām cattiya vētattil mikavum vistāramākap pārkkalām. avikaḷil, oru uttama paktaṉatu neñcattiṉ kaṉivum, teyvattil avaṉ vittirukkum uṟutiyum, avaṉ maṉanērmiyum mikatteḷivāy viḷaṅkukiṟaJ. avvaḷavu makimi poruntiya pāṭakarkaḷ taṟkālattil kiṭippatu ariJ. avar eḻutiya caṅkītaṅkaḷ, avikaḷi vācikkum uṇmiyāṉa paktarkaḷukku, avaravarkaḷ camayaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavikaḷāyp putitu putitāṉa karuttukaḷi maṉatil uṇṭākkakkūṭiya vitamāy amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. uṇmiyāṉa oru paktaṉ, tāṉ putitāka teyvattit tutikkac cila vārttikaḷ colla ārampippāṉēyāṉāl, av vārttikaḷ tāvītu aracaṉ caṅkītattil irukkiṟatākak kāṇpāṉ. avvaḷavu mēṉmi poruntiya paktaṉākiya tāvītu aracaṉ tām eḻutiya 92-m caṅkītam 1, 2, 3-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil
“karttarit tutippatum , uṉṉatamāṉavarē, umatu nāmattik kīrttaṉam paṇṇuvatum, pattu narampu vīṇiyiṉālum tampuruviṉālum, tiyāṉattōṭu vācikkum curamaṇṭalattiṉālum, kāliyilē umatu kirupiyiyum iravilē umatu cattiyattiyum aṟivippatum nalamāyirukkum.”
eṉṟum, 150-m caṅkītattil
“allēlūyā, tēvaṉi avaruṭiya paricutta stalattil tutiyuṅkaḷ; avaruṭiya vallami viḷaṅkum ākāya virivip pārttu avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ. avaruṭiya vallamiyuḷḷa kiriyikaḷukkāka avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ; māṭcimi poruntiya avaruṭiya makattuvattiṟkāka avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ. ekkāḷa toṉiyōṭē avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ; vīṇiyōṭum curamaṇṭalattōṭum avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ. tampurōṭum naṭaṉattōṭum avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ; yāḻōṭum tīṅkuḻalōṭum avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ. ōciyuḷḷa kittāḷaṅkaḷōṭum avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ; pērōciyuḷḷa kittāḷaṅkaḷōṭum avarit tutiyuṅkaḷ. cuvācamuḷḷa yāvum karttarit tutippatāka. allēlūyā.”
eṉṟum pāṭiyirukkiṟatik kavaṉippōmāṉāl, avar kālattiluḷḷa caṅkītattiṉ uyarviyum vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ mikutiyiyum avikaḷ yāviyum koṇṭu teyvattiyē pakticeytā reṉpatiyum nām kāṇalām. ivvuttamaṉē tēva cannitiyil pāṭakarkaḷip palapākaṅkaḷākap pirittu, irākat talivarkaḷi ēṟpaṭutti, muṟi muṟiyāyp pala vāttiyaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu teyvattit tutikkumpaṭi mutal mutal niyamittavar. ivar puttiraṉākiya cālomōṉ aracaṉum mikunta ñāṉamuṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ.
6. cālomōṉ rājaṉ caṅkītamum 288 pāṭakarkaḷi
ālayattil niyamittatum.
i. irājākkaḷ 4. 32, 33-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil.
“avaṉ mūvāyiram nītimoḻikaḷic coṉṉāṉ; avaṉuṭiya pāṭṭukaḷ āyirattu ainJ. līpaṉōṉilirukkiṟa kēturu maraṅkaḷ mutaṟkoṇṭu kavaril muḷikkiṟa īcōppup pūṇṭu varikkumuḷḷa maram mutaliya tāparaṅkaḷik kuṟittum mirukaṅkaḷ paṟavikaḷ ūrum pirāṇikaḷ maccaṅkaḷ ākiya ivikaḷik kuṟittum vākkiyaṅkaḷic coṉṉāṉ.”
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatik kāṇalām. inta ñāṉamuḷḷa aracaṉum, taṉ takappaṉip pōlavē tēvālayattil caṅkīta vāttiyattōṭu kaṭavuḷit tutittu ārātittuvantāṉ. tāṉ kaṭṭiṉa makimiporuntiya tēvālayattil muṟimuṟiyāyk kīrttaṉampāṭattiṭṭam ceytiruntāṉ eṉṟu 1nāḷākamam 25, 6, 7-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇpōm. atāvatu,
“ivarkaḷ aṉivarum rājāvuṭiya kaṭṭaḷippiramāṇamāy karttaruṭiya ālayattil tāḷaṅkaḷ tampurukaḷ curamaṇṭalaṅkaḷākiya kītavāttiyam vācikka, tēvaṉuṭiya ālayattiṉ ūḻiyamāka avaravar taṅkaḷtaṅkaḷ takappaṉmārākiya ācāp, etuttūṉ, ēmāṉ eṉpavarkaḷ vacattil iruntārkaḷ. karttarip pāṭum pāṭṭukaḷik kaṟṟukkoṇṭu, nipuṇarāṉa taṅkaḷ cakōtararōṭuṅkūṭa avarkaḷ ilakkattiṟku irunūṟṟeṇpatteṭṭup pērāyiruntārkaḷ.”
inta irunūṟṟeṇpatteṭṭup pērum ovvoru vakuppukkup paṉṉiraṇṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu pērāka 24 pākaṅkaḷākkappaṭṭu, anti canti mattiyāṉaṅkaḷil ovvorunāḷum taṅkaḷtaṅkaḷ muṟippaṭi tēvālayattil kāṉam ceytuvantārkaḷ. ivvaḷavu ērāḷamāṉa pāṭakarkaḷ taṅkaḷtaṅkaḷ muṟippaṭi ālayattil kāṉam ceytu vantatippōla, ulakattil vēṟu enta rājjiyattilāvatu enta ālayattilāvatu naṭantu vantateṉṟu nām ituvariyum kēḷvippaṭṭatilli. itu caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya iṟṟikku 2930 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉṉeṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭirukkiṟaJ.
7. pāpilōṉil nēpukātnēccār niṟuttiya poṟciliyum
ataṉ muṉ vācikkappaṭṭa vāttiyak karuvikaḷum.
pāpilōṉ eṉṉum kaltēyar tali nakaril aracāṭci ceytu vanta nēpukātnēccār eṉṉum rājaṉ, 60 muḻa uyaramum 6 muḻu akalamumāṉa oru poṟ ciliyip paṇṇuvittu, pāpilōṉ mākāṇattilirukkiṟa tūrā eṉṉum camapūmiyil niṟutti, acciliyiṉ piratiṣṭikkuc cakalarum varumpaṭi aḻittiruntāṉ eṉṟu tāṉiyēl tīrkkataricaṉam 3-m atikārattiṉ tuvakkattil nām pārkkalām. avvatikārattiṉ 4-m 5-m vākkiyaṅkaḷil.
“kaṭṭiyakkāraṉ uratta cattamāy: cakala jaṉaṅkaḷum, jātikaḷum, pāṣikkārarumāṉavarkaḷē, uṅkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭukiṟatu eṉṉa veṉṟāl: ekkāḷam, nākacuram,kiṉṉaram, vīṇi, curamaṇṭalam tampuru mutalāṉa cakalavita kīta vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ cattatti nīṅkaḷ kēṭkumpoḻutu, nīṅkaḷ tāḻa viḻuntu, rājāvākiya nēpucātnēccār niṟuttiṉa poṟ ciliyip paṇintukoḷḷakkaṭavīrkaḷ.* * * eṉṟāṉ.” 
ivvacaṉatti nām kavaṉikkiyil, kītavāttiyaṅkaḷ ātikālattil eppaṭi upayōkikkappaṭṭu vantaṉaveṉpatit tiṭṭamāyk kāṇkiṟōm.
8. pāpilōṉ araṇmaṉiyil kāli māli mattiyāṉaṅkaḷil
caṅkīta vāttiyaṅkaḷ vācikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu.
tāṉiyēl 6-m atikāram 18-m vākkiyattil
“piṉpu rājā taṉ araṇmaṉikkuppōy, irā muḻuvatum pōjaṉam paṇṇāmalum, kītavāttiyam mutalāṉavikaḷit taṉakku muṉpāka varavoṭṭāmalumiruntāṉ; avaṉukku nittiriyum varāmal pōyiṟṟu.”
eṉṟu colliyirukkiṟatik kāṇpōm. itaṉāl, rājākkaḷ kālikaḷil tāṅkaḷ tūṅki eḻuntirukkumpoḻutum, cāyantaram vēli yoḻintu ullācamāyirukkum kālattilum, irāttiri pōjaṉam paṇṇiṉa piṉpum, araṇmaṉiyil vāttiyaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkumpaṭi ceyvatu vaḻakkamāyiruntatākat terikiṟatu. taṟkālattilum itik kāṇalām. ivvaḻakkam, iṉṟu nēṟṟalla, cumār 2450 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa teṉṟu cattiyavētattil collappaṭukiṟa kaṇakkiṉāl teriyavarukiṟatu. ippaṭic caṅkītatti rāja araṇmaṉikaḷil upayōkittu vanta pāpilōṉ eṉṉum periya nakarippaṟṟic cila kāriyaṅkaḷi oruvāṟu kavaṉippatu, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ pūrva niliyi vicārikkum namakkup pirayōjaṉamāyirukkumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
9. pāpilōṉum niṉivēyum avikaḷic cērnta paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷum nimirōt eṉpavaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaJ.
pāpilōṉ niṉivē eṉṉum nakaraṅkaḷ, ciṉṉa āciyāvil tikaris natiyiṉ mēl kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ātiyākamam 10-m atikāram 8m vākkiya mutal innakaraṅkaḷiyum avikaḷic cērntaṉavākac collappaṭum maṟṟum cila paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷiyumpaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷik kāṇalām. atāvatu, nōvāviṉ kumārarkaḷ, cēm kām yāppēt.
“kāmuṭiya kumārar, kūṣ misrāyīm pūt kāṉāṉ eṉpavarkaḷ. kūṣ nimrōtip peṟṟāṉ. ivaṉ pūmiyilē parākkiramacāliyāṉāṉ. ivaṉ karttarukku muṉpākap palatta vēṭṭikkāraṉāyiruntāṉ; ākiyāl karttarukku muṉpākap palatta vēṭṭikkāraṉāṉa nimrōtippōla eṉṉum vaḻakkac col uṇṭāyiṟṟu. cineyār tēcattiluḷḷa pāpēl, ērēk, akkāt, kalṉē eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷ avaṉ āṇṭa rājyattiṟku ātistāṉaṅkaḷ. antat tēcattiliruntu ācūrukkup puṟappaṭṭuppōy niṉivēyiyum rekepōt paṭṭaṇattiyum kālākiyum niṉivēkkum kālākukkum naṭuvāka recēṉiyum kaṭṭiṉāṉ; itu periya paṭṭaṇam.”
ivvākkiyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkiyil, pāpilōṉ, nimrōttuṭiya rājyattiṉ mukkiyamāṉa stāṉam eṉṟu kāṇkiṟatu. itu tavira, niṉivēp paṭṭaṇattiyum atilum peritāṉa vēṟu cila paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷiyum kaṭṭiṉatākak kāṇkiṟōm. ākavē, pāpilōṉ cattiya vētākamattil colliyapaṭi 4261 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ uṇṭāṉaJ. nēpukātnēccār pāpilōṉil aracāṭci ceyta kālamō, kiṟistuvukku 580 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉum iṟṟikku 2494 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉumāṉaJ. kiṭṭat taṭṭa ki.mu.  540-m varuṣattil ciras (kōrēs) eṉpavaṉāl intappāpilōṉ rājyam aḻikkappaṭṭup pōyiṟṟu.
10. pāpilōṉ eṉṉum nakarattiṉ ciṟappum ataṉ aḻivum.
innakaram ulakattiluḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟilum mikunta pūrvīkamum atika vicālamumāṉatāka viruntaJ. taṟkālattil, ulakattil periyapaṭṭaṇamāka matikkappaṭum laṇṭaṉmā nakarattippārkkilum pāpilōṉ mūṉṟupaṅku periJ. laṇṭaṉmānakarattit taṟkālattup pāpilōṉ eṉṟaḻikkakkūṭiyatāka atu avvaḷavu ciṟappuṭiyatāyiruntaJ. laṇṭaṉ, tems (Thames) āṟṟiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum amintiruppatu pōlavē, pāpilōṉ, aipirāttu āṟṟiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum ceṅkalkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kipiṭiccuvarkaḷuṭaṉum paṭittuṟikaḷuṭaṉum irupakkaṅkaḷilum vicālamāṉa pātikaḷuṭaṉum pātikaḷiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil uṉṉatamāṉa mūṉṟu nāṉku aṭukku māḷikikaḷuṭaṉum veku oḻuṅkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭu, mika aḻakāṉa tōṟṟamuṭiyatāyiruntaJ. itaṉ naṭumattiyil irupākattiyum iṇikkakkūṭiya oru periya pālam vekupalamākavum uṉṉatamākavum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaJ. camamāṉa iṭattil caricaturamākap pakkattukkup pakkam 15 mil aḷavuṭaṉ 225 catura mil parappuḷḷatākak kaṭṭappaṭṭaJ. ippaṭṭaṇam mottattil 625 caricaturaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu, avikaḷ ovvoṉṟum 25, 25 terukkaḷāka vakukkappaṭṭiruntaJ. perum terukkaḷ ovvoṉṟum natiyiṉ irupakkaṅkaḷilum vantucērumpaṭi nērnērāka amikkappaṭṭu, ovvoṉṟum piramāṇṭamāṉa pittaḷik katavukaḷuṭiyatāyiruntaJ. nilikaḷum katavukaḷum, muṟṟilum pittaḷi. atupōlavē kōṭṭiyiṉ 120 katavukaḷum muḻutum pittaḷiyiṉāl ākiyavi. ippaṭṭaṇatticcuṟṟilum 75-aṭi uyaramum mēlpākattil 32-aṭi akalamumuṭiyatāka piramāṇṭamāṉa kōṭṭi kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaJ. naṭuvil ōṭiya āṟṟiṉjalam atikamākuṅ kālattil vaṭintu pōkiṟataṟkākak kōṭṭikkuveḷiyē 40 milcaricaturamum 1600 caturamil vistīraṇamum 35-aṭi āḻamumuḷḷa oruperiya kuḷamum, paṭṭaṇattic cuṟṟilum 200 muḻa āḻamum 50 muḻa akalamumāṉa akaḻum veṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa. cāmakkārarkaḷirukkumpaṭi kaṭṭiya kōpurattiṉ naṭumatti vaḻiyākak kōṭṭiyiṉmēl 4 kutiri pūṭṭiya periya rataṅkaḷ tārāḷamāyc cuṟṟivarumpaṭi pātiyumiruntaJ. kōṭṭiyiṉ naṭumattiyil vaṭṭamāṉa iraṇṭu uḷkōṭṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil, rājaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉiyum aticcērnta uttiyāṉavaṉaṅkaḷum maṟṟoṉṟil, pīlas eṉṉum cukkirapakavāṉukkukkaṭṭiya ālayamum mikap piramāṇṭāyiruntaṉa. avvālayattil mikavum uyaramāṉa pittaḷik katavukaḷ anēkamiruntaṉa. ataṉ nilikaḷum paṭikaḷum ellām pittaḷi. uḷpākattil aṉēka pirākāraṅkaḷ mikavum nērttiyāy amikkap paṭṭiruntatōṭu atiluḷḷa vikkirakaṅkaḷum avvālayattiṉ taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷum camiyal pāttiraṅkaḷum muṟṟilum pacumpoṉṉāka viruntaṉa. iravu pakal iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyamuṭiyāmal mayaṅkumpaṭi atika jaṉappaḻakkamuḷḷatākavum viyāpāram peruttatākavum annakaram viḷaṅkiṟṟu. aṅkuḷḷōr uyarnta rattiṉak kampaḷaṅkaḷ melliya cālvikaḷ aracarkaḷukkuriya pītāmpara mutaliya uyarntavastiraṅkaḷ ceyvatil mikavum ki tērntavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. iṟṟikku 3365 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ isravēl jaṉaṅkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭa erikōpaṭṭaṇattil (yōcuvā 7-21l)
