ikkumarināṭutāṉ, kiḻakkē cantāttīvukaḷ variyilum mēṟkē maṭakācikar tīvu variyiṉum akaṉṟu kiṭantatākak kūṟappaṭum ‘lemūriyā’ eṉṟa nilapparappām. innilapparappu orukālatteḻunta peru veḷḷattil āḻntupōyiṟṟeṉṟum avvāṟu āḻntupōṉa perunilam ivvulaka muḻuvataṟkum naṭuviṟkiṭanta perumparappākalāṉ makkaḷ mutaṉmutal innilattiliruntu piṉ nāṟṟiciyiṉum pirintu ceṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭaṉa reṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam itilirunta tollōr vaḻaṅkiyatu tamiḻppāṣiyā meṉṟum, palakāraṇaṅkaḷ kāṭṭi viḷakki niṟuviṉar mēṟpula viññāṉi kaḷuḷoruvar.”
ippaṭip paḻamiyāṉa nāṭṭiṟkut talinakaram teṉmaturiyeṉṟum, avviṭattiluḷḷōr tamiḻp pāṣiyiyē pēci vantārkaḷeṉṟum, 549 caṅkappulavarkaḷiruntārkaḷeṉṟum, tamiḻp pāṣiyiṟ ciṟanta 4,449 vitvāṉkaḷ varuṣantōṟum ovvoruvarāka 4,440 varuṣaṅkaḷvari tamiḻc caṅkattit toṭarntu naṭatti vantārkaḷeṉṟum, atupōlavē iṭic caṅkamum kaṭiccaṅkamum toṭarntu naṭantu vantaṉaveṉṟum piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷāl kāṇalām.
16. muccaṅkamirunta kālamum, avikaḷilirunta pulavarkaḷum, irājākkaḷum, araṅkēṟṟiya nūlkaḷum.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ varalāṟu,
1. mutaṟcaṅkam.
“im muccaṅkaṅkaḷiyum paṟṟi ‘iṟiyaṉārakapporuḷuri’ yiṉkaṭ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatē mikap paḻamiyāṉa caritam. ataṉ kaṇṇē, taliccaṅkamiruntār akattiyaṉārum tiripurameritta viricaṭik kaṭavuḷum kuṉṟeṟinta murukavēḷum murañciyūr muṭinākarāyarum nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉumeṉa ittoṭakkattār aiññūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭu nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉā reṉpaJ. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa ettuṇiyō ‘paripāṭa’lum ‘mutunāri’yum ‘mutukuruku’ṅ ‘kaḷariyāviri’yum eṉa ittoṭakkattaṉa. avar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅkam irīiyiṉār kāyciṉavaḻuti mutalākak kaṭuṅkōṉīṟāka eṇpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār eḻuvar pāṇṭiya reṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa maturi yeṉpa. avarkku nūl ‘akattiyam’ eṉpa eṉat taliccaṅkattip paṟṟiyum,



2. iṭiccaṅkam
iṉi iṭic caṅkamiruntār akattiyaṉārun tolkāppiyaṉārum iruntiyūrk karuṅkōḻimōciyum veḷḷūrk kāppiyaṉum ciṟupāṇṭa raṅkaṉum tiriyaṉ māṟaṉum tuvarik kōmāṉum kīrantiyumeṉa ittoṭakkattār aimpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭu mūvāyiratteḻu nūṟṟuvar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa ‘kaliyu’m ‘kuruku’m ‘veṇṭāḷi’yum viyāḻamāliyakavalu meṉa ittoṭakkattaṉaveṉpa. avarkku nūl ‘akattiya’mum ‘tol kāppiya’mum, ‘māpurāṇa’mum ‘ici nuṇukka’ mum ‘pūtapurāṇa’ mumeṉa ivi. avar mūvāyirat teḻunūṟṟi yāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avariccaṅkam irīiyiṉār veṇṭērc ceḻiyaṉ mutalāka muṭattirumāṟaṉīṟāka aimpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār aivar pāṇṭiyareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuratteṉpa. akkālattuppōlum pāṇṭiyaṉāṭṭik kaṭalkoṇṭatu eṉa iṭiccaṅkattippaṟṟiyum pala kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
ikkūṟṟukkaḷi yām ārāyntu pārkkumiṭattu, taliccaṅkatti ovvoru pāṇṭiyaṉum ēṟakkuṟiya aimpatimpa tāṇṭukaḷāka naṭātta atu naṭantu vantiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatum, iṭic caṅkatti ovvoru pāṇṭiyaṉum ēṟakkuṟiya aṟupattumūṉṟaṟupattu mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷāka naṭātta atu naṭantu vantiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatum pōtarukiṉṟaṉa. ic caṅkaṅkaḷiraṇṭum nīṭitta kālam naṭantu vantirukkavēṇṭumeṉpatilum palanūlkaḷiyaṟṟap paṭṭaṉaveṉpatilum, pāvalar palar caṅkatti yoṭṭi vāḻntaṉareṉpatilum, caṅkamiraṇṭum muṟiyēyirunta maturiyuṅ kapāṭa puramuṅ kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatilum, atu kāraṇamākap pala ariya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ aḻintupaṭṭaṉa veṉpatilum aiyappāṭilliyeṉṉalām. maṟṟup pāṇṭiya rācarkaḷ muṟiyē aimpatāṇṭum aṟupattu mūṉṟāṇṭu māka āṇṭu vantaṉareṉṟurittal aiyuṟaṟpālatē. āyiṉum, pāṇṭiyar eṇpattoṉpatiṉmarum pāṇṭiyar aimpattoṉpatiṉmarum orē toṭarcci yāka āṇṭuvantaṉa reṉṟu karutātu, naṭuvil iṭiyīṭupaṭṭu araciṉṟik kaḻinta āṇṭukaḷum ivvāṇṭukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭik koḷḷappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutuka. ivvāṟu taliccaṅkamirunta nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟiyāṇṭil iṭiyīṭupaṭṭuk kaḻintaṉa ettuṇi yāṇṭukaḷō? iṭiccaṅkamirunta mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟiyāṇṭil iṭi yīṭupaṭṭuk kaḻintaṉa ettuṇiyō? ivaṟṟiṟkum oruvāṟu uttēca vakiyāl takka kaṇakkiṭṭuk koḷḷiṉ mēṟkūṟiya pāṇṭiyar eṇpat toṉpatiṉmarum pāṇṭiyar aimpat toṉpatiṉmarum āṇṭa kālattiṉ aḷavu kuṟintu namputaṟ pālatāku meṉka. ittuṇi yuyttuṇaramāṭṭāta cilar vēṟupaṭak kūṟuvar.
3. kaṭiccaṅkam
kaḷaviyaṟ poruḷkaṇṭa kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉāriṉiṉṟum pattān talimuṟi yāḷarākiya nīlakaṇṭaṉār “kaṭiccaṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntār ciṟu mētāviyarum cēntam pūtaṉārum aṟivuṭiyaraṉārum peruṅ kuṉṟūrkiḻārum iḷantirumāṟaṉum maturiyāciriyar nallantuvaṉārum marutaṉiḷa nākaṉārum kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉāru meṉa ittoṭakkattār nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉpa, avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa ‘neṭuntokināṉūṟu’m ‘kuṟuntoki nāṉūṟu’m ‘naṟṟiṇināṉūṟu’m ‘aiṅkuṟunūṟu’m ‘patiṟṟuppattu’m ‘nūṟṟimpatukaliyu’m ‘eḻupatuparipāṭalu’m ‘kūttu’m ‘vari’yum ‘pērici’yum ‘ciṟṟici’yumeṉṟu ittoṭakkattaṉa. avarkku nūl ‘akattiya’mun ‘tolkāppiya’mumeṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu āyiratteṇṇūhṟṟim patiṟṟiyāṇ ṭeṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅkam irīiyiṉār kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭup pōntirunta muṭattirumāṟaṉ mutalāka ukkirap peruvaḻuti yīṟāka nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu ‘uttaramaturi’ yeṉpa. avaruṭkaviyaraṅ kēṟiṉār mūvar pāṇṭiyareṉpa” eṉṟu kaṭiccaṅkattip paṟṟik kūṟāniṉṟaṉar.”
[itōṭu kaṭiccaṅkak kālattiṉ iṟutiyil tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷi araṅkēṟṟiya kālattilirunta caṅkappulavarkaḷ cāṟṟukkavi koṭuttirukkiṟārkaḷ. atik koṇṭu mūṉṟāvatu caṅkattiṉ kaṭiciyi lirunta vitvacirōmaṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ cila teriyavarukiṟatu. aviyāvaṉa:-
	
mēṟkaṇṭa vitvacirōmaṇikaḷ tamiḻil vitta apimāṉatti avarkaḷ colliya pākkaḷiṉāl teḷivāy aṟiyalām.]
tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu
“ikkūṟṟi yārāyumiṭattup pāṇṭiyar nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmarum āṇṭa kālam iṭiyīṭu paṭṭuk kaḻinta kālattōṭuṅkūṭi āyirat toṇṇūṟ ṟimpatiṟṟi yāṇṭākiṉṟaJ. pāṇṭiyarovvoru varum (iṭiyīṭṭuk kālattoṭuṅkūṭṭi) muppatteṭṭāṇṭukaḷ aracāṭci ceytavarākiṉṟaṉar. itu namputaṟpāṟṟē. iṉittaliccaṅkamirunta nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatu varuṣaṅkaḷum iṭiccaṅkamirunta mūvāyiratteḻunūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷum kaṭiccaṅkamirunta āyirat toṇṇūṟṟimpatu varuṣaṅkaḷumākak kūṭi oṉpatiṉāyirattut toḷḷā yirattut toṇṇūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. itaṉik ki.pi. nūṟṟiliruntu piṟkaṇakkiṭṭuc ceṉṟāl taliccaṅkam ki.mu.  oṉpatiṉāyirat toṇṇūṟṟut toṇṇūṟu varuṣaṅkaṭku muṉṉart tāpikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ toṉmi māṭci kūṟivanta viṭattu, ki.mu.  8,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa eḻuttuc cātaṉaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷa veṉṟamiyāl ataṉiṉum ēṟakkuṟiyap pattoṉpatu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṉṉarē taliccaṅkamēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭateṉṟal cālāteṉa vātittaṟkum iṭamuṇṭu. * * * * * 
ivvāti kālattu nūlkaḷuṭ kaṭalkoṇṭaḻintaṉavum iṉṉum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭātaṉavum pōka, ippōḻtattuk kiṭippaṉa talic caṅkattār kālattuc ceyyappaṭṭa ‘akattiyam’ eṉṟa nūliṉ kaṭ cila cūttiraṅkaḷum, iṭiccaṅkattār kālattuc ceyyappaṭṭa ‘tolkāppiyam’  eṉṟa ilakkaṇa nūlum, kaṭiccaṅkattār kālattuc ceyyappaṭṭa ‘eṭṭuttoki’ eṉṟa nūṟṟokiyuḷaṭaṅkiya eṭṭu nūlkaḷum ‘pattuppāṭṭu’m, ‘patiṉeṇkīḻkkaṇakku’ mām. eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷip piṉvarum pāṭṭāluṇarka:
		

“naṟṟiṇi nalla kuṟuntoki yiṅkuṟunū
			ṟotta patiṟṟuppat tōṅku paripāṭal
			kaṟṟaṟintār ēttuṅ kaliyō ṭakampuṟameṉ
			ṟittiṟatta veṭṭut toki.”
ivveṭṭut tokiyuṭ ‘kalittoki, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu’ eṉṟa mūṉṟu nūlkaḷum accāki veḷippōntuḷa. immūṉṟaṉuḷ muṉṉiya iraṇṭum akapporuḷum piṉṉiyatoṉṟum puṟapporuḷuṅ kūṟuvaṉavām.”
mēṟkaṇṭa cila kuṟippukkaḷiṉāl cilappatikāram eḻutiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kaṭiccaṅkappulavar kālamiruntāreṉṟum, atu iṟṟikku 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ ilaṅkiyiṉ aracaṉākiya kayavākuviṉ kālameṉṟum viḷaṅkukiṟaJ. iṟiyaṉārakapporuḷukku uri eḻutiya nakkīraṉār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eḻutiya cilappatikārattiṉ cilavarikaḷi mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttāḷukiṟār eṉpatikkoṇṭum, nakkīraṉār kaṭiccaṅkap pulavarkaḷil oruvar eṉpatik koṇṭum, ivarkālamum kaṭiccaṅka kālameṉṟu tiṭṭamāyt terikiṟatu. iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷukku uriyeḻutiya nakkīraṉār uriyil, mutal ūḻiyiṉ iṟutiyiṉkaṇ teṉmaturiyakattut taliccaṅkam nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟi yāṇṭu iruntateṉṟum, atil varuṣam ovvoruvarāka 4,449 vitva cirōmaṇikaḷ caṅkattiṟkut talimi vakittārkaḷeṉṟum, kāyciṉa vaḻutimutal kaṭuṅkōṉīṟākavuḷḷa 89 pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ āṇṭu koṇṭiruntārkaḷeṉṟum, avarkaḷuḷ 7 pāṇṭiyarkaḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟit talimi vakittārkaḷeṉṟum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. avaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa cayamākīrttiyaṉākiya nilantarutiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil tolkāppiyam eṉṉum ciṟanta nūl eḻutappaṭṭaJ. ivarkālattil avaṉ aracāṇṭukoṇṭirunta teṉmaturiyum aticcērnta 49 nāṭukaḷum kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. puṟanāṉūṟṟil kaṭalkoṇṭa teṉṉāṭṭil paḵṟuḷi eṉṉum āṟuvaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉāl veṭṭappaṭṭateṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. ceṅkōṉṟaric celavu eṉṟatōr ciṟu nūliṉāl mutal ūḻiyilirunta cila nāṭukaḷiṉ peyarum malikaḷiṉ peyarum ūrkaḷiṉ peyarum cila pulavarkaḷiṉ peyarum, cila nūlkaḷiṉ peyarum veḷiyākiṉṟaṉa. itu kumariyāṟṟiṟkum paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum iṭiyiluḷḷa peruvaḷa nāṭṭaracaṉākiya ceṅkōvittaṉiyūrc cēntaṉ eṉṉum pulavaṉ pāṭiṉateṉṟu terikiṟatu. itu mutal ūḻiyiṉ kālamām.
(a) mutal ūḻiyil tamiḻppāṣiyiṉ uṉṉata nili
mutal ūḻikālattil nāratar akattiyar mutaliya periyōr eḻutiya cila cūttiraṅkaḷiṉāl tamiḻppāṣi viruttiyaṭintu mikavum uṉṉata māṉa niliyeyti mika maturamuṭiyatāy evvitak kalappum kaṭiṉamumiṉṟi amutamottiruntateṉṟu tamiḻiṉ arumi terinta yāvarum aṟivārkaḷ. piraḷayattiṟkuppiṉ tamiḻar pala iṭaṅkaḷukkum paraviṉatiṉālum pala pāṣi pēcuvōr iṭiccaṅkattilum kaṭiccaṅkattilum kalantatiṉālum pala pāṣikaḷum kalantu ataṉ taṉicciṟappuk kuṟintateṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. atupōlavē akkālattil teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ niliyum kalappuṟṟirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉikka nām maṟantupōkakkūṭāJ. itōṭu iṭiccaṅka kālattil, muntiyirunta akattiyam mutaliya cila pūrva nūlkaḷiṉ cilapākam koṟki allatu kapāṭapuram kaṭalkoṇṭapōtu aḻintaṉa. tirunelvēliyiṉ teṉ kīḻppakkattil, kaṭaṟkariyōrattil kaṭal alikaḷāl mōtappaṭṭa matilkaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅki niṟkum tiruccentūrk kōyilukkuc cumār 10 mil camīpattil, ippōtu koṟkiyeṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum kirāmattiṟkuk kīḻppakkamāyp periya ālayaṅkaḷuṭaṉum araṇmaṉikaḷuṭaṉum matilkaḷuṭaṉum oru nakaram kaṭalukkuḷ mūḻkiyiruppatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. ippaṭiyē teṉṉintiyāviṉ kīḻkkarikaḷil aṉēka ālayaṅkaḷum kōṭṭikaḷum kaṭalvāyppaṭṭu aḻikkappaṭṭirukkumeṉṟu eṇṇa iṭantarukiṟaJ. vicākappaṭṭaṇattukkuc camīpattil kaṭalukkuḷḷirukkum cuppiramaṇiyar ālayattiyum, makāpalipurattukkuc camīpattil kaṭalukkuḷḷirukkum civālayattiyum pārttavarkaḷukku itaṉ uṇmi teriyāmal pōkāJ. kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭa teṉmaturiyilum kapāṭapurattilum maturiyilum caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 9,000 varuṭaṅkaḷāyt tamiḻccaṅkam naṭantatāka aṟiyalām. 657 caṅkappulavarka ḷiruntārkaḷeṉṟum, 8,598 pulavarkaḷ talimi vakittārkaḷeṉṟum, 197 pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ ati ātarittu vantārka ḷeṉṟum, atil 15 pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār kaḷeṉṟum teḷivāyt terikiṟatu.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tampiyākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eḻutiya cilappatikāramum tiraṇatūmākkiṉi eḻutiya tolkāppiyamum kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār eḻutiya maṇimēkaliyum eṭṭuttokiyum pattuppāṭṭum patiṉeṇkīḻk kaṇakkum maṟṟum pala tamiḻnūlkaḷum caṅkap pulavarkaḷālum tamiḻnāṭṭu aracarkaḷālum iyaṟṟappaṭṭut tamiḻarkku mikap pūrva nūlkaḷāka viḷaṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. innūlkaḷ coṟcuvi poruṭcuvi poruntiyavikaḷāy ilakkaṇa vaḻuviṉṟi eḻutappaṭṭavikaḷāyt tamiḻ kaṟṟaṟintōr koṇṭāṭak kūṭiyavikaḷāy iṉṉum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu nām aṟivōm. iṟṟikku āyiramāyiram varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eḻutappaṭṭa it tamiḻnūlkaḷiṉ naṭicciṟappu taṟkālattavar mēlāṉateṉṟu matikkak kūṭiyatāka virukkiṟatē, tamiḻ mikap paḻamiyāṉatum ciṟantatumāṉa pāṣiyeṉṟu kāṭṭappōtumāṉaJ.
mēlum ulakattār yāvarālum koṇṭāṭappaṭum ciṟanta karuttukkaḷ yāviyum taṉṉakattilaṭakkikkoṇṭirukkum tiruvaḷḷuvanāyaṉār iyaṟṟiya tirukkuṟaḷiṉatu tamiḻarumiyiyum cuviyiyum uyarviyum aṟiyātavarum uṇṭō? avar cakōtariyākiya oḷaviyār eḻutiya irucol mutumoḻiyākiya ātticūṭiyiyum nāṟcol mutumoḻiyākiya koṉṟivēntaṉiyum mutalmutal maṉaṉam paṇṇāta tamiḻmakkaḷum uṇṭō? iccīviyattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta itōpatēcam yāvaṟṟiyum ilakuvāṉa naṭiyil takunta uvamāṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ñāpakappaṭuttak koṇṭirukkum kuṟaḷ, ātticūṭipōṉṟa nūlkaḷiṉ perumiyum, āyiramāyiram varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa avikaḷiṉ toṉmiyum itara pāṣiyiluḷḷa nūlkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭuppārkkum cāstira ārāyccik kārarukku mikavum teḷivāka viḷaṅkum. tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tiruvāy moḻiyeṉṉum paktinūlkaḷiṉ arumiyi eṭuttuc collavallavar yār?
17. taliccaṅka kālattil caṅkītattiṉ uyarnili.
im mutalūḻiyil 4,440 varuṣaṅkaḷākac caṅkamiruntaJ. caṅkattārāl ceyyappaṭṭa mutunāri, mutukuruku, pañcapāratīyam, kaḷariyāviri eṉpaviyum ivaṟṟiṟku mutal nūlāyirunta akattiyamum kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻ coṉṉa akattiyattil iciyiṉ (caṅkīta) pākamum icittamiḻiyē taṉittuc coṉṉa perunāri peruṅkuruku pañcapāratīyamum mutal ūḻiyil mikavum virivāka vaḻaṅkivantirukka vēṇṭum. annūlkaḷukkiṇaṅka aminta peruṅkalam allatu 1,000 tantikaḷ pūṭṭiya pēriyāḻ eṉṉum vīṇiyum 21, 17, 14 tantikaḷ pūṭṭiya maṟṟum cila vīṇikaḷum aḻintaṉa. peruṅkalattiyē nārata vīṇiyeṉpār. ‘yāḻāciriyaṉākiya nārataṉ’ eṉṟu akkālattu nūlkaḷil collappaṭuvati nām kavaṉikkiyil, mutal caṅkattiṉ kālattilē avar vīṇiyil vallavarāyiruntā reṉṟum maṟṟavarkaḷukku vīṇi kaṟpikkum kuruvāyiruntāreṉṟum caṅkītattip paṟṟip pañcapāratīyam eṉṉum nūl eḻutiyiruntāreṉṟum terikiṟatu. mutalūḻiyil eḻutiya caṅkīta nūlkaḷiluḷḷa cila akattiyac cūttiraṅkaḷum mikak koñcamāṉa nāratac cūttiraṅkaḷum mēṟkōḷāka aṅkaṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavaṉṟi muḻu nūlum akappaṭukiṟatilli. akappaṭṭa cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ cila iṟṟikku 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ eḻutiya cilappatikārattilum ataṟku aṭiyārkku nallārāl eḻutiya uriyilum veḷippaṭukiṉṟaṉa. jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉiruntakālattil 4,440 varuṣaṅkaḷ iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻccaṅkam iruntatiyum, 4,449 vitva cirōmaṇikaḷ talimi vakittatiyum, ac caṅkattil caṅkīta nūlkaḷ ērāḷamāyiruntatiyum, peruṅkalam mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷ iruntatiyum iṟṟikku 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta oru iḷavaracaṉum 1,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta aṭiyārkku nallārum collumpōtu ati yār maṟukkakkūṭum? itaṟku nūṟu, irunūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉpiṉṉāṉakālamē karikāl cōḻaṉ, kayavāku mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ kālamākum.
18. mutal ūḻiyil perumivāyntirunta caṅkītam piṉ ūḻiyil kuṟinta vitam.
pūrvakālattiṉ carittiraṅkaḷi nām naṉṟāykkavaṉippōmāṉāl, caṅkītamāṉatu teyva cannitiyil ārātaṉikaḷil mikavum upayōkikkap paṭṭu vantateṉṟu kāṇpōm. evvita piratippirayōjaṉattiyum virumpāmal, aracaṉmutal kuṭikaḷvariyum caṅkīta appiyācam ceytiruntārkaḷeṉṟu terikiṟatu. tāvītu rājaṉ eppaṭik karttaruṭiya peṭṭikku muṉpākat taṉ jaṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ caṅkītam pāṭi narttaṉam paṇṇik koṇṭu vantāṉō, appaṭiyē cumār 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil karikāl cōḻaṉum, maturiyil rājacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉum, ataṉmuṉ citamparattil naṭarājaṉum, parata cāstirattil tērntavarkaḷāy naṭaṉampaṇṇiṉārkaḷeṉṟu aṟivōm. tamatu paktaṉākiya pāṇapattiraṉukkāka vīṇiyil cātāri rākam pāṭiyatum, pāṇapattiraṉuṭiya maṉiviyiṉ vīṇākāṉam ciṟantateṉṟu cāṭci colla vantatum, paramaciva ṉeṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatē. ivvaḷavu mēṉmi poruntiya caṅkītamum paratamum nāḷ cellaccellak kuṟivupaṭṭu, camaṇarālum puttarālum arumiyaṟiyāta rājākkaḷālum kaṇṭikkappaṭṭut tēyntu taṟkālattil ēḻikaḷ kippukuntirukkiṟatē eṉṟu vicaṉappaṭukiṟēṉ.
jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉ mikavum arumiyāka eṇṇappaṭṭatum teyvacamukattil āṭippāṭik koṇṭāṭakkūṭiyatumāyirunta caṅkītattip pōtikkum ici nūlkaḷum ici vallōrum jalappiraḷayattāl aḻintapiṉ (nōvāviṉ pēḻiyil tappiya coṟpappērkaḷ pōla) cilar māttiram appiraḷayattiṟkut tappiṉārkaḷ. camuttirattil teṉṉintiyā viṟkut teṟkē 49 nāṭukaḷiṉ piratāṉa nakaramāyuḷḷa teṉmaturi yaḻintapōtu ataṉ vaṭakōṭiyilirunta coṟpa jaṉaṅkaḷ māttiram aḻiyāmal tappiṉārkaḷeṉṟu niṉikka iṭamirukkiṟatu. jalappiraḷayam varum kālam iṉṉateṉṟu teriyātiruntataṉāl aṉēka vitvāṉkaḷum ici vallōrum piraḷayattil aḻintupōṉārkaḷ. aḻintavarkaḷ pōka ataṉ vaṭapākamākiya teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa akattiyar pōṉṟa cila caṅkattalivarkaḷ, kaṭal koṇṭupōṉa mutal caṅkattippōla iraṇṭām caṅkamoṉṟik kapāṭapurattil ēṟpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. koṟkiyil āḷuki ceyyattoṭaṅkiṉa pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷippaṟṟi vicārittāl, teṉmaturiyi yāṇṭa rājākkaḷ tāṉ iṅku vantu āṇṭārkaḷeṉṟu colla iṭamilli. āṉāl anta rājavamcatticcērntavarkaḷē iṅkē aracāṇṭārkaḷeṉṟu niṉikkalām. koṟkiyiṟpōlavē ukkiramaṉṉaṉ kōṭṭi cuntarapāṇṭipuram teṉkāci karuvinallūr kōṭṭāṟu vaḷḷiyūr paṉṟikuḷam srī villiputtūr ātittapuram maṇaṟpaṭi ceṅkōṭṭi mutaliya ciṟu nāṭukaḷilum, pāṇṭiya vamcattavarkaḷ āṇṭu koṇṭiruntārkaḷeṉpatāka, iṭintupōṉa kōṭṭikaḷālum cuvarkaḷālum iṉṟum kāṇakkūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. teṉmaturikku vekutūram vaṭakki lirunta ciṟcila ciṟṟaracarkaḷālum, aḻintu pōkāmal miñciyirunta cila caṅkap pulavarkaḷālum iraṇṭām caṅkam maṟupaṭiyum ārampamāyiṟṟu. ivarkaḷil anēkar, iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻuḷ ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum iru tamiḻiyum naṉṟāy aṟintukoḷḷātavarka ḷeṉṟē collavēṇṭum. mutal ūḻiyil miñciya cilapērukkum ati eṭuttu niṟuttum ūkkamum kuṟintaJ. tāṅkaḷ aṉēka nūṟu vayatuṭiyavar kaḷāyc cātittuk kāṭṭiya ariya vittikaḷi aṟpa āyuḷuḷḷa maṟṟavarukkuc collavum malittārkaḷ pōlum. mutal ūḻiyi luḷḷavarkaḷ āyiram varuṣaṅkaḷākavum anēka āyiramvaruṣaṅkaḷākavu miruntārkaḷeṉṟu purāṇaṅkaḷ collukiṉṟaṉa. appurāṇaṅkaḷ uṇmikku miñcip pārittuk kūṟuvatāka nām eṇṇiṉālum, cattiya vētattilum iṟṟikku 6,000-varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ 960-varuṣam āyuḷuḷḷavarkaḷiruntārkaḷeṉṟum 4,500 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta nōvā taṉ 500-vatu vayatil cēmip peṟṟāṉ eṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. ivaṟṟi nōkkumiṭattu teṉmaturiyilirunta 4,400-kku mēṟpaṭṭa caṅkappulavarkaḷum 4,400-kku mēṟpaṭṭa caṅkattiṉ kālamum yuktikkup poruttamākat tōṉṟātirāJ. appaṭiyē akkālattilirunta caṅkattār iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻiyum nīṇṭakālam payiṉṟārka ḷeṉpatum uṇmitāṉeṉṟu oppukkoḷvōm. jalappiraḷayattiṟkup piṉṉuḷḷa maṟṟavar aṟpa āyuḷpeṟṟavarkaḷāṉatiṉāl 1,000-tanti pūṭṭiya pēriyāḻil paḻakuvati paṭippaṭiyāka viṭṭuviṭṭārkaḷ. nāratariṉ curuti muṟippaṭi kāṉam paṇṇum nuṭpatti aṟiyātu veṟuttārkaḷ. kaṇ mukam karam kāl ciram mutaliya aṅkaṅkaḷiṉ apinayattāl carittiraṅkaḷi viḷakkum paratacāstirattiṉ arumiyit terintukoḷḷātu mayaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ippaṭiyirukkuṅ kālattil, iṭiccaṅkamirunta kapāṭapuram kaṭalāl aḻikkappaṭṭaJ. aṅkirunta muṭattirumāṟaṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṟku vantu aṅkē kaṭampavaṉattikkaṇṭu ippōtirukkum kūṭal ālavāy eṉṉum vaṭamaturiyikkaṭṭi atil mūṉṟāvatu caṅkatti stāpittāṉ. ikkaṭiccaṅka kālattil puttamatamuma camaṇamatamum ūṭāṭiṉa. aṅku āṇṭukoṇṭirunta aracarkaḷil cilar putta matattiyum camaṇa matattiyum taḻuvalāṉārkaḷ. āriyarum meḷḷa meḷḷat teṉṉāṭṭil vantu nilittārkaḷ.
19. teṉṉintiya caṅkītamum atil āriyak kalappum.
mutal ūḻiyiṉ iṟutiyil ceyyappaṭṭa tolkāppiyam eṉṉum ilakkaṇanūl iṭiccaṅkattiṟkum kaṭiccaṅkattiṟkum ātāra nūlā yiruntateṉṟu itaṉmuṉ nām pārttirukkiṟōm. avvilakkaṇanūlil, mutal ūḻikālam cattiyamum neṟiyun tavaṟāta kālameṉṟum, piṉ ūḻi kālam oḻuṅku tavaṟiya kālameṉṟum, ataṟkēṟpac cila kaliyāṇa oḻuṅkukaḷi riṣikaḷ ceytārkaḷeṉṟum avvāciriyar collukiṟār. 
tolkāppiyam, poruḷatikāram, 4-vatu kaṟpiyal, 144-vatu cūttiram
		“mēlōr mūvarkkum puṇartta karaṇaṅ
		kīḻōrkkākiya kālamu muṇṭē”
itaṟku uriyeḻutiya nacciṉārkkiṉiyār, muṟkālattu nāṉku varuṇattārukkum karaṇam oṉṟāy nikaḻntatu eṉṟum, atu iraṇṭām ūḻi toṭaṅki vēḷāḷarkkut tavirntatu eṉṟum, taliccaṅkattārum mutalnūl āciriyar (akastiyar) kūṟiya muṟiyē karaṇam oṉṟeṉṟu ceyyuḷ ceytār eṉṟum collukiṟār.

tolkāppiyam, poruḷatikāram, 4vatu kaṟpiyal, 145-vatu cūttiram
		“poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉa
		riyar yāttaṉar karaṇameṉpa”
itaṟku uriyāciriyar āti ūḻikaḻintapiṉ iraṇṭām ūḻi mutalākap poyyum vaḻuvum ciṟantu tōṉṟiyatiṉāl iruṭikaḷākiya periyōr, mēlōr karaṇamum kīḻōr karaṇamum vēṟupaṭak kaṭṭiṉār eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. aiyar eṉpatu mutal nūlāciriyariyaṉṟu, vaṭa nūlōrik karutiyatu eṉkiṟār. piṉṉum ivvāciriyar āti ūḻiyiṉ antattē innūl ceytaliṉ, mutalnūlāciriyar kūṟiyavāṟē kaḷavu nikaḻnta piṉṉark kaṟpu nikaḻumāṟuṅ kūṟi, tām nūl ceykiṉṟa kālattu, poyyum vaḻuvumpaṟṟi iruṭikaḷ karaṇam yāttavāṟum kūṟiṉār eṉkiṟār. mēṟ kuṟitta tolkāppiyar cūttirattiyum nacciṉārkkiṉiyār ceyta uri yiyum nām kavaṉikkiyil, mutal nūlākiya akattiyattiṟku vaḻi nūlākiya tolkāppiyam eḻutiya kālattil mēlōrukku oru caṭaṅkum, kīḻōrukku vēṟu caṭaṅkumāka, vaṭa nūlōr vakuttārkaḷeṉṟum, talic caṅkattiruntōr kālattu, appētamillāmal nāṉku varuṇattārukkum caṭaṅku oṉṟāyiruntateṉṟum viḷaṅkukiṟaJ. akkālattil vētatti vevvēṟāka vakuttu nāṉku jātiyārum iṉṉiṉṉatu ceyyavēṇṭu meṉṟu vakuttatākap pārkkiṟōm. itaṉālē mutal ūḻiyil vētaṅkaḷ vakukkappaṭavilli yeṉṟum, varuṇa āccirama tarumaṅkaḷ uṇṭāka villiyeṉṟum, atu cattiyam tavaṟāmal naṭantukoṇṭa kālameṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. akkālamutal tamiḻōṭu vaṭapāṣiyum koñcaṅ koñcamākak kalantukoḷḷa ārampittaJ. atumutaṟkoṇṭu āriyarum tamiḻppāṣikkuc cila upakāraṅkaḷ ceytārkaḷeṉṟum tamiḻppāṣiyiṉ arumi teriyāta maṟṟavarkaḷ tamiḻiyum atiṉ pūrva nūlkaḷiyum tali yeṭukkātu ceytārkaḷeṉṟum cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstirikaḷ B.A.,eḻutiya ‘tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu’ iraṇṭām atikārattik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu teriyavarukiṟatu. itu ulaka iyaṟkitāṉē. oṉṟu atikappaḻamiyāṉapiṉ maṟupaṭiyum pēṇuvārillātu pōṉāl ekketiyaṭiyumō akkatiyē tamiḻppāṣikkum ēṟpaṭṭatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē piṟantu tamiḻiyē tāyppāṣiyākakkoṇṭa cila teṉṉāṭṭu antaṇar taṅkaḷ tāyiṉiṭam kaṟṟukkoṇṭa cila kāriyaṅkaḷit tamiḻil eḻuti vikkāmal tāṅkaḷ nūtaṉamāyk kaṟṟuk koṇṭa camaskirutattilēyē eḻutivikkum vaḻakkamuṭiyavar kaḷāṉārkaḷ. caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 360 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭuḷ oṉṟākiya cōḻa rājyattil mantiriyāyirunta kōvinta tīkṣitar avarkaḷ kumārar veṅkaṭamaki eṉpavar, pūrva teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkivanta rākaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ārōkaṇam avarōkaṇaṅkaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭutti, avikaḷil vaḻaṅkivarum curaṅkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa stāṉaṅkaḷil varukiṟa teṉṟu aṟiyakkūṭiyatāṉa mēḷakarttāvum ataṟkiṇaṅka lakṣaṇakītamum ceytu, “caturtaṇṭippirakāciki” eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ oru nūl veḷiyiṭṭār. annūl teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkivarum curaṅkaḷiyē kuṟikkak kūṭiyatāyiruntālum, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷi aṟintu koḷvataṟku ētuvāṉa nūlāyilli, ēṉeṉṟāl, karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ cārāmcattiyē coṉṉa ivar tamiḻil eḻutātu camaskiruta pāṣiyil eḻutivittatu, piṟar muṟṟilum mayaṅkuvataṟku iṭamā yirukkiṟatu. karnāṭaka caṅkītattik kaṟṟaṟintōril cilar, tuvāvimcati curutiyippaṟṟic collum vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷip pārttuviṭṭu, atu cariyeṉṟum itu tappeṉṟum collukiṟārkaḷ. 72 mēḷakkarttāviyum oppukkoḷḷāta cilar, iṉṟiyatiṉamum irukkiṟārkaḷ. 72 mēḷak karttāvukku rākamāliki pāṭiya mahā vittiyanātiyar avarkaḷ, centamiḻppaḻakkamuṭiyavarkaḷāyp periya purāṇattiṟku anēka mika arumiyāṉa kīrttaṉikaḷ ceytiruntālum, rākamālikikkuc camas kirutattil cākittiyam ceytatu eṉṉa apippirāyamō? ippaṭiyē ovvoru kālattil oṉṟiliruntu maṟṟoṉṟukku māṟuvatiṉāl kalapputtōṉṟi, kālakkiramattil vicuvāmittira ciruṣṭiyāy iṉan teriyāmal mayaṅka varuvatu iyalputāṉ. ākkiyōṉuṭiya karuttu iṉṉateṉṟu appāṣikkuṭiyavarē teriyāmal mayaṅkuvārāṉāl, maṟṟoru pāṣikkārar aṟivatu eppaṭi? cāraṅkatēvar tuvāvimcati curutikaḷippaṟṟi eḻutiya cila camaskiruta culōkaṅkaḷic caṟṟēṟak kuṟiya 20 vitamāy arttam paṇṇiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. ippaṭiyē vevvēṟu apippirāyamuḷḷa puttarum camaṇarumāṉa palavittuvāṉkaḷ palapala kālaṅkaḷil rājaṉi vacappaṭuttikkoṇṭum tamiḻvitvāṉkaḷik kaṭcicērttukkoṇṭum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ apippirāyaṅkaḷi meḷḷameḷḷa nilināṭṭiṉārkaḷ. ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum irutamiḻum ciṟṟiṉpattiyē viḷivikkakkūṭiyatā yirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu tāṅkaḷ veṟuttatumaṉṟi, muṟṟilum atu talikāṭṭātapaṭi tolikkap pukuntaṉar eṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil pārppōm.
20. puttarum camaṇarum kalantu caṅkītattiyum nāṭakattiyum tolikkappukuntatum atil aḻintupōṉa nūlkaḷum.
tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu,
“ittuṇipperumi vāynta nāṭakat tamiḻiṉ tōṟṟameṉṉi? tamiḻnāṭakam mutaliluṇṭāṉatu mataviṭayamākavē yeṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum. atu kaṭavuḷar tiruviḻākkālaṅkaḷil āṭal pāṭalkaḷiraṇṭiyuñ cēra nikaḻttuvatiṉiṉṟum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. cilakālattiṉ piṉṉarkkati naṭiyāṉa maṉappāṭaṅkaḷum uṭaṉkūṭiṉa; ataṉmēl mutaliṟ pāṭalāyuḷḷa campāṣaṇikaḷum piṉṉar vacaṉamāyuḷḷa campāṣaṇikaḷum avaṟṟuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟpāṭu nāṭakattamiḻ ‘vēttiyal, potuviyal’ eṉṟa irupiriviṉatāki aracarkaḷālum ēṉiyōrālum ātarittu vaḷarkkappaṭṭaJ. ki.mu.  mūṉṟānūṟṟāṇṭiṉātal allākkāl ataṉiṉuñ caṟṟu muṟkālattiṉatāl nāṭakat tamiḻ uyirniliyuṟṟiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. nāmuṇarnta paḻamiyāṉa nāṭakattamiḻ nūlkaḷ aṉittum akkālattē niṉṟu nilaviṉavātaliṉeṉka. ākavē atu kuṟṟaṅkuṟivu illātu uṇṭāṉatoru toḻileṉṟē ātiyil matikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ‘nāṭakaviya’ liṉ mukavurikkaṭ kūṟiya kūṟṟiyuṅ kāṇka.
ivvāṟu tōṉṟivaḷarnta nāṭakattamiḻ vīḻniliyiyaṭiyap pukuntaJ. ataṟkuṟṟa kāraṇam yātu? oḻukka nili vakukkappukunta āriyaruñ ciṉarum nāṭakak kāṭciyāṟ kāmamē aṟiviṉum mikapperukukiṉṟateṉṟa pōlikkoḷkiyuṭiyarāyt tamatu nūlkaḷiṟ kaṭiyappaṭuvavaṟṟuḷ nāṭakattiyuñ cērttuk kūṟiṉar. akkālattirunta aracarkaḷukkun turppōtaṉiceytu nāṭakat tamiḻit taliyeḻa voṭṭātu aṭakki vantaṉar. oḷaviyārun tiruvaḷḷuvarum oruṅkē pukaḻnta illaṟa vāḻkkiyiyē tīviṉi yaccattiṉpāṟ paṭuttuk kūṟuñ ciṉarkaḷ nāṭakattamiḻik kaṭintatu ōrāccariyamaṉṟu. ivvaḷavu kaṭṭuppāṭṭuk kiṭiyil nāṭakattamiḻ evvāṟu taliyeṭuttu ōṅkappōkiṉṟatu?”
tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu,
“ivvulaka vāḻkkikku aṟam poruḷiṉpameṉṟa mūṉṟuñ ciṟantaṉavāmeṉṉu muṇmiyi naṉkuṇarātu, ‘aṟamē yāvarum piṉpaṟṟutaṟkuriyatu, maṟṟu iṉpam kiviṭutaṟkuriyatu’ eṉṟu eṇṇi, iciyiṉāl iṉpam mikutaliṉ ataṉiyuṅ kaṭiyavēṇṭumeṉṟu pukuntu, āriyaruñ ciṉarum oruṅku cērntu, icittamiḻip peritum alittut tolikka muyaṉṟaṉar. (ici nāṭakaṅ kāmatti viḷikku meṉṟurittār uriyāciriyarkaḷuḷ taliniṉṟa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum) am muyaṟcikaḷil anēka nūlkaḷ, antō! aḻintupōyiṉa. ippōḻtattu eñciyiruppaṉa mikaccilavē. ivaṟṟi iṟivaṉ pātukāttaruḷka.
paṇṭitarāyiṉār kaṭintu nāṭakat tamiḻik kiviṭavē, atu pāmarar kiyakappaṭṭu iḻivaṭintu terukkūttaḷavilē niṟkiṉṟaJ. akkālattuc caṅkap pulavarkaḷ ceyta ‘paratam’, ‘akattiyam’, ‘muṟuval’, ‘cayantam’, ‘kuṇanūl’ ‘ceyiṟṟiyam’, ‘mativāṇar nāṭakat tamiḻnūl’, ‘kūttanūl’, ‘nūl’, eṉṟa nāṭakat tamiḻnūlkaḷellām yāṇṭuppō yoḷittaṉa?”
muṉ vacaṉaṅkaḷi kavaṉikkiyil, āriyarum puttarum camaṇarum teṉ intiyāviṟku vanta piṉpē tamiḻp pāṣiyil kalapput tōṉṟiṉateṉṟu nām niccayamāyc collalām. akattiya māmuṉivar eḻutiya pērakattiyam eṉṉum nūl mikavirivāyiruntatupaṟṟi, atik kuṟukkic ciṟṟakattiyam eṉṉum nūl ceytār eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. itiṉāl tamiḻ ilakkiyam mikunta virivuṭiyatāyiruntateṉṟu pulappaṭukiṟaJ. ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟkē ilakkaṇam eṉṟapaṭi, ilakkiyam mikavum pūraṇa niliyiliruntateṉṟu nām aṟiyavēṇṭum. avar eḻutiya ilakkaṇam virivāyiruntatupaṟṟi, tolkāppiya muṉivarāl iyaṟṟamiḻukku māttiram tolkāppiyam eḻutappaṭṭaJ. pavaṇanti muṉivarāl naṉṉūlum, vīramā muṉivarāl toṉṉūlum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum vīracōḻiyam ilakkaṇa viḷakkam pōppiyar ilakkaṇam ilakkaṇac curukkam ilakkaṇa viḷakkac cūṟāvaḷi ilakkaṇa cūṭāmaṇi muttuvīriyam mutaliya curuṅkiya nūlkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. ivikaḷum iyaṟṟa miḻiyē collukiṉṟaṉa. 700-kku mēṟpaṭṭa pāviṉaṅkaḷiruntatākac colliya pērakattiyattiliruntu vara varak kuṟukic cila pāviṉaṅkaḷukku māttiram ilakkaṇam collap paṭṭiruppati nōkka, pūrvattilirunta ici nūlum yāḻum paratamum varavarap paṭippaṭiyāyk kuṟintum tēyntum pōyiṉaveṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. ittōṭu teṉṉintiyāviṟkup pūrvakuṭikaḷallāta maṟṟavar yāvarum itaṟku aṉukūla catturukkaḷāṉārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam kaṭiccaṅka kālattil ippaṭip paṭṭavarkaḷāl icinūlkaḷ palavum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu teḷivākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. iyal icināṭakameṉṉum muttamiḻ coṉṉa akattiyattiliruntu iyaṟṟamiḻiṉ cila pākaṅkaḷukku māttiram tolkāppiyar ilakkaṇam coṉṉār. avar coṉṉatiliruntu ataṟkup piṉṉuḷḷōr kuṟukkic coṉṉārkaḷeṉṟu nām teḷivāy aṟiyalām. akattiyaruṭiya kālattil, caṅkītamum paratamum ivi campantamāṉa kūttukkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil mika virivāka iruntaṉaveṉṟu avar colliya cila cūttiraṅkaḷiṉāl terikiṟatu. tolkāppiyar mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉṟu nāṉku nilaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṟkuriya karup poruḷkaḷiyum paṟṟic colliya cūttiramāvatu:-
		“teyvamuṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟi
		ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii
		yavvaki piṟavuṅ karuveṉa moḻipa.”
itil ovvoru nilattiṟkuriya teyvam, uṇporuḷ allatu uṇavu tāṉiyaṅkaḷ, mirukaṅkaḷ, maraṅkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, paṟikaḷ, toḻilkaḷ, yāḻiṉ iṉaṅkaḷ iṉṉaviyeṉṟu collukiṟār. ovvoru nilaṅkaḷiṉ karupporuḷkaḷ. iṉṉaveṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyār uriyil mikat teḷivākac collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyar poruḷilakkaṇam colliyaviṭattut talivaṉun taliviyum ennilattiṟkuriyavarkaḷō annilattiṟkuriya teyvam uṇā mutaliya karupporuḷkaḷ māṟāmal kavi collavēṇṭumeṉṟu vitittirukkiṟār.
aivaki nilattiṉ karupporuḷ viparam
“mullikku uṇā, varakuñcāmiyum mutiriyum; mā, uḻiyum pulvāyum muyalum; maram, koṉṟiyuṅkuruntum; puḷ, kāṉakkōḻiyuñ civalum; paṟi, ēṟukōṭpaṟi; ceyti, nirimēyttalum varaku mutaliyaṉa kaḷi kaṭṭaluṅ kaṭāviṭutalum; yāḻ, mulliyāḻ, piṟavu meṉṟataṉāṟ, pū, mulliyum piṭavun taḷavun tōṉṟiyum; nīr, kāṉyāṟu; ūr, pāṭiyuñ cēriyum paḷḷiyum.
kuṟiñcikku uṇā, aivaṉa nellun tiṉiyum mūṅkilariciyum; mā, puliyum yāṉiyuṅ karaṭiyum paṉṟiyum; maram, akilum āramun tēkkun timicum vēṅkiyum; puḷ, kiḷiyum mayilum; paṟi, murukiyamun toṇṭakap paṟiyum; ceyti, tēṉ aḻittaluṅ kiḻaṅku akaḻtalun tiṉi mutaliyaṉa viḷittalum kiḷi kaṭitalum; yāḻ, kuṟiñciyāḻ; piṟavumeṉṟataṉāṟ, pūkāntaḷum vēṅkiyuñ cuṉikkuvaḷiyum; nīr, aruviyuñ cuṉiyum; ūr, ciṟukuṭiyuṅ kuṟicciyum.
marutattiṟku uṇā, cennellum veṇṇellum; mā, erumiyum nīrnāyum; maram, vañciyuṅ kāñciyum marutamum; puḷ, tārāvum nīrkkōḻiyum; paṟi, maṇamuḻavum, nellarikiṇiyum; ceyti, naṭutaluṅ kaḷi kaṭṭalum aritaluṅ kaṭāviṭutalum; yāḻ, maruta yāḻ. piṟavumeṉṟataṉāṟ, pū, tāmariyuṅ kaḻunīrum: nīr, yāṟṟunīrum maṉikkiṇaṟum poykiyum; ūr, ūrkaḷeṉpaṉavēyām.
neytaṟku uṇā, mīṉ viliyum uppu viliyum; mā, umaṇpakaṭu pōlvaṉa; mutalayuñ cuṟāvum mīṉātaliṉ māveṉṟal marapaṉṟu. maram, puṉṉiyum ñāḻaluṅ kaṇṭalum; puḷ, aṉṉamum aṉṟilum mutaliyaṉa; paṟi, mīṉ kōṭpaṟi; ceyti mīṉpaṭuttalum uppuviḷittalum avi viṟṟalum; yāḻ, neytal yāḻ. piṟavu meṉṟataṉāṟ, pū, kitiyum neytalum; nīr, maṇaṟkiṇaṟum uvarkkuḻiyum; ūr, paṭṭiṉamum pākkamum.
pālikku uṇā, āṟalittaṉavuñ cūṟikoṇṭaṉavum; mā, valiyaḻinta yāṉiyum puliyuñ cennāyum; maram, vaṟṟiṉa iruppiyum ōmiyum uḻiñiyum ñemiyum; puḷ kaḻukum paruntum puṟāvum; paṟi, cūṟi kōṭpaṟiyum nirikōṭpaṟiyum; ceyti, āṟalittaluñ cūṟi kōṭalum; yāḻ, pāliyāḻ; piṟavumeṉṟataṉāṟ, pū, marāvuṅ kurāvum pātiriyum; nīr arunīrkkūvaluñ” cuṉiyum; ūr, paṟantali.”
ivikaḷikkoṇṭu tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē atāvatu iṟṟikku cumār 8,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyē yāḻvakikaḷum avaṟṟiṟkuriya ilakkaṇa vitimuṟikaḷum mika virivāka iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu mikavum teḷivāyk kāṇalām. mulliyāḻ kuṟiñciyāḻ, marutayāḻ, neytalyāḻ, pāliyāḻ mutaliya yāḻkaḷiṉ cura amippippaṟṟi itiṉ piṉ pārppōm.
21. caṅkītattip paṟṟic collum pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉaveṉpaJ.
cilappatikārattiṉ urippāyirattil aṭiyārkku nallār icittamiḻik kūṟumiṭattil, ici nūlkaḷum pēriyāḻum aḻintu pōṉatip paṟṟiyum tām uri eḻutuvataṟku utaviyāyirunta cila nūlkaḷip paṟṟiyum collukiṟār. avi varumāṟu :-
“iṉi icittamiḻ nūlākiya perunāri peruṅkurukum piṟavum tēvaviruṭi nārataṉ ceyta pañcapāratīyam mutalāyuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷu miṟantaṉa. nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiya mutalāyuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷumiṟantaṉa. piṉṉum muṟuval cayantam kuṇanūl ceyiṟṟiya meṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷḷum oru cārār cūttiraṅkaḷ naṭakkiṉṟa attuṇiyallatu mutal naṭuiṟuti kāṇāmiyiṉ, aviyum iṟantaṉapōlum. iṟakkavē varum peruṅkala mutaliya piṟavumām. ivaṟṟuṭ peruṅkalamāvatu pēriyāḻ; atu kōṭṭiṉataḷavu paṉṉirucāṇum, vaṇaraḷavu cāṇum, pattaraḷavu paṉṉirucāṇum, ippeṟṟikkēṟṟa āṇikaḷum, tivavum, untiyum peṟṟu āyiraṅkōl toṭuttiyalvatu; eṉṉi? ‘āyiranarampiṟ ṟātiyāḻāku, mēṉiyuṟuppumoppaṉa koḷalē, pattarataḷavuṅ, kōṭṭiṉataḷavu, motta veṉpa virumūṉṟiraṭṭi, vaṇarcā ṇoḻitteṉavittaṉar pulavar’ eṉa nūluḷḷum ‘talamuta lūḻiyiṟṟāṉavar tarukkaṟap, pulamakaḷāḷar purinarappāyiram, valipeṟat toṭutta vākkami pēriyāḻc, celavu muṟi yellāñ celkiyiṟṟerintu, maṟṟiyāḻuṅ kaṟṟumuṟi piḻiyāṉ eṉak katiyiṉuḷḷuṅ kūṟiṉārākalāṟ pēriyāḻ mutaliyaṉavum iṟantaṉa veṉak koḷka.
iṉit tēvaviruṭiyākiya kuṟumuṉipāṟkēṭṭa māṇākkar paṉṉiruvaruṭ cikaṇṭiyeṉṉum aruntavamuṉi, iṭiccaṅkattu anākula ṉeṉṉum teyvappāṇṭiyaṉ tēroṭu vicumpucelvōṉ tilōttami yeṉṉun teyvamakaḷik kaṇṭu tēriṟkūṭiṉaviṭattuc caṉittāṉit tēvarum muṉivarum cariyā niṟkat tōṉṟiṉamiyiṟ cārakumāraṉeṉa appeyar peṟṟa kumaraṉ iciyaṟitaṟkuc ceyta ici nuṇukkamum, parāciva muṉivaril yāmaḷēntirar ceyta intirakāḷiyamum, aṟivaṉār ceyta pañcamarapum, ātivāyilār ceyta paratacēṉāpatīyamum, kaṭic caṅkamīrīiya pāṇṭiyaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiya pāṇṭiyaṉ mati nāṭakat tamiḻ nālu meṉavintum innāṭakak kāppiyak karuttaṟinta nūlkaḷeṉṟēṉum orupuṭiyoppumi koṇṭu muṭittalikkarutiṟṟu ivvuriyeṉak koḷka.”
mēṟkaṇṭa cila vacaṉaṅkaḷiṉāl, mutal ūḻiyiṉ kālattil tēvaviruṭi nāratar ceyta pañca pāratīyamum paratamum akattiyamum pēriyāḻum aḻintaṉa; iṭic caṅka kālattiluḷḷa muṟuval cayantam kuṇanūl ceyiṟṟiyam eṉṉum nūlkaḷum kuṟṟuyirāy aḻintaṉa; iṭic caṅka kālattil ceyyappaṭṭa ici nuṇukkam mutaliya cila nūlkaḷ innūl uriyeḻutuvataṟku utaviyāyiruntaṉaveṉṟum terikiṟatu. avar kaṭiciyākac collukiṟa nūlkaḷum pūraṇamāy ippōtu illi. itu tavira, caṅkītattip paṟṟi aṭiyārkku nallār kālattil iṉṉiṉṉa nūlkaḷiruntaṉaveṉṟu collum cila viparam, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ pūrvattiyaṟiya virumpum namakkup pirayōcaṉamāyikkum. ātalāl, avikaḷiyum iṅkup pārppōm.
“akattiyam : iḵtu iyal ici nāṭakameṉṉum muttamiḻilak kaṇattiyum terivippatākiya oru periya ilakkaṇa nūl; teṉ maturik kaṇirunta taliccaṅkap pulavarkaḷuḷ mutalvarākiya akattiya muṉivarā laruḷic ceyyappaṭṭaJ. itu nacciṉārkkiṉiyār kālattilēyē iṟantu pōyiṟṟeṉṟu terikiṟatu. āyiṉum itiluḷḷa cila cūttiraṅkaḷ maṭṭum paḻiyavurikaḷil āṅkāṅku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ici nuṇukkam : itu cārakumāraṉ allatu cayantakumāraṉeṉpavaṉ iciyaṟitaṟporuṭṭu, akatti muṉiyavar māṇākkar paṉṉiruvaruḷ oruvarākiya cikaṇṭiyeṉṉum aruntavamuṉivarāl veṇpāvāliyaṟṟap paṭṭa icit tamiḻ nūl; iḵtu iṭiccaṅkamirunta kālattuc ceyyap paṭṭateṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallāruriyālum, accaṅkap pulavarkku nūlākaviruntateṉṟu iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷuriyālum terikiṟatu.
intirakāḷiyam : itu yāmaḷēntirareṉṉum āciriyarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa icittamiḻ nūl; aṭiyārkku nallār uriyeḻutuvataṟku mēṟkōḷākak koṇṭa nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu.
kuṇanūl : itu nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu. itiluḷḷa cila cūttiraṅkaḷ maṭṭumē naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum nūl iṟantu pōyiṟ ṟeṉṟum aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
kūttanūl : itu nāṭakat tamiḻ nūl. itaṉ varalāṟu vēṟoṉṟun teriyavilli.
cayantam : itu nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu. itiluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ cila naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉavaṉṟi nūliṉ mutaṉaṭuviṟuti kāṇāmiyiṉ innūl iṟantatupōlumeṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallāreḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
ceyiṟṟiyam : itu ceyiṟṟiyaṉāreṉṉum āciriyarāṟ cūttirarūpamāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa nāṭakat tamiḻ nūl. itiṉ cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ cila naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉavaṉṟi nūliṉ mutaṉaṭuviṟuti kāṇāmiyiṉ iṟantatupōlumeṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tāḷavakiyōttu : itu tāḷavilakkaṇattik kūṟu nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itaṉ varalāṟu vēṟoṉṟun teriyavilli.
nūl : itu nāṭakat tamiḻ nūluḷoṉṟaṉ peyareṉṟu maṭṭun terikiṟatu. itaṉ varalāṟu vēṟu yātoṉṟun teriyavilli.
pañcapāratīyam : itu tēvaviruṭi nārataṉ ceyta icittamiḻ nūl; tammuṭiya kālattilēyē innūliṟantu pōyiṟṟeṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
pañcamarapu : iḵtu aṟivaṉāreṉṉum āciriyarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa icittamiḻ nūl. cilappatikāravuri eḻutuvataṟku aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkōḷākak koṇṭa nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu.
paratacēṉāpatīyam : iḵtu ātivāyilāreṉṉum āciriyarāl veṇpāvāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭakat tamiḻ nūl; cilappatikāravuri yeḻutuva taṟku aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkōḷākak koṇṭa nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu.
paratam : itu nāṭakat tamiḻ nūluḷoṉṟu; itaṉi iṟantupōṉa nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
peruṅkuruku : itu taliccaṅkap pulavariyaṟṟiya icittamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu; innūl tamatu kālattētāṉē iṟantupōyiṟṟeṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. itu mutukuru keṉṟum collappaṭum.
perunāri : itu taliccaṅkap pulavariyaṟṟiya icittamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu; aṭiyārkku nallār kālattētāṉē iḵtu iṟantupōyiṟṟeṉṟu terikiṟatu.
mativāṇar nāṭakat tamiḻnūl : itu cūttirappāvālum veṇpāvālum mativāṇaṉāreṉṉum pāṇṭiyaroruvarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭakat tamiḻ nūl; aṭiyārkku nallār uriyeḻutuvataṟku mēṟkōḷākak koṇṭa nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu.
muṟuval : itu paḻiya nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷoṉṟu; innūl akkālattē iṟantu pōyiṟṟeṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār eḻuti yirukkiṉṟaṉar.”
mēṟkaṇṭavikaḷi nām kavaṉikkiyil  jalappiraḷayattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa mutal caṅkakālattil, iciyip paṟṟiya nūlkaḷum vāttiyaṅkaḷum mutaṉmi peṟṟiruntaṉaveṉṟum iṭic caṅka kālattil cila aḻintu cila iruntaṉaveṉṟum kaṭiccaṅka kālattil cila kuṟṟuyirāki maṉaṉam ceyta cila cūttiraṅkaḷ māttiramirukkum nilikku vantaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm. taṟkālattil aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkōḷākak koṇṭa nūlkaḷumiṟantaṉa. uriyāciriyar mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiya cila coṟpa cūttiraṅkaḷ māttiram cilappatikārattit tēṭiyacciṭṭa makāmakōpāttiyāyar vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷiṉ puṇṇiyattāl kāṇak kiṭittaṉa. ippaṭit tēṭuvārum ātarippārumiṉṟi nam teṉṉintiya caṅkīta nūlkaḷ aḻintu pōyiṉamiyiṉ vicaṉikkak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. ivvāṟiruntālum parampariyāy maṉaṉam paṇṇiya cila rākaṅkaḷum paratamum tāḷamum muṟṟilum oḻintu pōkavilli. kōyilkaḷil ūḻiyam ceyyum nākacurakkārarkaḷālum naṭaṉakkārarkaḷālum avarkaḷ aṇṇāvikaḷālum mēḷakkārarkaḷālum vīṇi vācippavar kaḷālum kōyil umpaḷaṅkaḷiṉāl mikavum arumiyākak kāppāṟṟap paṭṭu ituvariyum nīṭittirukkiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭiyiruntālum caṅkītattit teḷivāy aṟintu koḷvataṟku utaviyāṉa nūlkaḷ pūrṇamāyillāta taṉāl pūrvamāyuḷḷa karnāṭaka cuttam keṭṭu curutikaḷip paṟṟic cantēkikkumpaṭiyāṉa nilikku vantatu eṉṟālum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattik kavaṉikkum intukkaḷum maṟṟum aṉṉiya tēcattārum atu mikunta tērcciyuṭiyateṉṟum cāstira muṟiyiyuṭiya teṉṟum maṟṟavikaḷōṭu kalavāta paricuttamuṭiyateṉṟum viyantu collak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. intu tēcattiṉ pūrvīka nākarīkamum avarkaḷ paktiyum teyva ārātaṉiyil upayōkitta avarkaḷ caṅkītamum avarkaḷ pāṣiyum mikavum koṇṭāṭak kūṭiyatāyiruntaṉa.

22. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ celvamum ataṉ nākarīkamum.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ toṉmiyiyum kalvic ciṟappiyum caṅkap pulavar perukkiyum, avarkaḷ ceyta caṅkīta nūlkaḷiṉ arumiyiyum pārtta nām, annāṭṭiṉ celvattiyum nākarīkattiyum oruvāṟu curukkamāyp pārppatu nallateṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ. vekukālattiṟkumuṉ naṭantatākac collappaṭum rāmāyaṇattil, cukkirīvaṉ vāṉararkaḷukkuc cītiyiṉ iruppiṭattic collukiyil, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭip paṟṟi mikavum viyantu pēcuvatik koṇṭu pāṇṭiyarkaḷ mikunta aṟivuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārka ḷeṉṟum mikac ceḻippuḷḷa nāṭṭiyuṭiyavar kaḷāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum terikiṟatu. poṉṉiṟattatāyum aḻakuṭiyatāyum muttumaṇi mutaliya vaṟṟāl aṇiyappaṭṭatāyum nakarattu araṇōṭu iṇikkappaṭṭatāyumuḷḷa pāṇṭiyar vāyiṟkatavik kāṇpīr eṉṟu teṉmukamākac cellum vāṉara vīrarkaḷip pārttuc cukkirīvaṉ coṉṉāṉ eṉpatāka vālmīki muṉivar collukiṟār. itil pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ āṇṭu koṇṭirunta kōṭṭi vācaṟ katavic ciṟappittuk kūṟukiṟār. ataṉ kōṭṭi vācalum katavum muttunavarattiṉaṅkaḷiṉāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭu mikavum aḻakuṭiyatā yirukkumeṉṟu collukiṟār. oru paṭṭaṇattiyum ataṉ aḻakiyum ataṉ celvattiyum collavantavar, appaṭṭaṇattiṉ vācaṟkataviṉ aḻakiyum atu viliyuyarnta taṅkattāl ceyyappaṭṭu navarattiṉaṅ kaḷālum muttukkaḷiṉālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭiruppatiyum paṟṟic collukiṟār. kōṭṭi vācal niliyum katavum ippaṭiyirukkumāṉāl maṟṟavikaḷic colla vēṇṭiyatilli yeṉṟē kuṟukkic coṉṉār. vāyiṟkatavik kāṇpīr eṉṟu colvatik kavaṉikkiyila avvāyiṟ katavu mikavum uṉṉatamāṉatākavum vicālamuṭiyatākavu mirukka vēṇṭum. peṉṉam periya mirukaṅkaḷum jīvajentukkaḷum akkālattil iruntaṉaveṉpati nām aṟivōm. pirmāṇṭamāṉa yāṉikaḷiṉ mēl navarattiṉaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa ampāri vittu atiṉ mēl rājākkaḷ pōkuṅkālattil atil niṟkiṟavaṉukkum avaṉ piṭittirukkiṟa muttuk kuṭikkum mēl mikavum uyaramākak kōṭṭivācalirukkak kaṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. katavukaḷ poṉṉāyiruntatiṉāl avikaḷiṉ pirakācattāl veku tūrattiliruntē atik kāṇalām eṉpatē karutJ. mēlum rāvaṇaṉuṭiya kōṭṭiyip piṭittapiṉ rāvaṇaṉ mukam, ki, kāl cuttam ceyyumiṭattil pōṭṭirunta peritum vilimatikkak kūṭātatumāṉa marakatap paccik kaṟkaḷik kaṇṭu irāmar āccariyattuṭaṉ pārttatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. itik koṇṭu rāvaṇaṉuṭiya kōṭṭiyum avaṉ aicuvariyamum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ vaḷappattiṟku ottatākavēyiruntateṉṟu colla ētuvirukkiṟatu. rāvaṇaṉuṭiya nāṭṭil ilaṅki oru pākamākac colla vēṇṭumēyoḻiya avaṉ piratāṉa iruppiṭam intumakā camuttirattil vekutūram teṟkē yiruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
pārata yuttam naṭanta kālattil pañcapāṇṭavarukku utaviyāka yuttam muṭiyum vari cēranāṭṭaracaṉākiya utiyaṉ cēralātaṉ eṉṉum cēraṉ avarkaḷukku uṇavu aḷittu cēṉikaḷukku mikavum utaviṉāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu.
rāvaṇaṉ mikunta palacāliyākavum pattut talikaḷuṭaṉ nīṇṭa kālam aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭu vantavaṉākavum avaṉuṭiya rājyam intiyāvil vintiyamali variyum vicālittiruntatākavum vintiya malikkut teṉ pakkattilirunta avaṉuṭiya piratāṉikaḷākiya karaṉ, tūṣaṇaṉ eṉṟavarkaḷ 14,000 veṅkala rataṅkaḷuṭaṉ, cicērā 900 iruppu rataṅkaḷuṭaṉ isravēlarōṭu caṇṭikku vantatu pōla, rāmaruṭaṉ yuttattukku vantārkaḷ eṉpatākavum nām aṟivōm. appaṭiyē ivaṉ mikunta kalivallōṉāka iruntāṉeṉṟum caṅkītattilum mukkiya tērcci peṟṟiruntāṉeṉṟum nām pārkkalām. kilācamaliyi vērōṭu piṭuṅki eṭukkiyil, ataṉ kīḻ paramacivaṉāl nacukkappaṭṭā ṉeṉṟum appōtu pāram poṟukkāmal paramacivaṉic cāmavētattiṉāl kāṉam paṇṇiṉāṉeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. avaṉ kālattilirunta rikkuvētam utātta anutāttamākiya ri, ta, ka, ni eṉṉum curaṅkaḷōṭu māttiram kāṉam ceyyappaṭṭatākavum ataṉ piṉ cāmavētatti ca, ma, pa eṉṉum cuvarita ōcikaḷiyum cērttu kāṉam paṇṇiṉāṉeṉṟum tōṉṟukiṟatu. cāmavētam caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkum cuvaraṅkaḷic campūrṇamāyuṭiya tāyiṟṟu. teyva tōttiraṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya cāma vētattic caṅkītattiṉ pūrṇacuraṅkaḷōṭu mutal mutal pāṭiṉavaṉ rāvaṇaṉē. vētattiṟku muṉ illāta cila curaṅkaḷi avaṉ cērttuk kāṉam paṇṇiṉatum itik koṇṭē paramacivaṉ maṉatu iṟaṅkic cāpa vimōcaṉam ceytatum nāḷatu variyum collappaṭukiJ. teṉ tēcattukkuriya paṇmuṟippaṭi ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇaṅkaḷip pūrtti ceytu cāmakāṉam paṇṇiṉāṉ eṉṟu kāṇkiṟōm.
iyal ici nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻ ilakkaṇamākiya akattiyatti akastiyar colvataṟku muṉpē caṅkītam, paratam mutaliya ariya vittikaḷi innāṭṭil mikavum vistāramāyiruntaṉaveṉṟu avar collum cila cūttiraṅkaḷik koṇṭu pulappaṭukiṟaJ. mēlum jalap piraḷayattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa kālattil ciṉṉākkārarum nākacurakkārarum iruntārkaḷeṉṟu cattiya vētattil collukiṟatik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu teṉ maturiyilum atic cērnta 49 nāṭukaḷilum caṅkītam mika vistārā māyiruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nampa iṭamirukkiṟatu. pāpilōṉ paṭṭaṇattilum niṉivēppaṭṭaṇattilum iruntatākac collappaṭum pittaḷik katavukaḷ pōla piramāṇṭamāṉa katavukaḷum nilikaḷum ikkālattil kāṇpatu ariJ. atuvum oṉṟiraṇṭalla nūṟṟukkaṇakkāyc collappaṭukiṟatu. ippōtu oṉṟu kūṭakkāṇōm. āṉāl nimirōt kaṭṭiya pāpilōṉ rājyattiṟku anēka kālaṅkaḷukku muṉpuḷḷa teṉ maturiyilō navarattiṉaṅkaḷ iḻitta mikap peritāṉa anēka poṟkatavukaḷiruntirukka vēṇṭum. rāvaṇaṉuṭiya kōṭṭiyum ataṉ ciṟappum celvamum teṉmaturip paṭṭaṇattiṟkuk kuṟintatāyirāveṉṟu niṉikka iṭamirukkiṟatu.
pañca pāṇṭavarkaḷ vaṉavācam ceyta kālattil tuvārakiyi āṇṭu koṇṭirunta kiruṣṇapakavāṉuṭiya taṅki cupattiriyik kaliyāṇam ceytirunta arccuṉaṉ maturikku (maṇavūr) vantāṉeṉṟum maturikkaracaṉākiya cittiravākaṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷākiya cittirāṅkatiyi maṇam ceytu mūṉṟu varuṣam pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilēyē yiruntāṉeṉṟum purāṇaṅkaḷil collappaṭukiṟatu. itu tavira arccuṉaṉ maccatēcattiṉ virāṭapurattil araṇmaṉiyiluḷḷa stirīkaḷukkup paratacāstiram colli vittāṉeṉṟum avaṉ makaṉ apimaṉṉaṉ kaliyāṇattiṟkut tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarākiya cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷ vantārkaḷeṉṟum collap paṭukiṟaJ. akkālattilirunta rājākkaḷiṉ oṟṟumiyum avarkaḷ nākarīkamum caṅkītattilum paratattilum avarkaḷ vittirunta piriyamum ikkālattiluḷḷa nām aṟivataṟkukkūṭāta vistāra muṭiyatā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. rāmaruṭiya piḷḷikaḷākiya kucaṉ, lavaṉ eṉṉum pāliyarkaḷ vālmīki muṉivarāl kaṟpikkappaṭṭu rājacapiyil rāmāyaṇak kati ceyvatil mikap pāṇṭittiya muṭiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatip pārkkilum vēṟu tiruṣṭāntamum vēṇṭumā? pāpilōṉ rājyatti āṇṭu koṇṭirunta tariyurājaṉ kālattil teyva ārātaṉi ceytu koṇṭirunta paktaṉākiya tāṉiyēlic ciṅkak kukiyil pōṭṭa vicaṉattāl tariyurājaṉ cāppiṭavum kītavāttiyaṅkaḷik kēṭkavum piriyamaṟṟavaṉāy irāmuḻuvatum viḻittiruntāṉeṉṟu collappaṭuva tiyum taṟkālattil irājākkaḷuṭiya māḷikikaḷil iravu pakal kītavāttiya muḻakkam kēṭpatiyum kāṇkiṟa nām iṟṟikku 2,500 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta vaḻakkamē pūrva teṉ maturiyilu miruntateṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. ippaṭip pūrvattār kalviyilum caṅkītattilum celvattilum ciṟantiruntārkaḷeṉṟu colluvatōṭu nakarukkuriya paṇṭacāli, navatāṉiyak kaṭikaḷ, parutti, paṭṭu, cariki, rōmam mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyta vitam vitamāṉa āṭikaḷ, akil, cantaṉam, puṉuku, javvātu, kaṟpūram mutaliya vācaṉit tiraviyaṅkaḷ, vayiram, kempu, muttu, pavaḷam mutaliya navarattiṉaṅkaḷ ākiya ivikaḷi mikutiyāy uṭiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ collum cilappatikārattil nakar kāṇ kātiyilum ūr kāṇ kātiyilum vistāramāy aṟiyalām.
23. teṉṉintiyāviṉ ciṟpavēliyiṉ uyarvu.
nām muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ ciṟpa vēliyip pārppōmāṉāl, mikunta piramippiyaṭivōm. kal kikaḷōṭu mēl vaḷivukaḷ aminta kōyil mēṟkūri tāḻvāraṅkaḷiyum, pōtikic curuḷkaḷiyum, orē kal kampattil aminta vikkirakaṅkaḷiyum, mikavum nērttiyāka merukiṭappaṭṭa karuṅkaṟ corūpaṅkaḷiyum, puṣpaṅkaḷiṉ itaḻkaḷ pōlac ceyyappaṭṭa cuti vēlikaḷiyum, karuṅkal villiyum ataṉ nāṇiyum, karuṅkal caṅkilikaḷiyum maturi, āviṭiyār kōyil, teṉkāci, makāpalipuram, tiruccentūr, srīraṅkam, citamparam, vēlūr, kāñcīpuram, tañcāvūr mutaliya stalaṅkaḷil kāṇalām. mēlum iṟṟikku 500 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ vijayanakarattiṉ rājaṉāyirunta kiruṣṇatēvarāya reṉpavar maturi, srīraṅkam pōṉṟa periya ālayaṅkaḷukku mukappuk kōpuram kaṭṭa ārampittirukkiṟār. vēliyiṉ aḻakiyum muḻutum kalvēliyāyiruppatiyum vācalkaḷukkāka niṟuttiya kal nilikaḷiyum anēkar aṟintiruppārkaḷ. avikaḷ iṉṟum ‘rāyar kōpurat taṭippaṭipōl’ eṉṟu paḻamoḻiyāy vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟoruvarāl pūrtti ceyya muṭiyāta avvaḷavu peritāyum ivvaḷavu kālattukkuḷ muṭintatoṉṟu colvataṟkiyalāta avvaḷavu vēlip pāṭuṭiyatāyum, pārttavarkaḷ piramippaṭiyak kūṭiyatāyu mirukkiṟateṉṟu nām aṟivōm.
24. teṉṉāṭṭiṉ teyvaṅkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ tamiḻ tiṟamum.
iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷukku uriyeḻutiya nakkīraṉār jalap piraḷayattiṟku aṉēka varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyē mutaṟ caṅkamirunta teṉṟum taliccaṅkattil akastiyarum tiripurameritta viricaṭik kaṭavuḷum kuṉṟameṟinta murukavēḷum ciṟanta caṅkap pulavarkaḷāyiruntu naṭattiṉārkaḷeṉṟum collukiṟār. itikkoṇṭu paramacivaṉ tāmē teṉmaturiyil aracāṇṭu vantāreṉṟum, avar kumāraṉākiya cuppiramaṇiyarum teṉmaturiyiliruntavareṉṟum, iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻiyum virutti ceytu caṅkat talimi peṟṟiruntārka ḷeṉṟum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. kaṭiccaṅka kālattil civaperumāṉ tarmiyeṉpavarukku akkālattil uttara maturiyil āṇṭu koṇṭirunta vaṅkicacūṭāmaṇi yeṉṉum caṇpakapāṇṭiyaṉ maṉatil niṉitta karuttiṟkiṇaṅkiya kaviyoṉṟu eḻutikkoṭuttu 1,000 poṉ paricu peṟumpaṭi ceytār eṉpatiyum,
	“tamiḻ aṟiyum perumāṉē
	taṉṉiccērntār naṉṉitiyē
	tiruvālavāy eṉṉum maturikkaracaṉē” - eṉṟum
	“pāṭṭukkurukun tamiḻc cokkanātar” - eṉṟum
	“teyvat tamiḻkūṭal” - eṉṟum
	“caṅkam poṅkum paṇmuttamiḻkkōrpayaṉē cavuntirapāṇṭiyaṉē”
eṉṟum pala vitvāṉkaḷ pukaḻvatiyum nām kavaṉikkumpoḻutu tamiḻppāṣi paramacivaṉ nēcitta pāṣiyeṉṟu teḷivāyt terikiṟatu. uttara maturiyil taṭātakip pirāṭṭiyārik kaliyāṇañ ceytu koṇṭa cuntira pāṇṭiyaṉum avar kumāraṉākiya ukkira pāṇṭiyaṉum maturiyil tamiḻ aracarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum ivarkaḷē civaperumāṉ murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ avatārameṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum pāṇṭiyarājarkaḷ yāvarukkum tamiḻnāṭaṉ, teṉṉavaṉ, tamiḻar kōmāṉ, kūṭaṟ kōmāṉ, kaṭampavaṉanātaṉ, maturēcaṉ (maturikkaracaṉ), pāṇṭi vaḷa nāṭaṉ eṉap pala peyaruṇmiyum, paramacivaṉiyum cuppiramaṇiyariyum avarkaḷ parampariyil utitta pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷiyum maṟṟavarkaḷ pukaḻntiruppa tiyum nām kāṇalām. civaperumāṉum avar parampariyilutitta pāṇṭiya varacarkaḷum teṉmaturi, koṟki, uttaramaturi eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil āṇṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻiyum vaḷarttu vantārkaḷeṉṟum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. ivarkaḷil mikunta piracitti peṟṟa aracarkaḷukkuk kōyil kaṭṭi avarkaḷit teyvaṅkaḷāka vaṇaṅki vantārkaḷ. ipparampariyiluḷḷōr yāvarum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭil mutal caṅkattukku mutaṉmiyāṉa paramacivaṉiyum avar kumāraṉākiya murukak kaṭavuḷiyum ārātittu vantārkaḷ. teṉṉintiyā vilum, cumattirā, jāvā eṉṉum tīvukaḷilum, cuppiramaṇiyar ālayaṅkaḷum vikṉēsvarar ālayaṅkaḷum iruppati nām iṉṟum kāṇalām. intiyāviṉ vaṭapākattilum maṟṟum iṭaṅkaḷilum ivvālayaṅkaḷirukkak kāṇpatu ariJ.
uttaramaturiyil cumār 130 talimuṟiyāka āṇṭu koṇṭu vanta rājākkaḷ civa perumāṉ eṉṉum cōmacuntirapāṇṭiyaṉiṉ vamcattārka ḷeṉpati, tiruvālavāy eṉṉum uttaramaturiyiṉ stalapurāṇattil allatu tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇattil teḷivākak kāṇalām. atil viṟaku viṟṟapaṭalam allatu yāḻ vācitta paṭalattilum icivātuveṉṟa paṭalattilum kāl māṟiyāṭiya paṭalattilum caṅkītattip paṟṟiyum paratattip paṟṟiyum tāḷattip paṟṟiyum curukkamāyc colliyirukkiṟatu.
25. muccaṅkaṅkaḷiṉ kālattikkoṇṭu intiyāviṉ pūrva carittiratti oruvāṟu tiṭṭamāy aṟiyalām eṉpatu.
iti vācikkum kaṉavāṉkaḷē! intiyāviṉ kālakkaṇakku taṟkālattippōla oṉṟu, iraṇṭu eṉṟa ilakkattōṭu collappaṭāmal iṉṉa maṉuviṉ kālameṉṟum iṉṉa yukameṉṟum ittaṉiyāvatu parivirutti yeṉṟum (120 varuṣam) iṉṉa peyaruṭiya varuṣameṉṟum (pava, yuva, eṉpatu pōl) iṉṉa rāci mātam eṉṟum (mēṭaravi) colluvatu vaḻakkam. itōṭu maṉitaṉiṉ aṟpa āyuḷi niṉittu vekutāṉiya varuṣam kaṭakaraviyil piṟantāṉeṉṟum colluvatu vaḻakkam. itil enta vekutāṉiya varuṣameṉṟum ettaṉiyāvatu pariviruttiyeṉṟum enta āyiliyat tiruviḻāveṉṟum, entayukameṉṟum collāmiyiṉāl niccayam collakkūṭāmal mayaṅkuvatu iyalpē. mēlum piturpakti atikamāyuḷḷa intiya rājavamcaṅkaḷil, muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ peyarē aṭikkaṭi vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu vaḻakkam. ippaṭi varuvatiṉāl intiyāvil kāla niccayam colluvatu caṟṟu varuttamākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
mēlum pūrvattil purāṇaṅkaḷiyum itikācaṅkaḷiyum eḻutiyavarkaḷ kāla niccayattiṉ arumiyi aṟiyāmal avarkaḷ maṉampōṉapaṭi kāla nirṇayam colliyirukkiṟārkaḷ. 360 nāḷ koṇṭatu maṉuṭa varuṭameṉṟum, appaṭi 360 koṇṭatu tēva varuṭameṉṟum vittukkoṇṭu, ovvoru rājākkaḷum tēva varuṭattil 60,000, 40,000, 10,000 varuṭaṅkaḷ āṇṭārkaḷeṉṟum aṉēka riṣikaḷ aṉēka ūḻi kālaṅ kaḷākavum anēka caturyukaṅkaḷākavu miruntārkaḷeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. itiṉāl intiya carittirattiṉ kāla nirṇayam cariyākac colvatu kūṭiya kāriyamākat tōṉṟavilli. entac carittiramum ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṟpaṉikaḷ kalantatākavēyirukkiṟatu. itōṭu nīti nūlkaḷ eḻutiya vitva cirōmaṇikaḷi kavaṉippōmāṉāl kāppu mutal kaṭi variyilum taṅkaḷ ūriyāvatu peyariyāvatu piṟanta varuṣattiyāvatu colvatu vaḻakkamāyilli. tirukkuṟaḷ, ātti cūṭi, koṉṟivēntaṉ, mūturi, naṉṉeṟi, nalvaḻi mutaliyavikaḷik kavaṉittāl ivvuṇmi teriyum. taṟkālattippōl pustakam pativu ceytu vikkiṟa (Register) vaḻakkamu milli. appaṭiyē eḻuti vittālum mutal ēṭṭilāvatu kaṭici ēṭṭilāvatu eḻuti vippārkaḷ. vitta koñca kālattiṟkellām mutal ēṭṭiyum kaṭici ēṭṭiyum mutal mutal cellukaḷ arippatu vaḻakkam. ippaṭi arittu viṭukiṟatum kālanteriyāmal pōvatum iyaṟki. cila nūṟu varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa nūlkaḷi anēka caturyukaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷateṉṟu collik koḷvatu perumiyeṉṟu oru tappāṉa apippirāyam vaḷarntuviṭṭatu. ippaṭik kālanteriyāmal pōvatōṭukūṭa cilar tāṅkaḷ eḻutiya nūlukkup periyavarkaḷ peyari pōṭṭuviṭṭāl ati pūrvamāka eṇṇappaṭumeṉṟu niṉittup peyar māṟṟi vikkiṟārkaḷ. vicēṣamāka nītinūlkaḷilum uṇmi viḷakkum cāstiraṅkaḷilum uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭiyē aṉupōkattukku varakkūṭiya cāstiraṅkaḷilum ippaṭic ceykiṟatilli. āṉāl kaṟpaṉikaḷum kaṭṭukkatikaḷum niṟinta nūlkaḷilē ivikaḷi nām kāṇalām. tiruṣṭāntamāka stala purāṇaṅkaḷ putitu putitāy uṇṭākkappaṭuvati aṟivōm. akkati colvataṟku viyāca pakavāṉum atikkēṭṭu jaṉamējayarukkuc colla naṭuvil oru cūtapurāṇikarum, atit tūṇṭiviṭa oru nāratarum eḻutuvataṟku maṟṟoru viyācarum, eṅkiruntāvatu vantuviṭuvārkaḷ. avarkaḷ colvati viyācar eppaṭiyāvatu eḻuti viṭuvār. ataṉ piṉ ivarkaḷ eḻutiṉatellām vētamum purāṇamumākiviṭum. cūtapurāṇikar eṉpatum nāratar eṉpatum viyācar eṉpatum peyar vēṟṟumiyāl vēṟākat tōṉṟiṉum purāṇam eḻutiṉavar oruvar eṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. eḻutiṉavar iṉṉāreṉṟu namakkut terintiruntum ivar ippaṭi anēka stalaṅkaḷukkup purāṇaṅkaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṟār eṉṟu nām aṟintum cūtapurāṇikar, nāratar, viyācar pōṉṟa makāṉkaḷ peyarkaḷum, cila tēvarkaḷ peyarkaḷum varuvatiṉāl māttiram avarāl eḻutappaṭṭa yāvum uṇmiyeṉṟu nampi viṭukiṟōm. paricīlaṉi paṇṇāta ikkuruṭṭu nampikki intiyāvukkē ciṟantatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ākiyiṉāl purāṇaṅkaḷ colvatik koṇṭu māttiram nām intiyāviṉ kālatti niccayippatu kūṭāta kāriyam.
āyiram āyiramāṉa varuṣaṅkaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa caṅkatikaḷi pūrva carittiraṅkaḷik koṇṭē oruvāṟu collalāmēyoḻiya niccayamāyc colluvatu kūṭiya kāriyamāyilli. muccaṅkamum avikaḷirunta varuṣamum evvita āṣēpaṇiyumiṉṟi nām oppuk koḷḷak kūṭiya carittiramuṭiyatākat terikiṟatu. avvaruṣattik koṇṭu maṟṟa naṭavaṭikkikaḷiyum nām ottup pārttāl intiya pūrvīka carittiraṅkaḷiṉ uṇmi viḷaṅkum. teṉ maturiyum atic cērnta 49 nāṭukaḷum taṇṇīrukkuḷ mūḻkip pōṉatāl vēṟu evvitamāṉa tuppun tulaṅka villi. appaṭiyillātiruntāl namakku vēṇṭiya anēka ātāraṅkaḷ aṅkē kiṭittirukkum.
aḻintupōṉa lemūriyāvip paṟṟiyum ataṉ cuṟṟiṭaṅkaḷilirukkum iyaṟki amippiṉ cila kuṟippukaḷip paṟṟiyum tamiḻppāṣiyiṉ pūrvattip paṟṟiyum tamiḻ moḻikaḷ pala pāṣiyil kalantiruppatip paṟṟiyum cāttira ārāyccikkārarkaḷ colpavi uṇmiyeṉṟu pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉumcila nāḷ cellac cella itilum mēlāṉa cila ātāraṅkaḷ kiṭikkalām. atipūrvamāyuḷḷa lemūriyāvil tamiḻp pāṣiyē pēcappaṭṭu vantateṉṟum tamiḻppāṣiyē maṟṟellā pāṣikaḷukkum muntiṉateṉṟum cāstirikaḷ kūṟum apippirāyam cariyeṉṟu nāmum oppuk koḷḷukiṟōm. intumakā camuttirattiṉ teṉpakkattiluḷḷa kerkiyūlaṉ tīvuvari vicālittirunta pūmiyē oru kālattil atāvatu mutal ūḻikkumuṉ tamiḻ makkaḷ kuṭiyirunta nāṭākavum 4,400 varuṣaṅkaḷāka mutaṟ caṅkamirunta pūmiyākavum tōṉṟukiṟatu. itōṭu innāṭṭiṉ teṉpāricattil pala malit toṭarkaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭa kumariyeṉṟa malic cikaramiruntatākavum atiliruntu paḵṟuḷiyāṟu vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉāl veṭṭap paṭṭatākavum collappaṭukiṟatu. mēṟṟici malikaḷiliruntu arapik kaṭalil viḻum periya āṟṟik kiḻakkut ticiyil varumpaṭi viki vāykkāl veṭṭi viṭṭatu pōla ituvumirukkalāmeṉṟu colla vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. itu oru periya jīvanatiyāyirukka vēṇṭum. innatiyum kumari āṟum imayamalipōlotta kumarimalikaḷiṉ toṭarcci yiṉiṉṟu uṟpattiyāyirukkalāmeṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. immaliyum natiyum aḻinta kālattil annāṭṭiṟku vaṭa elliyā yirunta kumariyāṟum aḻintu pōyiṟṟu. itaṉuṭiya pūrvīkattiyum aṟintu koḷvataṟku atika ētukkaḷillātiruntālum aḻintupōṉa mutaṟ caṅkattiṉ kaṭici kālattiliruntu tappik kapāṭapurattukku vanta muṭattirumāṟaṉ eṉṟa kaṭici pāṇṭiyaṉum akastiyar tolkāppiyar pōṉṟa mutaṟ caṅkap pulavarkaḷum oruvāṟu mutaṟ caṅkattiṉ perumiyi iraṇṭāñ caṅkattukkup pulappaṭutti yiruppārkaḷeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. maṟṟappaṭi 4,440 varuṣaṅkaḷ naṭanta tamiḻ caṅkatti 4,449 vitva cirōmaṇikaḷ talimi vakittārkaḷeṉṟa tiṭṭamāṉa kaṇakku ēṟpaṭṭirukkāJ. itaṟku cumār 3,700 varuṣaṅkaḷukku piṉṉuṇṭāṉa maṟṟoru aḻiviṉāl muntiṉa ūḻiyiṉ aḻivu, nuṭpamāṉa cila carittiraṅkaḷallāta aṭṭavaṇiyāka māttiram collappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. appaṭiyiruntālum vitvaparampariyāka itu vaḻaṅki vantatiṉāl itu uṇmit tavaṟutal eṉṟu niṉikka iṭamilli. mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam aḻintu iṟṟikku cumār 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷ āyiṟṟeṉpatu yāvarum aṟinta viṣayam. appaṭiyiruntālum oru tamiḻc caṅkamiruntatākac collappaṭavilli. kiṟistu piṟanta iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil mūṉṟām caṅkam kalintupōṉapiṉ cumār 1,000 varuṣaṅkaḷāka atāvatu ki.pi. 1,200 varuṣaṅkaḷ varikkum pāṇṭiya rājakulattavarkaḷālē aracākṣi ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. ataṉpiṉ cumār 50 varuṣaṅkaḷāka āti cultāṉ eṉṉum makamatiya aracaṉum ataṉpiṉ vicuvanāta nāyakkar, tirumali nāyakkar pōṉṟa cila nāyakka vamcattavarkaḷum ataṉpiṉ murāri rāyar, appāji rāyar, cantā cāyapu, kāṉ cāyapu pōṉṟa anēkam cillari rājākkaḷum oru varuṣam iraṇṭu varuṣam pōṉṟa aṟpa kālaṅkaḷ oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka kalakañ ceytu koṇṭu aracākṣi ceytu vantārkaḷ. 1,801-m varuṣam maturiyum aticcērnta nāṭukaḷum māṭcimi taṅkiya āṅkilēyar āḷukiyil varukiṟa variyum nāṭṭil camātāṉamillātiruntaJ. taṉ uyiriyum kuṭumpattār uyirkaḷiyum taṉatu poruḷkaḷiyum kāppāṟṟa caktiyillātu tavittuk koṇṭirukkuṅ kālattil oru tamiḻccaṅkam vittu naṭattuvatu kūṭiya kāriyamākumā? atōṭu aṉṉiya pāṣikaḷil eḻutiya pala nūlkaḷum vārttikaḷum iṉiya tīñcoṟ ṟamiḻp pāṣiyik kalaṅkaṭittaJ.
26. māṭcimi taṅkiya vikṭōriyā cakkaravarttiṉi avarkaḷ kālattil maturi nālāvatu tamiḻccaṅkam ārampamāyiṟṟu.
evvuyiriyum taṉṉuyirpōl niṉittu aracāṭci ceytu varum āṅkilēya turittaṉattār uḷ nāṭṭuk kalakaṅkaḷiyaṭakki veḷināṭṭār paṭiyeṭuppit taṭuttu tuṣṭa rājarkaḷi nīkki kuṭikaḷ cukamuṟṟu vāḻvataṟku aṉukūlamāṉa tanti, tapāl, perum pātikaḷ, iruppup pātikaḷ, nīr pācaṉa vacatikaḷ, vittiyacālikaḷ, kalviccālikaḷ, kalācālikaḷ, niyāyastalaṅkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷi ēṟpaṭutti intiyāvic camātāṉa nilikkuk koṇṭu vantārkaḷ. tāyillāk kuḻantikaḷukku uṟṟa tāy pōla karuṇi curantu taṉ makkaḷilum arumiyāy niṉikkum āṅkilēya araciyāy viḷaṅkiṉa vikṭōriyā makārāṇi avarkaḷ intiyāviṉ rāja cutantaramum piṉ ēka cakkarātipatyamum peṟṟut taruma nīti tavaṟāmal 1,837 mutal 1,901 variyum cumar 64 varuṣaṅkaḷāka mikunta camātāṉattōṭu aracāṭci ceytu vantārkaḷ. ivvuttama aracāṭciyiṉ payaṉāka intiya tēcattiṉ uḷnāṭṭuk kalakaṅkaḷ ōyntaṉa. nīti tavaṟiya pala koṭuñceyalkaḷ oḻintaṉa. jaṉaṅkaḷ oṉṟu cēravum cakōtara urimi pārāṭṭavum pala caṅkaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. tamiḻp pāṣiyiṉ pūrvīkattiyum ataṉ coṟcuvi poruṭcuviyu maṟinta palar paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷit tēṭavum ati ārāycci ceyyavum acciṭavum oru capi kūṭṭavum ārampittārkaḷ. āṅkilēya aracāṭciyil intiyāvil mutal cakkirātipatyam peṟṟa vikṭōriyā cakkaravarttiṉi avarkaḷiṉ aracāṭciyiṉ 64vatu varuṣamākiya 1901vatu varuṣattil nālāvatu caṅkam ārampamāyiṟṟu. kaliyukam 3,200 il mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam kalintu pōka ataṟkuc cumār 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṉ kaliyukam 5,002 il maturi nālāvatu tamiḻc caṅkaṅkūṭi 13 varuṣaṅkaḷāka naṭantu varukiṟatiyum atil 251kku mēṟpaṭṭa pala vitvacirōmaṇikaḷ cērntiruppatiyum tamiḻip paṟṟiya ārāycci naṭantu varuvatiyum nām kāṇkiyil, itaṟku muṉ naṭanta mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiyum ataṉ perumiyiyum uṇmit tavaṟutaleṉṟu yār maṟukkakkūṭum? pālavaṉattam jamīṉtār srīmāṉ pāṇṭitturi tēvar avarkaḷiṉ perumuyaṟciyāl innālām caṅkam ārampittaJ. innālāñ caṅkattiṟku pālavaṉattam tamiḻc caṅka meṉṟāvatu pāṇṭitturi tamiḻccaṅkameṉṟāvatu peyar vikkalāmē. appaṭiyillāmal maturit tamiḻc caṅkam eṉṟu vittatāṉatu, itaṉ muṉ maturiyil naṭanta tamiḻc caṅkattiṉ peyariyē mutaṟkāraṇamākak koṇṭu vikkappaṭṭateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. itaṉ muṉ pirapalamāṉa mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiruntatiṉāl itaṟku nālām caṅkameṉṟa peyar vantatu.
innālām caṅkattip paṟṟiya cila mukkiya kuṟippukaḷ atāvatu, ārampitta kālam, kūṭṭappaṭṭa caṅkattil talimi vakittavarkaḷ, caṅkatti ātarittu varum rājarkaḷum kaṉavāṉkaḷum, caṅkatti naṭatti varuvatil pirayāci eṭuttuk koḷḷuṅ kaṉavāṉkaḷum, caṅkattil aṅkattiṉarāka irukkum vitvāṉkaḷum ākiya ivarkaḷip paṟṟic curukkamāyp pārppatu muntiya mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ uṇmiyi aṟiyātavarkaḷukku avaṟṟiṉ uṇmiyi veḷippaṭuttumeṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.

27. muccaṅkaṅkaḷirunta kālattip paṟṟi oruvāṟu collakkūṭiyavi.
kiṟistu piṟantu iṟṟikku 1,914 varuṣaṅkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. kaliyukam piṟantu 5,014 varuṣaṅkaḷākiṉṟaṉa. kiṟistu piṟantu 100 varuṣaṅkaḷ variyilum mūṉṟāvatu caṅkamiruntatākavum ataṉpiṉ illāmal pōṉatākavum collappaṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟāvatu caṅkamō 1,850 varuṣaṅkaḷ uttara maturiyil nilittiruntaJ. ākiyiṉāl iṟṟikku 3,664 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam ārampamāyiṟṟeṉṟu teḷivāyt terikiṟatu. kaliyukam tuvāraki yaḻintapiṉ ārampamāyiṟṟeṉṟum caṟṟēṟak kuṟiya akkālattil intu camuttirattiṉ ōramāyuḷḷa pala iṭaṅkaḷil piraḷayam uṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟum itaṉmuṉ nām pārttirukkiṟōm. ap piraḷayattil iraṇṭām caṅkamirunta kapāṭapuram aḻipaṭṭateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. appaṭiyāṉāl kaliyukattil 1,350 varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṟakē mūṉṟāvatu caṅkam ārampittatākac colla vēṇṭum. inta 1,350 varuṣaṅkaḷil koṟkiyilirunta pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ eṅkē yiruntārka ḷeṉṟu kēṭka nēriṭum. avarkaḷ ippōtirukkum uttara maturikku aintu āṟu milukku kīḻpākattirunta maṇavūril vantu taṅki aṅkē araṇmaṉi kaṭṭi atiliruntārkaḷeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. avviṭattiliruntu ippō tirukkum maturiyil oru ciṟiya ālayamum paṭṭaṇamum kaṭṭi maturiyil aracāṭci ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ muṉṉirunta iṭam paḻa maturiyeṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. iṭintupōṉa araṇmaṉikaḷum mēṭukaḷum iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vikiyāṟṟaṅ kariyilirunta maṇavūril arccuṉaṉ vantu taṅkiyatākavum appōtirunta cittarāṅkataṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya makaḷi avaṉ kaliyāṇañ ceytatākavum purāṇaṅkaḷil collappaṭukiṟatu. itikkoṇṭu mūlap piraḷayattiṉāl kapāṭapuram aḻikkappaṭṭapiṉ paḻa maturiyil allatu maṇavūril pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷ neṭunāḷ iruntārkaḷeṉṟum maṇavūri liruntē maturiyik kaṭṭi ataṉpiṉ caṅkam stāpittārkaḷeṉṟum nām niṉikkalām. kaliyuka ārampattiluṇṭāṉa piraḷayam pōka ataṉmuṉ oru piraḷayamum uṇṭāṉatāka nām aṟikiṟōm. atu cattiya virataṉuṭiya kālattil naṭantatāka eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. kaliyukam ārampikkum muṉ 3,700 varuṣaṅkaḷ kapāṭa purattil pāṇṭiya rājāṅkamiruntatākavum caṅkamirunta tākavum collappaṭuvatik koṇṭu iṟṟikku 8,700 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ oru piraḷayamuṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. antap piraḷayattukku muṉ teṉmaturiyil āṇṭukoṇṭirunta nilantarutiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ kālattu irunta tolkāppiyar iṭiccaṅkatti liruntāreṉṟu colvatik kavaṉikkiyil tolkāppiyam 8,700 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷateṉṟum akkālattil teṉ maturi kaṭalāl aḻikkap paṭṭateṉṟum tōṉṟukiṟatu. teṉ maturiyil 4,440 varuṣaṅkaḷāka caṅkamiruntatākac colluvati nām kavaṉikkiyil iṟṟikku caṟṟēṟak kuṟiya 13,000 varuṣattukku muṉ mutaṟcaṅkam ēṟpaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. itaṉ talimuṟik kaṇakkukaḷip pārttālum ātaritta rājarkaḷil kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavarkaḷ kaṇakkip pārttālum akkālattut tamiḻ naṭiyip pārttālum iccaṅka kālam uṇmiyāṉateṉṟu teḷivākat teriyum. kaṟpaṉikaḷ pala kalanta purāṇaṅkaḷik koṇṭu itaṉ niccayam collak kūṭātu. purāṇaṅkaḷ anēkamāy iṟṟikku cumār 1,000, 1,500 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉtāṉ eḻutappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu aṟivāḷikaḷ palar niṉikkiṟārkaḷ. maturit tiruviḷiyāṭaṟpurāṇattil 5,000, 8,000, 10,000, 15,000 varuṣaṅkaḷāka ovvoru pāṇṭiyaṉum āṇṭāṉeṉṟu colliyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl muccaṅkaṅkaḷip paṟṟic collumiṭattu 50, 63, 38 varuṣaṅkaḷ ovvoru talimuṟikku varukiṟatu. itilum cantati yaṟṟu iṭiyiṟ kaḻinta kālamum cērntirukkalāmeṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. ivikaḷik koṇṭu tamiḻppāṣi mikap pūrvamāṉa teṉṟum iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻil caṅkītamum 13,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyēyiruntateṉṟum colvatu uṇmiyeṉṟu teḷivākak kāṇkiṟōm.
iti vācikkum kaṉavāṉkaḷē! iṟṟikku 13,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ oru tamiḻc caṅkamiruntateṉpatiyum muṭimaṉṉarkaḷum muṉivarkaḷum ariya kali vallōrum atil cērntu iyal ici nāṭakameṉṉum muttamiḻiyum virutti ceytārkaḷ eṉpatiyum oruvēḷi āccariyamākavum maṟṟoruvēḷi cantēkamākavum eṇṇuvōm. pūrvakālattiluḷḷōr kālaṅkaḷit tiṭṭamāyk kuṟikkakkūṭiya tāṉa kuṟippukaḷ vikkātataṉāl, nām ippōtu aṟintu koḷvatu pirayāciyāyt tōṉṟukiṟatu. kālam tiṭṭamāka aṟiya vēṇṭumāṉāl puṟa carittiraṅkaḷiyum ciṟcila ētukkaḷiyum koṇṭu māttiram aṟiyakkūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. avvaḻakkamē ippōtu eḻutappaṭum pustakaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ippōtu nām vaḻaṅki varum anēka puttakaṅkaḷil itik kāṇalām. ipputtakaṅkaḷ rijisṭar ceyyappaṭā tiruntāl kālavariyaṟi colla muṭiyāmal niṟkum. itōṭu pūrva nūlkaḷil tāṅkaḷ eḻutiya varuṣatti māttiram vekutāṉiya, virōtikirutu eṉṟu pōṭṭuviṭukiṟatu vaḻakkam. itiṉāl muntiṉa pustakam pintiṉatākavum pintiṉa pustakam muntiṉatākavum eṇṇa nēriṭukiṟaJ. iruntālum taṟkālattil nākarīkamaṟṟatu eṉṟu nām niṉikkum pūrva eḻuttukkaḷil, tiruṣṭāntamāka, e, ma eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷi peṟum vaḻakkattit tolkāppiyar colvati nām kavaṉikkiyil tamiḻppāṣi mikap pūrvamuḷḷateṉṟē tōṉṟukiṟatu. mēlum pūmiyiṉ iyaṟki vaḷarcciyik kuṟittuk kūṟum ekkēl (Haeckel) eṉṉum tattuva cāstiriyiṉ apippirāyam iṉṉateṉṟu terintirukkiṟōm. avar ippūmiyil jīvarācikaḷ 5 kōṭi varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉamēyē iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kaṟkaḷiṉ naṭumattiyil kāṇappaṭum ippi, caṅku, natti pōṉṟa ciṟu pirāṇikaḷiṉ ōṭukaḷiṉ vaṭivattik koṇṭu tīrmāṉikkiṟār. ippaṭippaṭṭa kaṟkaḷi nām aṭikkaṭi pārttirukkiṟōm. ivar jīvappirāṇikaḷiliruntu maṉuṣaṉ eppaṭi uṇṭāṉāṉ eṉkiṟa uṇmiyip pirāṇikaḷiṉ elumpukaḷik koṇṭum ataṉpiṉ karuppācayattiṉ amippik koṇṭum karuviṉ mutal tōṟṟattik koṇṭum paṟkaḷik koṇṭum, taliyiṉ amippik koṇṭum mika virivākac colkiṟār. avikaḷil maṉuṣaṉ entak kālattil uṇṭāṉāṉ eṉpatip paṟṟiyum ātiyil avaṉ vacitta iṭattip paṟṟiyum aṅkiruntavarkaḷ pēci vanta pāṣiyip paṟṟiyum avar collum cila apippirāyaṅkaḷip pārppatu pirayōcaṉamā yirukkumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
28. kuraṅkiṉattiliruntu maṉuṣaṉ uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉpatu.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 352.
“Hence in the genealogy of the mammals we must derive man immediately from the Catarrhive group, and locate the origin of the human race in the Old World. Only the early root form from which both descended was common to them.”
“mammēliya vakuppic cērnta mirukaṅkaḷiṉ vamca aṭṭavaṇiyi nām pārkkumpōtu maṉitaṉ kāṟṟarkiv (Catarrhive) eṉṉappaṭṭa kuraṅkukaḷiṉ iṉattiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉavaṉ eṉṟum, maṉita jātiyē paḻiya ulakattiliruntu jeṉittatu eṉṟum colla vēṇṭiyatā yirukkiṟatu. āṉāl iru jātikkum potuvāyiruntatu etuveṉṟāl, avi iraṇṭum ātiyil uṟpattiyāvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyirunta ātijeṉaṉa rūpamē.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷik kavaṉikkumpōtu maṉitaṉ kuraṅkiṉattiliruntē uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉṟum maṉuṣarkaḷukkum kuraṅkukaḷukkum uṟpattikkuk kāraṇamāyirunta āti mūlastāṉam orē vitamāyirukkiṟateṉṟum collukiṟār. appaṭi uṟpattiyāṉa ātimaṉuṭa jāti paḻiya ulakattil jeṉittatākac collukiṟār. ippaḻiya ulakam intu camuttirattil iruntu muḻukippōṉatāka nām niṉikkum lemūriyāk kaṇṭamē. ikkaṇṭattiṉ iyaṟki amippiluḷḷa cila ceṭi koṭikaḷiyum maraṅkaḷiyuṅ koṇṭu ataṟku cuṟṟilumuḷḷa cila tīvukaḷiyum tēcaṅkaḷiyum elli yāka avar colluvati nām itaṉpiṉ pārppōm.
kuraṅkukaḷiṉ iṉattil kiḻakku tēcaṅkaḷil vacikkum kuraṅkukaḷi liruntē maṉitaṉ uṟpattiyākiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 259.
“The other group, to which man belongs, are the Eopitheca or eastern apes; they are found in Asia and Africa, and were formerly in Europe. All the eastern apes agree with man of the features that are chiefly used in Zoological Classification to distinguish between the two Simian groups, especially in the dentition.”
“maṉita jātiyic cērttuc collum maṟṟoru vakuppu kuraṅkukaḷukku īyōpittakkā allatu kīḻ tēcattuk kuraṅkukaḷ eṉṟu pēr. avikaḷ taṟkālam āciyāvilum āppirikkāvilum akappaṭum. muṟ kālattil avikaḷ airōppāvil iruntaṉa. cimiyeṉ vakuppic cērnta iraṇṭuvita kuraṅkukaḷiṉ vittiyācattiyum miruka cāstirattil pirittu eḻutumpōtu kiḻakkutēcak kuraṅkukaḷukkum maṉitarukkum anēka viṣayaṅkaḷil, mukkiyamāy pal viṣayattil, atika poruttam irukkiṟatākat terikiṟatu.”
kiḻakkut tēcam eṉṟu āciyāviyum āppirikkāviyum kuṟikkiṟār. āciyāviṉ kariyōraṅ kaḷilum āppirikkāviṉ kariyōraṅkaḷilum vacikkum kuraṅkukaḷil oru vakuppē maṉita jāti uṇṭākuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruntateṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 261.
“The apes of the Old World, or all the living or fossil apes of Asia, Africa and Europe, have the same dentition as man.”
“paḻiya ulakattiluḷḷa kuraṅkukaḷum, āciyā, āppirikkā, airōppāk kaṇṭaṅkaḷil taṟkālam irukkum kuraṅkukaḷum, oru kālam iruntu aḻintu pōṉatāka kaṟkaḷil patitta rūpam mūlamāy nām aṟiyum kuraṅku jātikaḷum maṉitaṉip pōlotta paṟkaḷiyuṭittāyiruntaṉa.”
itil paḻiya ulakattiliruntu aḻintupōṉa kuraṅkukaḷ kaṟkaḷiṉ naṭuvil kāṇappaṭuvatiyum av uruvaṅkaḷ maṉitaṉip pōlotta paṟkaḷiyuṭitāyiruntaṉa eṉpatiyum teḷivākac collukiṟār. avikaḷil aḻintu pōkāmal tappi mīntavi ippōtu ententa iṭaṅkaḷilirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇpōm.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 257.
“These living survivors are scattered far over the southern part of the Old World. Most of the species live in Madagascar, some in Sunda Islands, others on the minland of Asia and Africa. Some of these were almost as big as men, such as the diluvial lemurogonon Megaladapis of Madagascar.”
“avikaḷil tappi mīntu uyirōṭiruppavikaḷ paḻiya ulakattiṉ teṉpākaṅkaḷil anēka iṭaṅkaḷil citaṟuṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. anta jātiyil miccamāṉavikaḷ matakāskār tīvilum, cila cantā tīvukaḷilum, maṟṟavi āciya āppirikkā kaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ taripākaṅkaḷilum vacikkiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷil cila maṉitarippōl periyavikaḷ, mukkiyamāy matakāskār tīviluḷḷa ṭilūviyal lemurākaṉāṉa mekalaṭēppis (diluvial lemurogonon Megaladapis) eṉṉum jātikkuraṅkukaḷ.”
avikaḷil aḻivukkut tappi uyirōṭiruppavi paḻiya ulakam allatu lemūriyāviṉ pakkattu iṭamāṉa matakāskār, cantā mutaliya tīvukaḷilum āciyā āppirikkā kaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ taripākaṅkaḷilum vacikkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatākat teḷivāyt terikiṟatu.
29. maṉita uṟpattiyiṉ kālattip paṟṟi.
maṉitarkaḷ uṟpattiyāṉa kālattiyum avarkaḷ pēcum cakti peṟṟa kālattiyum oruvāṟu iṉṉateṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 203.
“The first appearance of man, or, to be more precise, the development of man from some closely related group of Apes, probably falls in either the miocene or the pliocene period, the middle or the last section of the Tertiary period. Others believe that man properly so-called man endowed with speech was not evolved from the non-speaking ape-man (Pithecan thropus) until the following, the anthropozoic age. In this fifth and last section of the organic history of the earth we have the full development and dispersion of the various races of men, and so it is called the Anthropozoic as well as the Quaternary period. In the imperfect condition of palcontological and ethnographical science we cannot as yet give a confident answer to the question whether the evolution of the human race from some extinct ape or lemur took place at the beginning of this or towards the middle or the end of the Tertiary period. However this much is certin the development of civilisation falls in the anthropozoic age, and this is merely an insignificant fraction of the vast period of the whole history of life. When we remember this, it seems ridiculous to restrict the word “history” to the civilised period. If we devide into a hundred equal parts the whole period of history of life, from the spontaneous generation of the first monera to the present day, and if we then represent the relative duration of the five chief sections or ages, as calculated from the average thickness of the strata they contin, as percentages of this, we get something like the following relation :
	(i) 	Archeolithic or archeozoic (Primordial) 	age 	... 	53.6
	(ii) 	Paleolithic or paleozoic (Primary) 	”	...	32.1
	(iii)	Mesolithic or Mesozoic (Secondary)	”	...	11.5
	(iv)	Cenolithic or cenozoic (Tertiary)	”	...	2.3
	(v)	Anthropolithic or anthropozoic (Quaternary)”	...	0.5
					100.0
In any case, the “Historical period: is an insignificant quantity compared with the vast length of the precedings ages, in which there was no question of human existence on our planet. Even the important Cenozoic or Tertiary period, in which the first placentals or higher mammals appear, probably amounts to little over two per cent of the whole organic age.”

“maṉitaṉiṉ āti uṟpattiyiṉ kālam, allatu karārāyc colla vēṇṭumāṉāl, maṉitaṉ avaṉukku neruṅkiya campantamuḷḷa oruvitak kuraṅku jātiyiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉa kālamāṉatu miyōcīṉ (Miocene) kālattilāvatu, piḷiyōcīṉ (Pliocene) kālattilāvatu atāvatu Tertiary kālattiṉ matti allatu kaṭicikkālam eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. maṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṉa niṉikkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟāl, maṉitaṉ atāvatu cariyāyp pēcakkūṭiya tattuvamuḷḷa maṉitaṉ, pēca caktiyaṟṟa kuraṅku maṉita jātiyiṉiṉṟum uṟpattiyāṉa kālam intak kālattilalla, itaṟkup piṉṉuḷḷa Anthropozoic kālattil eṉkiṟārkaḷ.
pūmiyil pul pūṇṭu miruka uṟpatti uṇṭāṉa inta aintāvatu āṟāvatu kaṭici kālattil tāṉ maṉitaṉiṉ pūraṇa uṟpattiyum avaṉ pala jātiyāyp pirinta viṣayamum naṭantaṉa. atiṉāltāṉ ataṟku Anthropozoic atāvatu maṉitaṉ uyirip peṟṟa kālameṉṟum nāṉkāvatu (Quarternary Perios) kālameṉṟum peyar. pūrva mirukaṅkaḷip paṟṟip pēcum cāstiramum pūrva jātikaḷip paṟṟip pēcum cāstiramum pūraṇappaṭātatāl maṉita jāti, aḻintupōṉa oru vālaṟṟa kuraṅku allatu Lemur eṉṉum mirukattiliruntu inta nālāvatu kālattiṉ tuvakkattilō allatu Tertiary  kālattiṉ naṭuvilō kaṭiciyilō uṟpattiyāṉateṉṟa kēḷvikkup patil urippatu kaṣṭamāyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl oṉṟu māttiram niccayam. nākarīkam virttiyāṉatu Anthropozoic kālattiltāṉ. āṉāl jīvarācikaḷiṉ muḻu carittirattiyum aṭakkikkoṇṭirukkum piramāṇṭamāṉa kāla aḷaviṟkuḷ inta havōcaṭiyīṭiṇaṭiiu kālamāṉatu oru ciṟu aṇṭamtāṉ. iti nām niṉikkiyil carittiram eṉpatu nākarīka kālattik kuṟikkiṟatu eṉṟu colvatu ēḷaṉam paṇṇuvatu pōl irukkiṟaJ. āti aṇu (Monera) tāṉāy uṟpattiyāṉa kāla mutal taṟkālam varikkumuḷḷa jīvarācikaḷiṉ kālam muḻutiyum nūṟu camapākaṅkaḷākap pirittu, aintu mukkiya kālaṅkaḷiṉ aḷaviyum avikaḷukkuḷḷa saṟṟēṟṟāviṉ (Strata) parumaṉukkut takkatāka avikaḷiṉ kālattiyum niccayittāl, piṉvarum Percentage (atāvatu nūṟṟukkum antantak kālaṅkaḷukkumuḷḷa tāratammiyam) varum :-
(i) 	āti tuvakkak kalliṉ kālam (Archeolithic or Archeozoic) (Primordial)  ... 	53.6
(ii) 	paḻiya kalliṉ kālam (Paleolithic or Paleozoic) (Primary)		...	32.1
(iii)	mattiya kalliṉ kālam (Mesolithic or Mesozoic (Secondary)		...	11.5
(iv)	pintiya kālak kalliṉ kālam (Cenolithic or Cenozoic) (Tertiary)	...	2.3
(v)    	maṉitar uṟpatti kalliṉ kālam (Anthropolithic or Anthropozoic)
		(Quaternary)								...	0.5
											100.0
eṉṟālum carittira kālatti maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup pārttāl nammuṭiya kirakamākiya pūmiyilēyē maṉita vācaṉiyillāta pūrva kālattiṉ aḷavukkum itaṟkum aṉantakāla vittiyācam irukkiṟaJ. antappūrva kālaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭappaṭumpōtu itō atika ciṟiyatāy irukkiṟaJ. mammēliyā vakuppic cērnta uyarnta pirāṇikaḷ uṇṭāṉa mukkiyamāṉa Cenozoic kālaṅkūṭa mottattil nūṟṟil iraṇṭutāṉ.”
itil iṟṟikkumuṉ jīvaṉ uṟpattiyāṉatāka niṉikkum kālatti 5 kōṭi varuṣamāka vittuk koṇṭu ati nūṟu pākamākap pirittu atil aintu kālam collukiṟār. atil nālāvatu kālattiṉ mattiyil kuraṅkiṉamum ataṉpiṉ maṉuṣaṉum uṇṭāyiruntāṉeṉṟum aintāvatu kālattil maṉuṣaṉ pēcavum taṉ iṣṭam pōl maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkup pōkavum varavum kūṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉeṉṟum collukiṟār. inta aintāvatu kālam iraṇṭu laṭcattu aimpatiṉāyiram varuṣameṉṟu avar kaṇakkāl terikiṟatu. inta iraṇṭu laṭcattu aimpatiṉāyiram varuṣattiṉ tuvakkattil pēcavum cañcarikkavum ārampitta maṉuṣaṉ cumār iṟṟikku 50,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālāvatu cila nākarīkamum tērcciyum peṟṟu viruttiyākiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nām niṉikkalām. iṟṟikku cumār 20,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyē avaṉ eḻutat terintavaṉākavum pala kalikaḷ terintavaṉākavumiruntirukka vēṇṭum.
30. mutalmutal maṉita jāti uṇṭāṉa iṭam lemūriyāveṉpaJ.
ippaṭi mutalmutal tērcci peṟṟa maṉuṣa jāti paḻiya ulakam allatu lemūriyāk kaṇṭattilēyē yiruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum akkaṇṭam aḻintapiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintu pōyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 264.
“The third, and last, stage of our animal ancestry is the true or speaking man (Homo), who was gradually evolved from the preceding stage by the advance of animal language into articulate human speech. As to the time and place of this real “creation of man” we can only express tentative opinions. It was probably during the Diluvial period in the hotter zone of the Old World either on the minland in tropical Africa or Asia, or on an earlier continent (Lemuria now sunk below the waves of the Indian Ocean), which stretched from East Africa (Madagascar, Abyssinia) to East Asia (Sunda Islands, further India). I have given fully in my “History of creation” the weighty reasons for climing this descent of man from the anthropoid eastern apes and shown how we may conceive the spread of the various races from this “Paradise” over the whole earth. I have also dealt fully with the relations of the various races and species of men to each other.
“nām mirukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu uṟpattiyāṉa mūṉṟupaṭikaḷiyum collu miṭattu avikaḷil kaṭicip paṭiyēteṉṟāl ōmō (Homo) atāvatu pēcak kūṭiya maṉitaṉē. inta ōmō (Homo) eṉṟa maṉitaṉ muntiṉapaṭiyiṉiṉṟum mirukapāṣi maṉitapāṣiyāy virttiyāṉatil nāḷā varttiyil jeṉittavaṉ. inta maṉitaṉ eppaṭi uṇṭāṉāṉeṉṟāvatu evviṭattil uṇṭāṉāṉeṉṟāvatu atika niccayamākac colla muṭiyāJ. anēkamāy paḻiya ulakattiṉ kāṅkiyāṉa pākattil jalappiraḷaya kālattil āppirikkā allatu āciyāviṉ taḷapākattilāvatu, allatu ataṟku muṉṉirunta lemūriyā eṉṉum aḻintu pōṉa kaṇṭattilāvatu uṇṭā yirukka vēṇṭum. (intu camuttirattil mūḻkippōṉa intak kaṇṭamāṉatu kīḻ āpirikkā atāvatu matakāskar apiciṉiyā tēcam mutal kīḻ āciyā atāvatu cantā tīvukaḷ kiḻakkuk kōṭi intiyā vari vicālittiruntaJ.) “ciṣṭippiṉ carittiram” eṉṟu nāṉ eḻutiya nūlil maṉitaṉ eppaṭi (yavōcaṭiyīṭiṉi) kiḻakku tēca kuraṅkukaḷiliruntu uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉpataṟkum eppaṭi ātiyil palajātiyārum nāṉ colliya kaṇṭamākiya intap paratīciṉiṉṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintu pōṉārkaḷ eṉpataṟkum tiṭamāṉa ātāraṅkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟiyirukkiṟēṉ. pala jātiyārukku muḷḷa vittiyācaṅkaḷiyum, orēvitamāṉa jātikaḷukkuḷ irukkappaṭṭa neruṅkiya campantattiyum antap pustakattil vistāramāyc colli yirukkiṟēṉ.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷi nām kavaṉikkiyil pēcakkūṭiya maṉitaṉ, pēcakkūṭāta vālillāk kuraṅkiṉiṉṟum uṟpattiyāṉāṉ eṉṟum, miruka pāṣiyiṉiṉṟē maṉita pāṣi nāḷaṭivil viruttiyāṉateṉṟum, appaṭi viruttikku vanta atāvatu pēcakkūṭiya maṉitaṉ uṣṇap piratēcattil vacittāṉeṉṟum, atu oru kālattil muḻukippōyiṟṟeṉṟum, am muḻukip pōṉa iṭamē lemūriyāveṉṟum, atu āppirikkā, matakāskār, apiciṉiyā, cantā, intiyā mutaliya pūpākaṅkaḷukku camīpattilirunta teṉṟum, inta iṭamē ātiyil maṉitar uṇṭāṉa pāratīcāyirunta teṉṟum, atu aḻintu pōṉa piṉ ataṟku cuṟṟup pakkaṅkaḷiluḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintu pōṉārkaḷeṉṟum teḷivākat terikiṟatu. lemūriyā eṉṉum inta paḻiya ulakattil teṉ maturiyiruntateṉṟum, teṉmaturiyil tamiḻc caṅkam stāpikkappaṭṭu 4,400 varuṣaṅkaḷukkup piṉ kaṭalāl aḻintupōṉateṉṟum, avviṭattil pēcivanta tamiḻp pāṣi pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭu pala pāṣikaḷilum aṅkaṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṟateṉṟum itaṉmuṉ pārttōm. ittamiḻp pāṣiyē iyaṟkiyāyuḷḷa mirukapāṣikaḷiṉ cila ōcikaḷi yuṭiyatāyirukkiṟa teṉṟum itu oppaṟṟa taṉippāṣiyāy nāḷatu variyum iruntu varukiṟateṉṟum itaṉmuṉ kavaṉittōm. āṉāl appaṭi oru pāṣi ellā pāṣikaḷukkum ātiyāyiruntirukkumā eṉṟu nām niṉippōm. avviṣayattil ekkēl (ḻayanumanaṭa) eṉṉum makā tattuva cāstiriyār colluvatip pārppōm.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 203.
“All philogists of any competence in their science now agree that all human languages have been gradually evolved from very rudimentary beginnings.”
“taṅkaḷ cāstirattil nipuṇar eṉṟu oppuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟa ellāp pāṣā cāstirikaḷum maṉitarāl pēcappaṭum ellā pāṣikaḷum ciṟu tuvakkaṅkaḷiliruntu koñcam koñcamāy viruttikku vantavi eṉṟu oppukkoḷkiṟārkaḷ.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷik kavaṉikkumpoḻutu mirukapāṣiyiliruntu maṉitapāṣi pēca ārampittavarkaḷirunta lemūriyāvi āti pāṣi yirunta iṭameṉṟum, anta lemūriyā nāṭṭil ēḻu perumpākaṅkaḷilum pēcappaṭṭu vanta pāṣiyē ātipāṣiyeṉṟum colla niyāyamirukkiṟaJ.
31. lemūriyā nāṭṭilē mutal mutal maṉitarkaḷ uṟpattiyāṉatu pōla mutal mutal pēcappaṭṭa pāṣiyum lemūriyā nāṭṭilēyē uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉpatu
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 204.
“As we have been convinced from Comparative anatomy and ontogeny, and from paleontology, that all past and living vertebrates descent from a common ancestor, so the comparative study of dead and living Indo-Germanic tongues proves beyond question that they are all modifications of one primitive language. This view of their origin is now accepted by all the chief philologists who have worked in this branch and are unprejudiced.
“mirukaṅkaḷiṉ tēkakkūṟukaḷip parīṭcippatālum, avikaḷiṉ āti uṟpattiyi nōkkuvatālum, muṟkālattil uḷḷa mirukaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam tēkakkūṟu mutaliyavikaḷik kavaṉippatālum, ātikāla taṟkāla mutukelumpuḷḷa iṉaṅkaḷellām oru potu muṟpitāviṉiṉṟu uṟpattiyāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu nām niccayippatupōlavē, iraṇṭu jermāṉik (Indo-Germanic)  vakuppic cērnta iṟantupōṉa pāṣikaḷiyum ippōtu uyirōṭirukkum pāṣikaḷiyum nām parīṭcittup pārkkiyil avikaḷellām oru ātipāṣiyiṉiṉṟu uṇṭāyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu tīrmāṉikkalām. ippaṭi avikaḷ uṟpattiyāyiṉa eṉṟa koḷki yāṉatu inta viṣayattil cirattiyeṭuttut tāratammiyam pārkkāmal uḻitta ellā pāṣā vitvāṉkaḷālum oppuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.”
mēṟkaṇṭa mirukaṅkaḷiṉ tēkakkūṟupāṭukaḷiyum mutukelumpiyum kavaṉikkiyil ellā jīvarācikaḷum oru muṟpitāviṉiṉṟu uṇṭāṉavi yeṉṟu nām niṉikkiṟataṟku ētuvirukkiṟatu pōlavē, pūrvamāyuḷḷa aṉēka pāṣikaḷ ātiyil oru pāṣiyiliruntē uṇṭāṉaviyeṉṟu vicārikkum vivēkikaḷ oppuk koḷḷuvārkaḷ eṉkiṟār.
āti maṉitarkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷukkup pirintupōṉapiṉ avarkaḷ ātiyil pēciya pāṣiyē pala māṟutalkaḷiyaṭintu vevvēṟu peyarkaḷi aṭintateṉṟu itaṉmuṉ pārttirukkiṟōma. appaṭi vaḻaṅkiya pāṣikaḷuḷ ātipāṣiyiṉ vārttikaḷ kāṇappaṭuvatu pirāṇikaḷiṉ tattuva cāstirattik kāṭṭum tiruṣṭāntaṅkaḷip pārkkilum atika niccayañ collak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟateṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷil collukiṟār.
“The Evolution of Man.” By Professor Haeckel P. 205.
“We find just the same thing in comparing the various dead and living languages that have developed from a common primitive tongue. If we examine our genealogical tree of the Indo-Germanic languages in this light, we see at once that all the older or parent tongues, of which we regard the living varieties of the stem as divergent dughter grand-dughter languages, have been extinct for some time. The Aryo-Romanic, and the Slavo Germanic tongues have completely disappeared; so also the Aryan, the Greco-Roman, the Slavo-Lettic, and the ancient Germanic. Even their dughters and grand-dughters have been lost; all the living Indo-Germanic languages are only related in the sense that they are divergent discendants of common stem forms. Some forms have diverged more, and some less, from the original stem-form.
This easily demonstrable fact illustrates very well the analogous case of the origin of the vertebrate species. Phylogenetic comparative philology here yields a strong support to phylogenetic comparative zoology. But the one can adduce more direct evidence than the other, as the palentological material of philology the old monuments of the extinct tongue have been preserved much better than the paleontological material of zoology the fossilised bones and imprints of vertebrates.”
