“makammatiyar rājāṅkattiṟkuriya ōr jātiyārāy intukkaḷuṭaṉ mutal mutal kalantatu 11-m nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ. atu mutal intiya caṅkītattil ōrvita māṟutal uṇṭāka ārampittaJ. makammatiyar caṅkīta cāstiratti atikamāy apivirttikkuk koṇṭu varāmal, caṅkīta vittiyāp piyācatti ātarittu vantatākat terikiṟatu. avarkaḷ tāṅkaḷē cākittiyam, kavikaḷ ceytatākavum anēka putuvitamāṉa pāṭṭukaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇiṉatākavum, anēka putumātiriyāṉa vāttiyaṅkaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇiṉatākavum terikiṟatu. 1,294-m varuṣattil ṉuyuuya nakaram allāvuṭiṉ eṉpavarāl muṟṟuki pōṭappaṭṭu, 1,310-m varuṣattil teṉṉintiyāvāṉatu avaruṭiya taḷakarttaṉākiya mālik kāpar eṉpavaṉāl pūraṇamāy jeyikkappaṭṭa pōtu, caṅkītamāṉatu veku tirupti yāṉa nilimiyil iruntatākavum, ellāc caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷōṭu avarkaḷukkup pāṭam collik koṭutta intu vitvāṉkaḷiyum ciṟipiṭittuk koṇṭupōy vaṭakkē kuṭiyēṟṟiṉatākavum collap paṭukiṟatu. pērpōṉa pāracīkak kaviyum caṅkīta vitvāṉumāṉa amīrkusru eṉpavaṉ allāvuṭiṉ aracāṇṭa camayattil intiyāvukku vantatākavum, camastāṉa vitvāṉkaḷiṉ tiṟamiyic cōtikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu ṭillikku vantirunta takṣaṇattic cērnta nāyak kōpāl eṉpavaṉi jeyittatākavum collappaṭukiṟatu. amīrkusru eṉpavaṉ ātikālattu tiritantiri vīṇikku Satar eṉṉum pērkoṭuttatākavum avaṉ irākaṅkaḷi 12 mōkaṅkaḷākap pirittatākavum, avikaḷ piṟpāṭu maṟṟa makammatiyarāl 24 cōpaṅkaḷākavum 48 kusvaṅkaḷākavum pirikkappaṭṭa tākavum collappaṭukiṟatu.”
itikkoṇṭu vaṭanāṭṭil teṉṉintiya caṅkītattip pōl uyarvuḷḷa caṅkīta muṟi illiyeṉṟu kaṇṭa maṟṟavar teṉṉāṭṭi liruntu vitvāṉkaḷik koṇṭu pōy vaṭanāṭṭil kuṭiyēṟṟi, caṅkītatti virutti ceytārkaḷeṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. intiyā viṟkup paṭiyeṭuttu vanta makā alekcāntarum intiyāviliruntu caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷiyum maṟṟum cila vitvāṉkaḷiyum kūṭṭik koṇṭu pōṉāṉ eṉṟu parampariyāyc collappaṭukiṟatu.
vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅki vanta intiya caṅkītam makammatiyaruṭiya kālattil mikavum īṉastitiyaṭintu intustāṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅki vantatu eṉpatip piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil aṟiyalām.
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 5.
“In later years music became a distinct trade, especially under Musalman rulers and passed into the hands of the lower orders and the unlearned; and to this cuse operating through a long succession of years, the differences between the Hindustani and Karnatic systems must be in a great measure attributed.”
“piṟkālattil caṅkītamāṉatu vicēṣamāy makammatiya aracāṭciyiṉ kālattil, oru viyāpāram pōla ākiya, kīḻ jātiyāruṭiya kiyilum paṭippaṟiyātavarkaḷ kiyilum pōyc cērntaJ. inta vitamāy anēka āṇṭukaḷ kaḻiyavē anēka māṟutalkaḷ uṇṭāyviṭṭaṉa. inta māṟutalkaḷē anēkamāy karnāṭaka caṅkītattukkum intustāṉ caṅkītattukkumuḷḷa vittiyācattiṟku ātikāraṇam.”
akpar kālattilirunta caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷiyum avarkaḷ upayōkitta curaṅkaḷiṉ nuṭpattiyum piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil aṟiyalām.
The Indian Empire, by W.W. Hunter, P. 110-111.
“Hindu music, after a period of excessive elaboration sank under the Mahomedans into a state of arrested development. Of the 36 chief musicians in the time of Akbar, only 5 were Hindus. Not content with tones and semitones, the Indian musicians employ a more minute subdivision, together with a number of tonal modifications, which the Western ear neither recognises nor enjoys. Thus they divide the octave into 22 subtones instead of the 12 tones and semitones of the European scale. This is one of several fundamental differences, but it alone suffices to render Indian music barbaric to us; giving it the effect of a Scotch ballad in a minor key, sung intentionally a little out of tune.”
“vekutūram viruttiyaṭintirunta intiya caṅkītamāṉatu makammatiyar aracāṇṭa kālattil virutti kuṉṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akpar kālattil irunta 36 cirēṣṭa caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷuḷ intiyar aintē aintu pērtāṉ. muḻu curaṅkaḷum ari curaṅkaḷum pōtumeṉṟirāmal intiya caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ, kāl, arikkāl mutaliya nuṭpamāṉa vittiyācamuḷḷa ciṟu curaṅkaḷiyum upayōkikkiṟārkaḷ. inta aṇuppiramāṇamāṉa curaṅkaḷ airōppiyarukkut terikiṟatumilli, appērppaṭṭa curaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭum rākaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ piriyappaṭukiṟatumilli. airōppiya caṅkītattil iruppatupōl oru stāyiyi muḻutum ariyumāka 12 curaṅkaḷāyp pirikkāmal, 22 curaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟārkaḷ. iraṇṭu caṅkītattiṟkumuḷḷa anēka vittiyācaṅkaḷil itu oṉṟu. āṉāl inta oru kāraṇattiṉā lēyē intiya caṅkītam nammuṭiya kātukaḷukku anākarīkamuḷḷatākat tōṟṟukiṟaJ. atu tāḻnta curattil (āinṭica manala il) vēṇumeṉṟu curam kūṭṭik kuṟittup pāṭappaṭukiṟa Scotch ballad -ai ottirukkiṟa teṉṟu collalām.”
intiyāvi mikunta nērmiyuṭaṉ (1556-1605) cumār 50 varuṣaṅkaḷ āṇṭu koṇṭirunta, akpar cakkiravarttiyiṉ capiyil 36 cirēṣṭa caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷiruntārkaḷ eṉṟum, atil intiya caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ aintē aintu pēr māttiram iruntārkaḷeṉṟum terikiṟatu. tañcāvūr camastāṉattic cērnta ariyalūril irunta oru jākīrtār kacciyuvareṅka pūpati eṉpavar taṉ kālattil 365 tērnta karnāṭaka caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷi vittu ātarittār eṉṟum, avarkaḷāl kacciyuvareṅkapūpati eṉṟa muttiriyuṭaṉ anēka varṇaṅkaḷum kīrttaṉikaḷum pāṭappaṭṭiruk
kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum nām aṟikiyil vaṭatēcattil intiya caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ evvaḷavu coṟpamāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟu kāṇpōm. mēlum, avarkaḷ mika nuṭpamāṉa kāl, arikkāl, mutaliya curaṅkaḷiyum cērttu upayōkappaṭuttik koṇṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. kāl arikkāl curaṅkaḷi intiya caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ upayōkittuk koṇṭu vantārkaḷ eṉṟu coṉṉavar oru stāyiyil 22 curaṅkaḷākap pirikkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu collukiṟār. itu muṉṉukkup piṉ ovvātirukkiṟatu. ēḻu curaṅkaḷum 22 curaṅkaḷākap pirintatu eṉṟum, avikaḷ kāl arikkāl eṉṟa aḷavil varavilli yeṉṟum itiṉ piṉ pārppōm.
3. teṉṉintiya caṅkītam vēṟu, vaṭa intiya caṅkītam vēṟu eṉpatip paṟṟi.
itu variyum nām pārttavikaḷik koṇṭu, teṉṉintiyāvil nāratar akastiyar mutaliyavarkaḷāl stāpikkappaṭṭa teṉṉintiya caṅkītamāṉatu vaṭanāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu cila curaṅkaḷic cērttukkoṇṭu tēcikamāki intustāṉi caṅkītam eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ vaṭintiyāvil vaḻaṅki vantateṉṟu teḷivāyt terikiṟatu. mārkkam eṉṟa tēvakāṉam teṉṉintiyāvilum, intustāṉiyeṉṟa tēcikakāṉam vaṭintiyāvilum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟateṉṟu piṉvarum vacaṉaṅkaḷāl aṟiyalām.
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 12.
“Of the two systems practised in Southern India at the present time, the Hindustani is somewhat akin to that of Northern India and Bengal. It is practised mostly by Mussalman musicians while the Karnatic is confined more to those of the Southern races. The latter which may be called the national music of the South, is far more scientific and refined than the Hindustani and its professors arc, as a rule, men of much better education, a fact that is not without influence upon their music and seems apparent in all their melodies but particularly in the renderings they give of them.”
“taṟkālam teṉṉintiyāvil vaḻaṅkum iraṇṭu muṟikaḷil intustāṉi caṅkītamāṉatu, vaṭa intiyāvilum paṅkāḷattilum vaḻaṅkum caṅkītattiṟkuc caṟṟu ottirukkiṟaJ. atu vicēṣamāy makamatiya caṅkītakkārarālum karnāṭaka caṅkītamāṉatu teṉṉintiya jātiyārālum muṟiyē upayōkikkappaṭukiṉṟaJ. teṉṉintiya jātiyāriṉ caṅkīta meṉṟaḻikkappaṭum karnāṭaka caṅkītamāṉatu intustāṉi caṅkītattiviṭa atika cāstirōktamāṉatum atika cuttamāṉatu māyiruppatu mallāmal atippaṭikkum vitvāṉkaḷum intustāṉi caṅkītattip paṭippavari viṭak kalviyaṟivil tērntavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatu perumpālum uṇmiyē. ippaṭik kalviyil apiviruttiyilli yeṉṟu colluva tāṉatu intustāṉi caṅkītattil vaḻaṅkum irākaṅkaḷiyum vicēṣamāy avikaḷi avarkaḷ pirastāram ceytu kāṇpippatiyum kavaṉittāl naṉṟāy viḷaṅkum.”
mēl vacaṉaṅkaḷil teṉṉintiya jātiyāriṉ caṅkīta meṉṟaḻikkappaṭum karnāṭakamāṉatu intustāṉi caṅkītattiviṭa cāstirayuktamāṉateṉṟum kalappillāmal cuttamāyirukkiṟateṉṟum kalvi aṟivil tērntavarkaḷāl appiyācikkappaṭṭu varukiṟateṉṟum ciṟanta rākap pirastāramuṭiya tāyirukkiṟa teṉṟum collukiṟār.
teṉṉintiyāvil teṉpākattil aḻintupōṉa teṉṉintiya kaṇṭattil teṉ maturiyirunta kālattiyum atilirunta caṅkap pulavarkaḷiyum atil caṅkītam mikavum tērcci peṟṟirunta teṉpatiyum nām itaṉ muṉ pārttirukkiṟōm. avarkaḷil caṅkītattip paḻakiyavarkaḷum vīṇi vācikkat tērntavarkaḷum teyva cannitiyil narttaṉam paṇṇit teyvattit toḻutu koṇṭavarkaḷum yāreṉṟu vicārippōmēyāṉāl avvirājyattiyāṇṭa muṭimaṉṉarkaḷum avatāra mūrttikaḷum iḷavaracarkaḷum pirapukkaḷumāyiruntārkaḷeṉṟu nām perumi pārāṭṭik koḷvōm. kirīṭātipatikaḷākiya taṅkaḷ aracarkaḷ caṅkītattilum paratattilum mikunta piriyamuḷḷavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷeṉṟu aṟinta maṟṟavarum teṉṉintiyāviṟku vanta piṟarum caṅkītattiyum paratattiyum mikavum appiyācittut tērntavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. mēlum, rājāṅkatti viṭṭu tapacu ceyyac ceṉṟa rājariṣikaḷum avarkaḷ ciṣyavarkkaṅkaḷāṉa teṉṉāṭṭu antaṇarum taṅkaḷ tapacukku aṉukūlamākac caṅkītatti upayōkappaṭuttik koṇṭu vantārkaḷ. ivvitamāka jaṉaṅkaḷil uyarntavarkaḷāka matikkappaṭum tapacikaḷālum kaṟṟaṟintavarkaḷālum āriyarālum caṅkītam pēṇappaṭṭu mika arumiyuṭiyatāka eṇṇappaṭṭatu. ippaṭiyāvarālum arumiyākak koṇṭāṭappaṭṭa caṅkītam kōvil kaḷilum vitīka karumaṅkaḷilum kaliyāṇaṅkaḷilum upayōkikkappaṭṭu vantatu.
teṉṉāṭṭil pāṇṭiya rājyam nātaṉaṟṟup pōṉapiṉ caṅkītamum tēṭuvāraṟṟuppōka tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarkaḷiloruvarāṉa cōḻa rājākkaḷāl viruttiyaṭintu vantatu. tamiḻnāṭukaḷil oṉṟākiya cōḻa nāṭum cōḻa aracarkaḷi iḻantapiṉ vaṭuka rājākkaḷālum avarkaḷukkup piṉ makārāṣṭira rājākkaḷālum iṟṟikku 60 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ variyum āḷappaṭṭu vantatu. atil cōḻa rājākkaḷālum nāyakka rājākkaḷ cilarālum, makārāṣṭira rājākkaḷ cilarālum oruvāṟu caṅkītamum paratamum pēṇappaṭṭu vantaṉa. āti kālantoṭṭut toṭarcciyāyt teṉṉintiyā vilēyē caṅkītam vaḷarntu vantirukkiṟateṉpati piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 5.
“Music has almost without interruption flourished there (in Southern India) from very remote ages.
The higher branches of musical profession were formely confined to either Brahmins (Bhagavatars) or to men of very high caste. Music being of divine origin was regarded as sacred, and it was considered impious for any but men of the caste to wish to acquire any knowledge of its principles. It was and still is called the fifth Veda. Hence the ancient Brahmins of the country would have excommunicated any of their number who would have so far presumed as to betray the sacred writings to any but the elect, whose mouths only were esteemed sufficiently holy to utter words so sacred. Indeed it was the knowledge of which they were possessed that was the chief cuse of the reverence and adoration pid to the Brahmins of old and which gave them power and influence they prized so much. It was thus that the ancient musicians sang their own composition.”
“caṅkītamāṉatu teṉṉintiyāvil āti kālantoṭṭu toṭarcciyāy vaḷarntu vantirukkiṟatu.
muṟkālattil caṅkītatti uyarnta toḻilākak koṇṭavarkaḷ pirāmaṇa pākavatarkaḷāyāvatu uyarkulattic cērntavarkaḷāyāvatu iruntārkaḷ. caṅkītamāṉatu kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntu jaṉittapaṭiyāl atu vitīka muṟiyic cērntatāka eṇṇappaṭṭatu māttiramalla. ataṉ muṟikaḷi uyarkulattār māttiram paṭikkalām maṟṟavar paṭittāl atu paktikku virōtameṉṟum eṇṇappaṭṭatu. caṅkītamāṉatu āti mutal aintām vētamāyiruntatu māttiramalla, ellārālum appaṭiyē oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭu antap pērāl aḻikkappaṭukiṟatāyumirukkiṟaJ. ākiyāl intiyāviṉ āti pirāmaṇarallāta maṟṟavarukku yārāvatu collik koṭukkat tuṇiyumpaṭcattil avarkaḷi jātiyiliruntu vilakkip pōṭṭatu mallāmal, vētaviṣayaṅkaḷi eṭuttuc colvataṟkut taṅkaḷ vāykaḷ māttiramē paricuttamāṉaviyeṉṟu eṇṇiyum vantārkaḷ. āti pirāmaṇarukkuc celuttappaṭṭa vaṇakkamum mariyātiyum antastum atikāramum veku mēṉmiyāka eṇṇivanta avarkaḷ celvākkum intak kāraṇattiṉālēyē. ākiyāl āti caṅkītavitvāṉkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya conta cāhittiyaṅkaḷiyē pāṭi vantārkaḷ.”
ivvākkiyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkiyil caṅkītamāṉatu kaṭavuḷiṭatti liruntuṇṭāṉateṉṟum ati mikunta mēṉmiyāy ataṉ rakaciyaṅkaḷi maṟṟavar aṟiyāvaṇṇam vittiruntārkaḷeṉṟum collukiṟār.
āriyar teṉṉintiyāviṟku varum muṉṉatākavē caṅkītamum atic cērntatāṉa paratam, tāḷam mutaliya aṅkaṅkaḷum mika vistāramaṭintu vīṇi, pullāṅkuḻal, mirutaṅkam mutaliya vāttiyak karuvikaḷuṭaṉ appiyācikkappaṭṭu vantateṉṟum, avikaḷ kōyilkaḷilum rāja araṇmaṉikaḷilum mukkiyamāy upayōkappaṭṭu vantateṉṟum itaṉ muṉ pārttirukkiṟōm. āriyar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ teṉṉintiya caṅkītattik kaṟṟuk koṇṭu atil tērntavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. vēta culōkaṅkaḷi eppaṭi nāḷatu variyum piṟarukkuc collik koṭukkak kūṭātu eṉṟa eṇṇamuṭiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷō atupōlavē maṟṟavariṭat tiliruntu kaṟṟukkoṇṭa teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiyum piṟarukkuc collik koṭukka maṉamaṟṟavar kaḷāṉārkaḷ. aṉṟiyum pūrvamāyc ceyta tamiḻ uruppaṭikaḷi nīkkip putitākat taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ paramparikkeṉṟu camaskirutam teluṅkupōṉṟa pāṣikaḷil uruppaṭikaḷ ceytu avaṟṟiyē vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avikaḷ muṟṟilum pūrvatamiḻ naṭiyiyē aṉucarittu irukkiṟatēyoḻiya vēṟilli. itaṉāl kālakkiramattil teṉṉintiyāviṉiṉṟuṇṭāṉa caṅkītam vaṭanāṭṭiṉ camaskiruttiliruntuṇṭāṉatāka eṇṇumpaṭiyāyiṟṟu.
mēlum karnāṭaka caṅkītamum intustāṉ caṅkītamum vevvēṟu eṉpatiyum intustāṉi caṅkītattiviṭa kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntuṇṭāṉatāka eṇṇappaṭum karnāṭaka caṅkītattil kaṭavuḷukkirukkum tējacum pirakācamum cūḻntukoṇṭikkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum paktikkuriya nūlkaḷ yāvum ic caṅkītattil campantappaṭṭēyirukkiṟatākavum piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 2,3 and 4.
“Since the Sangita Parijata which is believed to be one of the latest of these Sanskrit works, had been written by Ahobila, two separate schools or systems of music have arisen and are now known by the names of Hindustani and Karnatic. The Karnatic appears to have been elaborated as a distinct system subsequent to the advent of the Aryans to the south of India. The two systems although sprung from the same origin have since undergone independantly considerable changes and are now totally distinct from each other.
Of Hindu music in Southern India, since the fall of the Hindu Empire of Vijayanagar Tanjore has been the only school and from it those of Travancore and other places have doubtless been founded.
Mahomedan music taken as a while, has little t recommend itself even at the present day. The ideans professed by Hindus offer a curious contrast for music from a Hindu standpoint. It is associated with all that is bright and sweet in life; its origin ascribed directly to divine providence cuses it to be regarded as surrounded by a halo of sanctity. Almost all the religious literature of the Hindus breathes music.”
“samaskiruta nūlkaḷil coṟpakālattukku muṉtāṉ eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉṟu oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭavikaḷil oṉṟākiya caṅkīta pārijātam (Sangita Parijatam) akōpilar eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭa kāla mutal iraṇṭu vittiyācamāṉa caṅkīta muṟikaḷ atāvatu intustāṉi, karnāṭakam eṉpavi uṇṭāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṉ teṉpākattil kuṭiyēṟiṉa kālattiṟkup piṟpāṭu karnāṭaka caṅkītamāṉatu pirattiyēkamāṉa ōr muṟiyāy ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. iraṇṭu muṟikaḷum orē uṟpattiyiliruntu uṇṭāṉapōtilum taṉittaṉiyē oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu campantamillāmalē pala māṟutalkaḷi yaṭintu oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu muṟṟum pētamāṉavikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
vijayanakara rājyam viḻunta kālamutal teṉṉintiya caṅkīta muṟikaḷil tañcinakar muṟi oṉṟutāṉ vicēṣamāṉatākat terikiṟatu. in nakariliruntutāṉ tiruvaṉantapurattukkum maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuc caṅkīta muṟikaḷ paraviṉa.
makammatiya caṅkītattit tokiyāka eṭuttukkoḷḷum paṭcattil, taṟkālam yāvarālum piriyappaṭṭu oppukkoḷḷakkūṭiya amcaṅkaḷ atil atikam kiṭiyātu. āṉāl intu caṅkītattip pārttālō, intukkaḷuṭiya caṅkīta amcaṅkaḷukkum yāvarum āccariyappaṭakkūṭiya cila vittiyācaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intukkaḷuṭiya caṅkītamāṉatu ētētu makimiyāyum namatu jīviyattil iṉpamāyum uḷḷatu eṉṟu niṉip pōmō avikaḷōṭellām campantappaṭṭēyirukkiṟatu. caṅkītamāṉatu kaṭavuḷiṭamiruntu uṇṭāṉatu eṉkiṟa eṇṇam pūrvamāyirukkiṟapaṭiyāl kaṭavuḷukkirukkum oruvitamāṉa tējacum pirakācamum ati eppōtum cūḻntu niṟkiṟatākak koṇṭāṭappaṭukiṟaJ. intukkaḷuṭiya paktikkuriya nūlkaḷellām caṅkītavācaṉiyuḷḷatākavēyirukkiṉṟaṉa.”
ivvākkiyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkiyil caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya 300 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta pārijātakkārar, akkālattiṟku cumār 400 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉirunta caṅkīta ratṉā karattiṉ curuti muṟi teṉṉintiya kāṉattil vaḻaṅki vanta curutikaḷukku ottu varavilli yeṉṟa apippirāyattiyuṭiyavarāy ippōtu vīṇiyil vaḻaṅkivarum 12 curaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk koṇṭu mītiyāṉa pattu ciṟu curaṅkaḷiyum taḷḷi aḷavu collukiṟār. avar vākkiyaṅkaḷil mītiyāṉa pattiyun taḷḷi rāka laṭcaṇam collukiṟēṉ eṉkiṟār. itikkoṇṭu avar kālattilēyē caṅkīta ratṉākarar curutimuṟi karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ curuti muṟikku ottatallaveṉṟa vivātam iruntatākat terikiṟatu. “tuvāvimcati curutikaḷ” eṉṟa col eppaṭi vantateṉpatip paṟṟi itaṉ piṉ pārppōm. karnāṭaka caṅkītam pārijātakkārarukkup piṟakuṇṭāṉateṉṟu tavaṟutalākac coṉṉavarkaḷiṉ apippirāyattik koṇṭu Capt. Day ippaṭi eḻutiṉārē yoḻiya maṟṟapaṭiyalla. avarukkut teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ uṇmi terintavarkaḷ viparam colliyiruppārkaḷāṉāl intiya caṅkītattiṉ ātāra niyāyaṅkaḷi veku nuṭpamāka ulakattukkuc colliyiruppār. intiyāviṉ pūrvattiyum ataṉ kalikaḷiyum vicārikka varum mēṟṟici kaṉavāṉkaḷil anēkar cila cila camayaṅkaḷil mikavum coṟpa aṟivuṭiyavarkaḷāl vaḻikāṭṭappaṭṭu avviṭattiluḷḷa kāriyaṅkaḷi avarkaḷ mūlamāy aṟintukoṇṭu pōkiṟārkaḷ. oru ciṟiya mūkkaṇām kayiṟṟil kaṭṭuppaṭṭa māṭu akkayiṟṟiṉ mūlamāka naṭattappaṭuvatupōla ituvum kāla iyalpākiṟaJ. itaṉāl vivēkikaḷ cila camayaṅkaḷil tappāṉa apippirāyam koḷvati aṭikkaṭi nām kāṇkiṟōm. intiya caṅkītattip paṟṟiya nuṭpam iṉṉateṉṟaṟiyāmal cumār 2,500 varuṣaṅkaḷākat tavaṟutalāṉa apippirāyamuṇṭāki vivātaṅkaḷ naṭantu varukiṟateṉpati nām aṟivōmāṉāl itip peritāka niṉikka māṭṭōm.
āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṉ teṉpākattil kuṭiyēṟiṉa kālattiṟkup piṟpāṭu karnāṭaka caṅkītamāṉatu pirattiyēkamāṉa ōr muṟiyāka ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭa teṉpatākat terikiṟateṉkiṟār. itu uṇmiyē. āriyarkaḷ kaṭic caṅka kālattilum ataṟkup piṉṉum tamiḻ nāṭṭil vantu tamiḻikkaṟṟu tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku uriyeḻutavum nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟavum ārampittārkaḷeṉṟum, ataṉāl camaskiruta moḻikaḷ aṅkaṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, tamiḻiṉ pūrvīkatti cantēkikkumpaṭiyāṉa vārttikaḷum kalantirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, aḻintupōṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ cāratti camaskirutattil ceytirukkalāmeṉṟum itaṉmuṉ coṉṉōm. atupōlavē karnāṭaka caṅkītameṉṟa pirattiyēkamāṉa oru muṟiyum intustāṉi eṉṟu taṟkālattil vaḻaṅkum vaṭanāṭṭiṉ caṅkīta muṟiyeṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭatāka iṅkē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivviraṇṭum orē uṟpattiyiliruntu uṇṭāṉatākavum appaṭi uṇṭāṉapōtilum taṉittaṉiyē oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu campantamillāta pala māṟutalkaḷiyum uṭittāyirukkiṉṟateṉṟum teḷivākac collukiṟār. itaṉāl veku pūrvamāyuḷḷa teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ curuti muṟiyiṉ rakaciyam teriyāmal tuvāvimcati curutikaḷeṉṟu eḻutiya camaskiruta muṟi yoṉṟum ataṉpiṉ teṉṉintiya kāṉattil pāṇṭittiyamaṭinta vitvāṉkaḷ teṉṉintiya caṅkītatti aṉucarittu eḻutiya pārijātam pōṉṟa camaskiruta nūlkaḷum āka iraṇṭumuṟiyuṇṭāṉatākac collukiṟār. atil teṉṉintiya caṅkītamuṟi tañcinakar oṉṟiltāṉ vicēṣamāyiruntatākavum ataṉpiṉ maṟṟiṭaṅkaḷukkup pōṉatākavum collukiṟār. cumār 300 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ tañci nakariliruntu vēṅkaṭamaki eḻutiya “caturtaṇṭippirakāciki”yum ataṉpiṉ atippōṉṟa pala ciṟu nūlkaḷum camaskirutattil eḻutappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum avikaḷ karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ uṇmiyiyē terivippavikaḷeṉṟum itaṉ muṉ colliyirukkiṟōm. nālu pālikaḷiliruntuṇṭākum 103 paṇkaḷum 12,000 rākaṅkaḷum  iṉṉaveṉṟu viparañcollum tamiḻ nūlkaḷ aḻintapiṉ avaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāvatāṉa āyappāliyiṉ 12 curaṅkaḷiyum avikaḷāl uṇṭākum 72 mēḷaṅkaḷiyum oḻuṅkupaṭutti ivvaḷavāvatu karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṟku eḻuti vittārkaḷēyeṉṟu mikavum cantōṣappaṭa vēṇṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl pūrvamāy vaḻaṅki vanta rākaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum cila cantēka coṟkaḷiyum muṟṟilum māṟṟi camaskiruta aṭcaraṅkaḷik koṇṭu lakkaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkum pataṅkaḷiyum cērttu curaṅkaḷukkum rākaṅkaḷukkum kāraṇap peyariṭṭu jāti vakuttu camaskirutattiṟkuriya cila vicēṣa laṭcaṇaṅkaḷellām atōṭu cērttu eḻutiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. intiyāviṉ pūrvamāyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷukkum paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷukkum rājarkaḷukkum vevvēṟu putiya camaskiruta peyarkaḷ koṭuttu camaskirutattil vaḻaṅkuvatupōla caṅkītattilum nūl eḻuti vittārkaḷ. pūrvantoṭṭu teṉṉintiyāviṉ kōyilkaḷil nāḷatuvariyum collikkoṇṭu varum tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tiruvāymoḻi mutaliya teyva stōttiraṅkaḷ ōtuvārālum maṟṟum paktarkaḷālum collappaṭukiṟati nām kēṭṭirukkiṟōm. avikaḷil iṉṉiṉṉa rākaṅkaḷil pāṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu apputtakaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ammuṟippaṭiyē nāḷatu variyum kāṉam ceytu koṇṭu varukiṟārkaḷeṉṟum terikiṟatu. appaṭiyiruntum atil vaḻaṅki vanta paṇ intaḷam, paṇkāntāram, paṇ kolli, paṇ cīkāmaram, paṇ takkēci, paṇ kuṟiñci, paṇ naṭṭapāṭi, paṇ kuṟuntoki, paṇ tiruttāṇṭakam ivipōṉṟa pūrvap peyarkaḷ camaskiruta nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkātiruppatip pirattiyaṭcamāyp pārkkiṟōm. āṉāl pūrvamāyp paṭikkappaṭṭu vanta tēvāraṅkaḷē nūtaṉamāṉa camaskiruta peyarkaḷiṉāl aḻikkappaṭum rākaṅkaḷākap peyar māṟi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. peyar māṟṟi vaḻaṅkukiṟa iyalpip paṟṟi itaṉ muṉ maṟṟavarāl kēṭṭirukkiṟōm. ivikaḷ yāvaṟṟiyuṅkoṇṭu pūrva kālattil vaḻaṅki vanta teṉṉintiya caṅkīta muṟi pitākaras (Pythagoras) pōṉṟa tattuva kirēkka cāstiriyiṉāl 2/3, 3/4 eṉṟum, paratar, caṅkīta ratṉākarar pōṉṟa camaskiruta cirōmaṇikaḷāl 22 eṉṟum, pōcāṉkē (Bosanquet) pōṉṟavarkaḷāl 53 eṉṟum, vēṅkaṭamaki, caṅkītapārijātakkārar mutaliyavarkaḷāl 12 eṉṟum vevvēṟuvitamāṉa apippirayāṅkaḷi yuṭiyatāyp pala nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ‘itutāṉ yāṉi’ yeṉṟu cātippavarkaḷpōla avaravarkaḷ niyāyaṅkaḷ pala colli nūlkaḷ eḻutiṉārkaḷ. ivvaḷavu cantēkappaṭṭa kālattil teṉṉintiya caṅkītatti ivvaḷavu nuṭpamāy vicārittatāṉatu ivaruṭiya pāṇṭittiyattit teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṟatu. uttara maturiyil pāṇṭiya rājāṅkam viḻunta kālattil cōḻarkaḷum ataṉpiṉ teluṅkarkaḷāṉa nāyakka rājarkaḷum pāṇṭiya rājyattic cila kālam āṇṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟu kāṇkiṟōm. akkālattil avviṭattiliruntu tāṅkaḷ arumiyāy niṉitta caṅkītattiyum ati appiyācittiruntavarkaḷiyum taṅkaḷ nāṭṭukkuk koṇṭupōy atikamāy virutti ceytārkaḷ. itaṉāl vijayanakaramum tañcinakarum mukkiyamāṉatāka nāḷatu variyum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ kītam tamiḻ aracarkaḷiyum tamiḻ teyvaṅkaḷiyum aṇṭiyē nāḷatuvariyum piḻittu vantirukkiṟateṉṟu aṟivāḷikaḷ aṟivārkaḷ.
karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṉ mutaṉmiyiyum atu kiyāḷappaṭṭu vanta vitattiyum kaṇṭaṟinta Capt. Daykaṭavuḷit tējacu cūḻntiruppatupōla teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiyum tējacu cūḻntiruntateṉṟu collukiṟār. immakāṉuṭiya perumiyi eṉṉeṉṟu colvōm? mēṟṟiciyiluḷḷa oru uttamarukku ivvuṉṉata eṇṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭāku māṉāl, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiyē tam jīvaṉākak koṇṭa vitvāṉkaḷukku ivveṇṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭāka vēṇṭāmā? uṇṭāṉāl mārkkam tavaṟi vaḻaṅkum kāṉaṅkaḷil piriyappaṭuvārkaḷā? mēlum, caṅkīta pārijātakkārarukkum (ki.pi. 1,600) caṅkīta ratṉākaram eḻutiya cāraṅkatēvarukkum (ki.pi. 1,200) avar kālattukku muntiya paratarukkum (ki.pi. 500) aṉēka āyira varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉē mutal ūḻiyil karnāṭaka caṅkītam ēṟpaṭṭateṉṟu nām aṟiya vēṇṭum. mēlum, pāṇṭiya rājyam aḻintapiṉ teṉṉintiyāvilirunta vijayanakaram, tañci nakaram, tiruvaṉantapuram, micūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil teṉṉintiya caṅkītam aracarkaḷiṉ aṭikkalam peṟṟu ātarikkappaṭṭu vantatu.
vaṭa intiyā kalakaṅkaḷiṉālum kuḻappattiṉālum niṟinta kālattil teṉṉintiyā camātāṉamāyiruntateṉṟum caṅkītam, viruttiyāṉateṉṟum piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 13.
“The theory, modes and notation in present use throughout the whole of India are derived from that tught originally by the earlier Sanskrit musicians; but owing to the south of India having been less disturbed by internal commotions and having been more subject to Hindu rule than either Deccan or Northern Provinces, the science of music would seem to have been mintined and cultivated long after the original art had been lost in the north.
Hence Southern India music or as it is more usually called Karnatic, bears as far as we can judge, a very close resemblance to what the Sanskrit must have been, and in many cases we can clearly trace the development and refinements introduced from time to time upon the original Ragas.”
“taṟkālam intiyā muḻutum upayōkapṭum caṅkīta cāstira vitikaḷum ārōkaṇaṅkaḷum curak kuṟippukaḷum ātiyil camaskiruta caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷuṭiya muṟiyiliruntē uṇṭāyiṉa. āṉāl teṉṉinti yāvil kuḻappaṅkaḷum kalakaṅkaḷum atikamāyillāmal intu rājāṅkattiṉ niḻalil intiyar amitalāyk kālantaḷḷi vantapaṭiyāl, takṣaṇam vaṭa intiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiviṭa innāṭṭil caṅkītamāṉatu niliyāy iruntu vantatumallāmal vaṭanāṭṭil cutta caṅkītam aḻintu pōṉa piṟakuṅkūṭa teṉṉintiyāvil caṅkītam apivirttiyaṭintē vantatu.
ākiyāl karnāṭaka caṅkītamāṉatu āti rākaṅkaḷil kāla vittiyācattukkut takuntapaṭi iṭik kiṭiyē uṇṭāṉa cuttamāyum aḻakāyumuḷḷa amcaṅkaḷi naṉṟē viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṟatu.”
tamiḻ ici nūlkaḷākiya akattiyam pañcapāratīyam perunāri peruṅkuruku mutaliya ici nūlkaḷ jalappiraḷayattāl aḻintu pōyiṉa veṉṟu muṉ pārttōm. ataṉ piṉṉuḷḷa cila nūlkaḷum kapāṭapuram kaṭal koṇṭa kālattil aḻintu pōyiṉa. arikuṟiyāṉa cila ciṟu nūlkaḷ kaṭiccaṅka kālattil pēṇuvāraṟṟuppōyiṉa. ataṉpiṉ camaskirutattil eḻutappaṭṭa cila nūlkaḷē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. ākilum, annūlkaḷil collappaṭukiṟavikaḷukkum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṟkum mikap pētamirukkiṟateṉṟu aṟivāḷikaḷ uṇarvārkaḷ. itaṉmuṉ, teṉṉintiya caṅkītam vēṟu, vaṭa intiya caṅkītam vēṟu eṉṟu pala kaṉavāṉkaḷiṉ apippirāyamum irukkiṟaJ. ivviraṇṭu muṟikaḷukkum evvita pētamirukkiṟateṉṟu piṉvarum cila vākkiyaṅkaḷil collap paṭukiṟatu :-
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 15.
“The exact definition of what constituted a sruti is difficult to determine; but it is thus gaguely given by the Sangita Ratnavali “A sruti is formed by the smallest intervals of sound and is perceivable by the ear; it is of 22 kinds; also every distinct audible sound is a sruti; it is a sruti becuse it is to be heard by the ear.”
Doubts however exist as to whether the intervals of the srutis were equal or not.
In the arrangement of the sruties, modern usage is diametrically opposite to the classical one; the latter placing them before the note to which they respectively belong, while the former gives position after the notes. It is difficult to dertermine when or by whom the alteration was effected. The arrangement of the frets of the Vina and other stringed instruments accord with the modern acceptation of the principle. Accordingly to the rule lid down in the classical treatises, the disposition of the notes is reversed in the case of the Darve instruments and out of this reversed arrangement perhaps the modern theory about the arrangement of the position of all sruties has been worked. (Tagore)”
“curutiyeṉpatu yātu eṉṟu nuṭpamāyc colluvatu kaṣṭamāṉālum, caṅkīta ratṉāvaḷiyil, nammuṭiya kātāl kēṭkakkūṭiya atinuṭpamāṉa iṭiveḷikaḷuḷḷa captantāṉ atu eṉṟum, atu 22 vakippaṭṭateṉṟum kātiṉāl kēṭṭaṟiyakkūṭiya ovvoru taṉittaṉiyāṉa iṭiveḷikkum antap peyareṉṟum kātiṉāl kēṭkappaṭuvatiṉāl ataṟkuc curutiyeṉṟa peyareṉṟum colliyirukkiṟatu.
āṉāl curaṅkaḷukku iṭiyil varum iṭiveḷikaḷ (ivēnaceyaṭaḷa) yāvum orē aḷavuṭiyaṉavō eṉpatip paṟṟi māttiram cantēkamuṇṭu.
curutikaḷi amikkum oḻuṅkil, taṟkāla muṟikkum āti muṟikkum nērvirōtamirukkiṟaJ. curutikaḷ varum iṭamāṉatu āti muṟippaṭi curutikku muntiyum taṟkāla muṟippaṭi curutikkup pintiyumāyirukkiṟatu. inta māṟutal eppōtu uṇṭāṉateṉṟum yārāl uṇṭāṉateṉṟum terivatu pirayāciyāciyāyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl vīṇi meṭṭukaḷ vikkappaṭum vitattiyum maṟṟa tanti vāttiyaṅkaḷiyum kavaṉittāl avi taṟkālam oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭa muṟippaṭiyē irukkiṉṟaṉa.
tāruvāttiyaṅkaḷil ātimuṟikku virōtamākavē meṭṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. oruvēḷi ippaṭi māṟāṉa muṟiyiliruntē taṟkāla curutikaḷuṭiya stāṉam kuṟikkappaṭṭa tāyirukkalām.”
mēṟkaṇṭa vākkiyaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkiyil caṅkītaratṉākarar atinuṭpamāṉa iṭiveḷi kaḷuḷḷa 22 curutikaḷ varukiṟatākac collukiṟār eṉṟu eḻutiṉār. āṉāl curaṅkaḷukku iṭiyil varum iṭiveḷikaḷ orē aḷavuṭiyaṉavā eṉṟu cantēkikkiṟār. ātimuṟikkum taṟkāla muṟikkum pētamiruppatik koṇṭē ippaṭic cantēkikkiṟār. āṉāl, vīṇi meṭṭukaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum vitattik kavaṉittāl taṟkālam oppuk koḷḷakkūṭiya muṟippaṭiyē yirukkiṟateṉṟu collukiṟār. atāvatu, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṟku ottatāka virukkiṟateṉṟu nām niṉikka vēṇṭum. tāru vāttiyaṅkaḷil ātimuṟikku virōtamāka meṭṭukaḷ vikkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉkiṟār. ivviṣayattil tēcikamāṉa intustāṉi paḻakiyapiṉ ataṟkut takuntapaṭiyē vāttiyaṅkaḷil curastāṉaṅkaḷum naḻuvalāyirukka vēṇṭiyatu avaciyantāṉē. intat tēcattiṟku takunta nūl caṅkīta ratṉākarameṉṟu anēkar apippirāyappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. caṅkīta ratṉākararuṭiya muṟippaṭi pārttāl intustāṉ kītattiṟku muḻutum ottirukka māṭṭāteṉṟu aṟivārkaḷ. intustāṉ caṅkīta muṟiyiyē eḻuta vanta Mr. Clements, Mr. Deval mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ curuti nirṇayam cāraṅkatēvar apippirāyappaṭi alla eṉpati itaṉ piṉ pārppōm. ēṉeṉṟāl, iṭiveḷikaḷ orē aḷavuṭiyavāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa cāraṅkar muṟikku virōtamāka vevvēṟu iṭiveḷikaḷuḷḷa curaṅkaḷi niccayam paṇṇiyirukkiṟārkaḷ. vaṭatēcattil upayōkikkum curattiṉ iṭiveḷi kaḷukkum teṉtēcattil upayōkikkum curattiṉ iṭiveḷikaḷukkum mikunta pētamuṇṭeṉpati piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
Oriental Music by Chinnasawmi Moodr. M.A., P. I, 4. P. 37.
“The mathematical ratios of the Indian Gamut likewise vary in the north and south of India. But this extremely complicated question may be left open for the present, becuse for all practical purposes the system of equal temperament which coincides almost exactly with the adjustment of frets on the Vina is found to meet all existing requirements more or less satisfactorily. It is admitted on all hands that this curious coincidence has been arrived at by the two nations through distinct processes, quite independently of each other; and historical research so far as it has been made, has established the fact that the Indian system has remined in statuquo for ages before the Lux ab oriente dawned upon the West.”
“vaṭa tēcattil upayōkappaṭum ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇaṅkaḷil oru curattukkum ataṟku aṭuttu varum curattukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa iṭiveḷi yippaṟṟiya kaṇakkukkum teṉ tēcattil vaḻaṅkum curuti iṭiveḷikaḷiṉ kaṇakkukkum vittiyācamuṇṭu. ākiyāl cikku mukkāṉa inta viṣayattippaṟṟi ippōtu yōcikkat tēviyilli. oru stāyiyiṉ curaṅkaḷic camapākamāyp pirittu avikaḷukku stāṉaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttum muṟiyāṉatu (Equal temperament) vīṇiyil vaḻaṅkum meṭṭukaḷiṉāl curutikaḷi niccayappaṭuttum muṟikku ottatākavēyiruppatāl namakkuc caṅkīta viṣayamāy avaciya māy vēṇṭiya kāriyaṅkaḷi yellām pūrtti paṇṇa atupōtumāṉatā yirukkiṟatu. ippaṭi iru jātiyārum oruvar vaḻiyi oruvar nōkkāmal vevvēṟuvitamāṉa vaḻikaḷiṉ mūlamāy orēvitamāṉa muṭivukku vantatāṉatu atika vintiyāṉa icik kuṟippāyirukkiṟatu eṉṟu yāvarum oppuk koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ. ākiyāl, intiya muṟiyāṉatu ātikālan toṭaṅki atāvatu kīḻttēcattiṉ caṅkītavoḷi mēltēcattil pirakācitta kālattukku muṉṉēyē toṭaṅki kiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟateṉṟu carittira ārāyccikaḷiṉ mūlamāy aṟiya iṭamirukkiṟatu.”
mēṟkaṇṭa vacaṉaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkumpōtu vīṇiyil vaḻaṅkum meṭṭukaḷiṉāl curutikaḷi niccayappaṭuttummuṟi oru stāyiyiṉ curaṅkaḷic camapākamāyp pirittu stāṉaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttum muṟikku ottiruppatiṉāl caṅkīta viṣayamāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷi pūrtti paṇṇa ituvē pōtumāṉatāyirukkiṟatu. itu viṣayattil mēṟṟiciyārum teṉṉintiyarum orē vitamāṉa apippirāyaṅ kaḷiyuṭiyavarkaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatu mikavum āccariyamāyirukkiṟateṉṟum carittira ārāycciyiṉ mūlamāy veku kālattiṟku muṉṉēyē itu intiyāviliruntateṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟateṉṟum collukiṟār. iṟṟikku 8,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉālēyē nālvakiyāḻum avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇamum teṉmaturiyiliruntateṉṟu nām itaṉmuṉ pārttirukkiṟōm. avikaḷippaṟṟiya viparamum curuti cērkkum muṟiyiyum piṉṉāl aṟivōm. cāraṅkatēvarāl collappaṭṭa tuvāvimcati muṟikaḷ karnāṭaka caṅkītattiṟku utaviyāyirukkumā allavā eṉpataṉip paṟṟi nām aṟivatu mika avaciyam. karnāṭaka caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷ iṉṉateṉṟu iṉṉum niccayamāyc collappaṭātiruppataṉāl tuvāvimcati curutikaḷukkum itaṟkumuḷḷa tāratammiyatti eṭuttuc colvatu avvaḷavu takutiyāyirukkamāṭṭāJ. iruvita curutikaḷiyum iṉimēl ottup pārkkumpoḻutu, iraṇṭiṟku muḷḷa tāratammiyattit teḷivāy aṟintu koḷvōm. āyiṉum, caṅkītaratṉākaram eṉṉum nūlil kaṇṭapaṭi curuti kuṟikkiṟōmeṉṟu colpavarkaḷiṉ kaṇakkukaḷic cāraṅkatēvar kaṇakkōṭu ottuppārkka vēṇṭiyatu mikavum mukkiyamāyirukkiṟatu.
cāraṅkatēvarō oru stāyiyil varum curaṅkaḷ camaaḷavuṭiyavi kaḷāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu iṭiyil vēṟu curaṅkaḷuṇṭākāmal oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu tīviramāyp paṭippaṭiyāyp pōka vēṇṭumeṉṟum collukiṟār. āṉāl vaṭatēcattil vaḻaṅkum kāṉam iṭiveḷikaḷil vittiyācamuṭiyatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. avikaḷ curaṅkaḷi aḷantu tantiyi 2/3, 3/4 eṉṟu pōṭuvatāl uṇṭākum pala pētaṅkaḷeṉṟu itaṉ piṉ pārppōm. teṉṉintiyāvil vaḻaṅkum karnāṭaka caṅkītamō ivviraṇṭiṟkum vittiyācamāṉa vēṟorumuṟiyeṉṟu teḷivāyt terikiṟatu. mēlum, teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷiyum caṅkīta ratṉākarattil vaḻaṅki vanta curutikaḷiyum naṉṟāy aṟinta pitākaras (Pythagoras)  eṉṉum kirēkka tattuva cāstiriyār teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil kaṇṭa ma, pa eṉṟa curaṅkaḷi kātiṟkēṭṭuc curuti kūṭṭum muṟiyit tām ñāpakappaṭuttik koḷḷum paḻakkamillāmiyāl 2/3, 3/4 eṉṉum aḷaviṉāl kuṟittuk koṇṭu mēṟṟēcattil caṅkītatti virutti ceytār. atu mutal teṉṉintiya caṅkīta curaṅkaḷiṉ aḷavukkum vittiyācam ēṟpaṭṭatu. nāḷ cellac cella ammuṟi cariyāṉa poruttamuṭiyaviyalla eṉṟu cilar vātikkavum itē mātiri aḷantupōṭum muṟiyic collum caṅkīta pārijātam pōṉṟa nūlkaḷip pārtta intiya vitvacirōmaṇikaḷ tāṅkaḷ pāṭum cila rākaṅkaḷukku atu poruttamāyiruppatiṉāl pitākaras kaṇṭupiṭitta ṭiyaṭāṉikskēl (Diatonic Scale) cariyeṉṟum ataṟkuppiṉ hārmaṉi (Harmony)  varumpaṭi āṅkilēyar kaṇṭupiṭitta cama aḷavuḷḷa curaṅkaḷ (Equal temperament) cariyallaveṉṟum kuṟi colla ārampittārkaḷ. ippaṭip palapēr collum apippirāyaṅkaḷik kavaṉittavarkaḷ caṅkītaratṉākara muṟippaṭi 22 curutikaḷiyum attōṭu kalakka veku pirayācippaṭṭārkaḷ. camaskiruta culōkaṅkaḷiluḷḷavi uṇmiyeṉṟu stāpikka muyaṉṟavarkaḷ teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum curutikaḷiyum avaṟṟi uḷḷati uḷḷapaṭi kāṭṭi utaviyāyirunta vīṇiyiyum tavaṟutal uṭiyateṉṟu coṉṉāloḻiya taṅkaḷ vārtti cellāteṉṟu aṟintu nūtaṉa apippirāyaṅkaḷiyum collukiṟārkaḷ. tuvāvimcati curuti eṉṉum iṭaṟukal iṉṉateṉṟaṟivārkaḷāṉāl ippaṭic collamāṭṭārkaḷ. aṟiyātatiṉālē curutiyip paṟṟic collum iṭamellām cantēkamum kutarkkamum, oḻuṅkīṉamum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷiyuṭiya teṉṉintiya kāṉattil vaḻaṅki varum cila rākaṅkaḷ tuvāvimcati curuti kaṇakkiṉpaṭiyum kuṟikkak kūṭiyatā yirukkiṟatiṉāl itu cariyeṉṟu collavum coṟpa ētuvirukkiṟatu. āṉāl 10, 15 rākaṅkaḷukkāka āyiram patiṉāyiramāṉa rākaṅkaḷi viṭṭuviṭukiṟatā? oru stāyiyil 12 curaṅkaḷ varukiṟa teṉpatiyum avikaḷ intiya caṅkītattil varum curaṅkaḷukkum vīṇiyil varum curaṅkaḷukkum cariyāyirukkiṟa teṉpatiyum piṉ varum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 20.
“The Hindu octave, like the European, is divided into twelve semitones. (The view is supported by both Sir W. Jones and Mr. Fowke. “Asiatic researches.”) Sir W. Jones remarks “I tried in vin to discover in practice any difference between the Indian scale and that of our own but knowing my ear to be very insufficiently exercised, I requested a German professor of music to accompany on his violin a Hindu lutenist who sang by note some popular airs on the loves of Krishna and Radha and he assured me that the scales were the same; and Mr. Shore afterwards informed me that when the voice of a native singer was in tune with his Harpsichord he found the Hindu series of seven notes to ascend like ours by a sharp third. From many experiments I am led to believe that a wrong idea as to the temperament of the Indian scale as practically employed has hitherto been held. I played over all the various scales shown later upon a pianoforte tuned to Equal temperament in the presence of several well known Hindustani and Karnatic musicians, all of whom assured me that they corresponded exactly to those of the Vina. Upon comparing the two instruments this was found to be the case as far as could be judged by the ear alone, in every instance. Maula Bux, a man of considerable attinments, took pins to explin to me that the tempering of the modern Indian scales differed in no whit from the European.”
“intiya stāyiyāṉatu airōppiya stāyiyip pōlavē 12 ari curaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṟaJ. (intak koḷkiyāṉatu Sir W. Jones eṉpavarālum Mr. Fowke eṉpavarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu Asiatic researches eṉṟa pustakattil colliyirukkiṟatu.) Sir W. Jones colvateṉṉa veṉṟāl aṉupōkattil intiya Scalekum nammuṭiya Scalekum uḷḷa vittiyācattik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpaṭi evvaḷavō pirayācappaṭṭum payaṉpaṭa villi. mēlum eṉṉuṭiya caṅkīta ñāṉam atika appiyācikkap paṭātiruntatāl caṅkītattil tērnta oru jarmaṉ vitvāṉi kiruṣṇaṉ, irātā ivarkaḷuṭiya nēcattip paṟṟiya cila cōkaracamuḷḷa kītaṅkaḷi oru intu vitvāṉ pāṭukiyil avar kūṭa piṭilil avari vācikkac collik kēṭṭapōtu anta jarmaṉ vitvāṉ iraṇṭu Scalekum yātoru vittiyācamilli eṉṟu eṉakku etirmoḻi pakarntār. Mr. Shore eṉpavarum oru intiya vitvāṉ taṉṉuṭiya Harpsichord or Piano vukku icintu pāṭukiyil intu Scale ārōkaṇattil cariyāy nammuṭiya ēḻu nōṭṭukkum ottiruntateṉṟum mūṉṟāvatu nōṭṭākiya kāntāram nammuṭiya E.. nōṭṭip pōlavē caṟṟuk kūṭuta (sharp) lāy iruntatākavum eṉṉiṭam coṉṉār. nāṉ cōtaṉi ceytu pārtta anēka kāriyaṅkaḷ mūlamāy nāṉ eṉṉa aṟintu koḷḷukiṟēṉ eṉṟāl, appiyācattil intu  Scale  uṭiya  temperament  aip paṟṟi oru tappāṉa apippirāyam ituvarikkum yāvaruṭiya maṉatilum iruntatu eṉpatē. Equal temperament  muṟiyāy uṇṭākkappaṭṭirunta oru Piano vil pērpōṉa intustāṉi karnāṭaka caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ muṉṉiliyil nāṉ pala Scale aiyum vācittuk kāṇpittapōtu avarkaḷellām ēkōpittu piyāṉā Piano viluḷḷa curaṅkaḷ vīṇiyiṉ curaṅkaḷukkuccariyāy ottiruntatu eṉṟu uṟutiyāyc coṉṉārkaḷ. iraṇṭu vāttiyaṅkaḷiyum ottiṭṭup pārkkiyil ceviyāṟalāyk kēṭkumpōtu iraṇṭu vāttiyaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa curaṅkaḷukkum yātoru vittiyācamilliyeṉṟu terintatu. caṅkīta vittikaḷilum maṟṟa vittikaḷilum pērpōṉa Maula Bux eṉpavar taṟkāla intiya Scale muṟikkum airōppiya muṟikkum kiñcittēṉum vittiyāca milliyeṉṟu eṉakku eṭuttukkāṭṭa atikap pirayācappaṭṭār.”
mēṟkaṇṭa vacaṉaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkiyil karnāṭaka caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum anēka viṣayaṅkaḷi aṟintukoḷḷa utaviyāyirukkum teṉṉintiya caṅkītam eṉṉum puttakattiyeḻutiya Capt. Day eṉpavar, āṅkilēyar taṟkālam vaḻaṅki varum 12 curaṅkaḷum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki varum 12 curaṅkaḷum evvitattilum cariyāyiruppatākap pala pariṭcikaḷ pārttatākac collukiṟār. atōṭu Equal temperament  muṟiyāṉa iccuraṅkaḷippaṟṟi oru tappāṉa apippirāyam ituvarikkum yāvaruṭiya maṉatilum iruntatu eṉkiṟār. intac cama aḷavuḷḷa curavarici nālvakiyāḻkaḷiṉ viparanterinta aṟivāḷikaḷ ivi pūrvam teṉmaturiyil tamiḻnāṭṭil iruntateṉṟum atilum nuṭpamāṉa curuti muṟi akkālattilēyē vaḻaṅki vantateṉṟum aṟivārkaḷ. pūrvamāyuḷḷa ammuṟi ataṉpiṉ aḷaviṉāl mēṟṟicikkum pōṉatiṉāl ataṉ nuṭpam iḻantu cila vikāraṅkaḷiyaṭantaJ. atupōlavē antap paṉṉiraṇṭu curaṅkaḷukkup pūrva nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅki varum 22 alakukaḷum rāci māṉamum poruttamum aṟiyāmal tavaṟutalāka oru stāyiyil 22 curuti varukiṟateṉṟu piṉṉuḷḷa nūlkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭaJ. ippaṭi eḻutappaṭṭa oru tavaṟutalāṉa muṟiyum ñalavāyapaṭicayaḷa koṇṭu pōṉa tiṭṭamillāta oru muṟiyum kūṭi pala puttakaṅkaḷāka veḷi vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. “tāṉ keṭṭatumallāmal cantirapuṣkaraṇiyiyum kūṭak keṭuttāṉ” eṉpatu pōlat tavaṟutalāy vaḻaṅkum inta iraṇṭum taṅkaḷippōla teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiyum mūkkaṟi paṇṇap pārkkiṟaJ. teṉṉintiya caṅkītam taṉṉi ātarittu vanta pāṇṭiyarājarkaḷ kālattiṟkuppiṉ vicēṣamāyc cōḻa nāṭṭil ātaravu peṟṟu vantatu. cōḻa nāṭṭil mikuntu vaḻaṅki vanta teṉṉintiya caṅkītam nīrvaṟṟiya kuḷattiṉ paṭcikaḷippōla pala iṭaṅkaḷukkum paṟantu koṇṭirukkiṟaJ.
eppaṭiyiruntālum teṉṉintiyāvil vicēṣamāyirukkum civa ṣēttiraṅkaḷum viṣṇu stalaṅkaḷumuḷḷa variyil karnāṭaka caṅkītam illāmal pōkāteṉpatu niccayam. ēṉeṉṟāl caṅkītattiṟku mikavum mukkiya vāttiyamāṉa vīṇiyum, kuḻalum pūrvakālattil vaḻaṅki vantatu pōlavē taṟkālattilum vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu. ivviraṇṭum muḻuc curaṅkaḷiyum ariccuraṅkaḷiyum ataṉ piṉvarum nuṭpaccuraṅkaḷiyum iṉṉateṉṟu kāṭṭakkūṭiya vitamāka amintirukkiṟaJ. ippaṭi mēṉmiyum māṟātatumāṉa oru muṟi karnāṭaka caṅkītattil ēṟkaṉavē amikkap paṭṭirukkiṟateṉṟu piṉvarum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇpōm.”
The Music and the Musical Instruments of Southern India, by C.R. Day, P. 29.
“The following table kindly sent me by Mr. Ellis shows the results obtined from a most minute and careful examination made by him and by Mr. A.J. Hipkins of a beautiful old Vina, in perfect condition now in my possession. This instrument is between two or three hundred years old and is from the collection in the Tanjore palace. the results as will be seen tend to prove that the frets were purposely arranged for something like equal temperament. We see therefore that in India much the same results have been independently arrived at by the native musicians as have been attined by subsequent science in Europe.”
“aṭiyil collum aṭṭavaṇiyāṉatu Mr. Ellis um Mr. A.J. Hipkins um cērntu oru ciṟanta paḻiya vīṇiyiliruntum ati nuṭpamum jākkirati yumāṉa ārāycciyiṉāl kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaJ. anta vīṇi ippōtum eṉ vacamirukkiṟatu. inta vāttiyam 200, 300 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ uḷḷatu. tañci araṇmaṉiyil cēkaram ceytu vikkappaṭṭiruntavikaḷil oṉṟu. meṭṭukaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum oḻuṅkip pārttāl Equal temperament  muṟikkiṇaṅkac curutikaḷi uṇṭākkuvataṟkākac ceyyap paṭṭatu pōlirukkiṟatu. atiliruntu nām aṟivateṉṉaveṉṟāl airōppāvil piṟkāla cāstira viṣayamāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa caṅkatikaḷ intiyāvil caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷāl taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ conta appiyācat tiṉālēyē itaṟku muṉṉē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē.”
mēṟkaṇṭa varikaḷik kavaṉikkiyil tañcāvūriliruntu iṅkilāntukkuk koṇṭupōṉa vīṇiyip paṟṟiya cila mukkiya viṣayaṅkaḷ collukiṟār. avvīṇi tañcāvūr araṇmaṉiyil cērttu vikkappaṭṭiruntavikaḷil oṉṟeṉṟum, atil patippikkappaṭṭirunta meṭṭukaḷ Equal  temperament  muṟikkiṇaṅka curutikaḷi uṇṭākkuvataṟku icintavikaḷāyiruntatākavum kāṇkiṟatu. ati Mr. Ellis um Mr. A.J. Hipkins um cērntu pariṭci pārttatākavum atil airōppāvil 2,000 varuṣamāka vaḻaṅki vanta Diatonic Scale curaṅkaḷip pōlillāmal hyacaaṭilē pāṭuvataṟku aṉukūlamāka cumār 100, 200 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ āṅkilēyē caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭittirukkum Equal temperament  muṟikku ottatāyirukkiṟateṉṟum vīṇiyiṉ curaṅkaḷ intiya caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷāl taṅkaḷ conta apippirāyattiṉālēyē airōppiyar kaṇṭupiṭikkumuṉṉē kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum collukiṟār.
ivvaḷavu tūram muyaṟci eṭuttu vicārittu vīṇiyil kāṇum curaṅkaḷ Equal temperament  muṟikkuc cariyāyirukkiṟateṉṟum itu ēṟkaṉavē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭirunta muṟiyeṉṟum uḷḷati uḷḷapaṭi veḷiyiṭṭa Capt. Day eṉṉum kaṉavāṉuṭiya perumiyi itaṉ muṉṉum colliyirukkiṟōm. ippērppaṭṭa oruvar iṉṉum caṟṟu nuṭpamāka vicārittāl immuṟi iṟṟikkuc cumār 1,800 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eḻutiya cilappatikārattilum ataṟku anēka āyira varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ eḻutiya tolkāppiyattilumuḷḷa cila varikaḷik koṇṭu atipūrvamāyuḷḷa tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta kītamuṟimiyil collappaṭṭirukkiṟateṉṟum ammuṟippaṭiyē nāḷatuvariyum tamiḻ makkaḷ kāṉam ceykiṟārkaḷeṉṟum aṟiyāmaṟpōkār. camōciyuḷḷa 12 curakāṉattōṭu nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷ cērntu vaḻaṅkum maṟṟoru perumuṟiyum teṉṉintiyāvil nāḷatuvariyum vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu. avikaḷil vaḻaṅki varumciṟu curaṅkaḷ iṉṉateṉṟu pāṭukiṟavarkaḷāl colla muṭiyāmalum kēṭkiṟavarkaḷāl kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāmalum iruppatiṉāl allavō teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṉ rakaciyatti aṟintu koḷḷāmal pōṉārkaḷ. vīṇiyeṉṉum ciṟanta vāttiyattiṉ amippum ataṉ rakaciyamum ataṉ pūrvamum aṟiyāta cilar meṭṭukaḷōṭu vaḻaṅkum vīṇikaḷ taṟkālam airōppāvil vaḻaṅki varum camōciyuḷḷa curaṅkaḷukku (Equal temperament ) ēṟṟavitamākac camīpakālattil tañcāvūril aracāṇṭu koṇṭirunta cēvappa nāyakkarukkāka amikkappaṭṭateṉṟum collukiṟārkaḷ. uṇmiyaṟintāl appaṭic collamāṭṭār. vīṇiyil kāṇappaṭum meṭṭukaḷiṉpaṭi uṇṭākum curaṅkaḷē namatu rākaṅkaḷil piratāṉamāyirukkiṟaviyeṉṟum atil iraṇṭoru curaṅkaḷ oru curattiṟku kūṭiya allatu kuṟinta immuṟiyē pūrva tamiḻ nūlkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭiruntateṉṟum pūrva tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vantateṉṟum teḷivāka itaṉpiṉ teṉṉintiya caṅkīta curuti muṟiyil aṟivōm.
oru kālattil cakala kalikaḷilumciṟappuṟṟōṅkiya tamiḻnāṭu pūrva pūmiyāyiruntateṉṟum atilēyē jātikaḷ uṟpattiyāṉārkaḷeṉṟum avarkaḷ pāṣiyē pala pāṣikaḷil kalantirukkiṟateṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷiruntateṉṟum atil iyal ici nāṭakameṉṉum muttamiḻ ārāycci ceyyappaṭṭateṉṟum icittamiḻākiya caṅkītattiṟ kuriya pala nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruntateṉṟum eḻutappaṭṭa caṅkīta nūlkaḷ irumuṟi kaṭalāl aḻintu pōyiṉaveṉṟum piṉvantavarkaḷāl caṅkītam viruttiyākāmal kuṟintateṉṟum curukkamākap pārttōm. ippaṭik kuṟinta niliyiluḷḷa caṅkītattippaṟṟit taṟkālattiluḷḷa cila kaṉavāṉkaḷ collum apippirāyattiyum kavaṉittōm. atil vaṭatēca kāṉam intustāṉ kāṉam teṉṉintiya kāṉam eṉṉum mūṉṟilum teṉṉintiya kāṉamē mārkka vitiyuṭiyateṉṟum cāstirayuktamāṉa teṉṟum palapala rākaṅkaḷiyuṭiyateṉṟum piṟar collum apip pirāyattit teḷivākak kaṇṭōm. teṉṉintiya kāṉam māṟāta amippuṭiyatāy aḻakuṭiyatāy tōṉṟuvataṟku ovvoru rākattil varum curaṅkaḷ ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇattil iṉṉiṉṉa curutiyuṭiya curaṅkaḷ vara vēṇṭumeṉṟum cāṭava oḷaṭava rākaṅkaḷil viṭappaṭṭa curaṅkaḷ avvirākattiṉ cañcāram muṟṟilum vilakkappaṭa vēṇṭu meṉṟum muṉpiṉṉāka varum curaṅkaḷ (vakkiracuraṅkaḷ) rākacañcāra muḻuvatilum taṉ iṉattiyē muṟṟilum ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇattilillāta curaṅkaḷ rākacañcārattil muṟi piṟaḻntu vaḻaṅkāmalirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum ovvoru rākattiṉ jīvacuraṅkaḷ iṉṉateṉṟum iṉṉiṉṉa kālattil iṉṉiṉṉa camayattil iṉṉiṉṉa rākaṅkaḷ poruttamāyirukku meṉṟum iṉṉiṉṉa paṇkaḷukku iṉṉiṉṉa rākam collappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum parampariyāyp pōtikkappaṭṭu vantirukkiṟatu. ivvitamāṉa muṟikaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu rājākkaḷiṉ māṉiyaṅkaḷiṉālum kōvil campaḷa umpaḷaṅkaḷiṉālum ātarikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa. mēlum rājacapiyilum kaliyāṇakālaṅkaḷilum paṭṭaṇap piravēca kālaṅkaḷilum kālākālattil caṅkītam upayōkikkappaṭṭu vantatu. araṇmaṉikaḷilum kōyilkaḷilum anticanti mattiyāṉam eṉṉum mūṉṟu kālaṅkaḷilum caṅkītam muḻaṅkat tiṉak kaṭṭaḷi ēṟpaṭutti ataṟku ēṟṟatākap pāṭakarkaḷi niyamittārkaḷ. oru rājyattil āṇṭukoṇṭirukkum rājaṉ taṉrājyattiṉ palapākaṅkaḷilum palakōyilkaḷ kaṭṭi ataṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷ tarum māṉiyaṅkaḷ viṭṭu ārātaṉi kālaṅkaḷil, maṇi, caṅku, pēriki, mattaḷam, nākacuram, pullāṅkuḻal, vīṇi mutaliya vāttiyaṅkaḷ vācippataṟkum tēvāram tiruvācakam tiruvāymoḻi mutaliya tōttiraṅkaḷ colvataṟkum pāṭuvataṟkum āṭuvataṟkum takutiyāṉa pērkaḷi niyamittu avarkaḷukku vīṭum campaḷa umpaḷaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭutti vippatu vaḻakkam. ippaṭiyē ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkum civaṣēttiraṅkaḷiyum viṣṇuṣēttiraṅkaḷiyum nām mikutiyāy aṟivōm. avikaḷil caṅkīta ūḻiyañ ceyyum vakuppār caṅkītattiyē mukkiyat toḻilākak koṇṭu atiyē appiyācittuk koṇṭum atiyē virutti ceytu koṇṭum nāḷatuvariyu mirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpati nām yāvarum aṟivōm. immuṟi iṉṟu nēṟṟalla, pala āyira varuṣaṅkaḷāka naṭantu varukiṟatu. itaṉālēyē teṉṉintiya caṅkītam tēcikam kalavāmal karnāṭaka cuttamāyirukkiṟateṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. vāyppāṭṭup pāṭum maṟṟavar kāṉattiṟkum karnāṭaka cuttamāy vācikkum oru nākacurattiṟkumuḷḷa pētatti aṟivāḷikaḷ kāṇpārkaḷ. kōyilkaḷil vācikkum vīṇi, pullāṅkuḻal, mirutaṅkam, nākacuram mutaliya iṉiya kāṉattil īṭupaṭṭa cila pirapukkaḷum maṟṟavarum atip pāṭam paṇṇi tāṅkaḷ āṉantittatōṭu piṟariyum āṉantikkac ceytārkaḷ. appaṭippaṭṭavarkaḷuḷ cilar mikunta pāṇṭittiyamaṭintu piṟarāl koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu caṉmāṉikkappaṭṭum vantārkaḷ. vēṟu cilar piratip pirayōjaṉatti virumpāmal pakavāṉit tutippatilēyē taṅkaḷ kālattic celaviṭṭārkaḷ. maṟṟum cilar caṅkīta cāstirattiṉ cilacila pākaṅkaḷi eḻutiṉārkaḷ. ippaṭit tōṉṟiya vitvacirōmaṇikaḷil mukkiyamāṉavarkaḷip paṟṟiyum cila kuṟippukaḷ pārppatu caṅkītattip paṟṟi vicārikkum namakkup piriyamāyirukkumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
VI. teṉṉintiya caṅkītatti appiyācittu vantavarkaḷip 
paṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ.
1. potuk kuṟippukaḷ.
cīrpeṟṟilaṅkiya teṉṉintiya kaṇṭamē atāvatu kumarināṭē lemūriyāk kaṇṭameṉṟum annāṭṭil pēcappaṭṭu vanta pāṣi tamiḻ eṉṟum aṅkē aracāṭci ceytu vantōr pāṇṭiya rājarkaḷeṉṟum avarkaḷmutal caṅkam kūṭṭi aṟiviṟ ciṟanta rājarkaḷiyum tapōtaṉarkaḷiyum vitvāṉkaḷiyum oṉṟu cērttup pala kalikaḷiyum ārāycci ceytu nūl eḻuti vantārkaḷeṉṟum itaṉ muṉ curukkamākap pārttōm. avaṟṟuḷ muttamiḻil oru pākamāka appiyācikkappaṭṭu vanta caṅkītam kumarināṭu aḻintapiṉ palavitattilum cīrkeṭṭut taṉakkuriya ātāra nūlkaḷiyum iḻantateṉṟum ataṉpiṉ iṭiccaṅka kālattilum kaṭic caṅka kālattilum mītiyāyirunta cila ciṟiya nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭum cilakālam niṉṟu piṉ maṟintu pōyiṉaveṉṟum itaṉ muṉ pārttōm. kaṭiccaṅka kālattilum ataṟkup piṟkālattilum teṉṉintiyāviṟku vanta palarālum teṉṉintiya caṅkītam appiyācikkappaṭṭateṉṟum ak kālattilēyē vevvēṟu pāṣikaḷil eḻutappaṭṭateṉṟum niṉikka ētuvirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl pūrva tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta kāṉattil varum 22 alakukaḷ eṉṟa vārttiyi iṉṉateṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷāmal 22 curutikaḷ oru stāyiyil varavēṇṭumeṉṟu cantēkappaṭum niliyil maṟṟap pāṣiyil eḻuti vittārkaḷ. āṉāl tamiḻnāṭṭil paḻakkatti lirukkum kāṉam muṟiyiyuṭiyatākavum 22 curutikaḷukku ovvāta tākavum nāḷatu variyumiruntu varukiṟatu. maṟṟa pāṣikaḷil 400, 500, 1,000, 2,000 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ eḻutappaṭṭatākat tāṅkaḷ collum cila cūttiram taṉṉilēyē talitūkki niṟkat takutiyillātiruntālum tali tūkki niṟkum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiyum taḷḷāṭumpaṭiyāṉa cantēkat tukkuk koṇṭu vantu viṭṭirukkiṟaJ. iccantēkamum aṉṉiya pāṣiyil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷi vācittaṟiyakkūṭiyavarkaḷiṭattiluṇṭākiṟatē yoḻiyak karnāṭaka caṅkītattip parampariyāyp paḻakiyavarum toṉṟu toṭṭu tēvastāṉaṅkaḷiṉāl ātarikkappaṭṭavarumākiya ūḻiyak kārarukkuḷ allatu caṅkītakkārarukkuḷ uṇṭākiṟatilli. oru stāyiyil 22 curutikaḷ varumāṉāl avi teṉṉintiya caṅkītattiṟku ottuvarāteṉṟum vētam collum curaṅkaḷukku cariyāyirukkamāṭṭā teṉṟum atu vaḻakkattukku varāta oru muṟiyeṉṟum teṉṉintiya kāṉam 22 curutiyiṉpaṭiyilliyeṉṟum collum kaṉavāṉkaḷiṉ apip pirāyattiyum itaṉ muṉ pārttirukkiṟōm. 22 curutiyeṉṟu aṟiyāmalum tuvāvimcati curutiyeṉṟa peyariyē kēṭkāmalumirukkiṟa anēka vitvāṉkaḷ iṉṟumirukkiṟārkaḷ. maṟṟum cilar tuvāvimcati curuti yeṉṟa collikkoṇṭu taṅkaḷ aṉupōkattilirukkum teṉṉintiya caṅkītattip parīṭcittup pārttuc colla iyalātavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. ippaṭi curutiyippaṟṟiya tiṭṭamāṉa nūl ātāramillātiruntālum parampariyāy caṅkītattiyē paṭittu atic cērntatāṉa vīṇi, pullāṅkuḻal, nākacuram, mēḷam, mirutaṅkam, paratam, vāyppāṭṭu mutaliyavikaḷil tērcci peṟṟuk kōyil tōṉṟiya kāla mutal kōyil campaḷattiṉālēyē ātarikkappaṭṭu varum jaṉaṅkaḷāl teṉṉintiya caṅkītam oruvāṟu kāppāṟṟappaṭṭum avarkaḷāl maṟṟavarkaḷukkuc collik koṭukkappaṭṭum vantirukkiṟatu. tāṉ uṇṭākkiṉatellām nallateṉṟu kaṇṭa karttaṉ taṉ camukattil cakala paricuttavāṉkaḷum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāttiyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ “karttar paricuttar, paricuttar, paricuttar” eṉṟu pāṭavum tutittuk koṇṭu āṭavum pārttu vīṟṟirukkiṟāreṉṟu colluvati nām kavaṉikkiyil caṅkītattiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkum ataṉ viruttikkum teyvamē mutalvareṉṟu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. mēlum ulakattiluḷḷa paktarkaḷum cakala jīvapirāṇikaḷumāṉa yāvaṟṟiṉ tutiyiyum karttaṉē aṭikiṟāṉ eṉṟu colvatik koṇṭu caṅkītattiṉ pirayōjaṉamum uyarnteṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. teyvattāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭa yāvum taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuriya iṉṉici yāl pakavāṉit tutikkiṟatākak kāṇkiṟōm.
ākāyamaṇṭalattil nām kāṇum kiraṅkaḷum vāṉajōtikaḷum taṅkaḷit tāṅkaḷē cuṟṟum vēkattiṉāl iṉiya nātamuṇṭākiṟaJ. avikaḷ parimāṇattil ciṟitu peritāyiruppatiṉāl vevvēṟu ōcikaḷ piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. periya ciṟakuṭiya oluṅkukaḷ paṟakkumpōtu uṇṭākum ōciyip pārkkilum atika ciṟitāṉa kocukkaḷ paṟakkumpōtuṇṭākum ōci uccamāyiruk kiṟateṉṟu nām aṟivōm. itōṭu metuvāyc cuṟṟumpoḻutu mantamāṉa ōciyum ativirivāyc cuṟṟumpoḻutu uccamāṉa ōciyum uṇṭākiṟatu. appaṭiyē vāṉajōtikaḷ tāṅkaḷ cuṟṟumpōtu uṇṭākum ōciyiṉāl pakavāṉit tutikkiṉṟaṉa. atupōlavē ovvoru jīvarkaḷum taṅkaḷ uttamamāṉa jīviyattālum paktiyālum kāṉattālum pakavāṉit tutikkiṟārkaḷ. tāvararācikaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṉiyiṉ maturattālum puṣpattiṉ vācaṉiyālum palaṉkaḷiṉ naṉmi yālum pakavāṉit tutittum maṟṟavarkaḷ tutikka ēviyum varukiṉṟaṉa. cōliyil vacikkum paṭcikaḷ pakavāṉit tutittup pāṭuvati nām naṉṟāy aṟivōm. appaṭiyē navamaṇikaḷ taṅkaḷ oḷiyiṉālum aruvikaḷ taṅkaḷ ōciyiṉālum āṟukaḷ taṅkaḷ oliyiṉālum, kaṭalkaḷ taṅkaḷ ali iriccalālum ōyāmal tutittukkoṇṭē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭit teyvattit tutittuk koṇṭēyirukkum uttamamāṉa vaḻakkam jīvapirāṇikaḷ yāvarukkum iyaṟkiyāy uriyaJ. appaṭiyiruntālum vicēṣamāka cilar māttiram caṅkītatti appiyācittu piṟar āṉantikkak kūṭiyatākac collattakuntavarkaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷ. appaṭippaṭṭavarkaḷ iṉṉakālatti liruntārkaḷeṉṟu tiṭṭamāyc collakkūṭāta carittiramuṭiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum oruvāṟu avarkaḷ caṅkītap payiṟciyiyum avarkaḷ perumiyiyum pārppatu nammi ñāpakappaṭuttum oru kuṟippāyāvatu irukkumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
pāṇṭiya rājyam kaṭiccaṅkam aḻintapiṉ palavitattilum cīrkeṭṭu cōḻa rājākkaḷ cilarāl jeyikkappaṭṭum piṉ cilakālam taṉi aracu peṟṟum māṟi māṟi cumār 2000 varuṣattiṟku muṉṉiruntē kuṟinta nilikku vantateṉṟum iṟṟikku 700, 800 varuṣaṅkaḷāka muṟṟilum anniyar āḷukikkuṭpaṭṭateṉṟum nām aṟivōm.
2. cōḻa rājyattiṉ caṅkīta nili
ki.mu.  272-231 variyum aracāṇṭu koṇṭirunta vaṭatēcattup putta camayattaracaṉāṉa acōkaṉuṭiya puttiraṉum puttiriyum teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantatākavum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya rājākkaḷuṭaṉ uṭaṉpaṭikki ceyta tākavum ki.mu.  247-il cōḻa rājyattār ilaṅkikkup paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟatākavum ataṉpiṉ ki.mu.  150-il maṟupaṭiyum paṭiyeṭuttatākavum ilaṅkic carittirattāl terikiṟatu.
ataṉpiṉ ki.pi. 50 mutal 95-m varuṣam variyum cōḻa rājyattiṟ karacaṉāyirunta mutalāvatu karikāl cōḻaṉ kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattit talinakarākkik koṇṭu pirapalamāka aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭiruntāṉ. avaṉ taṉ rājyam nīrvaḷam poruntiyatāyirukkavēṇṭip pala aṇikaḷum āṟṟiṟkuk karikaḷum kaṭṭuvittāṉ. anēka vāykkālkaḷum veṭṭiṉāṉ. kaṭalvaḷam perukki kappalkaḷ, paṭakukaḷ mutaliya nīrōṭum kalaṅkaḷ ceytu cīṉā, parmā, kirēkku, jāvā mutaliya tēcattārōṭu viyāpāram ceytu vantāṉ. karittoḻil kaṭaṟṟoḻil mutaliyavikaḷukku vēṇṭiya utavi purintu toḻilāḷarkaḷi virutti ceytāṉ. nīti cāstiram, vēta cāstiram, vittiya cāstiram, vāṉa cāstiram, yutta cāstiram ākiya aintu cāstiramum terintavari oru capiyākac cērttu avarkaḷ yōcaṉikēṭṭu aracāṭci naṭatti vantāṉ. avaṉ kālattil tamiḻp pāṣiyum, ataṉ ciṟanta kalikaḷum mikavum viruttiyaṭintaṉa. iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻ tērnta pulavarkaḷiyum tamiḻppulamiyiṟ ciṟanta peṇmaṇikaḷiyum mikavum ātarittu vantāṉ. mutalāvatu karikāl cōḻaṉākiya ivaṉ kālattil cēra rājyattil cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭiruntāṉ. ivaṉ tampi iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tuṟaviyāyt tavacupaṇṇikkoṇṭiruntār. pāṇṭiya rājyattil neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ maturiyil aracāṇṭu koṇṭiruntāṉ. veḷḷi ampalattuttuñciya peruvaḻuti eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉum ivaṉ kālattiliruntatākat terikiṟatu. atōṭu kāviri natiyiṉ caṅkamukattiluḷḷa kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil mācāttuvāṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṉ makaṉ kōvalaṉ annakariluḷḷa mātavi eṉṉum naṭaṉak kaṉṉikiyi avaḷ āṭal pāṭalkaḷāl nēcittut taṉ poruḷellām iḻantapiṉ maturimā nakarukkuc ceṉṟu mīnta poruḷākiya cilampi viṟkum camayam  atu araṇmaṉic cilampeṉṟu avviṭattuḷḷa poṟkollaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ camukam terivikkap pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kōvalaṉik kollumpaṭi ākkiṉi ceytāṉ. accamayattil kōvalaṉ maṉivi kaṇṇaki eṉpavaḷ rāja camukam ceṉṟu cilampu, tiruṭiya cilampalla, taṉ cilampeṉṟu rucupaṭuttiyavuṭaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tīra vicāriyāmal ikkāriyam ceytōmeṉṟu cimmācaṉat tiliruntu tiṭīreṉa viḻuntu iṟantāṉ. iccampavattil kūṭiya jaṉaṅkaḷ kōvalaṉip poyyāyk kuṟṟañcāṭṭik kolluvitta poṟkollar vītikku aḻutu ceṉṟa kaṇṇakiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟārkaḷ. avvīti muṟṟilum neruppāl aḻintaJ. iccampavam karikāl cōḻaṉ kālattil naṭantatākat terikiṟatu. tamiḻ nāṭukaḷ mūṉṟil oṉṟāṉa cōḻanāṭṭil vīṇi vācittaliṉ tiṟami kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. cēranāṭṭil iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāl vīṇiyiṉ viparam collappaṭukiṟatu. ivviraṇṭum pāṇṭiya rājyattil vistāramāyiruntateṉṟum caṅkap pulavarkaḷāl ārāycci ceyyappaṭṭu vantateṉṟum ciṟanta nūlkaḷ iruntateṉṟum itaṉ muṉ pārttirukkiṟōm. immūṉṟu rājyaṅkaḷilum mutaṉmi peṟṟirunta pāṇṭiya rājjiyam aḻintapiṉ atik kippaṟṟiya cōḻa rājāṅkattiṉ ātaraviṉ kīḻ caṅkītamum maṟṟum kalikaḷum viruttiyāki vantaṉa. pūrvattiluḷḷa rājarkaḷ oru rājyatti jeyikkum poḻutu avviṭatti luḷḷa poruḷkaḷip peritāy eṇṇāmal caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷiyum maṟṟum kali vallōriyum ciṟipiṭittup pōṉarkaḷeṉṟum taṅkaḷ nāṭṭil avarkaḷi vittu ātarittuk kalvi virutti ceytārkaḷeṉṟum itaṉmuṉ aṭikkaṭi pārttirukkiṟōm. atupōlavē karikāl cōḻaṉum palavakiyilum taṉ nāṭṭik kalvip poruḷālum celvap poruḷālum vaḷappaṭuttiṉāṉ. ivaṉippaṟṟi akkālattirunta uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār eḻutiya paṭṭiṉappāliyilum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eḻutiya cilappatikārattilum maṟṟum viparam yāvum virivākak kāṇalām.
itaṉpiṉ ivaṉ makaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi eṉṉum cōḻaṉ ki.pi. 95 mutal 105 variyum aracāṇṭāṉ.
ivaṉ puttiraṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉum avaṉ cakōtaraṉ perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpavaṉum ki.pi. 105 mutal 150 variyum aracāṇṭārkaḷ. perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpavar kālattil tirucirāppaḷḷikkuc camīpattiluḷḷa uṟiyūr rājatāṉiyākkappaṭṭatu pōlum. mēlum perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpavar oru rājacūyayākam ceytāreṉṟum ataṟkuc cōḻarājaṉum pāṇṭiyarājaṉum ilaṅki aracaṉum vantiruntār kaḷeṉṟum terikiṟatu. ivarukkuppiṉ iraṇṭu talimuṟi rājya miruntatākat terikiṟatu.
ki.pi. 130-246 varuṣam vari uṟiyūr cōḻa rājyattiṉ talinakarāyiruntaJ. ataṉ piṉ cōḻa rājarkaḷip paṟṟi viparam kāṇōm.
cumār ki.pi. 600-m varuṣattil kōcceṅkaṇṇāṉ eṉṉum cōḻarājaṉ oruvariruntatākavum ivar mikavum teyvapaktiyuṭiyavarāyc civālayaṅkaḷum viṣṇu ālayaṅkaḷumāka 70-kku mēṟpaṭṭa ālayaṅkaḷ kaṭṭiṉatākavum “Tanjore District Gazetteer”  eṉṉum pustakattiṉāl terikiṟatu.
ivarpiṉ vijayālayaṉ eṉṉum cōḻarājaṉ kāñcipurattiliruntu tañcāvūri jeyittu parakēcarivarmaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭappeyaruṭaṉ ukkal, kāñcivaram, tirukkōyilūr, cucīntiram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu āḷuki ceytu vantāreṉṟu aṅkaṅkuḷḷa kal cācaṉaṅkaḷāl terikiṟatu. ki.pi. 846 mutal 880 variyum ivar kālamām.
ivarukkuppiṉ ivar piḷḷiyākiya mutal ātittiya rājakēcarivarmaṉ ki.pi. 880-m varuṣa mutal 907-m varuṣam vari aracāṭci ceytār. ivar pāṇṭiyarājaṉākiya varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉiyum pallav aparajittiyum jeyittuk koṅku nāṭṭiyum taṉ āḷukik kuṭpaṭuttiṉār.
ivarukkuppiṉ parāntakar I allatu parakēcarivarmaṉ, vīranārāyaṇaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭap peyaruṭaṉ ki.pi. 907 mutal 947 variyum aracāṭci ceytār. ivar pāṇṭiyarājyattil rājacimmaṉōṭu ki.pi. 910-l caṇṭi ceytu jeyittār. citamparam kōyil periya capikkut taṅka ōṭukaḷ pōṭṭirukkiṟār. ivar āṭci ceyta 40 varuṣattil 37-m varuṣam ilaṅkikkup paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu avvirājaṉōṭu caṇṭi ceytu atit taṉ āḷukikkuṭpaṭuttiṉār. ivarukku aintu puttirarkaḷiruntārkaḷ.
ivarkaḷil mutalvaṉāṉa rājātityaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭappeyaruḷḷa rājakēcarivarmaṉ paṭṭattukku vantāṉ.
ivarkkup piṉ ivar tampiyākiya kaṇṭarātittaṉ paṭṭattukku vantār. ivar iṟanta piṉivar maṉivi kōṉēri rājapurattil oru kōyil kaṭṭi atil taṉ puruṣaṉ ciliyiyum stāpittu vittāḷ. ivar tampi aruñceyaṉ allatu aruccuṉā. ivar piḷḷi parāntakaṉ II allatu cuntaracōḻaṉ.
ivarkkuppiṉ ātittiyar II allatu karikālaṉ II.
ivarkkup piṉ parakēcarivarmaṉ allatu uttamaccōḻaṉ ki.pi. 970 mutal 985 variyum aracāṇṭār. ivar taṉakkuppiṉ rājarājaṉ paṭṭattukku varavēṇṭumeṉṟu vākkuk koṭuttu avar miṉarāy (Minor) irukkum poḻutu āṇṭu vantatākat terikiṟatu. rājarājacōḻaṉ eṉpavar cōḻarājarkaḷuḷ mikac ciṟantavarākat terikiṟatu. pūrvam cōḻarājyattiṉ talinakarāṉa kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil āṇṭu koṇṭirunta mutalām karikāl cōḻaṉ evvaḷavu pirapala rājaṉāyiruntāṉō appaṭiyē ivaṉumiruntāṉeṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. ivaṉ yuva rājaṉāyirukkuṅ kālattil arumoḻittēvaṉ eṉṟu peyar. tāṉ rājyattiṟku vanta mūṉṟām varuṣattil mummuṭiccōḻaṉ eṉṟu peyar vittuk koṇṭāṉ. paṉṉiraṇṭām varuṣattil kāntaḷūr mutaliya karip paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷip piṭittuk koṇṭāṉ. patiṉālām varuṣattiṉ tuvakkattil kaṅkapāṭi, nuḷampapāṭi, taṭiki pāṭi, veṅkināṭu mutaliyavikaḷi jeyittāṉ. patiṉālām varuṣattiṉ piṉ pākattil pāṇṭiyarājyattiyum patiṉāṟām varuṣam kollattiyum kaliṅkattiyum 20-m varuṣattil ilaṅkiyiyum jeyittāṉ. 29-m varuṣattil lakkatīvukaḷic cērnta 12,000 tīvukaḷiyum piṭittuk koṇṭāṉeṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. kaṭicikkālaṅkaḷil ivaṉukku jeyaṅ koṇṭāṉeṉṟum rājāsrayaṉ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ivar tañci mānakariluḷḷa pirakatīsvarar ālayattik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟa tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivvālayattil eḻutappaṭṭa cilācācaṉaṅkaḷil ivaṉum ivaṉ maṉiviyarum maṟṟum kuṭumpattārum pala tarumaṅkaḷ ceytatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. ivaṉ kālattil caṅkītamum maṟṟum aruṅkalikaḷum viruttiyākiyiruntatākak kāṇalām. ivaṉ cōḻarājyattiṉ pirapalamā yuḷḷa cila ālayaṅkaḷukkup pāṭakarkaḷi niyamittu avarkaḷukkuk kuṭiyiruppu iṭaṅkaḷum māṉiyaṅkaḷum tiṉakkaṭṭaḷiyum ēṟpaṭutti yirukkiṟāṉ. ivaṉ maraṇamaṭikiyil maturi tirunelvēli tavira ceṉṉi rājatāṉiyiluḷḷa pūmi muḻutiyum āṇṭukoṇṭiruntāṉ. iṉṉum tāṉ jeyikkiṟataṟku nāṭukaḷilliyē yeṉṟu varuttap paṭṭāṉām. mikavum parākkiramamuḷḷa ivvaracaṉ 1013-l iṟantāṉ.
ivaṉ kumāraṉ rājēntiraṉ allatu kaṅkikaṇṭa cōḻaṉ, muṭikoṇṭa cōḻaṉ, uttamacōḻaṉ eṉṟa peyarkaḷuṭaṉ ki.pi. 1010-1042 vari āṇṭāṉ. akkālattil kaliṅka nāṭṭiyum kaṅki natip piratēcaṅkaḷiyum parmā tēcattiyum jeyittut taṉ āḷukikkuḷ paṭuttiṉāṉ.
ivaṉ makaṉ rājātirājaṉ 1042-l paṭṭattukku vantāṉ. ivaṉukkum cōmēcura cāḷukkiyaṉukkum ōyātu caṇṭi naṭanta koṇṭiruntatu. ivaṉ 1052-m varuṣattil tuṅkapattirā natikkuc camīpattil kōppam eṉṟa iṭattil caṇṭiyil iṟantāṉ.
ivaṉ iṟantavuṭaṉ ivaṉ cakōtaraṉ cōmēcura cāḷukkiyaṉuṭaṉ caṇṭi ceytu veṟṟiyaṭintu 1052-1061 vari aracāṭci ceytāṉ.
ivaṉ piṉ vīra rājēntiraṉ allatu cakala puvaṉācirayar eṉpavar 1062-1070 variyum aracāṭci ceytār. ivar muntiṉa rājēntiraṉuṭiya cakōtarar. ivar kālattil kaṅkivāṭiyiliruntu vikkiramātittaṉ paṭi yeṭuttu vantāṉ. avaṉi jeyittu ōṭumpaṭi turattiṉār. vikkalaṉ eṉpavaṉi vēṅki nāṭṭil jeyittār. pejavāṭāvilum tuṅkapattirā caṅkamattilum cāḷukkiyari jeyittār. tāṉ paṭṭattukku vanta aintāvatu varuṭattil kēraḷam, pāṇṭiyam, kaliṅkam eṉṉum nāṭukaḷi jeyittu rājātirājaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭap peyaruṭaṉ aracāṭci ceytār.
ivaṉ makaṉ atirāja rājaṉ oru varuṣam āṇṭāṉ.
ataṉpiṉ rājarājaṉ kumāratti makaṉ rājēntira cōḻaṉ, kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭappeyaruṭaṉ kāñcipurattil rājap piratinitiyāyp paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatil niyamikkappaṭṭāṉ. kuntaḷatēcam eṉṟa kāvirikkariyiluḷḷa nāṭṭiṟku rājaṉāṉāṉ. pāṇṭiyarājaṉi jeyittāṉ. maṉṉārkuṭāvit taṉ vacappaṭuttikkoṇṭāṉ. tirunelvēli potiyamali, kaṉṉiyākumari, kōṭṭāṟu, ciyamali, kuṭamalināṭu, viḻiñam, cāli mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷi 1085-l jeyittāṉ. ivaṉ kālattil maturi tavira ceṉṉi rājatāṉi muḻutum cōḻa rājyattic cērntatāyiruntaJ. ivaṉ kālattil nila aḷavu ēṟpaṭṭatu. anta aḷavukōlukku srīpātam eṉṟu peyar. kaviccakkiravartti jeyaṅkoṇṭāṉ eṉpavar ivaṉ kālattiliruntār. vēṅki tēcattiyum kaliṅkattiyum 1084-l jeyittu cōḻarājākkaḷil ciṟantavarāy viḷaṅkiṉār. cakkilar ivar kālattiliruntār. ivar taṉ kālattil vaḻaṅki vanta cila cuṅkaṅkaḷit taḷḷiviṭṭataṉāl ivarkku cuṅkan tavirtta cōḻaṉ eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
ivarukkup piṉ vikkiramaccōḻaṉ 1118 mutal 1135 variyāṇṭu vantār. ivar kulōttuṅkaṉiṉ nālāvatu makaṉ.
ivarukkup piṉ iraṇṭāvatu kulōttuṅkaṉ 1135 mutal 1150 variyum tiripuvaṉac cakkiravartti eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ aracāṇṭu vantār. ivar kālattil oṭṭakkūttar iruntār.
iraṇṭāvatu rājāti rāja cōḻaṉ 1164 mutal 1178 variyum aracāṇṭār. ivar kālattil cakkilar, kampaṉ, oṭṭakkūttar, pukaḻēnti, aṭiyārkkunallār mutaliya vitva cirōmaṇi kaḷiruntatākat terikiṟatu.
mūṉṟāvatu kulōttuṅkaṉ 1178 mutal 1216 variyum aracāṭci ceytār. ivar mikunta teyva paktiyuṭiyavar. anēka civālayaṅkaḷip putitākak kaṭṭiyum anēka paḻiya civālayaṅkaḷip paḻutupārttum vittār.
ivar piṉ tiri vikkirama cakkiravartti allatu mūṉṟāvatu rājāti rāja cōḻaṉ 1216-l paṭṭattukku vantu 1244 variyum āṇṭu koṇṭiruntār.
ataṉpiṉ rājēntira cōḻa tēvar 1245-1267 variyum āṇṭār. ivarkaḷip paṟṟiya iṉṉum cila vivaraṅkaḷik kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār M.A. eḻutiya “intiya pūrva carittiram” eṉṟa pustakattilum “Tanjore District Gazertteer” eṉṉum pustakattilum kāṇalām.
rājēntira cōḻa tēvarukkuppiṉ atāvatu 1267-m varuṣattiṟkuppiṉ cariyāṉa rājākkaḷillāmal cōḻa rājyattiṉ palam varavarak kuṟintu cumār 300 varuṣaṅkaḷākap palavita tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum aṭintu vantatu. atil 1310-l mālikkāpar eṉṉum makammatiyar maturiyiyum, tirucirāppaḷḷiyiyum jeyittu 50 varuṣam āṇṭār. ataṉpiṉ cōḻa rājyam vijayanakarat taracarkaḷukkuḷ vantatu.
1335 mutal 1343 vari pukkarāyaṉum ataṉpiṉ virupāṭcaṉum cōḻa rājyatti eṭuttuk koṇṭārkaḷ.
1374-1375 variyum tirucirāppaḷḷiyik kampaṉṉa uṭiyār āṇṭu koṇṭu vantār.
1379 mutal 1391 vari tirucirāppaḷḷiyi arikararāyaṉ āṇṭār. tañcāvūril 1443-l eḻutiya kalveṭṭu tāṉa cācaṉa mūlamāyt tēvarāyariruntatākat terikiṟatu. 1455-l tirumalirāyaṉ koṭutta kalveṭṭuc cācaṉaṅkaḷum tañciyilirukkiṟaJ.
1475-m varuṣamutal 1500-m varuṣam vari vijayanakara mantirikaḷākiya cāḷuva rājākkaḷāl cōḻa rājyam āḷappaṭṭu vantirukkiṟatu. 1517 mutal 1518 variyum kiruṣṇatēvarāyar iruntatākac cācaṉaṅkaḷāl terikiṟatu. 1532-l accutatēvar pāṇṭiyaṉi jeyittut tāmparaparaṇiyiṉ kariyil oru stampam nāṭṭiṉār. 1537-l tañci nāki, karnāṭakak kari ivikaḷi jeyittu vijayanakara rājyattiṟkuc contamākkiṉār. 1539-l ivar tāṉa cācaṉam koṭuttirukkiṟār. inta accuta tēvarukku mantiriyā yirunta cēvappanāyakkar 1549 mutal 1572 variyum cōḻa rājyatti yāṇṭu vantār.
accutappanāyakkar 1572-1614 varikkum aracāṭci ceytār. ivar kālattil ivarukku mantiriyāyirunta kōvinta tīkṣataruṭiya piḷḷi vēṅkaṭamaki eṉpavar āyappāliyil varum paṉṉiru curaṅkaḷiyum avikaḷ curuti māṟumpōtuṇṭākum 72 mēḷakkarttāviyum ceytā reṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ viparam piṉpārppōm.
rekunātanāyakkar 1614 mutal āṇṭāreṉṟum avar piṉ vijayarākavanāyakkar rājya pārañceytu 1673-l maraṇamaṭintāreṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu.
ivarukkup piṉ makarāṣṭira rājyattil rājaṉāyirunta ṣājikkuc civāji eṉṟum veṅkāji yeṉṟum iraṇṭu kumārar iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷil mūttavaṉ makarāṣṭira rājyattiṟku atipatiyāṉāṉ. avaṉ tampiyākiya veṅkāji oru ciṟiya tuṇippaṭiyuṭaṉ paṭiyeṭuttu vantu tañcāvūri mikac culapamāka taṉ vacappaṭuttik koṇṭār. ivarukku ekkōjiyeṉṟu maṟupeyarum vaḻaṅkum. ivar kāla mutal cōḻa rājyam makarāṣṭira rājākkaḷāl āḷappaṭṭu vantatu. ivar 1674-1687 variyum aracāṇṭār.
ivar makaṉ ṣāji 1687-1711 variyum āṇṭār. ivar cakōtarar carapōji-ai 1711-1727 varuṣam variyum āṇṭu koṇṭiruntār.
ivar piṉ ivar cakōtarar tukkōji 1728-1735 variyum āḷuki ceytār. tukkōji iṟantapiṉ avar piḷḷikaḷ mūvaril mutalavarāṉa pāvā cākip oru varuṣamum avar maṉivi iraṇṭu varuṣamum āṇṭārkaḷ.
avarkaḷukkuppiṉ iraṇṭāvatu kumāraṉāṉa cāyāji 1740 vari iraṇṭu varuṣam āṇṭār.
mūṉṟāṅ kumāraṉāṉa piratāpaciṅ 1740-1763 variyum aracāṭci ceytār.
itaṉpiṉ 1763 mutal 1787 variyum tuḷajāji makarājā aracāṭci ceytu koṇṭiruntār. ivar kālattil caṅkīta vitti paḻiyapaṭi kavaṉattiṟku vantatu. ivar pala iṭaṅkaḷiliruntum vitvāṉkaḷi varavaḻittu appōtikkappōtu capi naṭattiṉatākat terikiṟatu. ivarum ivar paṭṭamakiṣiyum caṅkītattilum maṟṟum kalikaḷilum mikap pāṇṭittiyamuṭiyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ. tirunelvēliyil centilvēla aṇṇāvi paratattilum pāṭṭilum vīṇi vācippatilum mikunta pāṇṭittiyamuṭiyareṉṟu kēḷvippaṭṭup pallakkaṉuppa centil vēlavaṉ viruttāppayittiṉāl vara iyalāmal taṉ makaṉ makātēvaṉi aṉuppi vittār. avaṉ tañci vantapiṉ caṅkītam mikavum nalla nilikku vantateṉṟu nāḷatu variyum collappaṭukiṟatu.
ivar piṉ ivar makaṉ carapōji ciṟuvayatāyiruntatiṉāl tuḷajāji makārājāviṉ tampi amaraciṅ paṭṭattukku vantār. ivar 1787-1798 varuṣam vari āṇṭu koṇṭiruntār.
ivar piṉ carapōji II makārājā 1798-1824 variyum āṇṭu koṇṭiruntār.
ivarukkup piṉ ivar puttiraṉāṉa civāji 1824 mutal 1856 variyum āṇṭu koṇṭiruntār. ivarkkup piṉ nātaṉaṟṟuppōṉa cōḻarājyam kalvi, kittoḻil, cukātāram mutaliya naṉmikaḷi virutti ceytu varum kāruṇya āṅkilēya aracāṭcikkuṭpaṭṭaJ.
tuḷajāji makārājāviṉ kālattiliruntē caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ cōḻamaṇṭalattil atikamāṉārkaḷ eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉṟāl tuḷajāji makārājā taṉ nāṭṭiṉ atika ceḻippuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷukku māṉiyam koṭuttuk kuṭiyiruppu iṭamum koṭuttu mika mēṉmippaṭutti vittirukkiṟār. avar koṭutta māṉiyaṅkaḷil cila nāḷatu variyum apparampariyārāl aṉupavikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. makātēvaṉ aṇṇāviyiṉāl caṅkītam, caṅkītattiṉ nayamāṉa amcaṅkaḷ tañcāvūril viruttiyāṉa teṉṟum tōṉṟukiṟatu. avaṉ vamca parampariyārum avaṉ ciṣya parampariyārum cōḻamaṇṭalattilum maṟṟum tamiḻ nāṭṭilum paraviyirukkak kāṇkiṟōm.
cōḻarājyatti mutal mutal cōḻa rājākkaḷum piṉ vijaya nakarattārum ataṉ piṉ, nāyakka rājākkaḷum piṉ makarāṣṭira rājākkaḷum āṇṭukoṇṭu vantirukkiṟatāka itaṉmuṉ pārttōm.
itil ki.mu.  200, 300 varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya rājarkaḷiruntatākavum avarkaḷōṭu acōkaṉuṭiya kumāraṉum kumārattiyum uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytatākavum kāṇkiṟōm. āṉāl itaṟku vekukālattiṟku muṉṉālēyē cēracōḻa pāṇṭiya rājyaṅkaḷiruntatākap parampariyāyum purāṇaṅkaḷ itikācaṅkaḷ mūlamāyum aṟivōm. avikaḷil pāṇṭiyarājyam mikap pūrvīkamāṉa carittirattiyuṭiya teṉṟum ataṉālēyē pāṇṭiyarkaḷukkup paḻiyar eṉṟu peyar vantirukkalāmeṉṟum eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṟatu. pāṇṭiya rājyattiṉ mutal rājatāṉiyākiya teṉ maturiyiyum atilirunta tamiḻccaṅkattiyum ataṉi āṇṭu koṇṭu vanta rājākkaḷiyum tamiḻppāṣiyiyum paṟṟi itaṉmuṉ curukkamāyp pārttirukkiṟōm. akkālam toṭṭu innāḷ variyum pēccu vaḻakkattōṭum nūl vaḻakkattōṭum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum tamiḻppāṣiyiṉ aṉēkavārttikaḷ ulakattiluḷḷa pala pāṣikaḷōṭu kalantiruppatiyum itaṉmuṉ kavaṉittōm. pūrvam pāṇṭiyarājākkaḷiṉ celvākkum ceyalum varavarak kuṟintateṉṟum appaṭiyē avarkaḷ pēci vanta tamiḻp pāṣiyiṉ anēka ciṟanta vārttikaḷ varavara vaḻakkattilillāmal kuṟintateṉṟum kaṭic caṅka kālattiṟkup piṉ anniyarājarkaḷ vara anniya pāṣikaḷum atōṭu ērāḷamākak kalantateṉṟum ataṉ nimittam pala cantēkaṅkaḷuṇṭāvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟeṉṟum itaṉmuṉ pārttōm. kiṟistuvukku anēka āyira varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉṉiruntē pāṇṭiyarājyam mikavum uṉṉatamāṉa niliyiliruntu vantirukkiṟatu. ataṉpiṉ cōḻa rājarkaḷ palattu maṟṟa rājyaṅkaḷōṭum pāṇṭiyarājākkaḷōṭum caṇṭiceytu jeyittu vantirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉkiṟatākap pārkkiṟōm. ūḻikkālattiṉ māṟutalkaḷāl irājatāṉi iṭammāṟiṉālum peyar māṟāmal kaṭici variyum maturiyil pāṇṭiyarājākkaḷ āṇṭu vantirukkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl ūḻiyillātiruntum cōḻarājyattār oru iṭattil nilit tirāmal uṟiyūr, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, kāñcipuram, tañcāvūr mutaliya pala iṭaṅkaḷi oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyt talinakarākak koṇṭārkaḷeṉṟu terikiṟatu. mēlum maturiyit tavira ceṉṉi rājatāṉi muṟṟilum āṇṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum terikiṟatu. cōḻa rājyam pāṇṭiya rājyattippōla orē kulattavarālallāmal appōtikkappōtu vevvēṟu kulattavarāl cilacila kālam āṇṭu varappaṭṭaJ. pūrvam cōḻarājyatti āṇṭu koṇṭu vanta vamcattavarkaḷ nāḷatuvariyum cōḻar, cōḻatēvar, cōḻaṅkattēvar, vijayar, vijayatēvar, muṭikoṇṭāṉ eṉṟa peyarkaḷuṭaṉ jamīṉtārarākavum cilar perutta camucārikaḷākavum palar mika ēḻikaḷākavu mirukkiṟārkaḷeṉpatic cōḻanāṭṭil kāṇpōm. pūrva cōḻarājākkaḷ aṭikkaṭi pāṇṭiya rājyatti jeyittu contap paṭuttik koṇṭa kālattil pāṇṭiyarāja vamcattavar āṇṭu koṇṭirunta pala ciṟukōṭṭikaḷiyum ūrkaḷiyum tāṅkaḷ piṭittuk koṇṭu pāṇṭiyarājyattilum paraviṉārkaḷeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivarkaḷum oruvarukkoruvar poṟāmiyiṉāl oṟṟumi iḻantu kuṟinta nilikku vantārkaḷ. cēra rājyam oṉṟu māttiram ivvāpattukaḷukkellām tappi muṉpōlavē aracāṭciyuṭiyatā yiruntukoṇṭu varukiṟatu. cōḻarājyatti mēṉmippaṭutti āṇṭu vanta rājākkaḷil karikāl cōḻaṉiyē mutalvaṉākac colla vēṇṭum. avaṉ kālattil kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil paratattilum vīṇi vācippatilum mikat tērnta mātaviyum vīṇi vācippatil kitērnta kōvalaṉum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ vīṇivācippip paṟṟic cēranāṭṭiluḷḷa cēraṉceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tampi iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cilappatikārattil curukkamākac collukiṟār. atikkoṇṭu karikāl cōḻaṉ kālattilēyē tamiḻnāṭukaḷil vīṇi vāttiyamum paratanāṭṭiyamum pūrṇaniliyilirunta teṉṟu terikiṟatu. avarkaḷ kālattilēyē ṣaṭjama pañcama muṟiyāy varum paṉṉiru curaṅkaḷum āyappāli muṟiyil varukiṟatākak kāṇkiṟōm. appaṭi vaḻaṅki vanta muṟi taṟkālattavarukkut teriyāmiyiṉāl pūrvamuḷḷa vīṇikaḷil niliyāṉa meṭṭukaḷ vikkappaṭavilliyeṉṟum nakaṭṭi irākattukkut takuntavitam vittuk koḷḷum vīṇiyiruntateṉṟum nēṟṟiyuḷḷa cēvappanāyakkarukkāka āṅkilēyar curaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟa vitamāy nilivaramāṉa meṭṭukaḷ pōṭappaṭṭateṉṟum collukiṟavarkaḷumuṇṭu. itilum nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷ vaḻaṅkum vēṟucila muṟikaḷum avar kālattiliruntatu. avviparam itaṉ piṉ pārppōm.
kiṟistu piṟantu 50-95 variyum ikkarikālaṉ iruntār. ivar pala vitvāṉkaḷiyum, toḻilāḷarkaḷiyum pala iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu varavaḻittuk kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattil kuṭiyēṟṟiṉatākavum itaṉ muṉ pārttōm. ivarkkup piṉ cōḻa rājyattil viḷaṅkiya kōcceṅkaṇṇāṉ 70-kku mēṟpaṭṭa civālayaṅkaḷiyum viṣṇu ālayaṅkaḷiyum kaṭṭiṉatākak kāṇkiṟōm. ivar kālam cumār ki.pi. 600 āka irukkalāmeṉṟu cantēkikkak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.
vijayālayar kāñcipurattiliruntu 840-l tāñcāvūri jeyittā reṉṟum parakēcari varmaṉ eṉṟa mutal parāntakar eṉpavar 907-l paṭṭattukku vantu citamparam ālayattil taṅka ōṭukaḷ pōṭṭirukkiṟā reṉṟum aṟikiṟōm.
rājarājaṉ eṉṉum cōḻarājaṉ 985-l paṭṭattukku vantār. ciṟu pirāyattil ivarukku arumoḻittēvaṉ eṉṟum ataṉ piṉ mummuṭic cōḻaṉeṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅki vantatu. ivar kālattil cōḻarājyam mika uṉṉata niliyi yaṭintiruntaJ. ivar aracāṭciyiṉ kālattil tañci periyakōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kōyiliṉ cuṟṟuc cuvarkaḷil ivarāl veṭṭuvikkappaṭṭa anēkatāṉa cācaṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil ivarālum ivar kuṭumpattārālum koṭukkappaṭṭateṉṟu cācaṉaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. atōṭu ivar kālattilum kōyilkaḷilum caṅkītamuṟi naṭattum viparaṅkaḷiyum oru cācaṉattil kāṇalām. ivar kālattil pala iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu caṅkītattil tērntavarkaḷi varavaḻittu maṉi, māṉiyam, umpaḷam campaḷam koṭuttu veku peyari ātarittu vantatākap pārkkiṟōm. atu mutal caṅkītam tañci mānakaril pirapala viruttikku vantirukkalāmeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivarkku veku kālattiṟkup piṉ ivaruṭiya pēraṉ (makaḷ piḷḷi) mutal kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ paṭṭattukku vantār. ivar kālattil nilaṅkaḷiṉ aḷavu kaṇakku ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaJ. aḷavu kōlukku srīpātameṉṟu peyar. oru vēli nilattil oru catura aṅkulattiyum viṭāmal kuṟikkak kūṭiya nuṭpamāṉa aḷavu ivarāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaJ. ivar kālattil kavic cakkiravartti jeyaṅkoṇṭāṉiruntatākat terikiṟatu. iraṇṭāvatu kulōttuṅkaṉ kālattil atāvatu 1135-1150 variyilum oṭṭakkūttaṉum avar kālattukkup piṉ 1164-l paṭṭattukku vanta rājātirājaṉ kālattil cakkilar, kampaṉ, oṭṭakkūttaṉ, pukaḻēnti, aṭiyārkku nallār mutaliyavarkaḷiruntatākat terikiṟatu.
ataṉ piṉ 1572-m varuṣattil cōḻarājyattiṟku nāyakkarājarkaḷil iraṇṭāvatu aracaṉāy vanta accutappa nāyakkar kālattil avarukku mantiriyāyirunta kōvintatīkṣatar avarkaḷ kumāraṉ vēṅkaṭamakiyiruntār. ivar teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil vaḻaṅki vanta paṉṉiru curaṅkaḷiyum vittuk koṇṭu avikaḷiliruntu 72 mēḷakkarttā ceytu ataṟkuk kītamum lakṣaṇa cākittiyamum ceytār. ovvoru karttārākaṅkaḷilum iṉṉiṉṉa jaṉṉiya rākaṅkaḷ varalāmeṉṟum kuṟittār. teṉṉintiya caṅkītattil nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷōṭu vaḻaṅkum rākaṅkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa mēḷakkarttāvilaṭaṅkumeṉṟu avar kuṟittiruntālum nuṭpamāṉa curutikaḷ collappaṭavilli. ataṉāl taṟkālattuḷḷavar atiyum keṭuttu intustāṉi pōla oṉṟākka niṉikkiṟārkaḷ.
accutappa nāyakkar tañcāvūri āṇṭu koṇṭirunta kālattil caṅkītam kavaṉikkappaṭṭu vantateṉṟu teḷivāka itaṉāl aṟikiṟōm. itaṟku veku kālattiṟkuppiṉ 1763-l cōḻarājyattiṟku rājaṉāṉa tuḷajāji makārājā eṉṉum makārāṣṭira aracaṉ kālattil caṅkītam mikavum viruttiyāṉateṉṟu colla iṭantarukiṟaJ. ivar kālattil pūrva caṅkīta muṟikaḷil ciṟanta vīṇi, vāyppāṭṭu, paratam, apinayam, pullāṅkuḻal mutaliyavikaḷi virutti ceytāreṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivar kālattilirunta caṅkīta vitvāṉkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉār eṉpatiyum itaṉ piṉ pārppōm.
cōḻa rājyattil caṅkītam viruttiyākiyiruntateṉṟu colvataṟku ēṟṟavitamāy rājarājaṉ eṉṉum cōḻaṉ cumār 1000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ tañci periyakōyil cuvarkaḷil eḻuti vitta oru cācaṉatti iṅkē pārppōmāṉāl atuvē caṅkītam cumār 1000 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉpē viruttiyāṉa nilimiyiliruntateṉṟu kāṭṭum. atil cila mukkiya kuṟippi māttiram iṅkē eḻutuvatu pōtumāṉatākat tōṉṟiṉālum mikap pūrvamum muṟṟilum caṅkītakkārariyē colvatum ākiya accilācācaṉatti uḷḷapaṭiyē iṅkuk kāṭṭuvatu nalameṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. itu “South Indian inscriptions Vol. II. Pt. III” -l collappaṭukiṟatu.
3. tañcimānakaram pirakatīsvarar kōyilil kāṇappaṭum kalveṭṭuc cācaṉam.
itu kōyil veḷippirakārattiṉ matil cuvaril veḷippuṟattil vaṭamēṟku mūliyiluḷḷa kal varicikaḷil eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟaJ.
svasti srī tirumakaḷ pōlap perunilac celviyum taṉakkē yurimipūṇṭami maṉakkoḷak kāntaḷūrc cāli kalamaṟuttaruḷi veṅki nāṭuṅ kaṅkapāṭiyum taṭikipāṭiyum nuḷampapāṭiyum kuṭamalināṭuṅ kollamuṅ kaliṅkamum muraṭṭeḻil ciṅkaḷar īḻamaṇṭalamum iraṭṭapāṭi ēḻari ilakkamum muṉṉīrp paḻantīvu paṉṉīrāyiramum tiṇṭiṟalveṉṟit taṇṭāṟkoṇṭa taṉṉeḻil vaḷarūḻiyuḷellāyāṇṭun toḻutaka viḷaṅkum yāṇṭēceḻiyārit tēcukoḷ kōrājakēcari varmmarāṉa srī rājarājatēvarkkuyāṇṭu irupattoṉpatāvatu vari uṭiyār srī rājarājīsvaram uṭiyārukkut tiruppatiyam viṇṇappam ceyya uṭiyār srī rājarājatēvar kuṭutta piṭārarkaḷ nāṟpatteṇmarum ivarkaḷilē niliyāy uṭukki vācippāṉ oruvaṉum ivarkaḷilē niliyāyk koṭṭi mattaḷam vācippāṉ oruvaṉum āka aimpatiṉmarkkup peyarāl nicatam nellu mukkuṟuṇi nivantamāy rājakēsariyōṭokkum āṭavallāṉeṉṉum marakkālāl uṭiyār uḷḷūrppaṇṭārattēy peṟavum ivarkaḷil cettārkkum anātēcampōṉārkkum talimāṟu avvavarkku aṭutta muṟi kaṭavār annelluppeṟṟut tiruppatiyam viṇṇappañceyyavum avvavarkku aṭutta muṟi kaṭavār tāntām yōkyar allātuviṭil yōkyarāyiruppāri āḷiṭṭut tiruppatiyam viṇṇappañ ceyvittu annellup peṟavum avvavarkku aṭutta muṟi kaṭavāriṉṟiyoḻiyil anta niyāyattārē yōkyarāyiruppāri tiruppatiyum viṇṇappañceyya iṭṭu iṭṭa avaṉē avvavar peṟumpaṭi nellup peṟavum āka ippaṭi uṭiyār srī rājarāja tēvar tiruvāy moḻintaruḷiṉapaṭi kallil veṭṭiyatu :-
(a) iraṇṭāvatu cācaṉam
svasti srī tirumakaḷ pōlap perunilac celviyum taṉakkē yurimi pūṇṭami maṉakkoḷakkāntaḷūrc cāli kalamaṟuttaruḷi veṅki nāṭuṅ kaṅkapāṭiyum taṭikipāṭiyum nuḷampapāṭiyum kuṭamalināṭuṅ kollamum kaliṅkamum muraṭṭeḻil ciṅkaḷar īḻamaṇṭalamum iraṭṭapāṭi ēḻari ilakkamum muṉṉīrp paḻantīvu paṉṉīrāyiramum tiṇṭiṟal veṉṟit tāṇṭāṟkoṇṭa taṉṉeḻil vaḷarūḻiyuḷellāyāṇṭun toḻutaka viḷaṅkum yāṇṭē ceḻiyarit tēcukoḷ kōrājakēsari varmmarāṉa srī rājarājatēvarukku yāṇṭu irupattoṉpatāvatu vari uṭiyār srī rājarājīsvaram uṭiyārukku nivantakkārar uṭiyār srī rājarājatēvar kuṭutta nivantakkārarkaḷukkum uṭiyār srī rājarājīsvaram uṭiyār taḷiccērip peṇṭukaḷākac cōḻamaṇṭalat taḷiccērikaḷil niṉṟu koṇṭu vantu ēṟṟiṉa taḷiccēri peṇṭukaḷukkum nivantamākap paṅku ceytapaṭi paṅku vaḻi paṅku oṉṟiṉāl nilaṉvēliyiṉāl rājakēsariyōṭokkum āṭavallāṉeṉṉum marakkālāl nellu nūṟṟukkalamākavum ippaṭi paṅku peṟṟa ivarkaḷil cettārkkum aṉā tēcam pōṉārkkum talimāṟu ivvivarkku aṭuttamuṟi kaṭavār ikkāṇi peṟṟup paṇi ceyyavum aṭuttamuṟi kaṭavār tāntām yōkyar allātuviṭil yōkyarā yiruppāri āḷiṭṭu paṇi ceyvittuk koḷḷap peṟavum  aṭuttamuṟi kaṭavār illātuviṭil avvavar niyāyaṅkaḷukkut takkavaril avvavar niyāyaṅkaḷilārē yōkkiyarāyiruppāri āḷiṭṭu iṭṭa avaṉē kāṇi peṟavum āka ippaṭi uṭiyār srī rājarājatēvar tiruvāy moḻintaruḷiṉapaṭi kallil veṭṭiṉatu :-


taḷiccērip peṇṭukaḷ :-
	
	
	



	


	


	



	



	

	






	






kaṇakku
	


4. mēṟkaṇṭa cācaṉattil nām kavaṉikka vēṇṭiya cila kuṟippukaḷ.
rājarāja cōḻaṉē tañci periya kōyilik kaṭṭiṉāreṉpatu “nām eṭuppittatirukkaṟṟaḷi” eṉṟa avaratu cācaṉattiṟ kāṇum vācakattāl terikiṟatu. atōṭu avarum avar paṭṭa stirīkaḷum avar kuṭumpattāril cilarum taṅkaḷ contap poruḷkaḷiṉāl tañcāvūr tiruviyāṟu mutaliya kōyilkaḷukku anēka kiṅkariyaṅkaḷum tarmaṅkaḷum ceytirukkiṟārkaḷeṉṟu maṟṟum cila cācaṉaṅkaḷāl aṟikiṟōm. ivar ki.pi. 985-1013 variyum ēṟakkuṟiya 29 varuṣam āṇṭār eṉṟu collap paṭukiṟatu. tāṉ uyirōṭirukkum kālattil taṉ kumāraṉ rājēntiraṉukku 1010-l paṭṭaṅ kaṭṭittāṉ mēl pārttuk koṇṭu vantār. avaruṭiya aracāṭciyiṉ kaṭiciyil tañci periya kōyil kaṭṭappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. taṉ iṟutik kālattil iṉṉum nāṉ jeyippataṟku nāṭukaḷilliyē yeṉṟu avar varuttappaṭṭār eṉpatik koṇṭu ciṟu pirāyattilēyē paṭṭattukku vantatik koṇṭu viruttāppiyattiṟku muṉṉālēyē avar iṟantirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. tāṉ iṟakka 3 varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ makaṉiṭattil rājyatti oppuvittu viṭṭuk kōyil kaṭṭumāṉa vēlikaḷil taṉ muḻu nērattiyum celavaḻittirukka vēṇṭum. appaṭiyillātiruntāl pala yuttaṅkaḷum paṭiyeṭuppukaḷumuḷḷa kālattil kal kiṭikkāta tañciyil peṉṉam periya kalliṉāl ākiya nantiyum kōpurattiṉ ucciyil pāvutaṟku 251/2 aṭi caturamum 80 ṭaṉ kaṉamuḷḷa orē mūṭu kallum cēkarittuc ceyya muṭiyātu eṉṟu niṉikkiṟēṉ. mēlum kal vēli ceyta ciṟpikaḷukkum cutivēli ceytu vanta kottarkaḷukkum avarkaḷ vēli ceyyum kālattil tāmum kūṭavēyiruntu tiṉpaṇṭamum tāmpūlamum koṭuttu uṟcākappaṭutti vantatākavum terikiṟatu. oru camayattil mika arumiyāṉa vēli ceytu koṇṭirunta ciṟpācāri oruvaṉ tāmpūlattiṟkāka taṉ vēlikkāraṉiṭam piṉṉāl ki nīṭṭum camayattil niṉṟu pārttuk koṇṭirunta ivar aṟintu tāṉ tāmpūlam maṭittuk koṭuttatākavum atu caṟṟu vittiyācappaṭṭiruntatiyaṟinta ciṟpācāri tirumpip pārttu makārājaṉ eṉṟu kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟu karṇa parampariyāy nāḷatu variyum collik koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. itippōl iṉṉum anēka caṅkatikaḷic collak kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm.
iṉṉum mēṟkaṇṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷi nām kavaṉikkiyil rājarkaḷuṭiya vīram, avarkaḷaṭinta veṟṟi, avarkaḷukkuriya paṭṭap peyarkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷic cācaṉattiṉ talippil collappaṭum meyk kīrttiyil kāṇkiṟōm. ataṉ piṉ kōyilil āṭuvār, pāṭuvārāka ūḻiyañ ceyyum ūḻiyakkārarkaḷum avarkaḷ tiṉam peṟum campaḷam allatu akkaṇakkiṉpaṭi varuṣa campaḷam ivvaḷaveṉṟu avarkaḷ kuṭiyirukkum maṉikaḷiṉṉateṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu. ammuṟiyil oruvaṉukkuppiṉ oruvaṉ talimuṟi talimuṟiyāyp peṟum vivaramum atu tavaṟiṉ iṉṉār peṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟa vivaramum collappaṭukiṟatu. ivikaḷ yāviyum cācaṉattil vivaramāyk kāṇalām.
itil mutal cācaṉattil nāṟpatteṭṭu piṭārarkaḷum koṭṭi mattaḷam vācippāṉ oruvaṉum uṭukki vācippāṉ oruvaṉum āka aimpatu peyarkaḷ collappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ oru nāḷikku mukkuṟuṇi allatu āṟu marakkāl vītam campaḷam peṟṟuk kōyilil ūḻiyañ ceytu vara niyamikkappaṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. tiruppatikam viṇṇappañ ceyya ivarkaḷ niyamikkappaṭṭirukkiṟār kaḷeṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. tiruppatikam eṉpatu appar, cuntar, māṇikkavācakar, tirunāvukkariyar pōṉṟa periyavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kālattil taricitta teyvattiyum stalaṅkaḷiyum pukaḻntu pāṭiya pattup pattup pāṭalkaḷām. avikaḷē taṟkālattil tēvāram, tiruvācakam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivikaḷik kōvil pūjā kālaṅkaḷil paṇṇōṭum pakka vāttiyattōṭum pāṭumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiyatākat teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. ivi South Indian inscriptions Vol. II Part iii-l 252-m pakkattil collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
